namespace Xceed.Zip
{
	public enum DiskRequiredAction
	{
		Fail,
		Continue
	}
	public class DiskRequiredEventArgs : Xceed.FileSystem.FileSystemEventArgs
	{
		private DiskRequiredReason m_reason;

		private AbstractFile m_zipFile;

		private int m_number;

		private long m_splitSize;

		private DiskRequiredAction m_action;

		public DiskRequiredReason Reason => m_reason;

		public AbstractFile ZipFile
		{
			get
			{
				return m_zipFile;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value == null)
				{
					throw new ArgumentNullException("value", "The next zip file segment cannot be null.");
				}
				m_zipFile = value;
			}
		}

		public int DiskNumber => m_number;

		public long SplitSize
		{
			get
			{
				return m_splitSize;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value < 0)
				{
					Xceed.Utils.Exceptions.ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("value", value, "The split size cannot be less than zero.");
				}
				m_splitSize = value;
			}
		}

		public DiskRequiredAction Action
		{
			get
			{
				return m_action;
			}
			set
			{
				if (!Enum.IsDefined(typeof(DiskRequiredAction), value))
				{
					throw new ArgumentException("Unknown action.", "value");
				}
				m_action = value;
			}
		}

		internal DiskRequiredEventArgs(DiskRequiredReason reason, AbstractFile zipFile, int diskNumber, long splitSize, DiskRequiredAction defaultAction)
		{
			if (!Enum.IsDefined(typeof(DiskRequiredReason), reason))
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("Unknown reason.", "reason");
			}
			if (zipFile == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("zipFile");
			}
			if (diskNumber < 1)
			{
				Xceed.Utils.Exceptions.ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("diskNumber", diskNumber, "The disk number cannot be less than 1.");
			}
			if (splitSize < 0)
			{
				Xceed.Utils.Exceptions.ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("splitSize", splitSize, "The split size cannot be less than zero.");
			}
			if (!Enum.IsDefined(typeof(DiskRequiredAction), defaultAction))
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("Unknown default action.", "defaultAction");
			}
			m_reason = reason;
			m_zipFile = zipFile;
			m_number = diskNumber;
			m_splitSize = splitSize;
			m_action = defaultAction;
		}
	}
	public enum DiskRequiredReason
	{
		Reading,
		Updating,
		DiskFull,
		SplitSizeReached,
		Deleting
	}
	public class ZipEventsSession : FileSystemEventsSession
	{
		protected ItemProgressionEventArgs m_readingZipItemProgressionArgs;

		protected ByteProgressionEventArgs m_buildingZipByteProgressionArgs;

		protected ItemProgressionEventArgs m_buildingZipItemProgressionArgs;

		protected ByteProgressionEventArgs m_copyingZipByteProgressionArgs;

		protected ByteProgressionEventArgs m_gatheringZipContentByteProgressionArgs;

		protected ZipEvents m_zipEvents;

		protected ProgressionInfo m_zipCurrentFileBytes;

		protected ProgressionInfo m_zipAllFilesBytes;

		protected ProgressionInfo m_zipAllItems;

		public new ZipEvents Events => m_zipEvents;

		protected internal ZipEventsSession(ZipEvents events, object sender, object userData)
			: base(events, sender, userData)
		{
			m_zipEvents = events;
			m_zipCurrentFileBytes = new ProgressionInfo();
			m_zipAllFilesBytes = new ProgressionInfo();
			m_zipAllItems = new ProgressionInfo();
			m_readingZipItemProgressionArgs = new ItemProgressionEventArgs(m_zipAllItems);
			m_buildingZipByteProgressionArgs = new ByteProgressionEventArgs(m_zipCurrentFileBytes, m_zipAllFilesBytes);
			m_buildingZipItemProgressionArgs = new ItemProgressionEventArgs(m_zipAllItems);
			m_copyingZipByteProgressionArgs = new ByteProgressionEventArgs(m_zipCurrentFileBytes, m_zipAllFilesBytes);
			m_gatheringZipContentByteProgressionArgs = new ByteProgressionEventArgs(m_zipCurrentFileBytes, m_zipAllFilesBytes);
		}

		public void InitializeTotalItems(long totalItems)
		{
			m_zipAllItems.Processed = 0L;
			m_zipAllItems.Total = totalItems;
		}

		public void InitializeTotalBytes(long totalBytes)
		{
			m_zipAllFilesBytes.Processed = 0L;
			m_zipAllFilesBytes.Total = totalBytes;
		}

		public void InitializeCurrentTotalBytes(long totalBytes)
		{
			m_zipCurrentFileBytes.Processed = 0L;
			m_zipCurrentFileBytes.Total = totalBytes;
		}

		public void OnReadingZipItemProgression(FileSystemItem currentItem)
		{
			checked
			{
				if (m_zipEvents.WantsReadingZipItemProgression)
				{
					m_readingZipItemProgressionArgs.CurrentItem = currentItem;
					m_readingZipItemProgressionArgs.UserData = m_userData;
					try
					{
						m_zipEvents.OnReadingZipItemProgression(m_sender, m_readingZipItemProgressionArgs);
					}
					catch (Exception inner)
					{
						throw new TargetInvocationException(inner);
					}
					m_userData = m_readingZipItemProgressionArgs.UserData;
					m_zipAllItems.Processed++;
				}
			}
		}

		public void OnBuildingZipItemProgression(FileSystemItem currentItem)
		{
			checked
			{
				if (m_zipEvents.WantsBuildingZipItemProgression)
				{
					m_buildingZipItemProgressionArgs.CurrentItem = currentItem;
					m_buildingZipItemProgressionArgs.UserData = m_userData;
					try
					{
						m_zipEvents.OnBuildingZipItemProgression(m_sender, m_buildingZipItemProgressionArgs);
					}
					catch (Exception inner)
					{
						throw new TargetInvocationException(inner);
					}
					m_userData = m_buildingZipItemProgressionArgs.UserData;
					m_zipAllItems.Processed++;
				}
			}
		}

		public void OnBuildingZipByteProgression(long increment)
		{
			if (!m_zipEvents.WantsBuildingZipByteProgression)
			{
				return;
			}
			m_buildingZipByteProgressionArgs.UserData = m_userData;
			if (increment == 0 && m_zipCurrentFileBytes.Processed == 0)
			{
				try
				{
					m_zipEvents.OnBuildingZipByteProgression(m_sender, m_buildingZipByteProgressionArgs);
				}
				catch (Exception inner)
				{
					throw new TargetInvocationException(inner);
				}
			}
			int num;
			checked
			{
				num = (int)unchecked(m_zipCurrentFileBytes.Processed / 65536);
				m_zipCurrentFileBytes.Processed += increment;
				m_zipAllFilesBytes.Processed += increment;
			}
			if (m_zipCurrentFileBytes.Processed / 65536 > num || m_zipCurrentFileBytes.Percent == 100)
			{
				try
				{
					m_zipEvents.OnBuildingZipByteProgression(m_sender, m_buildingZipByteProgressionArgs);
				}
				catch (Exception inner2)
				{
					throw new TargetInvocationException(inner2);
				}
			}
			m_userData = m_buildingZipByteProgressionArgs.UserData;
		}

		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		[Obsolete("This should no longer be called as the zip file is now created directly in its final destination. You can override OnGatheringZipContentByteProgression to retrieve progress information during the step preceding the creation of the zip file.")]
		public void OnCopyingZipByteProgression(long increment)
		{
			if (m_zipEvents.WantsCopyingZipByteProgression)
			{
				if (increment == 0 && m_zipCurrentFileBytes.Processed == 0)
				{
					m_zipEvents.OnCopyingZipByteProgression(m_sender, m_copyingZipByteProgressionArgs);
				}
				int num;
				checked
				{
					num = (int)unchecked(m_zipCurrentFileBytes.Processed / 65536);
					m_zipCurrentFileBytes.Processed += increment;
					m_zipAllFilesBytes.Processed += increment;
					m_copyingZipByteProgressionArgs.UserData = m_userData;
				}
				if (m_zipCurrentFileBytes.Processed / 65536 > num || m_zipCurrentFileBytes.Percent == 100)
				{
					m_zipEvents.OnCopyingZipByteProgression(m_sender, m_copyingZipByteProgressionArgs);
				}
				m_userData = m_copyingZipByteProgressionArgs.UserData;
			}
		}

		public void OnGatheringZipContentByteProgression(long increment)
		{
			if (!m_zipEvents.WantsGatheringZipContentByteProgression)
			{
				return;
			}
			m_gatheringZipContentByteProgressionArgs.UserData = m_userData;
			if (increment == 0 && m_zipCurrentFileBytes.Processed == 0)
			{
				try
				{
					m_zipEvents.OnGatheringZipContentByteProgression(m_sender, m_gatheringZipContentByteProgressionArgs);
				}
				catch (Exception inner)
				{
					throw new TargetInvocationException(inner);
				}
			}
			int num;
			checked
			{
				num = (int)unchecked(m_zipCurrentFileBytes.Processed / 65536);
				m_zipCurrentFileBytes.Processed += increment;
				m_zipAllFilesBytes.Processed += increment;
			}
			if (m_zipCurrentFileBytes.Processed / 65536 > num || m_zipCurrentFileBytes.Percent == 100)
			{
				try
				{
					m_zipEvents.OnGatheringZipContentByteProgression(m_sender, m_gatheringZipContentByteProgressionArgs);
				}
				catch (Exception inner2)
				{
					throw new TargetInvocationException(inner2);
				}
			}
			m_userData = m_gatheringZipContentByteProgressionArgs.UserData;
		}

		public void OnBuildingZipItemCompletion()
		{
			if (m_zipEvents.WantsBuildingZipItemProgression && m_buildingZipItemProgressionArgs.AllItems.Processed > 0)
			{
				m_buildingZipItemProgressionArgs.UserData = m_userData;
				try
				{
					m_zipEvents.OnBuildingZipItemProgression(m_sender, m_buildingZipItemProgressionArgs);
				}
				catch (Exception inner)
				{
					throw new TargetInvocationException(inner);
				}
				m_buildingZipItemProgressionArgs.CurrentItem = null;
				m_buildingZipItemProgressionArgs.TargetItem = null;
				m_userData = m_buildingZipItemProgressionArgs.UserData;
			}
		}

		public void OnReadingZipItemCompletion()
		{
			if (m_zipEvents.WantsReadingZipItemProgression && m_readingZipItemProgressionArgs.AllItems.Processed > 0)
			{
				m_readingZipItemProgressionArgs.UserData = m_userData;
				try
				{
					m_zipEvents.OnReadingZipItemProgression(m_sender, m_readingZipItemProgressionArgs);
				}
				catch (Exception inner)
				{
					throw new TargetInvocationException(inner);
				}
				m_readingZipItemProgressionArgs.CurrentItem = null;
				m_readingZipItemProgressionArgs.TargetItem = null;
				m_userData = m_readingZipItemProgressionArgs.UserData;
			}
		}

		public void OnCopyingZipByteCompletion()
		{
			throw new FileSystemInternalException("The CopyingZipByteCompletion event is now obsolete and should not be raised.");
		}

		public void OnGatheringZipContentByteCompletion()
		{
			checked
			{
				if (m_zipCurrentFileBytes.Total - m_zipCurrentFileBytes.Processed > 0)
				{
					OnGatheringZipContentByteProgression(m_zipCurrentFileBytes.Total - m_zipCurrentFileBytes.Processed);
				}
			}
		}

		public void OnBuildingZipByteCompletion()
		{
			checked
			{
				if (m_zipCurrentFileBytes.Total - m_zipCurrentFileBytes.Processed > 0)
				{
					OnBuildingZipByteProgression(m_zipCurrentFileBytes.Total - m_zipCurrentFileBytes.Processed);
				}
			}
		}

		public void OnDiskRequired(DiskRequiredEventArgs e)
		{
			if (m_zipEvents.WantsDiskRequired)
			{
				e.CurrentItem = m_currentItem;
				e.TargetItem = m_targetItem;
				e.UserData = m_userData;
				try
				{
					m_zipEvents.OnDiskRequired(m_sender, e);
				}
				catch (Exception inner)
				{
					throw new TargetInvocationException(inner);
				}
				m_userData = e.UserData;
			}
		}
	}
	internal abstract class ExtraHeader
	{
		private short m_signature;

		public short Signature => m_signature;

		public virtual bool Volatile => false;

		private ExtraHeader()
		{
		}

		protected ExtraHeader(short signature)
		{
			m_signature = signature;
		}

		public abstract ExtraHeader CreateInstance(ItemHeader header);

		public abstract bool Read(ItemHeader header, byte[] data, int offset, int size, bool fromCentralHeader);

		public abstract short Write(ItemHeader header, BinaryWriter writer, bool forCentralHeader);
	}
	internal class AesExtraHeader : ExtraHeader
	{
		public const ushort AesSignature = 39169;

		public const short VendorVersion = 2;

		public const short VendorID = 17729;

		public override bool Volatile => true;

		public AesExtraHeader()
			: base(-26367)
		{
		}

		public override ExtraHeader CreateInstance(ItemHeader header)
		{
			return this;
		}

		public override bool Read(ItemHeader header, byte[] data, int offset, int size, bool fromCentralHeader)
		{
			if (header == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("header");
			}
			if (data == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("data");
			}
			if (offset < 0 || offset > data.Length)
			{
				Xceed.Utils.Exceptions.ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("offset", offset, "The offset is out of range.");
			}
			short num3;
			int encryptionStrength;
			checked
			{
				if (size < 0 || offset + size > data.Length)
				{
					Xceed.Utils.Exceptions.ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("size", size, "The size is out of range.");
				}
				if (size < 7)
				{
					throw new InvalidZipStructureException("The FileTimes extra header's reported size is too small.");
				}
				short num = BitConverter.ToInt16(data, offset);
				short num2 = BitConverter.ToInt16(data, offset + 2);
				byte b = data[offset + 4];
				num3 = BitConverter.ToInt16(data, offset + 5);
				if (num != 1 && num != 2)
				{
					throw new FileSystemNotSupportedException("Unknown AES extra header version.", null);
				}
				if (num2 != 17729)
				{
					throw new FileSystemNotSupportedException("Unknown AES extra header vendor ID.", null);
				}
				if (b < 1 || b > 3)
				{
					throw new InvalidZipStructureException("The AES extra header contains an invalid strength value.");
				}
				encryptionStrength = b * 64 + 64;
			}
			header.CompressionMethod = (ZipCompressionMethod)checked((ushort)num3);
			header.EncryptionMethod = EncryptionMethod.WinZipAes;
			header.EncryptionStrength = encryptionStrength;
			return true;
		}

		public override short Write(ItemHeader header, BinaryWriter writer, bool forCentralHeader)
		{
			if (header == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("header");
			}
			if (writer == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("writer");
			}
			if (!header.Encrypted || header.EncryptionMethod != EncryptionMethod.WinZipAes)
			{
				return 0;
			}
			writer.Write(base.Signature);
			writer.Write((short)7);
			writer.Write((short)2);
			writer.Write((short)17729);
			int num = (header.EncryptionStrength - 64) / 64;
			if (num < 1 || num > 3)
			{
				throw new FileSystemInternalException("Unexpected encryption strength while writing AES extra header.");
			}
			writer.Write((byte)num);
			writer.Write(checked((short)header.CompressionMethod));
			return 11;
		}
	}
	internal class ExtendedTimeStampExtraHeader : ExtraHeader
	{
		public const short ExtendedTimeStampSignature = 21589;

		public ExtendedTimeStampExtraHeader()
			: base(21589)
		{
		}

		public override ExtraHeader CreateInstance(ItemHeader header)
		{
			return this;
		}

		public override bool Read(ItemHeader header, byte[] data, int offset, int size, bool fromCentralHeader)
		{
			if (header == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("header");
			}
			if (data == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("data");
			}
			if (offset < 0 || offset > data.Length)
			{
				Xceed.Utils.Exceptions.ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("offset", offset, "The offset is out of range.");
			}
			checked
			{
				if (size < 0 || offset + size > data.Length)
				{
					Xceed.Utils.Exceptions.ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("size", size, "The size is out of range.");
				}
				if (size < 1)
				{
					throw new InvalidZipStructureException("The Extended TimeStamp extra header's reported size is too small.");
				}
				bool flag = (data[offset] & 1) == 1;
				bool flag2 = (data[offset] & 2) == 2;
				bool flag3 = (data[offset] & 4) == 4;
				offset++;
				size--;
				if (flag)
				{
					if (size < 4)
					{
						throw new InvalidZipStructureException("The Extended TimeStamp extra header's reported size is too small.");
					}
					offset += 4;
					size -= 4;
				}
				if (flag2 && !fromCentralHeader)
				{
					if (size < 4)
					{
						throw new InvalidZipStructureException("The Extended TimeStamp extra header's reported size is too small.");
					}
					if (header.LastAccessedDateTime == DateTime.MinValue)
					{
						header.LastAccessedDateTime = Dates.FromUnixDateTime(BitConverter.ToUInt32(data, offset));
					}
					offset += 4;
					size -= 4;
				}
				if (flag3 && !fromCentralHeader)
				{
					if (size < 4)
					{
						throw new InvalidZipStructureException("The Extended TimeStamp extra header's reported size is too small.");
					}
					if (header.CreationDateTime == DateTime.MinValue)
					{
						header.CreationDateTime = Dates.FromUnixDateTime(BitConverter.ToUInt32(data, offset));
					}
					offset += 4;
					size -= 4;
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

		public override short Write(ItemHeader header, BinaryWriter writer, bool forCentralHeader)
		{
			if (header == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("header");
			}
			if (writer == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("writer");
			}
			writer.Write(base.Signature);
			checked
			{
				if (forCentralHeader)
				{
					writer.Write((short)5);
					byte b = 1;
					if (header.LastAccessedDateTime != DateTime.MinValue)
					{
						b |= 2;
					}
					if (header.LastAccessedDateTime != DateTime.MinValue)
					{
						b |= 4;
					}
					writer.Write(b);
					writer.Write(Dates.ToUnixDateTime(header.LastModifiedDateTime));
					return 9;
				}
				byte b2 = 1;
				short num = 5;
				if (header.LastAccessedDateTime != DateTime.MinValue)
				{
					b2 |= 2;
					num += 4;
				}
				if (header.LastAccessedDateTime != DateTime.MinValue)
				{
					b2 |= 4;
					num += 4;
				}
				writer.Write(num);
				writer.Write(b2);
				writer.Write(Dates.ToUnixDateTime(header.LastModifiedDateTime));
				if ((b2 & 2) == 2)
				{
					writer.Write(Dates.ToUnixDateTime(header.LastAccessedDateTime));
				}
				if ((b2 & 4) == 4)
				{
					writer.Write(Dates.ToUnixDateTime(header.CreationDateTime));
				}
				return (short)(num + 4);
			}
		}
	}
}
namespace Xceed.Utils.Collections
{
	[ListBindable(false)]
	public abstract class ListBase : IList, ICollection, IEnumerable
	{
		private class Enumerator : IEnumerator
		{
			private ListBase m_listBase;

			private int m_currentIndex;

			private int m_version;

			public object Current => m_listBase.ListGetItem(m_currentIndex);

			internal Enumerator(ListBase list)
			{
				m_listBase = list;
				m_currentIndex = -1;
				m_version = list.m_version;
			}

			public bool MoveNext()
			{
				if (m_version != m_listBase.m_version)
				{
					throw new InvalidOperationException("Collection was modified; enumeration operation may not execute.");
				}
				checked
				{
					m_currentIndex++;
					if (m_currentIndex < m_listBase.m_count)
					{
						return true;
					}
					m_currentIndex = m_listBase.m_count;
					return false;
				}
			}

			public void Reset()
			{
				m_currentIndex = -1;
			}
		}

		private const int DefaultCapacity = 16;

		private object[] m_array;

		private int m_count;

		[NonSerialized]
		private int m_version;

		object IList.this[int index]
		{
			get
			{
				return ListGetItem(index);
			}
			set
			{
				ListSetItem(index, value);
			}
		}

		public virtual bool IsFixedSize => false;

		public virtual bool IsReadOnly => false;

		protected Array Array => m_array;

		public bool IsSynchronized => m_array.IsSynchronized;

		public int Count => m_count;

		public object SyncRoot => m_array.SyncRoot;

		void IList.RemoveAt(int index)
		{
			ListRemoveAt(index);
		}

		void IList.Insert(int index, object value)
		{
			ListInsert(index, value);
		}

		void IList.Remove(object value)
		{
			ListRemove(value);
		}

		bool IList.Contains(object value)
		{
			return ListContains(value);
		}

		void IList.Clear()
		{
			ListClear();
		}

		int IList.IndexOf(object value)
		{
			return ListIndexOf(value);
		}

		int IList.Add(object value)
		{
			return ListAdd(value);
		}

		protected void ListRemoveAt(int index)
		{
			if (IsReadOnly || IsFixedSize)
			{
				throw new NotSupportedException("Cannot remove an item from a ReadOnly or FixedSize list.");
			}
			InternalRemoveAt(index);
		}

		protected void ListInsert(int index, object value)
		{
			if (IsReadOnly || IsFixedSize)
			{
				throw new NotSupportedException("Cannot insert an item in a ReadOnly or FixedSize list.");
			}
			InternalInsert(index, value);
		}

		protected void ListRemove(object value)
		{
			if (IsReadOnly || IsFixedSize)
			{
				throw new NotSupportedException("Cannot remove an item from a ReadOnly or FixedSize list.");
			}
			InternalRemove(value);
		}

		protected bool ListContains(object value)
		{
			return Array.IndexOf(m_array, value, 0, m_count) != -1;
		}

		protected void ListClear()
		{
			if (IsReadOnly || IsFixedSize)
			{
				throw new NotSupportedException("Cannot clear a ReadOnly or FixedSize list.");
			}
			InternalClear();
		}

		protected int ListIndexOf(object value)
		{
			return Array.IndexOf(m_array, value, 0, m_count);
		}

		protected int ListAdd(object value)
		{
			if (IsReadOnly || IsFixedSize)
			{
				throw new NotSupportedException("Cannot add an item to a ReadOnly or FixedSize list.");
			}
			return InternalAdd(value);
		}

		protected object ListGetItem(int index)
		{
			if (index < 0 || index >= m_count)
			{
				Xceed.Utils.Exceptions.ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("index", index, "The index must be greater than or equal to 0 and less than Count.");
			}
			return m_array[index];
		}

		protected void ListSetItem(int index, object value)
		{
			if (IsReadOnly)
			{
				throw new NotSupportedException("Cannot set the value of an item contained in a ReadOnly list.");
			}
			InternalSetItem(index, value);
		}

		protected int ArrayAdd(object value)
		{
			int count = m_count;
			checked
			{
				if (m_count == m_array.Length)
				{
					EnsureCapacity(m_count + 1);
				}
				m_array[count] = value;
				m_count++;
			}
			m_version++;
			return count;
		}

		protected void ArrayInsert(int index, object value)
		{
			if (index < 0 || index > m_count)
			{
				Xceed.Utils.Exceptions.ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("index", index, "The index must be greater than or equal to 0 and less than or equal to Count.");
			}
			checked
			{
				if (m_count == m_array.Length)
				{
					EnsureCapacity(m_count + 1);
				}
				if (index < m_count)
				{
					Array.Copy(m_array, index, m_array, index + 1, m_count - index);
				}
				m_array[index] = value;
				m_count++;
			}
			m_version++;
		}

		protected void ArrayRemoveAt(int index)
		{
			if (index < 0 || index >= m_count)
			{
				Xceed.Utils.Exceptions.ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("index", index, "The index must be greater than or equal to 0 and less than Count.");
			}
			checked
			{
				m_count--;
				if (index < m_count)
				{
					Array.Copy(m_array, index + 1, m_array, index, m_count - index);
				}
				m_array[m_count] = null;
			}
			m_version++;
		}

		protected void ArrayClear()
		{
			m_array = new object[0];
			m_count = 0;
			m_version++;
		}

		protected void ArrayInit(object[] objects)
		{
			m_count = objects.Length;
			m_array = new object[m_count];
			objects.CopyTo(m_array, 0);
			m_version++;
		}

		internal bool ArraySetChildIndex(object child, int newIndex)
		{
			if (child == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("child");
			}
			int num = ListIndexOf(child);
			if (num == -1)
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("The specified child does not exist in this collection.", "child");
			}
			if (newIndex < 0)
			{
				newIndex = 0;
			}
			checked
			{
				if (newIndex >= m_count)
				{
					newIndex = m_count - 1;
				}
				if (newIndex == num)
				{
					return false;
				}
				if (newIndex < num)
				{
					Array.Copy(m_array, newIndex, m_array, newIndex + 1, num - newIndex);
				}
				else
				{
					Array.Copy(m_array, num + 1, m_array, num, newIndex - num);
				}
				m_array[newIndex] = child;
			}
			m_version++;
			return true;
		}

		protected internal int InternalAdd(object value)
		{
			OnValidate(value);
			bool cancel = false;
			int index = m_count;
			OnInsert(ref index, value, ref cancel);
			if (!cancel)
			{
				checked
				{
					if (m_count == m_array.Length)
					{
						EnsureCapacity(m_count + 1);
					}
					m_array[index] = value;
					m_count++;
				}
				m_version++;
				try
				{
					OnInsertComplete(index, value);
					return index;
				}
				catch
				{
					checked
					{
						m_count--;
						m_array[m_count] = null;
					}
					m_version++;
					throw;
				}
			}
			return -1;
		}

		protected internal void InternalInsert(int index, object value)
		{
			if (index < 0 || index > m_count)
			{
				Xceed.Utils.Exceptions.ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("index", index, "The index must be greater than or equal to 0 and less than or equal to Count.");
			}
			OnValidate(value);
			bool cancel = false;
			OnInsert(ref index, value, ref cancel);
			if (cancel)
			{
				return;
			}
			checked
			{
				if (m_count == m_array.Length)
				{
					EnsureCapacity(m_count + 1);
				}
				if (index < m_count)
				{
					Array.Copy(m_array, index, m_array, index + 1, m_count - index);
				}
				m_array[index] = value;
				m_count++;
			}
			m_version++;
			try
			{
				OnInsertComplete(index, value);
			}
			catch
			{
				checked
				{
					m_count--;
					if (index < m_count)
					{
						Array.Copy(m_array, index + 1, m_array, index, m_count - index);
					}
					m_array[m_count] = null;
				}
				m_version++;
				throw;
			}
		}

		protected internal void InternalRemoveAt(int index)
		{
			if (index < 0 || index >= m_count)
			{
				Xceed.Utils.Exceptions.ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("index", index, "The index must be greater than or equal to 0 and less than Count.");
			}
			object value = m_array[index];
			bool cancel = false;
			OnRemove(index, value, ref cancel);
			if (!cancel)
			{
				checked
				{
					m_count--;
					if (index < m_count)
					{
						Array.Copy(m_array, index + 1, m_array, index, m_count - index);
					}
					m_array[m_count] = null;
				}
				m_version++;
				OnRemoveComplete(index, value);
			}
		}

		protected internal void InternalRemove(object value)
		{
			OnValidate(value);
			int num = ListIndexOf(value);
			if (num != -1)
			{
				InternalRemoveAt(num);
			}
		}

		protected internal void InternalClear()
		{
			bool cancel = false;
			OnClear(ref cancel);
			if (!cancel)
			{
				m_array = new object[0];
				m_count = 0;
				m_version++;
				OnClearComplete();
			}
		}

		protected internal void InternalSetItem(int index, object value)
		{
			if (index < 0 || index >= m_count)
			{
				Xceed.Utils.Exceptions.ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("index", index, "The index must be greater than or equal to 0 and less than Count.");
			}
			OnValidate(value);
			object obj = m_array[index];
			bool cancel = false;
			OnSet(index, obj, value, ref cancel);
			if (!cancel)
			{
				m_array[index] = value;
				m_version++;
				try
				{
					OnSetComplete(index, obj, value);
				}
				catch
				{
					m_array[index] = obj;
					throw;
				}
			}
		}

		public void CopyTo(Array array, int startIndex)
		{
			if (m_count > checked(array.GetUpperBound(0) + 1 - startIndex))
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("Destination array is not long enough.");
			}
			Array.Copy(m_array, 0, array, startIndex, m_count);
		}

		IEnumerator IEnumerable.GetEnumerator()
		{
			return new Enumerator(this);
		}

		protected ListBase()
		{
			m_array = new object[0];
		}

		protected ListBase(int initialCapacity)
		{
			m_array = new object[initialCapacity];
		}

		protected ListBase(ListBase template)
			: this()
		{
			if (template == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("template");
			}
			m_count = template.m_count;
			if (m_count != 0)
			{
				m_array = new object[m_count];
				Array.Copy(template.m_array, 0, m_array, 0, m_count);
			}
			else
			{
				m_array = new object[0];
			}
		}

		protected void EnsureCapacity(int min)
		{
			int num = m_array.Length;
			if (num < min)
			{
				int num2 = ((num == 0) ? 16 : checked(num * 2));
				if (num2 < min)
				{
					num2 = min;
				}
				object[] array = new object[num2];
				Array.Copy(m_array, 0, array, 0, num);
				m_array = array;
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnInsert(ref int index, object value, ref bool cancel)
		{
		}

		protected virtual void OnInsertComplete(int index, object value)
		{
		}

		protected virtual void OnRemove(int index, object value, ref bool cancel)
		{
		}

		protected virtual void OnRemoveComplete(int index, object value)
		{
		}

		protected virtual void OnClear(ref bool cancel)
		{
		}

		protected virtual void OnClearComplete()
		{
		}

		protected virtual void OnSet(int index, object oldValue, object newValue, ref bool cancel)
		{
		}

		protected virtual void OnSetComplete(int index, object oldValue, object newValue)
		{
		}

		protected virtual void OnValidate(object value)
		{
			if (value == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("value");
			}
		}
	}
}
namespace Xceed.Zip
{
	internal class ExtraHeaderList : ListBase
	{
		public ExtraHeader this[int index] => (ExtraHeader)ListGetItem(index);

		public override bool IsFixedSize => false;

		public override bool IsReadOnly => false;

		internal ExtraHeaderList()
		{
		}

		internal ExtraHeaderList(ExtraHeaderList template)
			: base(template)
		{
		}

		public bool Contains(ExtraHeader value)
		{
			return ListContains(value);
		}

		public int IndexOf(ExtraHeader value)
		{
			return ListIndexOf(value);
		}

		public void RemoveAt(int index)
		{
			ListRemoveAt(index);
		}

		public void Insert(int index, ExtraHeader value)
		{
			ListInsert(index, value);
		}

		public void Remove(ExtraHeader value)
		{
			ListRemove(value);
		}

		public void Clear()
		{
			ListClear();
		}

		public int Add(ExtraHeader value)
		{
			return ListAdd(value);
		}

		public void AddRange(ExtraHeader[] values)
		{
			foreach (ExtraHeader value in values)
			{
				Add(value);
			}
		}

		public ExtraHeader Find(short signature)
		{
			foreach (ExtraHeader item in (IEnumerable)this)
			{
				if (item.Signature == signature)
				{
					return item;
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		protected override void OnValidate(object value)
		{
			base.OnValidate(value);
			if (!(value is ExtraHeader))
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("Only ExtraHeader types can be added to a ExtraHeaderList.", "value");
			}
		}
	}
	[Flags]
	public enum ExtraHeaders
	{
		None = 0,
		Unicode = 1,
		FileTimes = 2,
		ExtendedTimeStamp = 4
	}
	internal class FileTimesExtraHeader : ExtraHeader
	{
		public const short FileTimesSignature = 10;

		private const short FileTimesTag = 1;

		public FileTimesExtraHeader()
			: base(10)
		{
		}

		public override ExtraHeader CreateInstance(ItemHeader header)
		{
			return this;
		}

		public override bool Read(ItemHeader header, byte[] data, int offset, int size, bool fromCentralHeader)
		{
			if (header == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("header");
			}
			if (data == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("data");
			}
			if (offset < 0 || offset > data.Length)
			{
				Xceed.Utils.Exceptions.ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("offset", offset, "The offset is out of range.");
			}
			checked
			{
				if (size < 0 || offset + size > data.Length)
				{
					Xceed.Utils.Exceptions.ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("size", size, "The size is out of range.");
				}
				if (size < 4)
				{
					throw new InvalidZipStructureException("The FileTimes extra header's reported size is too small.");
				}
				int num = 4;
				while (num < size)
				{
					if (num + 4 > size)
					{
						throw new InvalidZipStructureException("The FileTimes extra header's reported size is too small.");
					}
					short num2 = BitConverter.ToInt16(data, offset + num);
					short num3 = BitConverter.ToInt16(data, offset + num + 2);
					num += 4;
					if (num2 == 0 && num3 == 0 && size - num == 24)
					{
						num2 = 1;
						num3 = (short)(size - num);
					}
					if (num + num3 > size)
					{
						throw new InvalidZipStructureException("The FileTimes extra header's reported size is too small.");
					}
					short num4 = num2;
					if (num4 == 1)
					{
						if (num3 < 24)
						{
							throw new InvalidZipStructureException("The FileTimes extra header contains an invalid map size.");
						}
						if (fromCentralHeader)
						{
							header.LastModifiedDateTime = DateTime.FromFileTime(BitConverter.ToInt64(data, offset + num));
							header.LastAccessedDateTime = DateTime.FromFileTime(BitConverter.ToInt64(data, offset + num + 8));
							header.CreationDateTime = DateTime.FromFileTime(BitConverter.ToInt64(data, offset + num + 16));
							header.CanChangeDates = false;
						}
						num += num3;
					}
					else
					{
						num += num3;
					}
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

		public override short Write(ItemHeader header, BinaryWriter writer, bool forCentralHeader)
		{
			if (header == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("header");
			}
			if (writer == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("writer");
			}
			writer.Write(base.Signature);
			if (forCentralHeader)
			{
				writer.Write((short)32);
				writer.Write(0);
				writer.Write((short)1);
				writer.Write((short)24);
				long value = header.LastModifiedDateTime.ToFileTime();
				writer.Write(value);
				if (header.LastAccessedDateTime == DateTime.MinValue)
				{
					writer.Write(value);
				}
				else
				{
					writer.Write(header.LastAccessedDateTime.ToFileTime());
				}
				if (header.CreationDateTime == DateTime.MinValue)
				{
					writer.Write(value);
				}
				else
				{
					writer.Write(header.CreationDateTime.ToFileTime());
				}
				return 36;
			}
			writer.Write((short)4);
			writer.Write(0);
			return 8;
		}
	}
	internal class UnicodeExtraHeader : ExtraHeader
	{
		public const short UnicodeSignature = 21838;

		private const int InnerUnicodeSignature = 1480807758;

		public UnicodeExtraHeader()
			: base(21838)
		{
		}

		public override ExtraHeader CreateInstance(ItemHeader header)
		{
			return this;
		}

		public override bool Read(ItemHeader header, byte[] data, int offset, int size, bool fromCentralHeader)
		{
			if (header == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("header");
			}
			if (data == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("data");
			}
			if (offset < 0 || offset > data.Length)
			{
				Xceed.Utils.Exceptions.ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("offset", offset, "The offset is out of range.");
			}
			checked
			{
				if (size < 0 || offset + size > data.Length)
				{
					Xceed.Utils.Exceptions.ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("size", size, "The size is out of range.");
				}
				int num = (fromCentralHeader ? 8 : 6);
				if (size < num)
				{
					throw new InvalidZipStructureException("The Unicode extra header's reported size is too small.");
				}
				int num2 = BitConverter.ToInt32(data, offset);
				offset += 4;
				if (num2 != 1480807758)
				{
					return false;
				}
				short num3 = BitConverter.ToInt16(data, offset);
				offset += 2;
				short num4 = 0;
				if (fromCentralHeader)
				{
					num4 = BitConverter.ToInt16(data, offset);
					offset += 2;
				}
				if (num3 * 2 + num4 * 2 > size - num)
				{
					throw new InvalidZipStructureException("The Unicode extra header's reported filename and comment sizes exceed the extra header size.");
				}
				if (num3 > 0)
				{
					string filename = System.Text.Encoding.Unicode.GetString(data, offset, num3 * 2).Replace('/', Path.DirectorySeparatorChar);
					offset += num3 * 2;
					filename = ItemHeader.FixFilename(filename);
					if (fromCentralHeader)
					{
						header.FileName = filename;
					}
				}
				if (num4 > 0)
				{
					header.FileComment = System.Text.Encoding.Unicode.GetString(data, offset, num4 * 2);
					offset += num4 * 2;
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

		public override short Write(ItemHeader header, BinaryWriter writer, bool forCentralHeader)
		{
			if (header == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("header");
			}
			if (writer == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("writer");
			}
			writer.Write(base.Signature);
			short num = 6;
			string s = ItemHeader.UnfixFilename(header.FileName);
			byte[] bytes = System.Text.Encoding.Unicode.GetBytes(s);
			checked
			{
				num += (short)bytes.Length;
				byte[] array = null;
				if (forCentralHeader)
				{
					array = System.Text.Encoding.Unicode.GetBytes(header.FileComment);
					num += (short)(array.Length + 2);
				}
				writer.Write(num);
				writer.Write(1480807758);
				writer.Write((short)unchecked(bytes.Length / 2));
				if (forCentralHeader)
				{
					writer.Write((short)unchecked(array.Length / 2));
				}
				if (bytes.Length > 0)
				{
					writer.Write(bytes);
				}
				if (forCentralHeader && array.Length > 0)
				{
					writer.Write(array);
				}
				return num;
			}
		}
	}
	internal class UnknownExtraHeader : ExtraHeader
	{
		private byte[] m_localData;

		private byte[] m_centralData;

		public UnknownExtraHeader(short signature)
			: base(signature)
		{
		}

		public override ExtraHeader CreateInstance(ItemHeader header)
		{
			throw new NotSupportedException("An UnknownExtraHeader cannot be used as a template.");
		}

		public override bool Read(ItemHeader header, byte[] data, int offset, int size, bool fromCentralHeader)
		{
			if (header == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("header");
			}
			if (data == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("data");
			}
			if (offset < 0 || offset > data.Length)
			{
				Xceed.Utils.Exceptions.ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("offset", offset, "The offset is out of range.");
			}
			if (size < 0 || checked(offset + size) > data.Length)
			{
				Xceed.Utils.Exceptions.ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("size", size, "The size is out of range.");
			}
			if (fromCentralHeader)
			{
				m_centralData = new byte[size];
				if (size > 0)
				{
					Array.Copy(data, offset, m_centralData, 0, size);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				m_localData = new byte[size];
				if (size > 0)
				{
					Array.Copy(data, offset, m_localData, 0, size);
				}
			}
			return true;
		}

		public override short Write(ItemHeader header, BinaryWriter writer, bool forCentralHeader)
		{
			if (header == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("header");
			}
			if (writer == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("writer");
			}
			byte[] array = (forCentralHeader ? m_centralData : m_localData);
			if (array == null)
			{
				return 0;
			}
			writer.Write(base.Signature);
			checked
			{
				writer.Write((short)array.Length);
				if (array.Length > 0)
				{
					writer.Write(array);
				}
				return (short)(array.Length + 4);
			}
		}
	}
	internal class Zip64ExtraHeader : ExtraHeader
	{
		public const short Zip64Signature = 1;

		public override bool Volatile => true;

		public Zip64ExtraHeader()
			: base(1)
		{
		}

		public override ExtraHeader CreateInstance(ItemHeader header)
		{
			return this;
		}

		public override bool Read(ItemHeader header, byte[] data, int offset, int size, bool fromCentralHeader)
		{
			if (header == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("header");
			}
			if (data == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("data");
			}
			if (offset < 0 || offset > data.Length)
			{
				Xceed.Utils.Exceptions.ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("offset", offset, "The offset is out of range.");
			}
			checked
			{
				if (size < 0 || offset + size > data.Length)
				{
					Xceed.Utils.Exceptions.ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("size", size, "The size is out of range.");
				}
				bool flag = header.UncompressedSize >= uint.MaxValue || (header.CompressedSize >= uint.MaxValue && !fromCentralHeader);
				bool flag2 = header.CompressedSize >= uint.MaxValue || (flag && !fromCentralHeader);
				if (flag)
				{
					if (size < 8)
					{
						throw new InvalidZipStructureException("The Zip64 extra header's reported size is too small.");
					}
					header.UncompressedSize = (long)BitConverter.ToUInt64(data, offset);
					size -= 8;
					offset += 8;
				}
				if (flag2)
				{
					if (size < 8)
					{
						throw new InvalidZipStructureException("The Zip64 extra header's reported size is too small.");
					}
					header.CompressedSize = (long)BitConverter.ToUInt64(data, offset);
					size -= 8;
					offset += 8;
				}
				if (fromCentralHeader)
				{
					if (header.LocalHeaderOffset >= uint.MaxValue)
					{
						if (size < 8)
						{
							throw new InvalidZipStructureException("The Zip64 extra header's reported size is too small.");
						}
						header.LocalHeaderOffset = (long)BitConverter.ToUInt64(data, offset);
						size -= 8;
						offset += 8;
					}
					if (header.LocalHeaderDisk >= 65535)
					{
						if (size < 4)
						{
							throw new InvalidZipStructureException("The Zip64 extra header's reported size is too small.");
						}
						header.LocalHeaderDisk = BitConverter.ToUInt32(data, offset);
						size -= 4;
						offset += 4;
					}
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

		public override short Write(ItemHeader header, BinaryWriter writer, bool forCentralHeader)
		{
			if (header == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("header");
			}
			if (writer == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("writer");
			}
			bool flag = header.UncompressedSize >= uint.MaxValue;
			bool flag2 = header.CompressedSize >= uint.MaxValue;
			if (!forCentralHeader && flag != flag2)
			{
				flag2 = (flag = true);
			}
			bool flag3 = forCentralHeader && header.LocalHeaderOffset >= uint.MaxValue;
			bool flag4 = forCentralHeader && header.LocalHeaderDisk >= 65535;
			checked
			{
				int num = (flag ? 8 : 0) + (flag2 ? 8 : 0) + (flag3 ? 8 : 0) + (flag4 ? 4 : 0);
				if (num == 0)
				{
					return 0;
				}
				Licenser.VerifyLicense(FeatureID.Zip64);
				writer.Write(base.Signature);
				writer.Write((ushort)num);
				if (flag)
				{
					writer.Write((ulong)header.UncompressedSize);
				}
				if (flag2)
				{
					writer.Write((ulong)header.CompressedSize);
				}
				if (flag3)
				{
					writer.Write((ulong)header.LocalHeaderOffset);
				}
				if (flag4)
				{
					writer.Write(header.LocalHeaderDisk);
				}
				return (short)num;
			}
		}
	}
}
namespace Xceed.FileSystem.Quick
{
	[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
	public abstract class AbstractQuickAction
	{
		protected SortedList<string, object[]> m_userDataList = new SortedList<string, object[]>();

		public AbstractQuickActionItem[] GetFolderContents(AbstractFolder folderToList, bool recursive, params string[] filters)
		{
			List<AbstractQuickActionItem> list = new List<AbstractQuickActionItem>();
			AbstractFile[] array = null;
			array = ((filters == null || filters.Length <= 1) ? folderToList.GetFiles(recursive, filters) : folderToList.GetFiles(recursive, new OrFilter(filters)));
			AbstractFile[] array2 = array;
			foreach (AbstractFile fileSystemItem in array2)
			{
				list.Add(OnGetFolderContentItemFound(fileSystemItem));
			}
			return list.ToArray();
		}

		public List<FileSystemItem> CreateItemListFromPaths(AbstractFolder sourceParentFolder, bool isFromLocalDisk, string[] filePaths, bool recursive, Filter notFilter)
		{
			List<FileSystemItem> list = new List<FileSystemItem>();
			foreach (string text in filePaths)
			{
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
				{
					throw new NullReferenceException("A null or empty file path was detected");
				}
				string empty = string.Empty;
				try
				{
					empty = Path.GetDirectoryName(text);
				}
				catch (PathTooLongException inner)
				{
					throw new FileSystemIOException("The parent folder full name is too long for item : " + text, null, inner);
				}
				string text2 = Path.GetFileName(text);
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(empty))
				{
					empty = ((!isFromLocalDisk) ? Path.DirectorySeparatorChar.ToString() : ".");
				}
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
				{
					text2 = "*";
				}
				AbstractFolder abstractFolder = null;
				if (!isFromLocalDisk)
				{
					abstractFolder = ((!(empty == Path.DirectorySeparatorChar.ToString()) && !(empty == Path.AltDirectorySeparatorChar.ToString())) ? sourceParentFolder.GetFolder(empty) : sourceParentFolder);
				}
				else
				{
					try
					{
						abstractFolder = new DiskFolder(Path.GetFullPath(empty));
					}
					catch (PathTooLongException inner2)
					{
						throw new FileSystemIOException("The parent folder full name is too long for item : " + empty, null, inner2);
					}
				}
				if (!abstractFolder.Exists)
				{
					throw new ApplicationException("The source parent folder does not exist: " + empty);
				}
				AbstractFile[] array = null;
				array = ((notFilter == null) ? abstractFolder.GetFiles(recursive, text2) : abstractFolder.GetFiles(recursive, text2, notFilter));
				list.AddRange(array);
			}
			return list;
		}

		public void RemoveFiles(AbstractFolder folder, bool recursive, params string[] filesToRemove)
		{
			try
			{
				if (filesToRemove == null || filesToRemove.Length == 0)
				{
					throw new ArgumentNullException("filesToRemoveArray");
				}
				foreach (string text in filesToRemove)
				{
					string empty = string.Empty;
					try
					{
						empty = Path.GetDirectoryName(text);
					}
					catch (PathTooLongException inner)
					{
						throw new FileSystemIOException("The parent's full name is too long for item : " + text, null, inner);
					}
					string text2 = Path.GetFileName(text);
					if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(empty))
					{
						empty = Path.DirectorySeparatorChar.ToString();
					}
					if (!empty.EndsWith(Path.DirectorySeparatorChar.ToString()) && !empty.EndsWith(Path.AltDirectorySeparatorChar.ToString()))
					{
						empty += Path.DirectorySeparatorChar;
					}
					if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
					{
						text2 = "*";
					}
					AbstractFolder abstractFolder = null;
					abstractFolder = ((!(empty == Path.DirectorySeparatorChar.ToString()) && !(empty == Path.AltDirectorySeparatorChar.ToString())) ? folder.GetFolder(empty) : folder);
					if (!abstractFolder.Exists)
					{
						throw new Exception("The folder does not exist : " + empty);
					}
					AbstractFile[] files = abstractFolder.GetFiles(recursive, text2);
					foreach (AbstractFile abstractFile in files)
					{
						if (abstractFile.Exists)
						{
							abstractFile.Delete();
						}
					}
				}
			}
			catch (FileSystemException innerException)
			{
				throw new ApplicationException("An error occurred while removing files from " + folder.HostedFullName, innerException);
			}
		}

		public void AddUserData(string eventName, object[] userData)
		{
			m_userDataList.Add(eventName, userData);
		}

		public object[] GetUserData(string eventName)
		{
			object[] value = null;
			m_userDataList.TryGetValue(eventName, out value);
			return value;
		}

		public void ResetUserData()
		{
			m_userDataList.Clear();
		}

		protected abstract AbstractQuickActionItem OnGetFolderContentItemFound(FileSystemItem fileSystemItem);
	}
	[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
	public class AbstractQuickActionItem
	{
		protected AbstractFile m_abstractFile;

		public string Name => m_abstractFile.Name;

		public string FullName => m_abstractFile.FullName;

		public FileAttributes Attributes => m_abstractFile.Attributes;

		public DateTime LastWriteDateTime => m_abstractFile.LastWriteDateTime;

		public DateTime LastAccessDateTime => m_abstractFile.LastAccessDateTime;

		public DateTime CreationDateTime => m_abstractFile.CreationDateTime;

		public long Size => m_abstractFile.Size;

		protected AbstractQuickActionItem(AbstractFile abstractFile)
		{
			if (abstractFile == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("abstractFile");
			}
			m_abstractFile = abstractFile;
		}
	}
}
