	public sealed class MultipleItemListFolder : AbstractFolder
	{
		private List<FileSystemItem> m_fileSystemItemList;

		private bool m_preservePaths;

		private bool m_abortCurrentOperation;

		public bool AbortCurrentOperation
		{
			get
			{
				return m_abortCurrentOperation;
			}
			set
			{
				m_abortCurrentOperation = value;
			}
		}

		protected override string DoName
		{
			get
			{
				return string.Empty;
			}
			set
			{
			}
		}

		protected override string DoFullName => string.Empty;

		protected override bool DoHasAttributes => false;

		protected override FileAttributes DoAttributes
		{
			get
			{
				throw new NotSupportedException();
			}
			set
			{
				throw new NotSupportedException();
			}
		}

		protected override bool DoHasCreationDateTime => false;

		protected override DateTime DoCreationDateTime
		{
			get
			{
				throw new NotSupportedException();
			}
			set
			{
				throw new NotSupportedException();
			}
		}

		protected override bool DoHasLastWriteDateTime => false;

		protected override DateTime DoLastWriteDateTime
		{
			get
			{
				throw new NotSupportedException();
			}
			set
			{
				throw new NotSupportedException();
			}
		}

		protected override bool DoHasLastAccessDateTime => false;

		protected override DateTime DoLastAccessDateTime
		{
			get
			{
				throw new NotSupportedException();
			}
			set
			{
				throw new NotSupportedException();
			}
		}

		protected override AbstractFolder DoParentFolder => null;

		protected override AbstractFolder DoRootFolder => null;

		protected override bool DoIsRoot => true;

		protected override bool DoExists => false;

		public MultipleItemListFolder(List<FileSystemItem> fileList, bool preservePaths)
		{
			m_fileSystemItemList = fileList;
			m_preservePaths = preservePaths;
		}

		protected override FileSystemItem[] DoGetChildItems(FileSystemEventsSession session)
		{
			return m_fileSystemItemList.ToArray();
		}

		protected override AbstractFile DoGetFile(FileSystemEventsSession session, string fileName)
		{
			return null;
		}

		protected override AbstractFolder DoGetFolder(FileSystemEventsSession session, string folderName)
		{
			return null;
		}

		protected override void DoRefresh(FileSystemEventsSession session)
		{
			throw new NotSupportedException();
		}

		protected override void DoCreate(FileSystemEventsSession session)
		{
			throw new NotSupportedException();
		}

		protected override void DoDelete(FileSystemEventsSession session)
		{
			throw new NotSupportedException();
		}

		public override bool IsSameAs(FileSystemItem target)
		{
			throw new NotSupportedException();
		}

		protected override bool IsPathRooted(string path)
		{
			throw new NotSupportedException();
		}

		protected override void BuildList(FileSystemEventsSession session, FileSystemItem destination, bool forward, bool recursive, bool includeFolders, Filter[] filters)
		{
			foreach (FileSystemItem fileSystemItem in m_fileSystemItemList)
			{
				string empty = string.Empty;
				try
				{
					empty = Path.GetDirectoryName(fileSystemItem.FullName);
				}
				catch (PathTooLongException inner)
				{
					throw new FileSystemIOException("The parent folder full name is too long for item : " + fileSystemItem.FullName, null, inner);
				}
				bool flag = false;
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(empty))
				{
					flag = true;
					empty = ".";
				}
				if (m_preservePaths)
				{
					AbstractFolder abstractFolder = null;
					AbstractFolder abstractFolder2 = (AbstractFolder)destination;
					string empty2 = string.Empty;
					try
					{
						empty2 = Path.GetPathRoot(fileSystemItem.FullName);
					}
					catch (PathTooLongException inner2)
					{
						throw new FileSystemIOException("The parent's full name is too long for item : " + fileSystemItem.FullName, null, inner2);
					}
					if (flag || string.IsNullOrEmpty(empty2) || empty == empty2)
					{
						abstractFolder = abstractFolder2;
					}
					else
					{
						string relativeName = empty.Substring(empty2.Length);
						abstractFolder = abstractFolder2.GetFolder(relativeName);
					}
					session.OnNewItemToProcess(fileSystemItem, abstractFolder.GetFile(fileSystemItem.Name));
				}
				else if (destination is AbstractFolder)
				{
					session.OnNewItemToProcess(fileSystemItem, ((AbstractFolder)destination).GetFile(fileSystemItem.Name));
				}
				else
				{
					session.OnNewItemToProcess(fileSystemItem, destination);
				}
			}
		}

		protected override FileSystemItem ListCopyTo(FileSystemEventsSession session, bool replaceExistingFiles)
		{
			ItemPair nextItemPair = session.GetNextItemPair(raiseBytesProgression: true);
			ItemPair itemPair = nextItemPair;
			do
			{
				if (m_abortCurrentOperation)
				{
					m_abortCurrentOperation = false;
					break;
				}
				if (itemPair != null)
				{
					ListCopyToCore(session, itemPair, replaceExistingFiles);
					itemPair = session.GetNextItemPair(raiseBytesProgression: true);
				}
			}
			while (itemPair != null);
			return nextItemPair.target;
		}

		protected override void ListCreate(FileSystemEventsSession session)
		{
			ItemPair nextItemPair;
			do
			{
				if (m_abortCurrentOperation)
				{
					m_abortCurrentOperation = false;
					break;
				}
				nextItemPair = session.GetNextItemPair(raiseBytesProgression: false);
				if (nextItemPair != null)
				{
					ListCreateCore(session, nextItemPair);
				}
			}
			while (nextItemPair != null);
		}

		protected override void ListDelete(FileSystemEventsSession session)
		{
			ItemPair nextItemPair;
			do
			{
				if (m_abortCurrentOperation)
				{
					m_abortCurrentOperation = false;
					break;
				}
				nextItemPair = session.GetNextItemPair(raiseBytesProgression: false);
				if (nextItemPair != null)
				{
					ListDeleteCore(session, nextItemPair);
				}
			}
			while (nextItemPair != null);
		}

		protected override FileSystemItem ListMoveTo(FileSystemEventsSession session, bool replaceExistingFiles)
		{
			ItemPair itemPair = null;
			ItemPair nextItemPair;
			do
			{
				if (m_abortCurrentOperation)
				{
					m_abortCurrentOperation = false;
					break;
				}
				nextItemPair = session.GetNextItemPair(raiseBytesProgression: true);
				if (nextItemPair != null)
				{
					itemPair = nextItemPair;
					nextItemPair = session.GetNextItemPair(raiseBytesProgression: true);
					ListMoveToCore(session, nextItemPair, replaceExistingFiles);
				}
			}
			while (nextItemPair != null);
			if (itemPair != null)
			{
				return itemPair.target;
			}
			throw new FileSystemInternalException("The lastItemPair is null in ListMoveTo");
		}
	}
}
namespace Xceed.Zip
{
	public static class QuickZip
	{
		public delegate bool DiskRequiredCallback(string zipFileName, int diskNumber, object userParams);

		public delegate void ByteProgressionCallback(string currentItemName, long currentItemBytesProcessed, long currentItemTotalBytes, byte currentItemPercent, long allFilesBytesProcessed, long allFilesTotalBytes, byte allFilesPercent, object userParams);

		public delegate void ItemProgressionCallback(string currentItemName, long itemsProcessed, long totalItemsCount, byte totalItemsPercent, ref bool abort, object userParams);

		private static QuickZipCompression mg_quickZipCompression = new QuickZipCompression();

		private static MultipleItemListFolder mg_multipleItemListFolder;

		public static QuickZipItem[] GetZipContents(string zipFileName, params string[] fileMasks)
		{
			return GetZipContents(zipFileName, string.Empty, recursive: true, fileMasks);
		}

		public static QuickZipItem[] GetZipContents(string zipFileName, bool recursive, params string[] fileMasks)
		{
			return GetZipContents(zipFileName, string.Empty, recursive, fileMasks);
		}

		public static QuickZipItem[] GetZipContents(string zipFileName, string decryptionPassword, bool recursive, params string[] fileMasks)
		{
			mg_quickZipCompression.ResetUserData();
			QuickZipItem[] array = null;
			try
			{
				if (zipFileName == null)
				{
					throw new ArgumentNullException("zipFileName");
				}
				if (decryptionPassword == null)
				{
					throw new ArgumentNullException("decryptionPassword");
				}
				DiskFile diskFile = new DiskFile(zipFileName);
				if (!diskFile.Exists)
				{
					throw new ArgumentException("The zip file must exist.", "zipFileName");
				}
				ZipEvents events = new ZipEvents();
				ZipArchive zipArchive = new ZipArchive(events, null, diskFile);
				zipArchive.DefaultDecryptionPassword = decryptionPassword;
				AbstractQuickActionItem[] folderContents = mg_quickZipCompression.GetFolderContents(zipArchive, recursive, fileMasks);
				array = Array.ConvertAll(folderContents, ConvertToQuickZipItem);
			}
			catch (FileSystemException inner)
			{
				throw new QuickZipException("An error occurred while removing files from the zip file.", inner);
			}
			if (array == null)
			{
				array = new QuickZipItem[0];
			}
			return array;
		}

		public static void Zip(string zipFileName, params string[] filesToZip)
		{
			Zip(zipFileName, string.Empty, EncryptionMethod.Compatible, replaceExistingFiles: true, recursive: true, preservePaths: true, filesToZip);
		}

		public static void Zip(string zipFileName, bool replaceExistingFiles, bool recursive, bool preservePaths, params string[] filesToZip)
		{
			Zip(zipFileName, string.Empty, EncryptionMethod.Compatible, replaceExistingFiles, recursive, preservePaths, filesToZip);
		}

		public static void Zip(string zipFileName, string encryptionPassword, bool replaceExistingFiles, bool recursive, bool preservePaths, params string[] filesToZip)
		{
			Zip(zipFileName, encryptionPassword, EncryptionMethod.Compatible, CompressionMethod.Deflated, replaceExistingFiles, recursive, preservePaths, filesToZip);
		}

		public static void Zip(string zipFileName, string encryptionPassword, EncryptionMethod encryptionMethod, bool replaceExistingFiles, bool recursive, bool preservePaths, params string[] filesToZip)
		{
			Zip(zipFileName, encryptionPassword, encryptionMethod, replaceExistingFiles, recursive, preservePaths, 0L, SplitNameFormat.None, filesToZip);
		}

		public static void Zip(string zipFileName, string encryptionPassword, EncryptionMethod encryptionMethod, CompressionMethod compressionMethod, bool replaceExistingFiles, bool recursive, bool preservePaths, params string[] filesToZip)
		{
			Zip(zipFileName, encryptionPassword, encryptionMethod, compressionMethod, replaceExistingFiles, recursive, preservePaths, 0L, SplitNameFormat.None, null, null, null, null, null, filesToZip);
		}

		public static void Zip(string zipFileName, string encryptionPassword, bool replaceExistingFiles, bool recursive, bool preservePaths, long splitSize, SplitNameFormat splitNameFormat, params string[] filesToZip)
		{
			Zip(zipFileName, encryptionPassword, EncryptionMethod.Compatible, CompressionMethod.Deflated, replaceExistingFiles, recursive, preservePaths, splitSize, splitNameFormat, null, null, null, null, null, filesToZip);
		}

		public static void Zip(string zipFileName, string encryptionPassword, EncryptionMethod encryptionMethod, bool replaceExistingFiles, bool recursive, bool preservePaths, long splitSize, SplitNameFormat splitNameFormat, params string[] filesToZip)
		{
			Zip(zipFileName, encryptionPassword, encryptionMethod, CompressionMethod.Deflated, replaceExistingFiles, recursive, preservePaths, splitSize, splitNameFormat, null, null, null, null, null, filesToZip);
		}

		public static void Zip(string zipFileName, string encryptionPassword, bool replaceExistingFiles, bool recursive, bool preservePaths, DiskRequiredCallback diskRequiredCallback, object userParams, params string[] filesToZip)
		{
			Zip(zipFileName, encryptionPassword, EncryptionMethod.Compatible, CompressionMethod.Deflated, replaceExistingFiles, recursive, preservePaths, 0L, SplitNameFormat.None, null, diskRequiredCallback, null, null, userParams, filesToZip);
		}

		public static void Zip(string zipFileName, string encryptionPassword, EncryptionMethod encryptionMethod, bool replaceExistingFiles, bool recursive, bool preservePaths, DiskRequiredCallback diskRequiredCallback, object userParams, params string[] filesToZip)
		{
			Zip(zipFileName, encryptionPassword, encryptionMethod, CompressionMethod.Deflated, replaceExistingFiles, recursive, preservePaths, 0L, SplitNameFormat.None, null, diskRequiredCallback, null, null, userParams, filesToZip);
		}

		internal static void Zip(string zipFileName, string encryptionPassword, EncryptionMethod encryptionMethod, bool replaceExistingFiles, bool recursive, bool preservePaths, long splitSize, SplitNameFormat splitNameFormat, DiskRequiredCallback diskRequiredCallback, object userParams, params string[] filesToZip)
		{
			Zip(zipFileName, encryptionPassword, encryptionMethod, CompressionMethod.Deflated, replaceExistingFiles, recursive, preservePaths, splitSize, splitNameFormat, null, diskRequiredCallback, null, null, userParams, filesToZip);
		}

		public static void Zip(string zipFileName, string encryptionPassword, bool replaceExistingFiles, bool recursive, bool preservePaths, XceedSfxPrefix sfx, params string[] filesToZip)
		{
			Zip(zipFileName, encryptionPassword, replaceExistingFiles, recursive, preservePaths, sfx, null, null, filesToZip);
		}

		public static void Zip(string zipFileName, string encryptionPassword, bool replaceExistingFiles, bool recursive, bool preservePaths, XceedSfxPrefix sfx, DiskRequiredCallback diskRequiredCallback, object userParams, params string[] filesToZip)
		{
			Zip(zipFileName, encryptionPassword, EncryptionMethod.Compatible, CompressionMethod.Deflated, replaceExistingFiles, recursive, preservePaths, 0L, SplitNameFormat.None, sfx, diskRequiredCallback, null, null, userParams, filesToZip);
		}

		public static void Zip(string zipFileName, string encryptionPassword, EncryptionMethod encryptionMethod, bool replaceExistingFiles, bool recursive, bool preservePaths, long splitSize, SplitNameFormat splitNameFormat, XceedSfxPrefix sfx, DiskRequiredCallback diskRequiredCallback, ByteProgressionCallback byteProgressionCallback, ItemProgressionCallback itemProgressionCallback, object userParams, params string[] filesToZip)
		{
			Zip(zipFileName, encryptionPassword, encryptionMethod, CompressionMethod.Deflated, replaceExistingFiles, recursive, preservePaths, splitSize, splitNameFormat, sfx, diskRequiredCallback, byteProgressionCallback, itemProgressionCallback, userParams, filesToZip);
		}

		public static void Zip(string zipFileName, string encryptionPassword, EncryptionMethod encryptionMethod, CompressionMethod compressionMethod, bool replaceExistingFiles, bool recursive, bool preservePaths, long splitSize, SplitNameFormat splitNameFormat, XceedSfxPrefix sfx, DiskRequiredCallback diskRequiredCallback, ByteProgressionCallback byteProgressionCallback, ItemProgressionCallback itemProgressionCallback, object userParams, params string[] filesToZip)
		{
			mg_quickZipCompression.ResetUserData();
			ZipArchive zipArchive = null;
			ZipEvents zipEvents = null;
			try
			{
				if (zipFileName == null)
				{
					throw new ArgumentNullException("zipFileName");
				}
				if (encryptionPassword == null)
				{
					throw new ArgumentNullException("encryptionPassword");
				}
				if (!Enum.IsDefined(typeof(EncryptionMethod), encryptionMethod))
				{
					throw new ArgumentException("Unknown encryption method.");
				}
				if (splitSize > 0 && splitNameFormat == SplitNameFormat.None)
				{
					throw new ArgumentException("The splitNameFormat cannot be None if splitSize is greater than 0.", "splitNameFormat");
				}
				if (filesToZip == null)
				{
					throw new ArgumentNullException("filesToZip");
				}
				zipEvents = new ZipEvents();
				if (diskRequiredCallback != null)
				{
					zipEvents.DiskRequired += OnDiskRequired;
					mg_quickZipCompression.AddUserData("DiskRequired", new object[2] { diskRequiredCallback, userParams });
				}
				if (byteProgressionCallback != null)
				{
					zipEvents.ByteProgression += OnByteProgression;
					mg_quickZipCompression.AddUserData("ByteProgression", new object[2] { byteProgressionCallback, userParams });
				}
				if (itemProgressionCallback != null)
				{
					zipEvents.ItemProgression += OnItemProgression;
					mg_quickZipCompression.AddUserData("ItemProgression", new object[2] { itemProgressionCallback, userParams });
				}
				DiskFile diskFile = new DiskFile(zipFileName);
				zipArchive = new ZipArchive(zipEvents, null, diskFile);
				if (zipArchive.DiskNumber > 1)
				{
					throw new NotSupportedException("QuickZip does not support updating spanned or split zip files.");
				}
				zipArchive.AllowSpanning = diskRequiredCallback != null;
				zipArchive.DefaultEncryptionPassword = encryptionPassword;
				zipArchive.DefaultEncryptionMethod = encryptionMethod;
				zipArchive.DefaultEncryptionStrength = 256;
				zipArchive.SplitNameFormat = splitNameFormat;
				zipArchive.SplitSize = splitSize;
				if (sfx != null && (compressionMethod == CompressionMethod.BZip2 || compressionMethod == CompressionMethod.PPMd))
				{
					throw new NotSupportedException("SFX module does not support BZip2 and PPMd compression methods");
				}
				zipArchive.DefaultCompressionMethod = compressionMethod;
				using (new AutoBatchUpdate(zipArchive))
				{
					if (sfx != null)
					{
						zipArchive.SfxPrefix = sfx;
					}
					FullNameFilter filter = new FullNameFilter(diskFile.FullName);
					NotFilter notFilter = new NotFilter(filter);
					List<FileSystemItem> fileList = mg_quickZipCompression.CreateItemListFromPaths(zipArchive, isFromLocalDisk: true, filesToZip, recursive, notFilter);
					mg_multipleItemListFolder = new MultipleItemListFolder(fileList, preservePaths);
					mg_multipleItemListFolder.CopyFilesTo(zipEvents, null, zipArchive, recursive, replaceExistingFiles, notFilter);
				}
			}
			catch (FileSystemException inner)
			{
				throw new QuickZipException("An error occurred while adding files to the zip file.", inner);
			}
		}

		public static void Unzip(string zipFileName, string destinationFolder, params string[] filesToUnzip)
		{
			Unzip(zipFileName, destinationFolder, string.Empty, replaceExistingFiles: true, recursive: true, preservePaths: true, null, null, filesToUnzip);
		}

		public static void Unzip(string zipFileName, string destinationFolder, bool replaceExistingFiles, bool recursive, bool preservePaths, params string[] filesToUnzip)
		{
			Unzip(zipFileName, destinationFolder, string.Empty, replaceExistingFiles, recursive, preservePaths, null, null, filesToUnzip);
		}

		public static void Unzip(string zipFileName, string destinationFolder, string decryptionPassword, bool replaceExistingFiles, bool recursive, bool preservePaths, params string[] filesToUnzip)
		{
			Unzip(zipFileName, destinationFolder, decryptionPassword, replaceExistingFiles, recursive, preservePaths, null, null, filesToUnzip);
		}

		public static void Unzip(string zipFileName, string destinationFolder, string decryptionPassword, bool replaceExistingFiles, bool recursive, bool preservePaths, DiskRequiredCallback diskRequiredCallback, object userParams, params string[] filesToUnzip)
		{
			Unzip(zipFileName, destinationFolder, decryptionPassword, replaceExistingFiles, recursive, preservePaths, diskRequiredCallback, null, null, userParams, filesToUnzip);
		}

		public static void Unzip(string zipFileName, string destinationFolder, string decryptionPassword, bool replaceExistingFiles, bool recursive, bool preservePaths, DiskRequiredCallback diskRequiredCallback, ByteProgressionCallback byteProgressionCallback, ItemProgressionCallback itemProgressionCallback, object userParams, params string[] filesToUnzip)
		{
			mg_quickZipCompression.ResetUserData();
			try
			{
				if (zipFileName == null)
				{
					throw new ArgumentNullException("zipFileName");
				}
				if (decryptionPassword == null)
				{
					throw new ArgumentNullException("decryptionPassword");
				}
				if (filesToUnzip == null || filesToUnzip.Length == 0)
				{
					throw new ArgumentNullException("filesToUnzip");
				}
				DiskFile diskFile = new DiskFile(zipFileName);
				if (!diskFile.Exists)
				{
					throw new ArgumentException("The zip file must exist.", "zipFileName");
				}
				ZipEvents zipEvents = new ZipEvents();
				if (diskRequiredCallback != null)
				{
					zipEvents.DiskRequired += OnDiskRequired;
					mg_quickZipCompression.AddUserData("DiskRequired", new object[2] { diskRequiredCallback, userParams });
				}
				if (byteProgressionCallback != null)
				{
					zipEvents.ByteProgression += OnByteProgression;
					mg_quickZipCompression.AddUserData("ByteProgression", new object[2] { byteProgressionCallback, userParams });
				}
				if (itemProgressionCallback != null)
				{
					zipEvents.ItemProgression += OnItemProgression;
					mg_quickZipCompression.AddUserData("ItemProgression", new object[2] { itemProgressionCallback, userParams });
				}
				ZipArchive zipArchive = new ZipArchive(zipEvents, null, diskFile);
				zipArchive.DefaultDecryptionPassword = decryptionPassword;
				DiskFolder destinationFolder2 = new DiskFolder(destinationFolder);
				FullNameFilter filter = new FullNameFilter(diskFile.FullName);
				NotFilter notFilter = new NotFilter(filter);
				List<FileSystemItem> fileList = mg_quickZipCompression.CreateItemListFromPaths(zipArchive, isFromLocalDisk: false, filesToUnzip, recursive, notFilter);
				mg_multipleItemListFolder = new MultipleItemListFolder(fileList, preservePaths);
				mg_multipleItemListFolder.CopyFilesTo(zipEvents, null, destinationFolder2, recursive, replaceExistingFiles, "*.*");
			}
			catch (FileSystemException inner)
			{
				throw new QuickZipException("An error occurred while extracting files from the zip file.", inner);
			}
		}

		public static void Remove(string zipFileName, params string[] filesToRemove)
		{
			Remove(zipFileName, recursive: true, filesToRemove);
		}

		public static void Remove(string zipFileName, bool recursive, params string[] filesToRemove)
		{
			if (zipFileName == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("zipFileName");
			}
			if (filesToRemove == null || filesToRemove.Length == 0)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("filesToRemove");
			}
			DiskFile diskFile = new DiskFile(zipFileName);
			if (!diskFile.Exists)
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("The zip file must exist.", "zipFileName");
			}
			ZipArchive zipArchive = new ZipArchive(diskFile);
			if (zipArchive.DiskNumber > 1)
			{
				throw new NotSupportedException("QuickZip does not support removing files from a spanned or split zip file.");
			}
			using (new AutoBatchUpdate(zipArchive))
			{
				try
				{
					foreach (string text in filesToRemove)
					{
						string empty = string.Empty;
						try
						{
							empty = Path.GetDirectoryName(text);
						}
						catch (PathTooLongException inner)
						{
							throw new FileSystemIOException("The parent's full name is too long for item : " + text, null, inner);
						}
						string text2 = Path.GetFileName(text);
						if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(empty))
						{
							empty = Path.DirectorySeparatorChar.ToString();
						}
						if (!empty.EndsWith(Path.DirectorySeparatorChar.ToString()) && !empty.EndsWith(Path.AltDirectorySeparatorChar.ToString()))
						{
							empty += Path.DirectorySeparatorChar;
						}
						if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
						{
							text2 = "*";
						}
						AbstractFolder folder = zipArchive.GetFolder(empty);
						if (!folder.Exists)
						{
							throw new ArgumentException("The folder does not exist in the zip file: " + empty, "filesToRemove");
						}
						AbstractFile[] files = folder.GetFiles(recursive, text2);
						foreach (AbstractFile abstractFile in files)
						{
							try
							{
								abstractFile.Delete();
							}
							catch (ItemDoesNotExistException)
							{
							}
						}
					}
				}
				catch (FileSystemException inner2)
				{
					throw new QuickZipException("An error occurred while removing files from the zip file.", inner2);
				}
			}
		}

		private static void OnDiskRequired(object sender, DiskRequiredEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.Reason != DiskRequiredReason.Deleting && (e.Action == DiskRequiredAction.Fail || e.Reason == DiskRequiredReason.Updating))
			{
				object[] userData = mg_quickZipCompression.GetUserData("DiskRequired");
				DiskRequiredCallback diskRequiredCallback = (DiskRequiredCallback)userData[0];
				object userParams = userData[1];
				e.Action = (diskRequiredCallback(e.ZipFile.FullName, e.DiskNumber, userParams) ? DiskRequiredAction.Continue : DiskRequiredAction.Fail);
			}
		}

		private static void OnByteProgression(object sender, ByteProgressionEventArgs e)
		{
			object[] userData = mg_quickZipCompression.GetUserData("ByteProgression");
			ByteProgressionCallback byteProgressionCallback = (ByteProgressionCallback)userData[0];
			object userParams = userData[1];
			byteProgressionCallback(e.CurrentItem.FullName, e.CurrentFileBytes.Processed, e.CurrentFileBytes.Total, e.CurrentFileBytes.Percent, e.AllFilesBytes.Processed, e.AllFilesBytes.Total, e.AllFilesBytes.Percent, userParams);
		}

		private static void OnItemProgression(object sender, ItemProgressionEventArgs e)
		{
			object[] userData = mg_quickZipCompression.GetUserData("ItemProgression");
			ItemProgressionCallback itemProgressionCallback = (ItemProgressionCallback)userData[0];
			object userParams = userData[1];
			bool abort = false;
			itemProgressionCallback(e.CurrentItem.FullName, e.AllItems.Processed, e.AllItems.Total, e.AllItems.Percent, ref abort, userParams);
			mg_multipleItemListFolder.AbortCurrentOperation = abort;
		}

		private static QuickZipItem ConvertToQuickZipItem(AbstractQuickActionItem item)
		{
			return (QuickZipItem)item;
		}
	}
	public class QuickZipItem : AbstractQuickActionItem
	{
		public long CompressedSize => ((ZippedFile)m_abstractFile).CompressedSize;

		public byte CompressionRatio
		{
			get
			{
				if (base.Size == 0 || CompressedSize >= base.Size)
				{
					return 0;
				}
				checked
				{
					return (byte)unchecked(checked((base.Size - CompressedSize) * 100) / base.Size);
				}
			}
		}

		public int Crc32 => ((ZippedFile)m_abstractFile).Crc32;

		public CompressionMethod CompressionMethod => ((ZippedFile)m_abstractFile).CompressionMethod;

		public bool Encrypted => ((ZippedFile)m_abstractFile).Encrypted;

		public EncryptionMethod EncryptionMethod => ((ZippedFile)m_abstractFile).EncryptionMethod;

		public int EncryptionStrength => ((ZippedFile)m_abstractFile).EncryptionStrength;

		public string Comment => ((ZippedFile)m_abstractFile).Comment;

		internal QuickZipItem(ZippedFile zippedFile)
			: base(zippedFile)
		{
			if (zippedFile == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("zippedFile");
			}
		}
	}
	internal class AesEncryptionStream : Stream
	{
		private const int AuthenticationCodeLength = 10;

		private bool m_disposed;

		private Stream m_innerStream;

		private HeaderFooterStream m_footerStream;

		private RijndaelCtrMode m_rijndael;

		private HMACSHA1 m_hmacSha1;

		public override bool CanRead => m_footerStream != null;

		public override bool CanSeek => false;

		public override bool CanWrite => m_footerStream == null;

		public override long Length
		{
			get
			{
				throw new NotSupportedException("Cannot return the length of an AesEncryptionStream.");
			}
		}

		public override long Position
		{
			get
			{
				throw new NotSupportedException("Cannot return the position of an AesEncryptionStream.");
			}
			set
			{
				throw new NotSupportedException("Cannot seek in an AesEncryptionStream.");
			}
		}

		public AesEncryptionStream(Stream innerStream, string password, int strength, bool writing)
		{
			if (innerStream == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("innerStream");
			}
			if (writing && !innerStream.CanWrite)
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("The inner stream must be writable.", "innerStream");
			}
			if (!writing && !innerStream.CanRead)
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("The inner stream must be readable.", "innerStream");
			}
			if (password == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("password");
			}
			if (password.Length == 0)
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("The password cannot be empty.", "password");
			}
			if (strength != 128 && strength != 192 && strength != 256)
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("The strength must be 128, 192 or 256.", "strength");
			}
			m_innerStream = innerStream;
			byte[] array = new byte[strength / 16];
			DeriveBytesRfc2898 deriveBytesRfc;
			checked
			{
				if (writing)
				{
					RNGCryptoServiceProvider rNGCryptoServiceProvider = new RNGCryptoServiceProvider();
					rNGCryptoServiceProvider.GetBytes(array);
					innerStream.Write(array, 0, array.Length);
				}
				else
				{
					int num;
					for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i += num)
					{
						num = innerStream.Read(array, i, array.Length - i);
						if (num == 0)
						{
							throw new IOException("Unexpected end of stream while reading encrypted stream.");
						}
					}
				}
				deriveBytesRfc = new DeriveBytesRfc2898(password, array);
			}
			int count = strength / 8;
			byte[] bytes = deriveBytesRfc.GetBytes(count);
			byte[] bytes2 = deriveBytesRfc.GetBytes(count);
			byte[] bytes3 = deriveBytesRfc.GetBytes(2);
			if (writing)
			{
				innerStream.Write(bytes3, 0, bytes3.Length);
			}
			else
			{
				byte[] array2 = new byte[2];
				int num2 = innerStream.Read(array2, 0, 2);
				if (num2 == 1)
				{
					num2 = innerStream.Read(array2, 1, 1);
				}
				if (num2 == 0)
				{
					throw new IOException("Unexpected end of stream while reading encrypted stream.");
				}
				if (bytes3[0] != array2[0] || bytes3[1] != array2[1])
				{
					throw new InvalidDecryptionPasswordException("The provided password does not match the verifier key.");
				}
			}
			m_rijndael = new RijndaelCtrMode(bytes, 16);
			m_hmacSha1 = new HMACSHA1(bytes2);
			if (!writing)
			{
				m_footerStream = new HeaderFooterStream(m_innerStream);
				m_footerStream.ReservedFooter = 10;
			}
		}

		public override void Flush()
		{
			if (m_footerStream == null)
			{
				m_innerStream.Flush();
			}
			else
			{
				m_footerStream.Flush();
			}
		}

		public override int Read(byte[] buffer, int offset, int count)
		{
			if (m_footerStream == null)
			{
				throw new NotSupportedException("Cannot read from this stream. It is open for writing.");
			}
			int num = m_footerStream.Read(buffer, offset, count);
			if (num > 0)
			{
				m_hmacSha1.TransformBlock(buffer, offset, num, buffer, offset);
				m_rijndael.Encrypt(buffer, offset, num);
			}
			return num;
		}

		public override long Seek(long offset, SeekOrigin origin)
		{
			throw new NotSupportedException("Cannot seek in an AesEncryptionStream.");
		}

		public override void SetLength(long value)
		{
			throw new NotSupportedException("Cannot set the length of an AesEncryptionStream.");
		}

		public override void Write(byte[] buffer, int offset, int count)
		{
			if (m_footerStream != null)
			{
				throw new NotSupportedException("Cannot write in this stream. It is open for reading.");
			}
			if (buffer == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("buffer");
			}
			if (offset < 0)
			{
				Xceed.Utils.Exceptions.ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("offset", offset, "The offset cannot be less than zero.");
			}
			if (count < 0)
			{
				Xceed.Utils.Exceptions.ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("count", count, "The count cannot be less than zero.");
			}
			if (count > checked(buffer.Length - offset))
			{
				Xceed.Utils.Exceptions.ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("count", count, "The count exceeds the buffer's remaining length after offset.");
			}
			if (count > 0)
			{
				m_rijndael.Encrypt(buffer, offset, count);
				m_hmacSha1.TransformBlock(buffer, offset, count, buffer, offset);
			}
			m_innerStream.Write(buffer, offset, count);
		}

		~AesEncryptionStream()
		{
			Dispose(disposing: false);
		}

		protected override void Dispose(bool disposing)
		{
			if (m_disposed)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (disposing)
			{
				if (m_footerStream == null)
				{
					m_hmacSha1.TransformFinalBlock(new byte[0], 0, 0);
					byte[] hash = m_hmacSha1.Hash;
					if (hash.Length < 10)
					{
						throw new FileSystemInternalException("Unexpected HMACSHA1 authentication value length.");
					}
					m_innerStream.Write(hash, 0, 10);
				}
				else
				{
					byte[] array = new byte[m_footerStream.ReservedFooter];
					m_footerStream.GetFooter(array, 0);
					m_hmacSha1.TransformFinalBlock(new byte[0], 0, 0);
					byte[] hash2 = m_hmacSha1.Hash;
					if (hash2.Length < array.Length)
					{
						throw new FileSystemInternalException("Unexpected HMACSHA1 authentication value length.");
					}
					for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i = checked(i + 1))
					{
						if (hash2[i] != array[i])
						{
							throw new ZipIOException("The calculated authentication value does not match the reported value.");
						}
					}
				}
				m_innerStream.Close();
				m_rijndael.Dispose();
			}
			m_innerStream = null;
			m_rijndael = null;
			m_hmacSha1 = null;
			m_disposed = true;
		}

		private void CheckIfDisposed()
		{
			if (m_disposed)
			{
				throw new ObjectDisposedException("ChecksumStream", "Cannot access a stream once it has been closed.");
			}
		}
	}
}

namespace Xceed.Zip
{
	internal enum Dirtiness : ushort
	{
		Clean,
		Dirty
	}
	public enum EncryptionMethod
	{
		Compatible = 1,
		WinZipAes
	}
	internal sealed class EndHeader
	{
		public const uint Zip64Signature = 101075792u;

		public const uint Zip64LocatorSignature = 117853008u;

		public const uint Signature = 101010256u;

		private long m_currentOffset;

		private uint m_currentDiskNumber = uint.MaxValue;

		private uint m_centralDiskNumber;

		private ulong m_nbCentralOnCurrDisk;

		private ulong m_nbCentralHeaders;

		private ulong m_centralDirSize;

		private long m_centralDirOffset;

		private ushort m_zipCommentLength;

		private string m_zipComment = string.Empty;

		private uint m_zip64EndHeaderDisk;

		private long m_zip64EndHeaderOffset;

		public uint DiskNumber
		{
			get
			{
				return m_currentDiskNumber;
			}
			set
			{
				m_currentDiskNumber = value;
			}
		}

		public long Offset
		{
			get
			{
				return m_currentOffset;
			}
			set
			{
				m_currentOffset = value;
			}
		}

		public uint CentralDiskNumber
		{
			get
			{
				return m_centralDiskNumber;
			}
			set
			{
				m_centralDiskNumber = value;
			}
		}

		public ulong CentralHeadersCurrentDisk
		{
			get
			{
				return m_nbCentralOnCurrDisk;
			}
			set
			{
				m_nbCentralOnCurrDisk = value;
			}
		}

		public ulong CentralHeadersTotal
		{
			get
			{
				return m_nbCentralHeaders;
			}
			set
			{
				m_nbCentralHeaders = value;
			}
		}

		public ulong CentralDirectorySize
		{
			get
			{
				return m_centralDirSize;
			}
			set
			{
				m_centralDirSize = value;
			}
		}

		public long CentralDirectoryOffset
		{
			get
			{
				return m_centralDirOffset;
			}
			set
			{
				m_centralDirOffset = value;
			}
		}

		public string ZipComment
		{
			get
			{
				return m_zipComment;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value.Length > 65535)
				{
					throw new ArgumentException("The zip comment can not exceed 65535 characters.");
				}
				m_zipCommentLength = checked((ushort)value.Length);
				m_zipComment = value;
			}
		}

		public bool Zip64Format
		{
			get
			{
				if (m_currentDiskNumber < 65535 && m_centralDiskNumber < 65535 && m_nbCentralOnCurrDisk < 65535 && m_nbCentralHeaders < 65535 && m_centralDirSize < uint.MaxValue)
				{
					return m_centralDirOffset >= uint.MaxValue;
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

		public uint Zip64EndHeaderDiskNumber
		{
			get
			{
				return m_zip64EndHeaderDisk;
			}
			set
			{
				m_zip64EndHeaderDisk = value;
			}
		}

		public long Zip64EndHeaderOffset
		{
			get
			{
				return m_zip64EndHeaderOffset;
			}
			set
			{
				m_zip64EndHeaderOffset = value;
			}
		}

		public EndHeader()
		{
		}

		public EndHeader(EndHeader original)
		{
			ZipComment = original.ZipComment;
		}

		public EndHeader(BinaryReader reader)
		{
			ReadFromStream(reader);
		}

		public void ReadFromStream(BinaryReader reader)
		{
			if (reader == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("reader");
			}
			m_currentOffset = reader.BaseStream.Position;
			m_currentDiskNumber = reader.ReadUInt16();
			m_centralDiskNumber = reader.ReadUInt16();
			m_nbCentralOnCurrDisk = reader.ReadUInt16();
			m_nbCentralHeaders = reader.ReadUInt16();
			m_centralDirSize = reader.ReadUInt32();
			m_centralDirOffset = reader.ReadUInt32();
			m_zipCommentLength = reader.ReadUInt16();
			if (m_zipCommentLength > 0)
			{
				byte[] array = reader.ReadBytes(m_zipCommentLength);
				if (array.Length != m_zipCommentLength)
				{
					throw new EndOfStreamException("Unexpected end of stream while reading an ending header from a zip file.");
				}
				m_zipComment = System.Text.Encoding.Default.GetString(array, 0, array.Length);
			}
		}

		public void ReadZip64EndHeader(BinaryReader reader)
		{
			if (reader == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("reader");
			}
			m_currentOffset = reader.BaseStream.Position;
			reader.ReadUInt64();
			reader.ReadUInt16();
			reader.ReadUInt16();
			m_currentDiskNumber = reader.ReadUInt32();
			m_centralDiskNumber = reader.ReadUInt32();
			m_nbCentralOnCurrDisk = reader.ReadUInt64();
			m_nbCentralHeaders = reader.ReadUInt64();
			m_centralDirSize = reader.ReadUInt64();
			m_centralDirOffset = checked((long)reader.ReadUInt64());
		}

		public void ReadZip64EndLocator(BinaryReader reader)
		{
			if (reader == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("reader");
			}
			m_zip64EndHeaderDisk = reader.ReadUInt32();
			checked
			{
				m_zip64EndHeaderOffset = (long)reader.ReadUInt64();
				m_currentDiskNumber = reader.ReadUInt32();
				if (m_currentDiskNumber != 0)
				{
					m_currentDiskNumber--;
				}
			}
		}

		public void WriteToStream(ZipStream stream)
		{
			if (stream == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("stream");
			}
			MemoryStream memoryStream = new MemoryStream();
			BinaryWriter binaryWriter = new BinaryWriter(memoryStream);
			checked
			{
				try
				{
					if (Zip64Format)
					{
						binaryWriter.Write(117853008u);
						binaryWriter.Write(Zip64EndHeaderDiskNumber);
						binaryWriter.Write((ulong)Zip64EndHeaderOffset);
						binaryWriter.Write(DiskNumber + 1);
					}
					binaryWriter.Write(101010256u);
					binaryWriter.Write(ItemHeader.SafeUInt16(m_currentDiskNumber));
					binaryWriter.Write(ItemHeader.SafeUInt16(m_centralDiskNumber));
					binaryWriter.Write(ItemHeader.SafeUInt16(m_nbCentralOnCurrDisk));
					binaryWriter.Write(ItemHeader.SafeUInt16(m_nbCentralHeaders));
					binaryWriter.Write(ItemHeader.SafeUInt32(m_centralDirSize));
					binaryWriter.Write(ItemHeader.SafeUInt32(m_centralDirOffset));
					binaryWriter.Write(m_zipCommentLength);
					if (m_zipCommentLength > 0)
					{
						binaryWriter.Write(System.Text.Encoding.Default.GetBytes(m_zipComment));
					}
				}
				finally
				{
					binaryWriter.Close();
				}
				byte[] array = memoryStream.ToArray();
				stream.WriteNoBreak(array, 0, array.Length);
			}
		}

		public void WriteZip64EndHeader(ZipStream stream)
		{
			if (stream == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("stream");
			}
			MemoryStream memoryStream = new MemoryStream();
			BinaryWriter binaryWriter = new BinaryWriter(memoryStream);
			try
			{
				binaryWriter.Write(101075792u);
				binaryWriter.Write(44uL);
				binaryWriter.Write((ushort)45);
				binaryWriter.Write((ushort)45);
				binaryWriter.Write(DiskNumber);
				binaryWriter.Write(CentralDiskNumber);
				binaryWriter.Write(CentralHeadersCurrentDisk);
				binaryWriter.Write(CentralHeadersTotal);
				binaryWriter.Write(CentralDirectorySize);
				binaryWriter.Write(checked((ulong)CentralDirectoryOffset));
			}
			finally
			{
				binaryWriter.Close();
			}
			byte[] array = memoryStream.ToArray();
			stream.WriteNoBreak(array, 0, array.Length);
		}
	}
	public class ZipException : FileSystemException
	{
		private AbstractFile m_zipFile;

		public override string Message
		{
			get
			{
				string message = base.Message;
				if (ZipFile != null)
				{
					try
					{
						return message + Xceed.Utils.Environment.XceedEnvironment.NewLine + "Zip file: " + ZipFile.FullName;
					}
					catch
					{
					}
				}
				return message;
			}
		}

		public AbstractFile ZipFile
		{
			get
			{
				return m_zipFile;
			}
			set
			{
				m_zipFile = value;
			}
		}

		public ZipException(string message)
			: this(message, null, null, null)
		{
		}

		public ZipException(string message, AbstractFile zipFile)
			: this(message, zipFile, null, null)
		{
		}

		public ZipException(string message, AbstractFile zipFile, Exception inner)
			: this(message, zipFile, null, inner)
		{
		}

		public ZipException(string message, AbstractFile zipFile, FileSystemItem item)
			: this(message, zipFile, item, null)
		{
		}

		public ZipException(string message, AbstractFile zipFile, FileSystemItem item, Exception inner)
			: base(message, item, inner)
		{
			m_zipFile = zipFile;
		}
	}

	internal enum FeatureID
	{
		NoFeatureSpecified,

	internal class FullNameFilter : Filter
	{
		private string m_fullName;

		private bool m_caseSensitive;

		public string FullName => m_fullName;

		public bool CaseSensitive => m_caseSensitive;

		public override bool IsItemMatching(FileSystemItem item)
		{
			if (item == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("item");
			}
			if (!IsItemInScope(item))
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (string.Compare(item.FullName, FullName, !CaseSensitive) == 0)
			{
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public FullNameFilter(string fullName)
			: this(fullName, FilterScope.File)
		{
		}

		public FullNameFilter(string fullName, FilterScope scope)
			: base(scope)
		{
			if (fullName == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("fullName");
			}
			fullName = fullName.TrimEnd();
			if (fullName.Length == 0)
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("The full name is an empty string, or contains only whitespace.", "fullName");
			}
			if (fullName.StartsWith(">"))
			{
				m_caseSensitive = true;
				fullName = fullName.Remove(0, 1);
			}
			try
			{
				if (Path.GetFullPath(fullName) != fullName)
				{
					throw new ArgumentException("The full name cannot be a relative path.", "fullName");
				}
			}
			catch (PathTooLongException inner)
			{
				throw new FileSystemIOException("The item's full name is too long : " + fullName, null, inner);
			}
			m_fullName = fullName;
		}
	}
	[Flags]
	internal enum ZipGeneralBitFlags : ushort
	{
		Encrypted = 1,
		NormalCompLevel = 0,
		MaxCompLevel = 2,
		LowCompLevel = 4,
		LowestCompLevel = 6,
		CompLevelMask = 6,
		DescriptorHeader = 8,
		UsefulBitsMask = 0x1FFF
	}
	[Flags]
	internal enum ZipCompressionMethod : ushort
	{
		Stored = 0,
		Deflated = 8,
		Deflated64 = 9,
		BZip2 = 0xC,
		BWT = 0x12,
		PPMd = 0x62,
		Unknown = 0x63
	}
	[Flags]
	internal enum ZipInternalFlags : ushort
	{
		TextFile = 1
	}
	[Flags]
	internal enum ZipDOSAttributes : uint
	{
		Normal = 0u,
		ReadOnly = 1u,
		Hidden = 2u,
		System = 4u,
		Volume = 8u,
		Directory = 0x10u,
		Archive = 0x20u,
		Compressed = 0x800u
	}
	[Flags]
	internal enum ZipVersionField : ushort
	{
		OsDos = 0,
		OsWin32 = 0xB00,
		VerAny = 0xA,
		VerRecent = 0x14,
		VerDeflate64 = 0x15,
		VerZip64 = 0x2D,
		VerBZip2 = 0x2E,
		VerPPMd = 0x3F
	}
	internal class ItemHeader
	{
		public const uint CentralSignature = 33639248u;

		public const uint LocalSignature = 67324752u;

		public const uint DescriptorSignature = 134695760u;

		private ZipVersionField m_versionMadeBy = ZipVersionField.VerZip64;

		private ZipVersionField m_versionNeeded = ZipVersionField.VerRecent;

		private ZipGeneralBitFlags m_generalBitFlags = ZipGeneralBitFlags.DescriptorHeader;

		private ZipCompressionMethod m_compressionMethod;

		private DateTime m_lastModifiedDateTime = DateTime.MinValue;

		private DateTime m_lastAccessedDateTime = DateTime.MinValue;

		private DateTime m_creationDateTime = DateTime.MinValue;

		private uint m_crc32;

		private long m_compressedSize;

		private long m_uncompressedSize;

		private ZipInternalFlags m_internalFileAttribs;

		private ZipDOSAttributes m_externalFileAttribs;

		private uint m_diskNumberStart;

		private long m_localHeaderOffset;

		private string m_fileName = string.Empty;

		private string m_fileComment = string.Empty;

		private ushort m_originalLastModFileTime;

		private ExtraHeaderList m_extraHeaders = new ExtraHeaderList();

		private bool m_canChangeDates = true;

		private EncryptionMethod m_encryptionMethod = EncryptionMethod.Compatible;

		private int m_encryptionStrength = 256;

		public ZipVersionField VersionMadeBy
		{
			get
			{
				return m_versionMadeBy;
			}
			set
			{
				m_versionMadeBy = value;
			}
		}

		public ZipVersionField VersionNeeded
		{
			get
			{
				return m_versionNeeded;
			}
			set
			{
				m_versionNeeded = value;
			}
		}

		public ZipGeneralBitFlags GeneralBitFlags
		{
			get
			{
				return m_generalBitFlags;
			}
			set
			{
				m_generalBitFlags = value;
			}
		}

		public ZipCompressionMethod CompressionMethod
		{
			get
			{
				return m_compressionMethod;
			}
			set
			{
				m_compressionMethod = value;
			}
		}

		public ZipInternalFlags InternalFileAttribs
		{
			get
			{
				return m_internalFileAttribs;
			}
			set
			{
				m_internalFileAttribs = value;
			}
		}

		public ZipDOSAttributes ExternalFileAttribs
		{
			get
			{
				return m_externalFileAttribs;
			}
			set
			{
				m_externalFileAttribs = value;
			}
		}

		public DateTime LastModifiedDateTime
		{
			get
			{
				return m_lastModifiedDateTime;
			}
			set
			{
				if (CanChangeDates)
				{
					m_lastModifiedDateTime = value;
				}
			}
		}

		public DateTime LastAccessedDateTime
		{
			get
			{
				return m_lastAccessedDateTime;
			}
			set
			{
				if (CanChangeDates)
				{
					m_lastAccessedDateTime = value;
				}
			}
		}

		public DateTime CreationDateTime
		{
			get
			{
				return m_creationDateTime;
			}
			set
			{
				if (CanChangeDates)
				{
					m_creationDateTime = value;
				}
			}
		}

		public bool CanChangeDates
		{
			get
			{
				return m_canChangeDates;
			}
			set
			{
				m_canChangeDates = value;
			}
		}

		public uint Crc32
		{
			get
			{
				return m_crc32;
			}
			set
			{
				m_crc32 = value;
			}
		}

		public long CompressedSize
		{
			get
			{
				return m_compressedSize;
			}
			set
			{
				m_compressedSize = value;
			}
		}

		public long UncompressedSize
		{
			get

				return m_uncompressedSize;
			}
			set
			{
				m_uncompressedSize = value;
			}
		}

		public uint LocalHeaderDisk
		{
			get
			{
				return m_diskNumberStart;
			}
			set
			{
				m_diskNumberStart = value;
			}
		}

		public long LocalHeaderOffset
		{
			get
			{
				return m_localHeaderOffset;
			}
			set
			{
				m_localHeaderOffset = value;
			}
		}

		public string FileName
		{
			get
			{
				return m_fileName;
			}
			set
			{
				m_fileName = value;
			}
		}

		public ExtraHeaderList ExtraHeaders => m_extraHeaders;

		public string FileComment
		{
			get
			{
				return m_fileComment;
			}
			set
			{
				m_fileComment = value;
			}
		}

		public bool Encrypted
		{
			get
			{
				checked
				{
					return (ushort)unchecked((int)(m_generalBitFlags & ZipGeneralBitFlags.Encrypted)) != 0;
				}
			}
			set
			{
				if (value)
				{
					m_generalBitFlags = (ZipGeneralBitFlags)checked((ushort)unchecked((int)(m_generalBitFlags | ZipGeneralBitFlags.Encrypted)));
				}
				else
				{
					m_generalBitFlags = (ZipGeneralBitFlags)checked((ushort)unchecked((int)(m_generalBitFlags & ~ZipGeneralBitFlags.Encrypted)));
				}
			}
		}

		public bool HasDescriptorHeader
		{
			get
			{
				checked
				{
					return (ushort)unchecked((int)(m_generalBitFlags & ZipGeneralBitFlags.DescriptorHeader)) != 0;
				}
			}
			set
			{
				if (value)
				{
					m_generalBitFlags = (ZipGeneralBitFlags)checked((ushort)unchecked((int)(m_generalBitFlags | ZipGeneralBitFlags.DescriptorHeader)));
				}
				else
				{
					m_generalBitFlags = (ZipGeneralBitFlags)checked((ushort)unchecked((int)(m_generalBitFlags & ~ZipGeneralBitFlags.DescriptorHeader)));
				}
			}
		}

		public CompressionLevel CompressionLevel
		{
			get
			{
				if (m_compressionMethod == ZipCompressionMethod.Stored)
				{
					return CompressionLevel.None;
				}
				return (ZipGeneralBitFlags)checked((ushort)unchecked((int)(m_generalBitFlags & ZipGeneralBitFlags.LowestCompLevel))) switch
				{
					ZipGeneralBitFlags.LowestCompLevel => CompressionLevel.Lowest, 
					ZipGeneralBitFlags.LowCompLevel => CompressionLevel.Lowest, 
					ZipGeneralBitFlags.MaxCompLevel => CompressionLevel.Highest, 
					_ => CompressionLevel.Normal, 
				};
			}
			set
			{
				m_generalBitFlags = (ZipGeneralBitFlags)checked((ushort)unchecked((int)(m_generalBitFlags & ~ZipGeneralBitFlags.LowestCompLevel)));
				switch (value)
				{
				case CompressionLevel.Highest:
					m_generalBitFlags = (ZipGeneralBitFlags)checked((ushort)unchecked((int)(m_generalBitFlags | ZipGeneralBitFlags.MaxCompLevel)));
					return;
				case CompressionLevel.Normal:
				case (CompressionLevel)7:
				case (CompressionLevel)8:
					m_generalBitFlags = (ZipGeneralBitFlags)checked((ushort)unchecked((int)m_generalBitFlags));
					return;
				}
				if (value < CompressionLevel.Normal && value >= CompressionLevel.Lowest)
				{
					m_generalBitFlags = (ZipGeneralBitFlags)checked((ushort)unchecked((int)(m_generalBitFlags | ZipGeneralBitFlags.LowCompLevel)));
				}
				else if (value > CompressionLevel.None)
				{
					m_generalBitFlags = (ZipGeneralBitFlags)checked((ushort)unchecked((int)(m_generalBitFlags | ZipGeneralBitFlags.LowestCompLevel)));
				}
				else
				{
					m_compressionMethod = ZipCompressionMethod.Stored;
				}
			}
		}

		public bool IsTextFile
		{
			get
			{
				checked
				{
					return (ushort)unchecked((int)(m_internalFileAttribs & ZipInternalFlags.TextFile)) != 0;
				}
			}
			set
			{
				if (value)
				{
					m_internalFileAttribs = (ZipInternalFlags)checked((ushort)unchecked((int)(m_internalFileAttribs | ZipInternalFlags.TextFile)));
				}
				else
				{
					m_internalFileAttribs = (ZipInternalFlags)checked((ushort)unchecked((int)(m_internalFileAttribs & ~ZipInternalFlags.TextFile)));
				}
			}
		}

		public bool IsFolder
		{
			get
			{
				return (m_externalFileAttribs & ZipDOSAttributes.Directory) != 0;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value)
				{
					m_externalFileAttribs |= ZipDOSAttributes.Directory;
				}
				else
				{
					m_externalFileAttribs &= ~ZipDOSAttributes.Directory;
				}
			}
		}

		public FileAttributes FileAttributes
		{
			get
			{
				FileAttributes fileAttributes = (FileAttributes)0;
				if ((m_externalFileAttribs & ZipDOSAttributes.ReadOnly) != ZipDOSAttributes.Normal)
				{
					fileAttributes |= FileAttributes.ReadOnly;
				}
				if ((m_externalFileAttribs & ZipDOSAttributes.Hidden) != ZipDOSAttributes.Normal)
				{
					fileAttributes |= FileAttributes.Hidden;
				}
				if ((m_externalFileAttribs & ZipDOSAttributes.System) != ZipDOSAttributes.Normal)
				{
					fileAttributes |= FileAttributes.System;
				}
				if ((m_externalFileAttribs & ZipDOSAttributes.Directory) != ZipDOSAttributes.Normal)
				{
					fileAttributes |= FileAttributes.Directory;
				}
				if ((m_externalFileAttribs & ZipDOSAttributes.Archive) != ZipDOSAttributes.Normal)
				{
					fileAttributes |= FileAttributes.Archive;
				}
				if ((m_externalFileAttribs & ZipDOSAttributes.Compressed) != ZipDOSAttributes.Normal)
				{
					fileAttributes |= FileAttributes.Compressed;
				}
				if (fileAttributes == (FileAttributes)0)
				{
					return FileAttributes.Normal;
				}
				return fileAttributes;
			}
			set
			{
				ZipDOSAttributes zipDOSAttributes = ZipDOSAttributes.Normal;
				if ((value & FileAttributes.ReadOnly) != 0)
				{
					zipDOSAttributes |= ZipDOSAttributes.ReadOnly;
				}
				if ((value & FileAttributes.Hidden) != 0)
				{
					zipDOSAttributes |= ZipDOSAttributes.Hidden;
				}
				if ((value & FileAttributes.System) != 0)
				{
					zipDOSAttributes |= ZipDOSAttributes.System;
				}
				if ((value & FileAttributes.Directory) != 0)
				{
					zipDOSAttributes |= ZipDOSAttributes.Directory;
				}
				if ((value & FileAttributes.Archive) != 0)
				{
					zipDOSAttributes |= ZipDOSAttributes.Archive;
				}
				if ((value & FileAttributes.Compressed) != 0)
				{
					zipDOSAttributes |= ZipDOSAttributes.Compressed;
				}
				m_externalFileAttribs &= (ZipDOSAttributes)4294901760u;
				m_externalFileAttribs |= zipDOSAttributes;
			}
		}

		public ushort EncryptionKey
		{
			get
			{
				ushort dosDate = 0;
				ushort dosTime = 0;
				Dates.ToDosDateTime(m_lastModifiedDateTime, out dosDate, out dosTime);
				return dosTime;
			}
		}

		public ushort OriginalEncryptionKey => m_originalLastModFileTime;

		public EncryptionMethod EncryptionMethod
		{
			get
			{
				return m_encryptionMethod;
			}
			set
			{
				m_encryptionMethod = value;
			}
		}

		public int EncryptionStrength
		{
			get
			{
				return m_encryptionStrength;
			}
			set
			{
				m_encryptionStrength = value;
			}
		}

		public ItemHeader()
		{
		}

		public ItemHeader(string filename)
		{
			m_fileName = filename;
			IsFolder = filename.EndsWith(Path.DirectorySeparatorChar.ToString());
			foreach (ExtraHeader item in (IEnumerable)ZipHandler.DefaultExtraHeaders)
			{
				m_extraHeaders.Add(item.CreateInstance(this));
			}
		}

		public static string FixFilename(string filename)
		{
			filename = filename.Replace('/', Path.DirectorySeparatorChar);
			if (filename.StartsWith(Path.DirectorySeparatorChar.ToString()))
			{
				return Path.DirectorySeparatorChar + "." + filename;
			}
			return Path.DirectorySeparatorChar + filename;
		}

		public static string UnfixFilename(string filename)
		{
			if (filename.StartsWith(Path.DirectorySeparatorChar.ToString()))
			{
				return filename.Substring(1).Replace(Path.DirectorySeparatorChar, '/');
			}
			return filename.Replace(Path.DirectorySeparatorChar, '/');
		}

		public static uint SafeUInt32(long value)
		{
			if (value > uint.MaxValue)
			{
				return uint.MaxValue;
			}
			return (uint)value;
		}

		public static uint SafeUInt32(ulong value)
		{
			if (value > uint.MaxValue)
			{
				return uint.MaxValue;
			}
			return (uint)value;
		}

		public static ushort SafeUInt16(long value)
		{
			if (value > 65535)
			{
				return ushort.MaxValue;
			}
			return (ushort)value;
		}

		public static ushort SafeUInt16(ulong value)
		{
			if (value > 65535)
			{
				return ushort.MaxValue;
			}
			return (ushort)value;
		}

		public static ushort SafeUInt16(uint value)
		{
			if (value > 65535)
			{
				return ushort.MaxValue;
			}
			return (ushort)value;
		}

		public void ReadCentralHeader(BinaryReader reader)
		{
			if (reader == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("reader");
			}
			VersionMadeBy = (ZipVersionField)reader.ReadUInt16();
			VersionNeeded = (ZipVersionField)reader.ReadUInt16();
			GeneralBitFlags = (ZipGeneralBitFlags)reader.ReadUInt16();
			CompressionMethod = (ZipCompressionMethod)reader.ReadUInt16();
			ushort num = reader.ReadUInt16();
			ushort dosDate = reader.ReadUInt16();
			try
			{
				LastModifiedDateTime = Dates.FromDosDateTime(dosDate, num);
			}
			catch
			{
			}
			Crc32 = reader.ReadUInt32();
			CompressedSize = reader.ReadUInt32();
			UncompressedSize = reader.ReadUInt32();
			ushort num2 = reader.ReadUInt16();
			ushort num3 = reader.ReadUInt16();
			ushort num4 = reader.ReadUInt16();
			LocalHeaderDisk = reader.ReadUInt16();
			InternalFileAttribs = (ZipInternalFlags)reader.ReadUInt16();
			ExternalFileAttribs = (ZipDOSAttributes)reader.ReadUInt32();
			LocalHeaderOffset = reader.ReadUInt32();
			if (num2 == 0)
			{
				FileName = string.Empty;
			}
			else
			{
				byte[] array = reader.ReadBytes(num2);
				if (array.Length != num2)
				{
					throw new EndOfStreamException("Unexpected end of stream while reading a central header from a zip file.");
				}
				string filename = Xceed.Utils.Strings.Encoding.OEMEncoding.GetString(array, 0, array.Length).Replace('/', Path.DirectorySeparatorChar);
				filename = FixFilename(filename);
				if (filename.EndsWith(Path.DirectorySeparatorChar.ToString()))
				{
					IsFolder = true;
				}
				FileName = filename;
			}
			ExtraHeaders.Clear();
			if (num3 > 0)
			{
				byte[] array2 = reader.ReadBytes(num3);
				if (array2.Length != num3)
				{
					throw new EndOfStreamException("Unexpected end of stream while reading a central header from a zip file.");
				}
				ReadExtraHeaders(ZipHandler.AvailableExtraHeaders, array2, fromCentralHeader: true);
			}
			if (num4 == 0)
			{
				FileComment = string.Empty;
			}
			else
			{
				byte[] array3 = reader.ReadBytes(num4);
				if (array3.Length != num4)
				{
					throw new EndOfStreamException("Unexpected end of stream while reading a central header from a zip file.");
				}
				FileComment = System.Text.Encoding.Default.GetString(array3, 0, array3.Length);
			}
			m_originalLastModFileTime = num;
		}

		public long WriteCentralHeader(ZipStream stream, out int size)
		{
			if (stream == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("stream");
			}
			string s = UnfixFilename(FileName);
			byte[] bytes = Xceed.Utils.Strings.Encoding.OEMEncoding.GetBytes(s);
			byte[] array = WriteExtraHeaders(ZipHandler.AvailableExtraHeaders, forCentralHeader: true);
			byte[] bytes2 = System.Text.Encoding.Default.GetBytes(FileComment);
			MemoryStream memoryStream = new MemoryStream();
			BinaryWriter binaryWriter = new BinaryWriter(memoryStream);
			try
			{
				binaryWriter.Write(33639248u);
				binaryWriter.Write((ushort)VersionMadeBy);
				binaryWriter.Write((ushort)VersionNeeded);
				binaryWriter.Write((ushort)GeneralBitFlags);
				if (Encrypted && EncryptionMethod == EncryptionMethod.WinZipAes)
				{
					binaryWriter.Write((ushort)99);
				}
				else
				{
					binaryWriter.Write((ushort)CompressionMethod);
				}
				ushort dosTime = 0;
				ushort dosDate = 0;
				Dates.ToDosDateTime(LastModifiedDateTime, out dosDate, out dosTime);
				binaryWriter.Write(dosTime);
				binaryWriter.Write(dosDate);
				binaryWriter.Write(Crc32);
				binaryWriter.Write(SafeUInt32(CompressedSize));
				binaryWriter.Write(SafeUInt32(UncompressedSize));
				checked
				{
					binaryWriter.Write((ushort)bytes.Length);
					binaryWriter.Write((ushort)array.Length);
					binaryWriter.Write((ushort)bytes2.Length);
					binaryWriter.Write(SafeUInt16(LocalHeaderDisk));
				}
				binaryWriter.Write((ushort)InternalFileAttribs);
				binaryWriter.Write((uint)ExternalFileAttribs);
				binaryWriter.Write(SafeUInt32(LocalHeaderOffset));
				if (bytes.Length > 0)
				{
					binaryWriter.Write(bytes);
				}
				if (array.Length > 0)
				{
					binaryWriter.Write(array);
				}
				if (bytes2.Length > 0)
				{
					binaryWriter.Write(bytes2);
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				binaryWriter.Close();
			}
			byte[] array2 = memoryStream.ToArray();
			size = array2.Length;
			stream.WriteNoBreak(array2, 0, size);
			return checked(stream.Position - size);
		}

		public bool ReadLocalHeader(BinaryReader reader)
		{
			if (reader == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("reader");
			}
			ZipVersionField zipVersionField = (ZipVersionField)reader.ReadUInt16();
			ZipGeneralBitFlags zipGeneralBitFlags = (ZipGeneralBitFlags)reader.ReadUInt16();
			ZipCompressionMethod zipCompressionMethod = (ZipCompressionMethod)reader.ReadUInt16();
			ushort dosTime = reader.ReadUInt16();
			ushort dosDate = reader.ReadUInt16();
			_ = DateTime.MinValue;
			try
			{
				Dates.FromDosDateTime(dosDate, dosTime);
			}
			catch
			{
			}
			uint num = reader.ReadUInt32();
			long num2 = reader.ReadUInt32();
			long num3 = reader.ReadUInt32();
			ushort num4 = reader.ReadUInt16();
			ushort num5 = reader.ReadUInt16();
			if (num4 > 0)
			{
				byte[] array = reader.ReadBytes(num4);
				if (array.Length != num4)
				{
					throw new EndOfStreamException("Unexpected end of stream while reading a local header from a zip file.");
				}
				Xceed.Utils.Strings.Encoding.OEMEncoding.GetString(array, 0, array.Length).Replace('/', Path.DirectorySeparatorChar);
			}
			if (num5 > 0)
			{
				byte[] array2 = reader.ReadBytes(num5);
				if (array2.Length != num5)
				{
					throw new EndOfStreamException("Unexpected end of stream while reading a local header from a zip file.");
				}
				ReadExtraHeaders(ZipHandler.AvailableExtraHeaders, array2, fromCentralHeader: false);
			}
			checked
			{
				if ((ushort)unchecked((int)(zipGeneralBitFlags & ZipGeneralBitFlags.UsefulBitsMask)) != (ushort)unchecked((int)(GeneralBitFlags & ZipGeneralBitFlags.UsefulBitsMask)))
				{
					GeneralBitFlags = zipGeneralBitFlags;
					return false;
				}
				if (zipVersionField != VersionNeeded || (zipCompressionMethod != ZipCompressionMethod.Unknown && zipCompressionMethod != CompressionMethod))
				{
					return false;
				}
				if (!HasDescriptorHeader && (num != Crc32 || (num2 != uint.MaxValue && num2 != CompressedSize) || (num3 != uint.MaxValue && num3 != UncompressedSize)))
				{
					return false;
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

		public long WriteLocalHeader(ZipStream stream, out int size)
		{
			if (stream == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("stream");
			}
			string s = UnfixFilename(FileName);
			byte[] bytes = Xceed.Utils.Strings.Encoding.OEMEncoding.GetBytes(s);
			byte[] array = WriteExtraHeaders(ZipHandler.AvailableExtraHeaders, forCentralHeader: false);
			if (CompressedSize >= uint.MaxValue || UncompressedSize >= uint.MaxValue)
			{
				VersionNeeded = ZipVersionField.VerZip64;
			}
			else if (CompressionMethod == ZipCompressionMethod.Deflated64)
			{
				VersionNeeded = ZipVersionField.VerDeflate64;
			}
			else if (CompressionMethod == ZipCompressionMethod.BZip2)
			{
				VersionNeeded = ZipVersionField.VerBZip2;
				VersionMadeBy = ZipVersionField.VerBZip2;
			}
			else if (CompressionMethod == ZipCompressionMethod.BWT)
			{
				VersionNeeded = ZipVersionField.VerRecent;
				VersionMadeBy = ZipVersionField.VerDeflate64;
			}
			else
			{
				VersionNeeded = ZipVersionField.VerRecent;
			}
			MemoryStream memoryStream = new MemoryStream();
			BinaryWriter binaryWriter = new BinaryWriter(memoryStream);
			try
			{
				binaryWriter.Write(67324752u);
				binaryWriter.Write((ushort)VersionNeeded);
				binaryWriter.Write((ushort)GeneralBitFlags);
				if (Encrypted && EncryptionMethod == EncryptionMethod.WinZipAes)
				{
					binaryWriter.Write((ushort)99);
				}
				else
				{
					binaryWriter.Write((ushort)CompressionMethod);
				}
				ushort dosTime = 0;
				ushort dosDate = 0;
				if (LastModifiedDateTime != DateTime.MinValue)
				{
					Dates.ToDosDateTime(LastModifiedDateTime, out dosDate, out dosTime);
				}
				binaryWriter.Write(dosTime);
				binaryWriter.Write(dosDate);
				binaryWriter.Write(Crc32);
				binaryWriter.Write(SafeUInt32(CompressedSize));
				binaryWriter.Write(SafeUInt32(UncompressedSize));
				checked
				{
					binaryWriter.Write((ushort)bytes.Length);
					binaryWriter.Write((ushort)array.Length);
					if (bytes.Length > 0)
					{
						binaryWriter.Write(bytes);
					}
					if (array.Length > 0)
					{
						binaryWriter.Write(array);
					}
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				binaryWriter.Close();
			}
			byte[] array2 = memoryStream.ToArray();
			size = array2.Length;
			stream.WriteNoBreak(array2, 0, array2.Length);
			return checked(stream.Position - size);
		}

		public bool ReadDescriptorHeader(BinaryReader reader)
		{
			if (reader == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("reader");
			}
			uint num = reader.ReadUInt32();
			checked
			{
				long num2;
				long num3;
				if (CompressedSize >= uint.MaxValue || UncompressedSize >= uint.MaxValue)
				{
					num2 = (long)reader.ReadUInt64();
					num3 = (long)reader.ReadUInt64();
				}
				else
				{
					num2 = reader.ReadUInt32();
					num3 = reader.ReadUInt32();
				}
				if (num != Crc32 || num2 != CompressedSize || num3 != UncompressedSize)
				{
					Crc32 = num;
					CompressedSize = num2;
					UncompressedSize = num3;
					return false;
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

		public long WriteDescriptorHeader(ZipStream stream, out int size)
		{
			if (stream == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("stream");
			}
			MemoryStream memoryStream = new MemoryStream();
			BinaryWriter binaryWriter = new BinaryWriter(memoryStream);
			checked
			{
				try
				{
					binaryWriter.Write(134695760u);
					binaryWriter.Write(Crc32);
					if (CompressedSize >= uint.MaxValue || UncompressedSize >= uint.MaxValue)
					{
						binaryWriter.Write((ulong)CompressedSize);
						binaryWriter.Write((ulong)UncompressedSize);
					}
					else
					{
						binaryWriter.Write((uint)CompressedSize);
						binaryWriter.Write((uint)UncompressedSize);
					}
				}
				finally
				{
					binaryWriter.Close();
				}
				byte[] array = memoryStream.ToArray();
				size = array.Length;
				stream.WriteNoBreak(array, 0, array.Length);
				return stream.Position - size;
			}
		}

		private void ReadExtraHeaders(ExtraHeaderList availableExtraHeaders, byte[] extraField, bool fromCentralHeader)
		{
			if (extraField == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("extraField");
			}
			int num = 0;
			int num2 = extraField.Length;
			checked
			{
				try
				{
					CanChangeDates = true;
					while (num < num2 && num2 - num >= 4)
					{
						short signature = BitConverter.ToInt16(extraField, num);
						short num3 = BitConverter.ToInt16(extraField, num + 2);
						num += 4;
						if (num2 - num < num3)
						{
							break;
						}
						bool flag = false;
						ExtraHeader extraHeader = null;
						if (!fromCentralHeader)
						{
							extraHeader = ExtraHeaders.Find(signature);
						}
						if (extraHeader == null)
						{
							extraHeader = availableExtraHeaders.Find(signature);
							if (extraHeader != null)
							{
								extraHeader = extraHeader.CreateInstance(this);
								flag = !extraHeader.Volatile;
							}
						}
						if (extraHeader == null)
						{
							extraHeader = new UnknownExtraHeader(signature);
							flag = true;
						}
						try
						{
							extraHeader.Read(this, extraField, num, num3, fromCentralHeader);
						}
						catch (InvalidZipStructureException)
						{
							break;
						}
						num += num3;
						if (flag && fromCentralHeader)
						{
							ExtraHeaders.Add(extraHeader);
						}
					}
				}
				finally
				{
					CanChangeDates = true;
				}
			}
		}

		private byte[] WriteExtraHeaders(ExtraHeaderList availableExtraHeaders, bool forCentralHeader)
		{
			using MemoryStream memoryStream = new MemoryStream();
			BinaryWriter binaryWriter = new BinaryWriter(memoryStream);
			try
			{
				foreach (ExtraHeader item in (IEnumerable)ExtraHeaders)
				{
					item.Write(this, binaryWriter, forCentralHeader);
				}
				foreach (ExtraHeader item2 in (IEnumerable)availableExtraHeaders)
				{
					if (item2.Volatile)
					{
						ExtraHeader extraHeader3 = item2.CreateInstance(this);
						extraHeader3.Write(this, binaryWriter, forCentralHeader);
					}
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				binaryWriter.Close();
			}
			return memoryStream.ToArray();
		}
	}
	public static class Licenser
	{

		private static string _licenseKey = string.Empty;

		public static string LicenseKey
		{
			get
			{
				return _licenseKey;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value == null)
				{
					throw new ArgumentNullException("LicenseKey");
				}
				CreateLicense(value);
				_licenseKey = value;
				Xceed.Compression.Licenser.LicenseKey = _licenseKey;
				Xceed.FileSystem.Licenser.LicenseKey = _licenseKey;
			}
		}

		internal static bool IsTrial
		{
			get
			{
				foreach (ZipLicense license in LicenseList)
				{
					if (!license.IsTrial)
					{
						return false;
					}
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

		internal static bool AllowZip64
		{
			get
			{
				foreach (ZipLicense license in LicenseList)
				{
					switch (license.Product)
					{
					case Xceed.Utils.Licensing.LicenseProduct.XceedZipNET:
					case Xceed.Utils.Licensing.LicenseProduct.XceedZipNETCF:
					case Xceed.Utils.Licensing.LicenseProduct.XceedZipNETSfx:
						if (license.VersionMajor > 2 || (license.VersionMajor == 2 && license.VersionMinor >= 3))
						{
							return true;
						}
						break;
					case Xceed.Utils.Licensing.LicenseProduct.XceedFtpNET:
						if (license.VersionMajor >= 3)
						{
							return true;
						}
						break;
					case Xceed.Utils.Licensing.LicenseProduct.XceedWorkflowActivitiesNET:
						if (license.VersionMajor >= 1)
						{
							return true;
						}
						break;
					}
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		internal static bool AllowExtraHeaders
		{
			get

					case Xceed.Utils.Licensing.LicenseProduct.XceedZipNETCF:
					case Xceed.Utils.Licensing.LicenseProduct.XceedZipNETSfx:
						if (license.VersionMajor > 2 || (license.VersionMajor == 2 && license.VersionMinor >= 2))
						{
							return true;
						}
						break;
					case Xceed.Utils.Licensing.LicenseProduct.XceedFtpNET:
						if (license.VersionMajor >= 3)
						{
							return true;
