		internal static void VerifyLicense()
		{
			if (LicenseList.Count == 0 && LicenseManager.UsageMode != LicenseUsageMode.Designtime)
			{
				CreateLicense(string.Empty);
			}
		}

		internal static void VerifyLicense(FeatureID featureId)
		{
			VerifyLicense();
			if (LicenseList.Count == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			string text = null;
			switch (featureId)
			{
			default:
				return;
			case FeatureID.Deflate64:
				if (!AllowDeflate64)
				{
					text = "Deflate64 feature available in Xceed Zip for .NET v2.0 or higher";
				}
				break;
			case FeatureID.BZip2_BWT:
				if (!AllowBZip2_BWT)
				{
					text = "BZip2 and BWT feature available in Xceed Zip for .NET v3.4 or higher";
				}
				break;
			case FeatureID.ExtraHeaders:
				if (!AllowExtraHeaders)
				{
					text = "Extra Headers feature available in Xceed Zip for .NET v2.2 or higher";
				}
				break;
			case FeatureID.PPMd:
				if (!AllowPPMd)
				{
					text = "PPMd feature available in Xceed Zip for .NET v3.5 or higher";
				}
				break;
			case FeatureID.NewSfx:
				if (!AllowNewSFX)
				{
					text = "Self-Extracting feature only available in product Xceed Zip for .NET with Self-Extractor";
				}
				break;
			case FeatureID.Spanning:
				if (!AllowSpanning)
				{
					text = "Splitting and Spanning features available in Xceed Zip for .NET v2.0 or higher";
				}
				break;
			case FeatureID.Zip64:
				if (!AllowZip64)
				{
					text = "Zip64 format feature available in Xceed Zip for .NET v2.3 or higher";
				}
				break;
			case FeatureID.WinZipAes:
				if (!AllowWinZipAes)
				{
					text = "AES encryption feature available in Xceed Zip for .NET v3.1 or higher.";
				}
				break;
			case FeatureID.Sfx:
				return;
			}
			if (text != null)
			{
				Xceed.Utils.Licensing.XceedLicense xceedLicense = LicenseList[checked(LicenseList.Count - 1)] as Xceed.Utils.Licensing.XceedLicense;
				xceedLicense.DisplayFeatureRequireUpgrade(xceedLicense.VersionMajor, xceedLicense.VersionMinor, xceedLicense.Product, text);
				Xceed.Utils.Exceptions.ThrowException.ThrowLicenseException(null, null, "You are using a v" + xceedLicense.VersionMajor + "." + xceedLicense.VersionMinor + " registered license key but are accessing the " + text + ".");
			}
		}

		private static void CreateLicense(string licenseKey)
		{
			Xceed.Utils.Licensing.XceedLicense xceedLicense = ZipLicense.CreateLicense(licenseKey, typeof(Licenser));
			lock (LicenseList.SyncRoot)
			{
				foreach (ZipLicense license in LicenseList)
				{
					if (license.Product == xceedLicense.Product && license.VersionMajor == xceedLicense.VersionMajor && license.VersionMinor == xceedLicense.VersionMinor)
					{
						LicenseList.Remove(license);
						break;
					}
				}
				LicenseList.Add(xceedLicense);
			}
		}
	}
	internal class QuickZipCompression : AbstractQuickAction
	{
		protected override AbstractQuickActionItem OnGetFolderContentItemFound(FileSystemItem fileSystemItem)
		{
			if (fileSystemItem is ZippedFile)
			{
				return new QuickZipItem((ZippedFile)fileSystemItem);
			}
			return null;
		}
	}
}
namespace Xceed.Zip.ReaderWriter
{
	internal enum FeatureID
	{
		NoFeatureSpecified
	}
	public static class Licenser
	{
		private static ArrayList LicenseList = new ArrayList();

		private static string _licenseKey = string.Empty;

		public static string LicenseKey
		{
			get
			{
				return _licenseKey;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value == null)
				{
					throw new ArgumentNullException("LicenseKey");
				}
				CreateLicense(value);
				_licenseKey = value;
				Xceed.Compression.Licenser.LicenseKey = _licenseKey;
			}
		}

		internal static bool IsTrial
		{
			get
			{
				foreach (ReaderWriterLicense license in LicenseList)
				{
					if (!license.IsTrial)
					{
						return false;
					}
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

		internal static void VerifyLicense()
		{
			if (LicenseList.Count == 0 && LicenseManager.UsageMode != LicenseUsageMode.Designtime)
			{
				CreateLicense(string.Empty);
			}
		}

		internal static void VerifyLicense(FeatureID featureId)
		{
			VerifyLicense();
			if (LicenseList.Count != 0)
			{
			}
		}

		private static void CreateLicense(string licenseKey)
		{
			Xceed.Utils.Licensing.XceedLicense xceedLicense = ReaderWriterLicense.CreateLicense(licenseKey, typeof(Licenser));
			lock (LicenseList.SyncRoot)
			{
				foreach (ReaderWriterLicense license in LicenseList)
				{
					if (license.Product == xceedLicense.Product && license.VersionMajor == xceedLicense.VersionMajor && license.VersionMinor == xceedLicense.VersionMinor)
					{
						LicenseList.Remove(license);
						break;
					}
				}
				LicenseList.Add(xceedLicense);
			}
		}
	}
	public class ZipItemLocalHeader
	{
		private CompressionMethod m_compressionMethod = CompressionMethod.Deflated64;

		private CompressionLevel m_compressionLevel = CompressionLevel.Normal;

		private EncryptionMethod m_encryptionMethod = EncryptionMethod.Compatible;

		private string m_encryptionPassword;

		private string m_fileName;

		public CompressionMethod CompressionMethod
		{
			get
			{
				return m_compressionMethod;
			}
			set
			{
				m_compressionMethod = value;
			}
		}

		public CompressionLevel CompressionLevel
		{
			get
			{
				return m_compressionLevel;
			}
			set
			{
				m_compressionLevel = value;
			}
		}

		public string EncryptionPassword
		{
			get
			{
				return m_encryptionPassword;
			}
			set
			{
				m_encryptionPassword = value;
			}
		}

		public EncryptionMethod EncryptionMethod
		{
			get
			{
				return m_encryptionMethod;
			}
			set
			{
				m_encryptionMethod = value;
			}
		}

		public string FileName
		{
			get
			{
				return m_fileName;
			}
			set
			{
				m_fileName = value;
			}
		}

		public bool IsFolder => m_fileName.EndsWith(Path.DirectorySeparatorChar.ToString());

		public ZipItemLocalHeader(string fileName)
			: this(fileName, CompressionMethod.Deflated64, CompressionLevel.Normal)
		{
		}

		public ZipItemLocalHeader(string fileName, CompressionMethod compressionMethod, CompressionLevel compressionLevel)
			: this(fileName, compressionMethod, compressionLevel, EncryptionMethod.Compatible, string.Empty)
		{
		}

		public ZipItemLocalHeader(string fileName, CompressionMethod compressionMethod, CompressionLevel compressionLevel, EncryptionMethod encryptionMethod, string encryptionPassword)
		{
			if (!fileName.StartsWith(Path.DirectorySeparatorChar.ToString()))
			{
				fileName = Path.DirectorySeparatorChar + fileName;
			}
			m_fileName = fileName;
			if (compressionLevel == CompressionLevel.None)
			{
				throw new NotSupportedException("Compression method Stored is not supported by Real-Time Zip.");
			}
			if (compressionLevel == CompressionLevel.None)
			{
				throw new NotSupportedException("Compression level None is not supported by Real-Time Zip.");
			}
			m_compressionMethod = compressionMethod;
			m_compressionLevel = compressionLevel;
			m_encryptionMethod = encryptionMethod;
			m_encryptionPassword = encryptionPassword;
		}

		internal ZipItemLocalHeader(ReaderWriterItemInfo streamingInfo)
		{
			m_fileName = streamingInfo.FileName;
			m_compressionLevel = streamingInfo.CompressionLevel;
			switch (streamingInfo.CompressionMethod)
			{
			case ZipCompressionMethod.BWT:
				m_compressionMethod = CompressionMethod.BWT;
				break;
			case ZipCompressionMethod.BZip2:
				m_compressionMethod = CompressionMethod.BZip2;
				break;
			case ZipCompressionMethod.Deflated:
				m_compressionMethod = CompressionMethod.Deflated;
				break;
			case ZipCompressionMethod.Deflated64:
				m_compressionMethod = CompressionMethod.Deflated64;
				break;
			case ZipCompressionMethod.Stored:
				m_compressionMethod = CompressionMethod.Stored;
				break;
			case ZipCompressionMethod.PPMd:
				m_compressionMethod = CompressionMethod.PPMd;
				break;
			default:
				throw new ZipReaderException(string.Concat("CompressionMethod ", streamingInfo.CompressionMethod, " not supported by ZipReader."));
			}
			switch ((ZipGeneralBitFlags)checked((ushort)unchecked((int)(streamingInfo.GeneralBitFlags & ZipGeneralBitFlags.LowestCompLevel))))
			{
			case ZipGeneralBitFlags.LowestCompLevel:
				m_compressionLevel = CompressionLevel.Lowest;
				break;
			case ZipGeneralBitFlags.LowCompLevel:
				m_compressionLevel = CompressionLevel.Lowest;
				break;
			case ZipGeneralBitFlags.MaxCompLevel:
				m_compressionLevel = CompressionLevel.Highest;
				break;
			default:
				if (m_compressionMethod == CompressionMethod.Stored)
				{
					m_compressionLevel = CompressionLevel.None;
				}
				else
				{
					m_compressionLevel = CompressionLevel.Normal;
				}
				break;
			}
			m_encryptionMethod = streamingInfo.EncryptionMethod;
		}

		public override string ToString()
		{
			return FileName;
		}
	}
	public class ZipReader : IDisposable
	{
		private ZipItemLocalHeader m_currentZipItemLocalHeader;

		private ReaderWriterItemInfo m_currentItemInfo;

		private Stream m_innerStream;

		private Stream m_readerStream;

		private CompressedStream m_compressedStream;

		private ChecksumStream m_checksumStream;

		private ZipReaderState m_zipReaderState;

		private string m_encryptionPassword;

		private byte[] m_lastReadBuffer = new byte[0];

		private int m_lastReadBufferOffset;

		private long m_innerStreamPosition;

		private byte[] m_readerBuffer = new byte[8];

		private long m_uncompressedSize;

		public event EventHandler<ZipReaderByteProgressionEventArgs> ByteProgression;

		public event EventHandler<ZipReaderInvalidPasswordEventArgs> InvalidPassword;

		public ZipReader(Stream innerStream)
			: this(innerStream, string.Empty)
		{
		}

		public ZipReader(Stream innerStream, string encryptionPassword)
		{
			Licenser.VerifyLicense();
			if (innerStream == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("innerStream", "Inner stream can't be null.");
			}
			if (!innerStream.CanSeek)
			{
				innerStream = new PositionedStream(innerStream);
			}
			NotifyStream notifyStream = new NotifyStream(innerStream, null)
			{
				Transient = true
			};
			if (notifyStream != null)
			{
				notifyStream.ReadingFromStream += ZipReader_ReadingFromStream;
				m_innerStream = notifyStream;
				m_zipReaderState = ZipReaderState.INTIALIZE_ZIP_READER;
				m_encryptionPassword = encryptionPassword;
				return;
			}
			throw new ZipReaderException("Error while creating NotifyStream with inner stream.");
		}

		public ZipItemLocalHeader ReadItemLocalHeader()
		{
			do
			{
				if (m_zipReaderState == ZipReaderState.INTIALIZE_ZIP_READER || m_currentZipItemLocalHeader.IsFolder)
				{
					switch (ReadSignature())
					{
					case ZipSignatureType.CentralHeader:
						m_zipReaderState = ZipReaderState.READ_CENTRAL_HEADER;
						CentralHeaderReached();
						return null;
					case ZipSignatureType.LocalHeader:
						break;
					default:
						throw new ZipReaderException("Unable to find file LocalHeader or CentralDirectory signature of zip file.");
					}
					m_zipReaderState = ZipReaderState.READ_LOCAL_HEADER;
				}
				else if (m_zipReaderState == ZipReaderState.READ_CENTRAL_HEADER)
				{
					CentralHeaderReached();
					return null;
				}
				m_currentItemInfo = new ReaderWriterItemInfo();
				DoReadItemLocalHeader(m_currentItemInfo);
				m_currentZipItemLocalHeader = new ZipItemLocalHeader(m_currentItemInfo);
				m_currentZipItemLocalHeader.EncryptionPassword = m_encryptionPassword;
				InitializeZipReader(m_currentItemInfo);
				m_zipReaderState = ZipReaderState.READING_DATA;
				if (m_currentZipItemLocalHeader.IsFolder && m_currentItemInfo.Encrypted)
				{
					ZipSignatureType zipSignatureType = ReadSignature();
					if (zipSignatureType != ZipSignatureType.DataDescriptor)
					{
						throw new ZipReaderException("TODODOC Unable to read DataDescriptor for encrypted folder");
					}
					m_zipReaderState = ZipReaderState.READ_DATA_DESCRIPTOR;
					ReadItemDataDescriptorHeader(m_currentItemInfo);
					m_zipReaderState = ZipReaderState.INTIALIZE_ZIP_READER;
				}
			}
			while (m_currentZipItemLocalHeader.IsFolder);
			return m_currentZipItemLocalHeader;
		}

		public int ReadItemData(byte[] buffer, int offset, int count)
		{
			int num = 0;
			if (m_zipReaderState == ZipReaderState.READING_DATA)
			{
				num = m_readerStream.Read(buffer, offset, count);
				checked
				{
					m_uncompressedSize += num;
					_ = m_currentItemInfo.UncompressedSize;
					_ = 0;
				}
				if (num == 0)
				{
					OnByteProgression();
					try
					{
						m_readerStream.Close();
					}
					catch
					{
					}
					m_zipReaderState = ZipReaderState.READ_DATA_DESCRIPTOR;
					m_lastReadBufferOffset = ReadNextHeaderInLastReadBuffer(m_currentItemInfo);
					if (m_currentItemInfo.HasDescriptorHeader)
					{
						if (m_zipReaderState != ZipReaderState.READ_DATA_DESCRIPTOR)
						{
							throw new ZipReaderException("DataDescriptorHeader signature was expected but not found.");
						}
						ReadItemDataDescriptorHeader(m_currentItemInfo);
						m_zipReaderState = ZipReaderState.INTIALIZE_ZIP_READER;
					}
					if ((!m_currentItemInfo.Encrypted || (m_currentItemInfo.Encrypted && m_currentItemInfo.EncryptionMethod != EncryptionMethod.WinZipAes)) && m_checksumStream.Checksum != (int)m_currentItemInfo.Crc32)
					{
						throw new ZipReaderException("The calculated CRC does not match the CRC written in the data descriptor header for item " + m_currentItemInfo.FileName + ".");
					}
					m_uncompressedSize = 0L;
				}
				else
				{
					OnByteProgression();
				}
			}
			return num;
		}

		public void Dispose()
		{
			try
			{
				if (m_readerStream != null)
				{
					m_readerStream.Dispose();
				}
			}
			catch
			{
			}
			finally
			{
				m_readerStream = null;
			}
			try
			{
				if (m_compressedStream != null)
				{
					m_compressedStream.Dispose();
				}
			}
			catch
			{
			}
			finally
			{
				m_compressedStream = null;
			}
			try
			{
				if (m_checksumStream != null)
				{
					m_checksumStream.Dispose();
				}
			}
			catch
			{
			}
			finally
			{
				m_checksumStream = null;
			}
			m_innerStream = null;
			m_lastReadBuffer = null;
			m_readerBuffer = null;
			m_encryptionPassword = null;
			m_currentZipItemLocalHeader = null;
			m_currentItemInfo = null;
		}

		internal void InitializeZipReader(ReaderWriterItemInfo itemInfo)
		{
			CompressionMethod compressionMethod = CompressionMethod.Deflated;
			switch (itemInfo.CompressionMethod)
			{
			case ZipCompressionMethod.BWT:
				compressionMethod = CompressionMethod.BWT;
				break;
			case ZipCompressionMethod.BZip2:
				compressionMethod = CompressionMethod.BZip2;
				break;
			case ZipCompressionMethod.Deflated:
				compressionMethod = CompressionMethod.Deflated;
				break;
			case ZipCompressionMethod.Deflated64:
				compressionMethod = CompressionMethod.Deflated64;
				break;
			case ZipCompressionMethod.Stored:
				if (!itemInfo.FileName.EndsWith(Path.DirectorySeparatorChar.ToString()) && itemInfo.CompressedSize == 0)
				{
					throw new ZipReaderException(string.Concat("Compression method Stored is not supported by ", GetType(), " when LocalHeader does not contains CompressedSize field (item ", itemInfo.FileName, ")"));
				}
				compressionMethod = CompressionMethod.Stored;
				break;
			case ZipCompressionMethod.PPMd:
				compressionMethod = CompressionMethod.PPMd;
				break;
			default:
				throw new ZipReaderException(string.Concat("CompressionMethod ", itemInfo.CompressionMethod, " not supported by ZipReader."));
			}
			Stream stream = GetRawInnerStream();
			if (itemInfo.CompressedSize != 0)
			{
				stream = new Xceed.Utils.Streams.WindowStream(new TransientStream(stream), itemInfo.CompressedSize);
			}
			checked
			{
				if (itemInfo.Encrypted)
				{
					bool flag = false;
					do
					{
						flag = false;
						if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(m_encryptionPassword))
						{
							flag = true;
						}
						if (!flag)
						{
							switch (itemInfo.EncryptionMethod)
							{
							case EncryptionMethod.Compatible:
							{
								long userSeed = ((!itemInfo.HasDescriptorHeader) ? ((long)(itemInfo.Crc32 >> 16)) : unchecked((int)itemInfo.OriginalEncryptionKey));
								ZCryptStream zCryptStream = null;
								try
								{
									zCryptStream = new ZCryptStream(stream, m_encryptionPassword, userSeed, encrypt: false);
								}
								catch (ZCryptException)
								{
									try
									{
										userSeed = unchecked((int)itemInfo.EncryptionKey);
										stream = GetRawInnerStream();
										if (itemInfo.CompressedSize != 0)
										{
											stream = new Xceed.Utils.Streams.WindowStream(new TransientStream(stream), itemInfo.CompressedSize);
										}
										zCryptStream = new ZCryptStream(stream, m_encryptionPassword, userSeed, encrypt: false);
									}
									catch (ZCryptException)
									{
										flag = true;
									}
								}
								finally
								{
									if (!flag)
									{
										zCryptStream.Transient = true;
										stream = zCryptStream;
										zCryptStream = null;
										m_lastReadBufferOffset += 12;
									}
								}
								break;
							}
							case EncryptionMethod.WinZipAes:
								try
								{
									stream = new TransientStream(stream);
									stream = new AesEncryptionStream(stream, m_encryptionPassword, itemInfo.EncryptionStrength, writing: false);
									m_lastReadBufferOffset += 18;
								}
								catch (InvalidDecryptionPasswordException)
								{
									flag = true;
								}
								break;
							default:
								throw new ZipReaderException("Unknown encryption method: " + itemInfo.EncryptionMethod);
							}
						}
						if (flag)
						{
							string newPassword = null;
							bool abort = true;
							OnInvalidPassword(ref newPassword, ref abort);
							if (abort)
							{
								throw new ZipReaderException("Password exception. ZipReader process aborted.");
							}
							m_encryptionPassword = newPassword;
							stream = GetRawInnerStream();
							if (itemInfo.CompressedSize != 0)
							{
								stream = new Xceed.Utils.Streams.WindowStream(new TransientStream(stream), itemInfo.CompressedSize);
							}
						}
					}
					while (flag);
				}
				m_compressedStream = new CompressedStream(stream, compressionMethod, itemInfo.CompressionLevel);
				m_compressedStream.Transient = true;
				if (itemInfo.Encrypted && itemInfo.EncryptionMethod == EncryptionMethod.WinZipAes)
				{
					m_readerStream = m_compressedStream;
					return;
				}
				m_checksumStream = new ChecksumStream(m_compressedStream, ChecksumType.CRC32);
				m_readerStream = m_checksumStream;
			}
		}

		internal void OnInvalidPassword(ref string newPassword, ref bool abort)
		{
			EventHandler<ZipReaderInvalidPasswordEventArgs> invalidPassword = this.InvalidPassword;
			ZipReaderInvalidPasswordEventArgs e = new ZipReaderInvalidPasswordEventArgs(m_currentZipItemLocalHeader);
			if (invalidPassword != null)
			{
				invalidPassword(this, e);
				newPassword = e.NewPassword;
				abort = e.Abort;
			}
			else
			{
				abort = true;
			}
		}

		internal ZipSignatureType ReadSignature()
		{
			byte[] array = new byte[4];
			Read(array, 0, 4);
			return BitConverter.ToUInt32(array, 0) switch
			{
				33639248u => ZipSignatureType.CentralHeader, 
				134695760u => ZipSignatureType.DataDescriptor, 
				67324752u => ZipSignatureType.LocalHeader, 
				_ => throw new ZipReaderException("No zip header signature found."), 
			};
		}

		internal void DoReadItemLocalHeader(ReaderWriterItemInfo itemInfo)
		{
			checked
			{
				if (itemInfo.LocalHeaderOffset == 0)
				{
					long num = m_innerStreamPosition - m_lastReadBuffer.Length + m_lastReadBufferOffset;
					if (num > 0)
					{
						num -= 4;
						itemInfo.LocalHeaderOffset = num;
					}
				}
			}
			itemInfo.VersionNeeded = (ZipVersionField)ReadUInt16();
			itemInfo.GeneralBitFlags = (ZipGeneralBitFlags)ReadUInt16();
			itemInfo.CompressionMethod = (ZipCompressionMethod)ReadUInt16();
			ushort dosTime = ReadUInt16();
			ushort dosDate = ReadUInt16();
			itemInfo.LastModifiedDateTime = DateTime.MinValue;
			try
			{
				itemInfo.LastModifiedDateTime = Dates.FromDosDateTime(dosDate, dosTime);
			}
			catch
			{
			}
			itemInfo.Crc32 = ReadUInt32();
			itemInfo.CompressedSize = ReadUInt32();
			itemInfo.UncompressedSize = ReadUInt32();
			ushort num2 = ReadUInt16();
			ushort num3 = ReadUInt16();
			checked
			{
				itemInfo.LocalHeaderSize += 30 + num2 + num3;
				itemInfo.FileName = string.Empty;
				if (num2 > 0)
				{
					byte[] array = ReadBytes(num2);
					if (array.Length != num2)
					{
						throw new ZipReaderException("Error while reading file name from local header. Read bytes: " + array.Length + ", expected file name length: " + num2);
					}
					itemInfo.FileName = Xceed.Utils.Strings.Encoding.OEMEncoding.GetString(array, 0, array.Length).Replace('/', Path.DirectorySeparatorChar);
				}
				if (num3 > 0)
				{
					byte[] array2 = ReadBytes(num3);
					if (array2.Length != num3)
					{
						throw new ZipReaderException("Error while reading extra fields from local header. Read bytes: " + array2.Length + ", expected extra field length: " + num3);
					}
					ReadAesExtraHeaders(itemInfo, array2);
				}
			}
		}

		internal void ReadItemDataDescriptorHeader(ReaderWriterItemInfo itemInfo)
		{
			uint crc = ReadUInt32();
			checked
			{
				long compressedSize;
				long uncompressedSize;
				if (itemInfo.CompressedSize >= uint.MaxValue || itemInfo.UncompressedSize >= uint.MaxValue || m_uncompressedSize >= uint.MaxValue)
				{
					compressedSize = (long)ReadUInt64();
					uncompressedSize = (long)ReadUInt64();
				}
				else
				{
					compressedSize = ReadUInt32();
					uncompressedSize = ReadUInt32();
				}
				itemInfo.Crc32 = crc;
				itemInfo.CompressedSize = compressedSize;
				itemInfo.UncompressedSize = uncompressedSize;
			}
		}

		internal void ReadAesExtraHeaders(ReaderWriterItemInfo itemInfo, byte[] extraFieldsData)
		{
			using MemoryStream memoryStream = new MemoryStream(extraFieldsData);
			StreamSearcher streamSearcher = new StreamSearcher(memoryStream);
			if (streamSearcher.Find((ushort)39169))
			{
				short num4;
				int encryptionStrength;
				checked
				{
					uint num = BitConverter.ToUInt16(extraFieldsData, (int)memoryStream.Position);
					if (num != 7)
					{
						throw new ZipReaderException("The AES extra header's reported size is too small.");
					}
					short num2 = BitConverter.ToInt16(extraFieldsData, (int)memoryStream.Position + 2);
					short num3 = BitConverter.ToInt16(extraFieldsData, (int)memoryStream.Position + 4);
					byte b = extraFieldsData[(int)memoryStream.Position + 6];
					num4 = BitConverter.ToInt16(extraFieldsData, (int)memoryStream.Position + 7);
					if (num2 != 1 && num2 != 2)
					{
						throw new ZipReaderException("Unknown AES extra header version.", null);
					}
					if (num3 != 17729)
					{
						throw new ZipReaderException("Unknown AES extra header vendor ID.", null);
					}
					if (b < 1 || b > 3)
					{
						throw new ZipReaderException("The AES extra header contains an invalid strength value.");
					}
					encryptionStrength = b * 64 + 64;
				}
				itemInfo.CompressionMethod = (ZipCompressionMethod)checked((ushort)num4);
				itemInfo.EncryptionMethod = EncryptionMethod.WinZipAes;
				itemInfo.EncryptionStrength = encryptionStrength;
			}
		}

		internal void ReadZip64ExtraHeaders(ReaderWriterItemInfo itemInfo, byte[] extraFieldsData)
		{
			checked
			{
				using MemoryStream memoryStream = new MemoryStream(extraFieldsData);
				StreamSearcher streamSearcher = new StreamSearcher(memoryStream);
				if (!streamSearcher.Find((short)1))
				{
					return;
				}
				int num = 0;
				uint num2 = BitConverter.ToUInt16(extraFieldsData, (int)memoryStream.Position);
				bool flag = false;
				bool flag2 = true;
				bool flag3 = true;
				if (flag2)
				{
					if (num2 < 8)
					{
						throw new ZipReaderException("The Zip64 extra header's reported size is too small.");
					}
					if (!itemInfo.HasDescriptorHeader)
					{
						itemInfo.UncompressedSize = (long)BitConverter.ToUInt64(extraFieldsData, num);
					}
					num2 -= 8;
					num += 8;
				}
				if (flag3)
				{
					if (num2 < 8)
					{
						throw new ZipReaderException("The Zip64 extra header's reported size is too small.");
					}
					if (!itemInfo.HasDescriptorHeader)
					{
						itemInfo.CompressedSize = (long)BitConverter.ToUInt64(extraFieldsData, num);
					}
					num2 -= 8;
					num += 8;
				}
				if (!flag)
				{
					return;
				}
				if (itemInfo.LocalHeaderOffset >= uint.MaxValue)
				{
					if (num2 < 8)
					{
						throw new ZipReaderException("The Zip64 extra header's reported size is too small.");
					}
					itemInfo.LocalHeaderOffset = (long)BitConverter.ToUInt64(extraFieldsData, num);
					num2 -= 8;
					num += 8;
				}
				if (itemInfo.LocalHeaderDisk >= 65535)
				{
					if (num2 < 4)
					{
						throw new ZipReaderException("The Zip64 extra header's reported size is too small.");
					}
					itemInfo.LocalHeaderDisk = BitConverter.ToUInt32(extraFieldsData, num);
					num2 -= 4;
					num += 4;
				}
			}
		}

		internal void OnByteProgression()
		{
			if (this.ByteProgression != null)
			{
				ZipReaderByteProgressionEventArgs e = null;
				e = ((m_currentItemInfo.UncompressedSize == 0) ? new ZipReaderByteProgressionEventArgs(m_currentZipItemLocalHeader, m_uncompressedSize) : new ZipReaderByteProgressionEventArgs(m_currentZipItemLocalHeader, m_uncompressedSize, m_currentItemInfo.UncompressedSize));
				this.ByteProgression(this, e);
				e = null;
			}
		}

		internal void ZipReader_ReadingFromStream(object sender, DataNotifyStreamArgs args)
		{
			if (args.Count == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			checked
			{
				m_innerStreamPosition += args.Count;
				int num = args.Offset;
				if (m_currentItemInfo != null && m_currentItemInfo.Encrypted && m_currentItemInfo.EncryptionMethod == EncryptionMethod.WinZipAes && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(m_encryptionPassword))
				{
					num -= 10;
				}
				if (m_lastReadBuffer != null && args.Count < m_lastReadBuffer.Length && num != 0)
				{
					if (m_lastReadBufferOffset >= args.Count)
					{
						Buffer.BlockCopy(m_lastReadBuffer, args.Count, m_lastReadBuffer, 0, m_lastReadBuffer.Length - args.Count);
						Buffer.BlockCopy(args.Buffer, args.Offset, m_lastReadBuffer, m_lastReadBuffer.Length - args.Count, args.Count);
						m_lastReadBufferOffset -= args.Count;
					}
					else
					{
						byte[] array = new byte[m_lastReadBuffer.Length + args.Count - m_lastReadBufferOffset];
						Buffer.BlockCopy(m_lastReadBuffer, m_lastReadBufferOffset, array, 0, m_lastReadBuffer.Length - m_lastReadBufferOffset);
						Buffer.BlockCopy(args.Buffer, args.Offset, array, m_lastReadBuffer.Length - m_lastReadBufferOffset, args.Count);
						m_lastReadBuffer = array;
						m_lastReadBufferOffset = 0;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					if (m_lastReadBuffer == null)
					{
						m_lastReadBuffer = new byte[args.Count];
					}
					else if (m_lastReadBuffer.Length != args.Count)
					{
						m_lastReadBuffer = new byte[args.Count];
					}
					Buffer.BlockCopy(args.Buffer, args.Offset, m_lastReadBuffer, 0, args.Count);
					m_lastReadBufferOffset = 0;
				}
			}
		}

		internal int Read(byte[] buffer, int offset, int count)
		{
			int num = 0;
			checked
			{
				if (m_lastReadBuffer != null && m_lastReadBuffer.Length > m_lastReadBufferOffset)
				{
					int num2 = m_lastReadBuffer.Length - m_lastReadBufferOffset;
					if (num2 >= count)
					{
						Buffer.BlockCopy(m_lastReadBuffer, m_lastReadBufferOffset, buffer, 0, count);
						num = count;
						m_lastReadBufferOffset += count;
					}
					else
					{
						Buffer.BlockCopy(m_lastReadBuffer, m_lastReadBufferOffset, buffer, 0, num2);
						num += num2;
						m_lastReadBufferOffset += num2;
						int num3 = count - num2;
						num += m_innerStream.Read(buffer, num2, num3);
						m_lastReadBufferOffset += num3;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					num = m_innerStream.Read(buffer, offset, count);
					m_lastReadBufferOffset += num;
				}
				return num;
			}
		}

		internal void CentralHeaderReached()
		{
			try
			{
				if (m_compressedStream != null)
				{
					m_compressedStream.Close();
					m_compressedStream = null;
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
			try
			{
				if (m_checksumStream != null)
				{
					m_checksumStream.Close();
					m_checksumStream = null;
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
			try
			{
				if (m_readerStream != null)
				{
					m_readerStream.Close();
					m_readerStream = null;
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
			try
			{
				if (m_innerStream != null)
				{
					m_innerStream.Close();
					m_innerStream = null;
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
		}

		internal int FindSignatureInBuffer(uint searchedSignature, byte[] readBuffer, int readBufferOffset)
		{
			int result = -1;
			checked
			{
				using (MemoryStream memoryStream = new MemoryStream(readBuffer))
				{
					memoryStream.Seek(readBufferOffset, SeekOrigin.Begin);
					StreamSearcher streamSearcher = new StreamSearcher(memoryStream);
					if (streamSearcher.Find(searchedSignature))
					{
						result = (int)memoryStream.Position;
					}
					else if (searchedSignature == 67324752)
					{
						searchedSignature = 33639248u;
						memoryStream.Seek(0L, SeekOrigin.Begin);
						if (streamSearcher.Find(searchedSignature))
						{
							result = (int)memoryStream.Position;
						}
					}
				}
				return result;
			}
		}

		internal int ReadNextHeaderInLastReadBuffer(ReaderWriterItemInfo itemInfo)
		{
			bool flag = false;
			int num = 0;
			uint num2 = 0u;
			num2 = ((!itemInfo.HasDescriptorHeader) ? 67324752u : 134695760u);
			if (itemInfo.CompressedSize == 0)
			{
				num = FindSignatureInBuffer(num2, m_lastReadBuffer, m_lastReadBufferOffset);
				if (num > -1)
				{
					flag = true;
				}
			}
			checked
			{
				if (!flag)
				{
					byte[] array = new byte[8];
					Buffer.BlockCopy(m_lastReadBuffer, m_lastReadBuffer.Length - 4, array, 0, 4);
					m_innerStream.Read(m_readerBuffer, 0, 4);
					Buffer.BlockCopy(m_lastReadBuffer, 0, array, 4, 4);
					num = FindSignatureInBuffer(num2, array, 0);
					if (num > -1)
					{
						num -= 4;
						flag = true;
					}
					if (!flag)
					{
						throw new ZipReaderException("Unable to find Zip header: " + num2.ToString("X") + " (after item " + m_currentItemInfo.FileName + ")");
					}
				}
				switch (num2)
				{
				case 33639248u:
					m_zipReaderState = ZipReaderState.READ_CENTRAL_HEADER;
					break;
				case 134695760u:
					m_zipReaderState = ZipReaderState.READ_DATA_DESCRIPTOR;
					break;
				case 67324752u:
					m_zipReaderState = ZipReaderState.READ_LOCAL_HEADER;
					break;
				default:
					throw new ZipReaderException("Unexpected ZipReader state.");
				}
				return num;
			}
		}

		internal Stream GetRawInnerStream()
		{
			Stream stream = null;
			if (m_lastReadBuffer.Length > m_lastReadBufferOffset)
			{
				byte[] array = new byte[checked(m_lastReadBuffer.Length - m_lastReadBufferOffset)];
				Buffer.BlockCopy(m_lastReadBuffer, m_lastReadBufferOffset, array, 0, array.Length);
				return new Xceed.Utils.Streams.CombinedStream(array, m_innerStream);
			}
			return m_innerStream;
		}

		internal ushort ReadUInt16()
		{
			Read(m_readerBuffer, 0, 2);
			return (ushort)(m_readerBuffer[0] | (m_readerBuffer[1] << 8));
		}

		internal uint ReadUInt32()
		{
			Read(m_readerBuffer, 0, 4);
			return (uint)(m_readerBuffer[0] | (m_readerBuffer[1] << 8) | (m_readerBuffer[2] << 16) | (m_readerBuffer[3] << 24));
		}

		internal ulong ReadUInt64()
		{
			Read(m_readerBuffer, 0, 8);
			uint num = (uint)(m_readerBuffer[0] | (m_readerBuffer[1] << 8) | (m_readerBuffer[2] << 16) | (m_readerBuffer[3] << 24));
			uint num2 = (uint)(m_readerBuffer[4] | (m_readerBuffer[5] << 8) | (m_readerBuffer[6] << 16) | (m_readerBuffer[7] << 24));
			return ((ulong)num2 << 32) | num;
		}

		internal byte[] ReadBytes(int byteCount)
		{
			byte[] array = new byte[byteCount];
			Read(array, 0, byteCount);
			return array;
		}
	}
	public class ZipReaderByteProgressionEventArgs : EventArgs
	{
		private long m_bytesProcessed;

		private long m_uncompressedSize;

		private ZipItemLocalHeader m_zipItemLocalHeader;

		public long BytesProcessed => m_bytesProcessed;

		public long UncompressedSize => m_uncompressedSize;

		public short Percent
		{
			get
			{
				if (m_uncompressedSize == -1)
				{
					return -1;
				}
				checked
				{
					return (byte)unchecked(checked(m_bytesProcessed * 100) / m_uncompressedSize);
				}
			}
		}

		public ZipItemLocalHeader ZipItemLocalHeader
		{
			get
			{
				if (m_zipItemLocalHeader == null)
				{
					throw new NullReferenceException("ZipItemLocalHeader is null.");
				}
				return m_zipItemLocalHeader;
			}
		}

		public ZipReaderByteProgressionEventArgs(ZipItemLocalHeader zipItemLocalHeader, long bytesProcessed)
			: this(zipItemLocalHeader, bytesProcessed, -1L)
		{
		}

		public ZipReaderByteProgressionEventArgs(ZipItemLocalHeader zipItemLocalHeader, long bytesProcessed, long totalBytesCount)
		{
			m_bytesProcessed = bytesProcessed;
			m_uncompressedSize = totalBytesCount;
			m_zipItemLocalHeader = zipItemLocalHeader;
		}
	}
	public class ZipReaderException : Exception
	{
		private Exception m_innerException;

		private string m_message;

		public ZipReaderException(string message)
			: this(message, null)
		{
		}

		public ZipReaderException(string message, Exception innerException)
			: base(message, innerException)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(message))
			{
				m_message = string.Empty;
			}
			else
			{
				m_message = message;
			}
			m_innerException = innerException;
		}
	}
	public class ZipReaderInvalidPasswordEventArgs : EventArgs
	{
		private ZipItemLocalHeader m_zipItemLocalHeader;

		private string m_newPassword;

		private bool m_abort;

		public ZipItemLocalHeader ZipItemLocalHeader
		{
			get
			{
				if (m_zipItemLocalHeader == null)
				{
					throw new NullReferenceException("ZipItemLocalHeader is null.");
				}
				return m_zipItemLocalHeader;
			}
		}

		public bool Abort
		{
			get
			{
				return m_abort;
			}
			set
			{
				m_abort = value;
			}
		}

		public string NewPassword
		{
			get
			{
				return m_newPassword;
			}
			set
			{
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(value))
				{
					throw new ArgumentException("New password can't be null or empty.");
				}
				m_newPassword = value;
			}
		}

		public string OldPassword => m_zipItemLocalHeader.EncryptionPassword;

		public ZipReaderInvalidPasswordEventArgs(ZipItemLocalHeader zipItemLocalHeader)
		{
			m_zipItemLocalHeader = zipItemLocalHeader;
			m_newPassword = string.Empty;
		}
	}
	internal enum ZipReaderState
	{
		READ_CENTRAL_HEADER,
		READ_DATA_DESCRIPTOR,
		READ_LOCAL_HEADER,
		INTIALIZE_ZIP_READER,
		READING_DATA
	}
	internal enum ZipSignatureType
	{
		LocalHeader,
		DataDescriptor,
		CentralHeader
	}
	internal class ReaderWriterConstants
	{
		public const uint CentralSignature = 33639248u;

		public const uint LocalSignature = 67324752u;

		public const uint DescriptorSignature = 134695760u;

		public const uint Zip64EndHeaderSignature = 101075792u;

		public const uint Zip64EndHeaderLocatorSignature = 117853008u;

		public const uint EndHeaderSignature = 101010256u;

		public const ushort AesExtraHeaderSignature = 39169;

		public const short AesVendorVersion = 2;

		public const short AesVendorID = 17729;

		public const short Zip64ExtraHeaderSignature = 1;
	}

		public bool Encrypted
		{
			get
			{
				checked
				{
					return (ushort)unchecked((int)(m_generalBitFlags & ZipGeneralBitFlags.Encrypted)) != 0;
				}
			}
			set
			{
				if (value)
				{
					m_generalBitFlags = (ZipGeneralBitFlags)checked((ushort)unchecked((int)(m_generalBitFlags | ZipGeneralBitFlags.Encrypted)));
				}
				else
				{
					m_generalBitFlags = (ZipGeneralBitFlags)checked((ushort)unchecked((int)(m_generalBitFlags & ~ZipGeneralBitFlags.Encrypted)));
				}
			}
		}

		public bool HasDescriptorHeader
		{
			get
			{
				checked
				{
					return (ushort)unchecked((int)(m_generalBitFlags & ZipGeneralBitFlags.DescriptorHeader)) != 0;
				}
			}
			set
			{
				if (value)
				{
					m_generalBitFlags = (ZipGeneralBitFlags)checked((ushort)unchecked((int)(m_generalBitFlags | ZipGeneralBitFlags.DescriptorHeader)));
				}
				else
				{
					m_generalBitFlags = (ZipGeneralBitFlags)checked((ushort)unchecked((int)(m_generalBitFlags & ~ZipGeneralBitFlags.DescriptorHeader)));
				}
			}
		}

		public CompressionLevel CompressionLevel
		{
			get
			{
				if (m_compressionMethod == ZipCompressionMethod.Stored)
				{
					return CompressionLevel.None;
				}
				return (ZipGeneralBitFlags)checked((ushort)unchecked((int)(m_generalBitFlags & ZipGeneralBitFlags.LowestCompLevel))) switch
				{
					ZipGeneralBitFlags.LowestCompLevel => CompressionLevel.Lowest, 
					ZipGeneralBitFlags.LowCompLevel => CompressionLevel.Lowest, 
					ZipGeneralBitFlags.MaxCompLevel => CompressionLevel.Highest, 
					_ => CompressionLevel.Normal, 
				};
			}
			set
			{
				m_generalBitFlags = (ZipGeneralBitFlags)checked((ushort)unchecked((int)(m_generalBitFlags & ~ZipGeneralBitFlags.LowestCompLevel)));
				switch (value)
				{
				case CompressionLevel.Highest:
					m_generalBitFlags = (ZipGeneralBitFlags)checked((ushort)unchecked((int)(m_generalBitFlags | ZipGeneralBitFlags.MaxCompLevel)));
					return;
				case CompressionLevel.Normal:
				case (CompressionLevel)7:
				case (CompressionLevel)8:
					m_generalBitFlags = (ZipGeneralBitFlags)checked((ushort)unchecked((int)m_generalBitFlags));
					return;
				}
				if (value < CompressionLevel.Normal && value >= CompressionLevel.Lowest)
				{
					m_generalBitFlags = (ZipGeneralBitFlags)checked((ushort)unchecked((int)(m_generalBitFlags | ZipGeneralBitFlags.LowCompLevel)));
				}
				else if (value > CompressionLevel.None)
				{
					m_generalBitFlags = (ZipGeneralBitFlags)checked((ushort)unchecked((int)(m_generalBitFlags | ZipGeneralBitFlags.LowestCompLevel)));
				}
				else
				{
					m_compressionMethod = ZipCompressionMethod.Stored;
				}
			}
		}

		public bool IsTextFile
		{
			get
			{
				checked
				{
					return (ushort)unchecked((int)(m_internalFileAttribs & ZipInternalFlags.TextFile)) != 0;
				}
			}
			set
			{
				if (value)
				{
					m_internalFileAttribs = (ZipInternalFlags)checked((ushort)unchecked((int)(m_internalFileAttribs | ZipInternalFlags.TextFile)));
				}
				else
				{
					m_internalFileAttribs = (ZipInternalFlags)checked((ushort)unchecked((int)(m_internalFileAttribs & ~ZipInternalFlags.TextFile)));
				}
			}
		}

		public bool IsFolder
		{
			get
			{
				return (m_externalFileAttribs & ZipDOSAttributes.Directory) != 0;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value)
				{
					m_externalFileAttribs |= ZipDOSAttributes.Directory;
				}
				else
				{
					m_externalFileAttribs &= ~ZipDOSAttributes.Directory;
				}
			}
		}

		public FileAttributes FileAttributes
		{
			get
			{
				FileAttributes fileAttributes = (FileAttributes)0;
				if ((m_externalFileAttribs & ZipDOSAttributes.ReadOnly) != ZipDOSAttributes.Normal)
				{
					fileAttributes |= FileAttributes.ReadOnly;
				}
				if ((m_externalFileAttribs & ZipDOSAttributes.Hidden) != ZipDOSAttributes.Normal)
				{
					fileAttributes |= FileAttributes.Hidden;
				}
				if ((m_externalFileAttribs & ZipDOSAttributes.System) != ZipDOSAttributes.Normal)
				{
					fileAttributes |= FileAttributes.System;
				}
				if ((m_externalFileAttribs & ZipDOSAttributes.Directory) != ZipDOSAttributes.Normal)
				{
					fileAttributes |= FileAttributes.Directory;
				}
				if ((m_externalFileAttribs & ZipDOSAttributes.Archive) != ZipDOSAttributes.Normal)
				{
					fileAttributes |= FileAttributes.Archive;
				}
				if ((m_externalFileAttribs & ZipDOSAttributes.Compressed) != ZipDOSAttributes.Normal)
				{
					fileAttributes |= FileAttributes.Compressed;
				}
				if (fileAttributes == (FileAttributes)0)
				{
					return FileAttributes.Normal;
				}
				return fileAttributes;
			}
			set
			{
				ZipDOSAttributes zipDOSAttributes = ZipDOSAttributes.Normal;
				if ((value & FileAttributes.ReadOnly) != 0)
				{
					zipDOSAttributes |= ZipDOSAttributes.ReadOnly;
				}
				if ((value & FileAttributes.Hidden) != 0)
				{
					zipDOSAttributes |= ZipDOSAttributes.Hidden;
				}
				if ((value & FileAttributes.System) != 0)
				{
					zipDOSAttributes |= ZipDOSAttributes.System;
				}
				if ((value & FileAttributes.Directory) != 0)
				{
					zipDOSAttributes |= ZipDOSAttributes.Directory;
				}
				if ((value & FileAttributes.Archive) != 0)
				{
					zipDOSAttributes |= ZipDOSAttributes.Archive;
				}
				if ((value & FileAttributes.Compressed) != 0)
				{
					zipDOSAttributes |= ZipDOSAttributes.Compressed;
				}
				m_externalFileAttribs &= (ZipDOSAttributes)4294901760u;
				m_externalFileAttribs |= zipDOSAttributes;
			}
		}

		public ushort EncryptionKey
		{
			get
			{
				ushort dosDate = 0;
				ushort dosTime = 0;
				Dates.ToDosDateTime(m_lastModifiedDateTime, out dosDate, out dosTime);
				return dosTime;
			}
		}

		public ushort OriginalEncryptionKey => m_originalLastModFileTime;

		public EncryptionMethod EncryptionMethod
		{
			get

				return m_encryptionMethod;

			set
			{
				m_encryptionMethod = value;
			}
		}

			}
		}

		{
		}
