	internal class ReaderWriterUtils
	{
		public static string FixFilename(string filename)
		{
			filename = filename.Replace('/', Path.DirectorySeparatorChar);
			if (filename.StartsWith(Path.DirectorySeparatorChar.ToString()))
			{
				return Path.DirectorySeparatorChar + "." + filename;
			}
			return Path.DirectorySeparatorChar + filename;
		}

		public static string UnfixFilename(string filename)
		{
			if (filename.StartsWith(Path.DirectorySeparatorChar.ToString()))
			{
				return filename.Substring(1).Replace(Path.DirectorySeparatorChar, '/');
			}
			return filename.Replace(Path.DirectorySeparatorChar, '/');
		}

		public static ushort SafeUInt16(long value)
		{
			if (value > 65535)
			{
				return ushort.MaxValue;
			}
			return (ushort)value;
		}

		public static ushort SafeUInt16(ulong value)
		{
			if (value > 65535)
			{
				return ushort.MaxValue;
			}
			return (ushort)value;
		}

		public static ushort SafeUInt16(uint value)
		{
			if (value > 65535)
			{
				return ushort.MaxValue;
			}
			return (ushort)value;
		}

		public static uint SafeUInt32(long value)
		{
			if (value > uint.MaxValue)
			{
				return uint.MaxValue;
			}
			return (uint)value;
		}

		public static uint SafeUInt32(ulong value)
		{
			if (value > uint.MaxValue)
			{
				return uint.MaxValue;
			}
			return (uint)value;
		}
	}
}
namespace Xceed.Utils.Licensing
{
	internal abstract class XceedLicense
	{
		internal struct ProductVersion
		{
			public Xceed.Utils.Licensing.LicenseProduct Product;

			public byte Major;

			public byte Minor;

			public bool SupportSampleKey;

			public ProductVersion(Xceed.Utils.Licensing.LicenseProduct product, byte major, byte minor)
			{
				Product = product;
				Major = major;
				Minor = minor;
				SupportSampleKey = true;
			}

			public ProductVersion(Xceed.Utils.Licensing.LicenseProduct product, byte major, byte minor, bool supportSampleKey)
			{
				Product = product;
				Major = major;
				Minor = minor;
				SupportSampleKey = supportSampleKey;
			}
		}

		private const string PackagePathKey = "Software\\Xceed Software";

		private const string LicenseAnalyzerPathKey = "Software\\Xceed Software\\LicenseAnalyzer";

		private const string InstallDirValueKey = "InstallDirectory";

		private const string TrialLastDisplayValueKey = "TrialLastDisplay";

		private const string LastDisplayTrialDateValueKey = "TrialVerification";

		private const string OlderVersionStateValueKey = "OlderVersionState";

		private const string LicensesRegistryLocation = "Licenses\\4507C5F2-9077-40DE-A621-64EC59BF6829\\";

		private string m_licenseKey = "";

		private bool m_trial;

		private Xceed.Utils.Licensing.LicenseProduct m_product;

		private byte m_versionMajor;

		private byte m_versionMinor;

		private byte m_nbLicenses;

		private bool m_siteLicense;

		private static string s_installDirectory = string.Empty;

		private bool m_cannotFindAnalyzer;

		private bool m_isFromRegistry;

		private ArrayList m_performedFeratureAnalysis = new ArrayList();

		public bool IsFromRegistry
		{
			get
			{
				return m_isFromRegistry;
			}
			set
			{
				m_isFromRegistry = value;
			}
		}

		public string LicenseKey => m_licenseKey;

		public bool IsTrial => m_trial;

		public Xceed.Utils.Licensing.LicenseProduct Product => m_product;

		public byte VersionMajor => m_versionMajor;

		public byte VersionMinor => m_versionMinor;

		public byte NbLicenses => m_nbLicenses;

		public bool IsSiteLicense => m_siteLicense;

		protected abstract ProductVersion[] AllowedVersions { get; }

		protected virtual ProductVersion[] LatestVersions => new ProductVersion[1] { AllowedVersions[0] };

		protected abstract Type LicenseeType { get; }

		private static Xceed.Utils.Licensing.TrialState CurrentTrialState
		{
			get
			{
				if (!RunningOnDevelopperMachine)
				{
					return Xceed.Utils.Licensing.TrialState.Welcome;
				}
				Microsoft.Win32.RegistryKey registryKey = null;
				try
				{
					registryKey = Registry.CurrentUser.OpenSubKey("Software\\Xceed Software\\LicenseAnalyzer");
					if (registryKey == null)
					{
						return Xceed.Utils.Licensing.TrialState.Welcome;
					}
					if (registryKey.GetValue("TrialLastDisplay") is string s)
					{
						try
						{
							return (Xceed.Utils.Licensing.TrialState)int.Parse(s);
						}
						catch (Exception)
						{
							return Xceed.Utils.Licensing.TrialState.Welcome;
						}
					}
				}
				finally
				{
					registryKey?.Close();
				}
				return Xceed.Utils.Licensing.TrialState.Welcome;
			}
			set
			{
				if (!RunningOnDevelopperMachine)
				{
					return;
				}
				Microsoft.Win32.RegistryKey registryKey = null;
				try
				{
					registryKey = Registry.CurrentUser.CreateSubKey("Software\\Xceed Software\\LicenseAnalyzer");
					if (registryKey != null)
					{
						Microsoft.Win32.RegistryKey registryKey2 = registryKey;
						int num = (int)value;
						registryKey2.SetValue("TrialLastDisplay", num.ToString());
					}
				}
				catch
				{
				}
				finally
				{
					registryKey?.Close();
				}
			}
		}

		private static bool RunningOnDevelopperMachine
		{
			get
			{
				try
				{
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(s_installDirectory))
					{
						return true;
					}
					Microsoft.Win32.RegistryKey registryKey = Registry.LocalMachine.OpenSubKey("Software\\Xceed Software");
					if (registryKey == null)
					{
						registryKey = Registry.CurrentUser.OpenSubKey("Software\\Xceed Software");
						if (registryKey == null)
						{
							return false;
						}
					}
					s_installDirectory = registryKey.GetValue("InstallDirectory") as string;
					if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(s_installDirectory))
					{
						return false;
					}
				}
				catch
				{
					return false;
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

		public XceedLicense()
		{
		}

		public void Initialize(string licenseKey, Type licenserType)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(licenseKey))
			{
				licenseKey = GetLicenseKeyFromRegistry();
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(licenseKey))
				{
					ThrowNoKeyInCodeAndRegistry();
				}
			}
			Xceed.Utils.Licensing.XceedLicenseManager lm = new Xceed.Utils.Licensing.XceedLicenseManager();
			ValidateLicenseKey(ref lm, ref licenseKey, performNagIfNecessary: true);
			m_licenseKey = licenseKey;
			m_nbLicenses = lm.NbLicenses;
			m_siteLicense = lm.IsSiteLicense;
			m_trial = lm.Originator >= Xceed.Utils.Licensing.LicenseOrigin.TrialVersion && lm.Originator <= Xceed.Utils.Licensing.LicenseOrigin.TrialRenew9;
			m_product = lm.Product;
			m_versionMajor = lm.VerMajor;
			m_versionMinor = lm.VerMinor;
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(m_licenseKey) || m_trial || !m_isFromRegistry)
			{
				return;
			}
			Assembly entryAssembly = Assembly.GetEntryAssembly();
			object[] customAttributes = entryAssembly.GetCustomAttributes(typeof(AssemblyConfigurationAttribute), inherit: false);
			if (customAttributes != null && customAttributes.Length > 0)
			{
				AssemblyConfigurationAttribute assemblyConfigurationAttribute = (AssemblyConfigurationAttribute)customAttributes[0];
				if (assemblyConfigurationAttribute.Configuration == "XCEED-SAMPLE-APPLICATION")
				{
					return;
				}
			}
			ThrowNoKeyInCodeAndRegistry();
		}

		protected virtual void ThrowNoKeyInCodeAndRegistry()
		{
			Xceed.Utils.Exceptions.ThrowException.ThrowLicenseException(LicenseeType, null, LicenseeType.FullName + ".LicenseKey must be set to a valid trial or registered license key in the code of your application before using this product. Please refer to the Licensing topic in the documentation for specific instructions on how to avoid this exception.");
		}

		protected virtual void ThrowKeyInvalid()
		{
			Xceed.Utils.Exceptions.ThrowException.ThrowLicenseException(LicenseeType, null, "The license key used to license this Xceed product is invalid. " + LicenseeType.FullName + ".LicenseKey must be set to a valid trial or registered license key before using this product. Please refer to the Licensing topic in the documentation for specific instructions on how to avoid this exception.");
		}

		protected virtual void ThrowKeyInvalidProductVersion()
		{
			Xceed.Utils.Exceptions.ThrowException.ThrowLicenseException(LicenseeType, null, "The license key used to license this Xceed product does not match the current product. Please make sure that you are correctly licensing the product as described in the documentation and then recompile in order to avoid this exception.");
		}

		protected virtual void ThrowTrialKeyExpired()
		{
			Xceed.Utils.Exceptions.ThrowException.ThrowLicenseException(LicenseeType, null, "The trial license key used to license this Xceed product has expired. Please contact Xceed if you need additional time to evaluate the library, or if you wish to purchase a registered license.");
		}

		protected virtual ProductVersion LatestVersion(Xceed.Utils.Licensing.LicenseProduct product)
		{
			if (product == Xceed.Utils.Licensing.LicenseProduct.InvalidProduct)
			{
				return AllowedVersions[0];
			}
			ProductVersion[] allowedVersions = AllowedVersions;
			for (int i = 0; i < allowedVersions.Length; i++)
			{
				ProductVersion result = allowedVersions[i];
				if (result.Product == product)
				{
					return result;
				}
			}
			return AllowedVersions[0];
		}

		private string GetLicenseKeyFromRegistry()
		{
			string licenseKey = string.Empty;
			try
			{
				Xceed.Utils.Licensing.XceedLicenseManager lm = new Xceed.Utils.Licensing.XceedLicenseManager();
				Microsoft.Win32.RegistryKey registryKey = null;
				ProductVersion[] latestVersions = LatestVersions;
				for (int i = 0; i < latestVersions.Length; i++)
				{
					ProductVersion productVersion = latestVersions[i];
					if (!productVersion.SupportSampleKey)
					{
						continue;
					}
					registryKey = Registry.ClassesRoot.OpenSubKey("Licenses\\4507C5F2-9077-40DE-A621-64EC59BF6829\\" + Xceed.Utils.Licensing.XceedLicenseManager.GetProductCodeName(productVersion.Product));
					if (registryKey == null)
					{
						continue;
					}
					Microsoft.Win32.RegistryKey registryKey2 = registryKey;
					byte major = productVersion.Major;
					string text = major.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
					byte minor = productVersion.Minor;
					licenseKey = (string)registryKey2.GetValue(text + "." + minor.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture));
					registryKey.Close();
					if (licenseKey != null)
					{
						try
						{
							ValidateLicenseKey(ref lm, ref licenseKey, performNagIfNecessary: false);
						}
						catch (LicenseException)
						{
							continue;
						}
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			catch (SecurityException)
			{
			}
			if (licenseKey == null)
			{
				licenseKey = string.Empty;
			}
			m_isFromRegistry = true;
			return licenseKey;
		}

		private void ValidateLicenseKey(ref Xceed.Utils.Licensing.XceedLicenseManager lm, ref string licenseKey, bool performNagIfNecessary)
		{
			try
			{
				lm.AnalyzeLicense(licenseKey);
			}
			catch (Xceed.Utils.Licensing.XceedLicenseManagerException)
			{
				ThrowKeyInvalid();
			}
			bool flag = false;
			ProductVersion[] allowedVersions = AllowedVersions;
			for (int i = 0; i < allowedVersions.Length; i++)
			{
				ProductVersion productVersion = allowedVersions[i];
				if (productVersion.Product == lm.Product && productVersion.Major == lm.VerMajor && productVersion.Minor == lm.VerMinor)
				{
					flag = true;
					ProductVersion productVersion2 = LatestVersion(lm.Product);
					if ((productVersion.Major != productVersion2.Major || productVersion.Minor != productVersion2.Minor) && RunningOnDevelopperMachine && performNagIfNecessary)
					{
						DisplayRegisteredOlderVersion(productVersion.Major, productVersion.Minor, productVersion.Product);
					}
					break;
				}
			}
			if (!flag)
			{
				ThrowKeyInvalidProductVersion();
			}
			if (lm.Originator < Xceed.Utils.Licensing.LicenseOrigin.TrialVersion || lm.Originator > Xceed.Utils.Licensing.LicenseOrigin.TrialRenew9)
			{
				return;
			}
			checked
			{
				int days = new TimeSpan(lm.Date.Ticks - DateTime.Today.Ticks).Days;
				if (days <= 0)
				{
					if (RunningOnDevelopperMachine && performNagIfNecessary)
					{
						DisplayTrialExpired(-1 * default(TimeSpan).Days, lm.Product);
					}
					ThrowTrialKeyExpired();
				}
				else if (RunningOnDevelopperMachine && performNagIfNecessary)
				{
					DisplayTrial(days, lm.Product);
				}
			}
		}

		private bool DisplayWelcome(int daysLeft, Xceed.Utils.Licensing.LicenseProduct product)
		{
			if (!PreprocessDisplay(Xceed.Utils.Licensing.LicenseInformationType.Trial))
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (LastDisplayWasToday())
			{
				return false;
			}
			CurrentTrialState = Xceed.Utils.Licensing.TrialState.FirstTrial;
			string arguments = "?productID=" + Xceed.Utils.Licensing.XceedLicenseManager.GetProductCodeName(product) + "&assemblyVersion=3.6.7614.12380&assemblyName=" + Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly().FullName + "&daysLeft=" + daysLeft;
			string licenseNotificationPath = s_installDirectory + "\\LicenseAnalyzer\\WelcomeInformation.hta";
			return DisplayInformation(licenseNotificationPath, arguments, product);
		}

		private bool DisplayFirstTrial(int daysLeft, Xceed.Utils.Licensing.LicenseProduct product)
		{
			if (!PreprocessDisplay(Xceed.Utils.Licensing.LicenseInformationType.Trial))
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (LastDisplayWasToday())
			{
				return false;
			}
			CurrentTrialState = Xceed.Utils.Licensing.TrialState.Trial;
			string arguments = "?productID=" + Xceed.Utils.Licensing.XceedLicenseManager.GetProductCodeName(product) + "&assemblyVersion=3.6.7614.12380&assemblyName=" + Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly().FullName + "&daysLeft=" + daysLeft;
			string licenseNotificationPath = s_installDirectory + "\\LicenseAnalyzer\\FirstTrialInformation.hta";
			return DisplayInformation(licenseNotificationPath, arguments, product);
		}

		private bool DisplayTrial(int daysLeft, Xceed.Utils.Licensing.LicenseProduct product)
		{
			if (CurrentTrialState == Xceed.Utils.Licensing.TrialState.Welcome)
			{
				return DisplayWelcome(daysLeft, product);
			}
			if (CurrentTrialState == Xceed.Utils.Licensing.TrialState.FirstTrial)
			{
				return DisplayFirstTrial(daysLeft, product);
			}
			if (!PreprocessDisplay(Xceed.Utils.Licensing.LicenseInformationType.Trial))
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (LastDisplayWasToday())
			{
				return false;
			}
			string arguments = "?productID=" + Xceed.Utils.Licensing.XceedLicenseManager.GetProductCodeName(product) + "&assemblyVersion=3.6.7614.12380&assemblyName=" + Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly().FullName + "&daysLeft=" + daysLeft;
			string licenseNotificationPath = s_installDirectory + "\\LicenseAnalyzer\\TrialInformation.hta";
			return DisplayInformation(licenseNotificationPath, arguments, product);
		}

		private bool DisplayTrialExpired(int daysLeft, Xceed.Utils.Licensing.LicenseProduct product)
		{
			if (!PreprocessDisplay(Xceed.Utils.Licensing.LicenseInformationType.TrialExpired))
			{
				return false;
			}
			string arguments = "?productID=" + Xceed.Utils.Licensing.XceedLicenseManager.GetProductCodeName(product) + "&assemblyVersion=3.6.7614.12380&assemblyName=" + Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly().FullName + "&daysLeft=" + daysLeft;
			string licenseNotificationPath = s_installDirectory + "\\LicenseAnalyzer\\TrialExpiredInformation.hta";
			return DisplayInformation(licenseNotificationPath, arguments, product);
		}

		private bool DisplayRegisteredOlderVersion(byte keyMajorVersion, byte keyMinorVersion, Xceed.Utils.Licensing.LicenseProduct product)
		{
			if (!PreprocessDisplay(Xceed.Utils.Licensing.LicenseInformationType.RegisteredOlderVersion))
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (OlderVersionWasPerformedBefore(product))
			{
				return false;
			}
			ProductVersion productVersion = LatestVersion(product);
			string arguments = "?productID=" + Xceed.Utils.Licensing.XceedLicenseManager.GetProductCodeName(product) + "&assemblyVersion=3.6.7614.12380&assemblyName=" + Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly().FullName + "&usingKeyVersion=" + keyMajorVersion + "." + keyMinorVersion + "&latestKeyVersion=" + productVersion.Major + "." + productVersion.Minor;
			string licenseNotificationPath = s_installDirectory + "\\LicenseAnalyzer\\UpgradeInformation.hta";
			return DisplayInformation(licenseNotificationPath, arguments, product);
		}

		private bool PreprocessDisplay(Xceed.Utils.Licensing.LicenseInformationType informationType)
		{
			if (!IsDisplayInformationAllowed(informationType))
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (!System.Environment.UserInteractive)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (m_cannotFindAnalyzer)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (!RunningOnDevelopperMachine)
			{
				m_cannotFindAnalyzer = true;
				return false;
			}
			if (informationType == Xceed.Utils.Licensing.LicenseInformationType.FeatureRequireUpgrade)
			{
				if (m_performedFeratureAnalysis.Contains(informationType))
				{
					return false;
				}
				m_performedFeratureAnalysis.Add(informationType);
			}
			return true;
		}

		private bool DisplayInformation(string licenseNotificationPath, string arguments, Xceed.Utils.Licensing.LicenseProduct product)
		{
			Process process = null;
			ProcessStartInfo processStartInfo = null;
			try
			{
				processStartInfo = new ProcessStartInfo(licenseNotificationPath, arguments);
				processStartInfo.ErrorDialog = false;
				processStartInfo.UseShellExecute = true;
				process = new Process();
				process.StartInfo = processStartInfo;
				if (!File.Exists(licenseNotificationPath))
				{
					m_cannotFindAnalyzer = true;
					return false;
				}
				if (!process.Start())
				{
					m_cannotFindAnalyzer = true;
					return false;
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				m_cannotFindAnalyzer = true;
				return false;
			}
			finally
			{
				process?.Dispose();
			}
			return true;
		}

		private static bool LastDisplayWasToday()
		{
			Microsoft.Win32.RegistryKey registryKey = null;
			try
			{
				registryKey = Registry.CurrentUser.CreateSubKey("Software\\Xceed Software\\LicenseAnalyzer");
				if (registryKey == null)
				{
					return false;
				}
				if (registryKey.GetValue("TrialVerification") is string s)
				{
					try
					{
						if (long.Parse(s) == DateTime.Today.Date.Ticks)
						{
							return true;
						}
					}
					catch
					{
						return true;
					}
				}
				registryKey.SetValue("TrialVerification", DateTime.Today.Date.Ticks);
				return false;
			}
			catch
			{
				return true;
			}
			finally
			{
				registryKey?.Close();
			}
		}

		protected virtual bool IsDisplayInformationAllowed(Xceed.Utils.Licensing.LicenseInformationType informationType)
		{
			return true;
		}

		private static bool OlderVersionWasPerformedBefore(Xceed.Utils.Licensing.LicenseProduct product)
		{
			Microsoft.Win32.RegistryKey registryKey = null;
			try
			{
				registryKey = Registry.CurrentUser.CreateSubKey("Software\\Xceed Software\\LicenseAnalyzer\\" + Xceed.Utils.Licensing.XceedLicenseManager.GetProductCodeName(product));
				if (registryKey == null)
				{
					return true;
				}
				if (registryKey.GetValue("OlderVersionState") is string value)
				{
					try
					{
						if (bool.Parse(value))
						{
							return true;
						}
					}
					catch
					{
						return true;
					}
				}
				registryKey.SetValue("OlderVersionState", true);
				return false;
			}
			catch
			{
				return true;
			}
			finally
			{
				registryKey?.Close();
			}
		}

		internal bool DisplayFeatureRequireUpgrade(byte keyMajorVersion, byte keyMinorVersion, Xceed.Utils.Licensing.LicenseProduct product, string featureVersionDescription)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(featureVersionDescription))
			{
				featureVersionDescription = "a feature requiring an upgrade";
			}
			if (!PreprocessDisplay(Xceed.Utils.Licensing.LicenseInformationType.FeatureRequireUpgrade))
			{
				return false;
			}
			ProductVersion productVersion = LatestVersion(product);
			string arguments = "?productID=" + Xceed.Utils.Licensing.XceedLicenseManager.GetProductCodeName(product) + "&assemblyVersion=3.6.7614.12380&assemblyName=" + Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly().FullName + "&featureDescription=" + featureVersionDescription + "&usingKeyVersion=" + keyMajorVersion + "." + keyMinorVersion + "&latestKeyVersion=" + productVersion.Major + "." + productVersion.Minor;
			string licenseNotificationPath = s_installDirectory + "\\LicenseAnalyzer\\FeatureUpgradeInformation.hta";
			LatestVersion(product);
			return DisplayInformation(licenseNotificationPath, arguments, product);
		}

		internal bool DisplayAdvancedFeature(byte keyMajorVersion, byte keyMinorVersion, Xceed.Utils.Licensing.LicenseProduct product, string featureVersionDescription)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(featureVersionDescription))
			{
				featureVersionDescription = "a feature requiring an upgrade";
			}
			if (!PreprocessDisplay(Xceed.Utils.Licensing.LicenseInformationType.AdvancedFeature))
			{
				return false;
			}
			ProductVersion productVersion = LatestVersion(product);
			string arguments = "?productID=" + Xceed.Utils.Licensing.XceedLicenseManager.GetProductCodeName(product) + "&assemblyVersion=3.6.7614.12380&assemblyName=" + Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly().FullName + "&featureDescription=" + featureVersionDescription + "&usingKeyVersion=" + keyMajorVersion + "." + keyMinorVersion + "&latestKeyVersion=" + productVersion.Major + "." + productVersion.Minor;
			string licenseNotificationPath = s_installDirectory + "\\LicenseAnalyzer\\AdvancedFeatureInformation.hta";
			LatestVersion(product);
			return DisplayInformation(licenseNotificationPath, arguments, product);
		}
	}
}
namespace Xceed.Zip.ReaderWriter
{
	internal class ReaderWriterLicense : Xceed.Utils.Licensing.XceedLicense
	{
		protected override ProductVersion[] AllowedVersions => new ProductVersion[1]
		{
			new ProductVersion(Xceed.Utils.Licensing.LicenseProduct.XceedRealTimeZipNET, 1, 0)
		};

		protected override ProductVersion[] LatestVersions => new ProductVersion[1]
		{
			new ProductVersion(Xceed.Utils.Licensing.LicenseProduct.XceedRealTimeZipNET, 1, 0)
		};

		protected override Type LicenseeType => typeof(Licenser);

		public static Xceed.Utils.Licensing.XceedLicense CreateLicense(string licenseKey, Type licenserType)
		{
			Xceed.Utils.Licensing.XceedLicense xceedLicense = new ReaderWriterLicense();
			xceedLicense.Initialize(licenseKey, licenserType);
			if (xceedLicense.LicenseKey == null || xceedLicense.LicenseKey.Length == 0)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return xceedLicense;
		}

		private ReaderWriterLicense()
		{
		}
	}
	public class ZipWriter : IDisposable
	{
		private ZipWriterState m_zipWriterState;

		private Stream m_innerStream;

		private CompressionMethod m_compressionMethod = CompressionMethod.Deflated64;

		private CompressionLevel m_compressionLevel = CompressionLevel.Normal;

		private EncryptionMethod m_encryptionMethod = EncryptionMethod.Compatible;

		private string m_decryptionPassword;

		private long m_uncompressedSize;

		private long m_compressedSize;

		private long m_streamPositionBeforeCompression;

		private CompressedStream m_compressedStream;

		private ChecksumStream m_checksumStream;

		private Stream m_zipWriterStream;

		private List<ReaderWriterItemInfo> m_itemInfoList;

		private long m_centralDirectoryOffset;

		private long m_centralDirectorySize;

		private ZipItemLocalHeader m_currentZipItemLocalHeader;

		private ReaderWriterItemInfo m_currentItemInfo;

		private bool m_zipFileClosed;

		public event EventHandler<ZipWriterByteProgressionEventArgs> ByteProgression;

		public ZipWriter(Stream innerStream)
		{
			Licenser.VerifyLicense();
			m_itemInfoList = new List<ReaderWriterItemInfo>();
			if (innerStream == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("Inner stream can't be null.");
			}
			if (!innerStream.CanSeek)
			{
				innerStream = new PositionedStream(innerStream);
			}
			m_innerStream = innerStream;
			m_uncompressedSize = 0L;
			m_centralDirectorySize = 0L;
			m_zipWriterState = ZipWriterState.WRITE_LOCAL_HEADER;
		}

		public void WriteItemLocalHeader(ZipItemLocalHeader zipItemLocalHeader)
		{
			if (zipItemLocalHeader.IsFolder)
			{
				throw new ZipWriterException("ZipWriter does not support writing folder items.");
			}
			if (m_zipWriterState == ZipWriterState.WRITE_DATA)
			{
				m_zipWriterState = ZipWriterState.WRITE_DATA_DESCRIPTOR;
				WriteItemDataDescriptor(m_currentItemInfo);
				m_zipWriterState = ZipWriterState.WRITE_LOCAL_HEADER;
			}
			m_compressionMethod = zipItemLocalHeader.CompressionMethod;
			m_compressionLevel = zipItemLocalHeader.CompressionLevel;
			m_encryptionMethod = zipItemLocalHeader.EncryptionMethod;
			m_decryptionPassword = zipItemLocalHeader.EncryptionPassword;
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(zipItemLocalHeader.FileName))
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("FileName can't be null or empty.");
			}
			m_currentZipItemLocalHeader = zipItemLocalHeader;
			m_currentItemInfo = new ReaderWriterItemInfo(zipItemLocalHeader);
			m_compressedSize = 0L;
			m_uncompressedSize = 0L;
			ReaderWriterItemInfo currentItemInfo = m_currentItemInfo;
			currentItemInfo.GeneralBitFlags = (ZipGeneralBitFlags)checked((ushort)unchecked((int)(currentItemInfo.GeneralBitFlags & ~ZipGeneralBitFlags.LowestCompLevel)));
			if (!m_currentItemInfo.Encrypted && m_encryptionMethod != EncryptionMethod.WinZipAes)
			{
				if (m_compressionLevel == CompressionLevel.Highest)
				{
					ReaderWriterItemInfo currentItemInfo2 = m_currentItemInfo;
					currentItemInfo2.GeneralBitFlags = (ZipGeneralBitFlags)checked((ushort)unchecked((int)(currentItemInfo2.GeneralBitFlags | ZipGeneralBitFlags.MaxCompLevel)));
				}
				else if (m_compressionLevel < CompressionLevel.Highest && m_compressionLevel >= CompressionLevel.Normal)
				{
					ReaderWriterItemInfo currentItemInfo3 = m_currentItemInfo;
					currentItemInfo3.GeneralBitFlags = (ZipGeneralBitFlags)checked((ushort)unchecked((int)currentItemInfo3.GeneralBitFlags));
				}
				else if (m_compressionLevel < CompressionLevel.Normal && m_compressionLevel >= CompressionLevel.Lowest)
				{
					ReaderWriterItemInfo currentItemInfo4 = m_currentItemInfo;
					currentItemInfo4.GeneralBitFlags = (ZipGeneralBitFlags)checked((ushort)unchecked((int)(currentItemInfo4.GeneralBitFlags | ZipGeneralBitFlags.LowCompLevel)));
				}
				else if (m_compressionLevel > CompressionLevel.None)
				{
					ReaderWriterItemInfo currentItemInfo5 = m_currentItemInfo;
					currentItemInfo5.GeneralBitFlags = (ZipGeneralBitFlags)checked((ushort)unchecked((int)(currentItemInfo5.GeneralBitFlags | ZipGeneralBitFlags.LowestCompLevel)));
				}
			}
			ReaderWriterItemInfo currentItemInfo6 = m_currentItemInfo;
			currentItemInfo6.GeneralBitFlags = (ZipGeneralBitFlags)checked((ushort)unchecked((int)(currentItemInfo6.GeneralBitFlags | ZipGeneralBitFlags.DescriptorHeader)));
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(m_decryptionPassword))
			{
				ReaderWriterItemInfo currentItemInfo7 = m_currentItemInfo;
				currentItemInfo7.GeneralBitFlags = (ZipGeneralBitFlags)checked((ushort)unchecked((int)(currentItemInfo7.GeneralBitFlags | ZipGeneralBitFlags.Encrypted)));
			}
			m_currentItemInfo.EncryptionMethod = m_encryptionMethod;
			m_currentItemInfo.EncryptionStrength = 256;
			switch (m_compressionMethod)
			{
			case CompressionMethod.BWT:
				m_currentItemInfo.CompressionMethod = ZipCompressionMethod.BWT;
				break;
			case CompressionMethod.BZip2:
				m_currentItemInfo.CompressionMethod = ZipCompressionMethod.BZip2;
				break;
			case CompressionMethod.Deflated:
				m_currentItemInfo.CompressionMethod = ZipCompressionMethod.Deflated;
				break;
			case CompressionMethod.Deflated64:
				m_currentItemInfo.CompressionMethod = ZipCompressionMethod.Deflated64;
				break;
			case CompressionMethod.PPMd:
				m_currentItemInfo.CompressionMethod = ZipCompressionMethod.PPMd;
				break;
			case CompressionMethod.Stored:
				throw new ZipWriterException("Compression method Stored is not supported by " + GetType());
			default:
				throw new ZipWriterException(string.Concat("Compression method ", m_compressionMethod, " is not supported by ", GetType()));
			}
			m_innerStream.Flush();
			m_currentItemInfo.LocalHeaderOffset = m_innerStream.Position;
			byte[] localHeaderBytes = GetLocalHeaderBytes(m_currentItemInfo);
			m_innerStream.Write(localHeaderBytes, 0, localHeaderBytes.Length);
			m_streamPositionBeforeCompression = m_innerStream.Position;
			InitializeZipWriter(m_currentItemInfo);
			m_itemInfoList.Add(m_currentItemInfo);
			m_zipWriterState = ZipWriterState.WRITE_DATA;
		}

		public void WriteItemData(byte[] buffer, int offset, int count)
		{
			checked
			{
				m_uncompressedSize += unchecked((uint)count);
				m_zipWriterStream.Write(buffer, offset, count);
				OnByteProgression(m_uncompressedSize);
			}
		}

		public void CloseZipFile()
		{
			if (!m_zipFileClosed)
			{
				if (m_zipWriterState == ZipWriterState.WRITE_DATA)
				{
					m_zipWriterState = ZipWriterState.WRITE_DATA_DESCRIPTOR;
					WriteItemDataDescriptor(m_currentItemInfo);
				}
				m_zipWriterState = ZipWriterState.WRITE_CENTRAL_DIRECTORY;
				WriteCentralHeader();
				ulong num = WriteZip64EndHeader();
				if (m_itemInfoList.Count > 65535 || checked(num - (ulong)m_centralDirectoryOffset) >= uint.MaxValue || m_centralDirectoryOffset >= uint.MaxValue)
				{
					WriteZip64EndHeaderLocator(num);
				}
				WriteZipEndHeader();
				CloseInnerStreams();
				m_itemInfoList.Clear();
				m_zipFileClosed = true;
			}
		}

		public void Dispose()
		{
			try
			{
				CloseZipFile();
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
			finally
			{
				m_itemInfoList = null;
				m_currentZipItemLocalHeader = null;
				m_currentItemInfo = null;
				m_decryptionPassword = null;
			}
		}

		internal void CloseInnerStreams()
		{
			try
			{
				if (m_compressedStream != null)
				{
					m_compressedStream.Dispose();
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
			finally
			{
				m_compressedStream = null;
			}
			try
			{
				if (m_checksumStream != null)
				{
					m_checksumStream.Dispose();
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
			finally
			{
				m_checksumStream = null;
			}
			try
			{
				if (m_zipWriterStream != null)
				{
					m_zipWriterStream.Dispose();
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
			finally
			{
				m_zipWriterStream = null;
			}
			try
			{
				if (m_innerStream != null)
				{
					m_innerStream.Flush();
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
			finally
			{
				m_innerStream = null;
			}
		}

		internal byte[] GetAESExtraFieldsBytes(ReaderWriterItemInfo itemInfo)
		{
			byte[] array = null;
			using MemoryStream memoryStream = new MemoryStream();
			BinaryWriter binaryWriter = new BinaryWriter(memoryStream);
			try
			{
				binaryWriter.Write((ushort)39169);
				binaryWriter.Write((short)7);
				binaryWriter.Write((short)2);
				binaryWriter.Write((short)17729);
				int num = (itemInfo.EncryptionStrength - 64) / 64;
				if (num < 1 || num > 3)
				{
					throw new ZipWriterException("Unexpected encryption strength while writing AES extra header.");
				}
				binaryWriter.Write((byte)num);
				binaryWriter.Write(checked((short)itemInfo.CompressionMethod));
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new ZipWriterException("Error while writing AES extra fields.", innerException);
			}
			finally
			{
				binaryWriter.Close();
			}
			return memoryStream.ToArray();
		}

		internal byte[] GetZip64ExtraFieldsBytes(ReaderWriterItemInfo itemInfo, bool forCentralHeader)
		{
			byte[] array = null;
			checked
			{
				using MemoryStream memoryStream = new MemoryStream();
				BinaryWriter binaryWriter = new BinaryWriter(memoryStream);
				try
				{
					bool flag = itemInfo.UncompressedSize >= uint.MaxValue;
					bool flag2 = itemInfo.CompressedSize >= uint.MaxValue;
					if (!forCentralHeader && flag != flag2)
					{
						flag2 = (flag = true);
					}
					bool flag3 = forCentralHeader && itemInfo.LocalHeaderOffset >= uint.MaxValue;
					bool flag4 = forCentralHeader && itemInfo.LocalHeaderDisk >= 65535;
					int num = (flag ? 8 : 0) + (flag2 ? 8 : 0) + (flag3 ? 8 : 0) + (flag4 ? 4 : 0);
					if (num == 0)
					{
						return new byte[0];
					}
					binaryWriter.Write((short)1);
					binaryWriter.Write((ushort)num);
					if (flag)
					{
						binaryWriter.Write((ulong)itemInfo.UncompressedSize);
					}
					if (flag2)
					{
						binaryWriter.Write((ulong)itemInfo.CompressedSize);
					}
					if (flag3)
					{
						binaryWriter.Write((ulong)itemInfo.LocalHeaderOffset);
					}
					if (flag4)
					{
						binaryWriter.Write(itemInfo.LocalHeaderDisk);
					}
				}
				catch (Exception innerException)
				{
					throw new ZipWriterException("Error while writing AES extra fields.", innerException);
				}
				finally
				{
					binaryWriter.Close();
				}
				return memoryStream.ToArray();
			}
		}

		internal byte[] GetExtraFieldsBytes(ReaderWriterItemInfo itemInfo, bool forCentralHeader)
		{
			byte[] array = null;
			using MemoryStream memoryStream = new MemoryStream();
			BinaryWriter binaryWriter = new BinaryWriter(memoryStream);
			try
			{
				byte[] aESExtraFieldsBytes;
				if (itemInfo.Encrypted && itemInfo.EncryptionMethod == EncryptionMethod.WinZipAes && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(m_decryptionPassword))
				{
					aESExtraFieldsBytes = GetAESExtraFieldsBytes(itemInfo);
					if (aESExtraFieldsBytes.Length > 0)
					{
						binaryWriter.Write(aESExtraFieldsBytes, 0, aESExtraFieldsBytes.Length);
					}
				}
				aESExtraFieldsBytes = GetZip64ExtraFieldsBytes(itemInfo, forCentralHeader);
				if (aESExtraFieldsBytes.Length > 0)
				{
					binaryWriter.Write(aESExtraFieldsBytes, 0, aESExtraFieldsBytes.Length);
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				binaryWriter.Close();
			}
			return memoryStream.ToArray();
		}

		internal byte[] GetLocalHeaderBytes(ReaderWriterItemInfo itemInfo)
		{
			byte[] array = null;
			string s = ReaderWriterUtils.UnfixFilename(itemInfo.FileName);
			using MemoryStream memoryStream = new MemoryStream();
			BinaryWriter binaryWriter = new BinaryWriter(memoryStream);
			try
			{
				ZipVersionField zipVersionField = ZipVersionField.VerZip64;
				binaryWriter.Write(67324752u);
				binaryWriter.Write((ushort)zipVersionField);
				binaryWriter.Write((ushort)itemInfo.GeneralBitFlags);
				itemInfo.VersionNeeded = zipVersionField;
				itemInfo.VersionMadeBy = zipVersionField;
				if (itemInfo.Encrypted && itemInfo.EncryptionMethod == EncryptionMethod.WinZipAes)
				{
					binaryWriter.Write((ushort)99);
				}
				else
				{
					binaryWriter.Write((ushort)itemInfo.CompressionMethod);
				}
				ushort dosTime = 0;
				ushort dosDate = 0;
				DateTime today = DateTime.Today;
				Dates.ToDosDateTime(today, out dosDate, out dosTime);
				binaryWriter.Write(dosTime);
				binaryWriter.Write(dosDate);
				itemInfo.LastModifiedDateTime = today;
				binaryWriter.Write(0u);
				itemInfo.Crc32 = 0u;
				binaryWriter.Write(0u);
				binaryWriter.Write(0u);
				itemInfo.CompressedSize = 0L;
				itemInfo.UncompressedSize = 0L;
				byte[] bytes = Xceed.Utils.Strings.Encoding.OEMEncoding.GetBytes(s);
				byte[] extraFieldsBytes = GetExtraFieldsBytes(itemInfo, forCentralHeader: false);
				checked
				{
					binaryWriter.Write((ushort)bytes.Length);
					binaryWriter.Write((ushort)extraFieldsBytes.Length);
					if (bytes.Length > 0)
					{
						binaryWriter.Write(bytes);
					}
					if (extraFieldsBytes.Length > 0)
					{
						binaryWriter.Write(extraFieldsBytes, 0, extraFieldsBytes.Length);
					}
				}
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new ZipWriterException("Error while writing LocalHeader.", innerException);
			}
			finally
			{
				binaryWriter.Close();
			}
			return memoryStream.ToArray();
		}

		internal void InitializeZipWriter(ReaderWriterItemInfo itemInfo)
		{
			Stream stream = null;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(m_decryptionPassword))
			{
				switch (itemInfo.EncryptionMethod)
				{
				case EncryptionMethod.Compatible:
				{
					ushort dosDate = 0;
					ushort dosTime = 0;
					Dates.ToDosDateTime(itemInfo.LastModifiedDateTime, out dosDate, out dosTime);
					stream = new ZCryptStream(m_innerStream, m_decryptionPassword, (int)dosTime, encrypt: true);
					((ZCryptStream)stream).Transient = true;
					break;
				}
				case EncryptionMethod.WinZipAes:
					stream = new TransientStream(m_innerStream);
					stream = new AesEncryptionStream(stream, m_decryptionPassword, itemInfo.EncryptionStrength, writing: true);
					break;
				default:
					throw new ZipWriterException("Unsupported encryption method: " + itemInfo.EncryptionMethod);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				stream = m_innerStream;
			}
			m_compressedStream = new CompressedStream(stream, m_compressionMethod, m_compressionLevel);
			if (itemInfo.Encrypted && itemInfo.EncryptionMethod == EncryptionMethod.WinZipAes)
			{
				m_zipWriterStream = m_compressedStream;
				m_compressedStream.Transient = false;
			}
			else
			{
				m_compressedStream.Transient = true;
				m_checksumStream = new ChecksumStream(m_compressedStream, ChecksumType.CRC32);
				m_zipWriterStream = m_checksumStream;
			}
		}

		internal void WriteItemDataDescriptor(ReaderWriterItemInfo itemInfo)
		{
			m_zipWriterStream.Dispose();
			m_zipWriterStream = null;
			checked
			{
				m_compressedSize = m_innerStream.Position - m_streamPositionBeforeCompression;
				itemInfo.CompressedSize = m_compressedSize;
				itemInfo.UncompressedSize = m_uncompressedSize;
				m_zipWriterState = ZipWriterState.WRITE_DATA_DESCRIPTOR;
				byte[] array = null;
				using (MemoryStream memoryStream = new MemoryStream())
				{
					BinaryWriter binaryWriter = new BinaryWriter(memoryStream);
					try
					{
						binaryWriter.Write(134695760u);
						if (itemInfo.Encrypted && itemInfo.EncryptionMethod == EncryptionMethod.WinZipAes)
						{
							binaryWriter.Write(0u);
							itemInfo.Crc32 = 0u;
						}
						else
						{
							binaryWriter.Write(m_checksumStream.Checksum);
							itemInfo.Crc32 = unchecked((uint)m_checksumStream.Checksum);
						}
						if (m_compressedSize >= uint.MaxValue || m_uncompressedSize >= uint.MaxValue)
						{
							binaryWriter.Write((ulong)m_compressedSize);
							binaryWriter.Write((ulong)m_uncompressedSize);
						}
						else
						{
							binaryWriter.Write((uint)m_compressedSize);
							binaryWriter.Write((uint)m_uncompressedSize);
						}
					}
					finally
					{
						binaryWriter.Close();
					}
					array = memoryStream.ToArray();
				}
				m_innerStream.Write(array, 0, array.Length);
			}
		}

		internal void WriteCentralHeader()
		{
			m_centralDirectoryOffset = m_innerStream.Position;
			m_centralDirectorySize = 0L;
			foreach (ReaderWriterItemInfo itemInfo in m_itemInfoList)
			{
				string s = ReaderWriterUtils.UnfixFilename(itemInfo.FileName);
				byte[] bytes = Xceed.Utils.Strings.Encoding.OEMEncoding.GetBytes(s);
				byte[] extraFieldsBytes = GetExtraFieldsBytes(itemInfo, forCentralHeader: true);
				byte[] bytes2 = System.Text.Encoding.Default.GetBytes(itemInfo.FileComment);
				byte[] array = null;
				using (MemoryStream memoryStream = new MemoryStream())
				{
					BinaryWriter binaryWriter = new BinaryWriter(memoryStream);
					try
					{
						binaryWriter.Write(33639248u);
						binaryWriter.Write((ushort)itemInfo.VersionMadeBy);
						binaryWriter.Write((ushort)itemInfo.VersionNeeded);
						binaryWriter.Write((ushort)itemInfo.GeneralBitFlags);
						if (itemInfo.Encrypted && itemInfo.EncryptionMethod == EncryptionMethod.WinZipAes)
						{
							binaryWriter.Write((ushort)99);
						}
						else
						{
							binaryWriter.Write((ushort)itemInfo.CompressionMethod);
						}
						ushort dosTime = 0;
						ushort dosDate = 0;
						Dates.ToDosDateTime(itemInfo.LastModifiedDateTime, out dosDate, out dosTime);
						binaryWriter.Write(dosTime);
						binaryWriter.Write(dosDate);
						binaryWriter.Write(itemInfo.Crc32);
						binaryWriter.Write(ReaderWriterUtils.SafeUInt32(itemInfo.CompressedSize));
						binaryWriter.Write(ReaderWriterUtils.SafeUInt32(itemInfo.UncompressedSize));
						checked
						{
							binaryWriter.Write((ushort)bytes.Length);
							binaryWriter.Write((ushort)extraFieldsBytes.Length);
							binaryWriter.Write((ushort)bytes2.Length);
							binaryWriter.Write(ReaderWriterUtils.SafeUInt16(itemInfo.LocalHeaderDisk));
						}
						binaryWriter.Write((ushort)itemInfo.InternalFileAttribs);
						binaryWriter.Write((uint)itemInfo.ExternalFileAttribs);
						binaryWriter.Write(ReaderWriterUtils.SafeUInt32(itemInfo.LocalHeaderOffset));
						if (bytes.Length > 0)
						{
							binaryWriter.Write(bytes);
						}
						if (extraFieldsBytes.Length > 0)
						{
							binaryWriter.Write(extraFieldsBytes);
						}
						if (bytes2.Length > 0)
						{
							binaryWriter.Write(bytes2);
						}
					}
					catch (Exception innerException)
					{
						throw new ZipWriterException("Error while writing central header.", innerException);
					}
					finally
					{
						binaryWriter.Close();
					}
					array = memoryStream.ToArray();
				}
				m_innerStream.Write(array, 0, array.Length);
				checked
				{
					m_centralDirectorySize += array.Length;
				}
			}
		}

		internal void WriteZip64EndHeaderLocator(ulong zip64EndHeaderOffset)
		{
			byte[] array = null;
			using (MemoryStream memoryStream = new MemoryStream())
			{
				BinaryWriter binaryWriter = new BinaryWriter(memoryStream);
				try
				{
					binaryWriter.Write(117853008u);
					binaryWriter.Write(0u);
					binaryWriter.Write(zip64EndHeaderOffset);
					binaryWriter.Write(1u);
				}
				finally
				{
					binaryWriter.Close();
				}
				array = memoryStream.ToArray();
			}
			m_innerStream.Write(array, 0, array.Length);
		}

		internal ulong WriteZip64EndHeader()
		{
			byte[] array = null;
			ulong result = 0uL;
			checked
			{
				using (MemoryStream memoryStream = new MemoryStream())
				{
					BinaryWriter binaryWriter = new BinaryWriter(memoryStream);
					try
					{
						result = (ulong)m_innerStream.Position;
						binaryWriter.Write(101075792u);
						binaryWriter.Write(44uL);
						binaryWriter.Write((ushort)45);
						binaryWriter.Write((ushort)45);
						binaryWriter.Write(0u);
						binaryWriter.Write(0u);
						binaryWriter.Write((ulong)m_itemInfoList.Count);
						binaryWriter.Write((ulong)m_itemInfoList.Count);
						binaryWriter.Write((ulong)m_centralDirectorySize);
						binaryWriter.Write((ulong)m_centralDirectoryOffset);
					}
					finally
					{
						binaryWriter.Close();
					}
					array = memoryStream.ToArray();
				}
				m_innerStream.Write(array, 0, array.Length);
				return result;
			}
		}

		internal void WriteZipEndHeader()
		{
			byte[] array = null;
			checked
			{
				using (MemoryStream memoryStream = new MemoryStream())
				{
					BinaryWriter binaryWriter = new BinaryWriter(memoryStream);
					try
					{
						binaryWriter.Write(101010256u);
						binaryWriter.Write((ushort)0);
						binaryWriter.Write((ushort)0);
						binaryWriter.Write((ushort)m_itemInfoList.Count);
						binaryWriter.Write((ushort)m_itemInfoList.Count);
						binaryWriter.Write(ReaderWriterUtils.SafeUInt32(m_centralDirectorySize));
						binaryWriter.Write(ReaderWriterUtils.SafeUInt32(m_centralDirectoryOffset));
						binaryWriter.Write((ushort)0);
					}
					finally
					{
						binaryWriter.Close();
					}
					array = memoryStream.ToArray();
				}
				m_innerStream.Write(array, 0, array.Length);
				m_currentItemInfo = null;
			}
		}

		internal void OnByteProgression(long totalProcessed)
		{
			if (this.ByteProgression != null)
			{
				ZipWriterByteProgressionEventArgs e = new ZipWriterByteProgressionEventArgs(m_currentZipItemLocalHeader, totalProcessed);
				this.ByteProgression(this, e);
				e = null;
			}
		}
	}
	public class ZipWriterByteProgressionEventArgs : EventArgs
	{
		private long m_bytesProcessed;

		private ZipItemLocalHeader m_zipItemLocalHeader;

		public long BytesProcessed => m_bytesProcessed;

		public ZipItemLocalHeader ZipItemLocalHeader
		{
			get
			{
				if (m_zipItemLocalHeader == null)
				{
					throw new NullReferenceException("ZipItemLocalHeader is null.");
				}
				return m_zipItemLocalHeader;
			}
		}

		public ZipWriterByteProgressionEventArgs(ZipItemLocalHeader zipItemLocalHeader, long bytesProcessed)
		{
			m_bytesProcessed = bytesProcessed;
			m_zipItemLocalHeader = zipItemLocalHeader;
		}
	}
	public class ZipWriterException : Exception
	{
		private Exception m_innerException;

		private string m_message;

		public ZipWriterException(string message)
			: this(message, null)
		{
		}

		public ZipWriterException(string message, Exception innerException)
			: base(message, innerException)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(message))
			{
				m_message = string.Empty;
			}
			else
			{
				m_message = message;
			}
			m_innerException = innerException;
		}
	}
	internal enum ZipWriterState
	{
		WRITE_LOCAL_HEADER,
		WRITE_DATA,
		WRITE_DATA_DESCRIPTOR,
		WRITE_CENTRAL_DIRECTORY
	}
}
namespace Xceed.Zip.Sfx
{
	internal interface INotifyChanges
	{
		event ChangedEventHandler Changed;
	}
	public class NotifyListBase : ListBase, INotifyChanges
	{
		private ChangedEventHandler ChangedStorage;

		event ChangedEventHandler INotifyChanges.Changed
		{
			add
			{
				ChangedStorage = (ChangedEventHandler)Delegate.Combine(ChangedStorage, value);
			}
			remove
			{
				ChangedStorage = (ChangedEventHandler)Delegate.Remove(ChangedStorage, value);
			}
		}

		internal NotifyListBase()
		{
		}

		internal NotifyListBase(NotifyListBase template)
			: base(template)
		{
		}

		private void OnChanged()
		{
			if (ChangedStorage != null)
			{
				ChangedStorage(this, new EventArgs());
			}
		}

		internal void OnItemChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			OnChanged();
		}

		protected override void OnInsertComplete(int index, object value)
		{
			if (value is INotifyChanges notifyChanges)
			{
				notifyChanges.Changed += OnItemChanged;
			}
			OnChanged();
		}

		protected override void OnRemoveComplete(int index, object value)
		{
			if (value is INotifyChanges notifyChanges)
			{
				notifyChanges.Changed -= OnItemChanged;
			}
			OnChanged();
		}

		protected override void OnClear(ref bool cancel)
		{
			foreach (object item in base.Array)
			{
				if (item is INotifyChanges notifyChanges)
				{
					notifyChanges.Changed -= OnItemChanged;
				}
			}
			OnChanged();
		}

		protected override void OnSetComplete(int index, object oldValue, object newValue)
		{
			if (oldValue is INotifyChanges notifyChanges)
			{
				notifyChanges.Changed -= OnItemChanged;
			}
			if (newValue is INotifyChanges notifyChanges2)
			{
				notifyChanges2.Changed += OnItemChanged;
			}
			OnChanged();
		}
	}

			set

				OnChanged();
			}
		}

		event ChangedEventHandler INotifyChanges.Changed
		{
			add
			{
				ChangedStorage = (ChangedEventHandler)Delegate.Combine(ChangedStorage, value);
			}
			remove
			{
				ChangedStorage = (ChangedEventHandler)Delegate.Remove(ChangedStorage, value);

		}

		}

		private void OnChanged()

			{
				ChangedStorage(this, new EventArgs());
			}
		}
	}

		}

		{
			if (ChangedStorage != null)
			{
				ChangedStorage(this, new EventArgs());
			}
		}
	}

	public class RegistryKey : INotifyChanges
	{
		private ChangedEventHandler ChangedStorage;

		private string m_keyName = string.Empty;

		private string m_valueName = string.Empty;

		private object m_value;

		public string KeyName
		{
			get
			{
				return m_keyName;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value == null)
				{
					throw new ArgumentNullException("KeyName", "You must provide the name of the registry key to create.");
				}
				if (value.Length == 0)
				{
					throw new ArgumentException("You must provide the name of the registry key to create.", "KeyName");
				}
				m_keyName = value;
				OnChanged();
			}
		}

		public string ValueName
		{
			get
			{
				return m_valueName;
			}
			set
			{
				m_valueName = value;
				OnChanged();
			}
		}

		public object Value
		{
			get
			{
				return m_value;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value == null)
				{
					throw new ArgumentNullException("Value", "You must provide a non-null value, as its type dictates the key type.");
				}
				if (value is string || value is int || value is byte[])
				{
					m_value = value;
				}
				else
				{
					m_value = value.ToString();
				}
				OnChanged();
			}
		}

		event ChangedEventHandler INotifyChanges.Changed
		{
			add
			{
				ChangedStorage = (ChangedEventHandler)Delegate.Combine(ChangedStorage, value);
			}
			remove
			{
				ChangedStorage = (ChangedEventHandler)Delegate.Remove(ChangedStorage, value);
			}
		}

		public RegistryKey(string keyName, string valueName, object value)
		{
			if (keyName == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("keyName", "You must provide the name of the registry key to create.");
			}
			if (keyName.Length == 0)
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("You must provide the name of the registry key to create.", "keyName");
			}
			if (value == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("value", "You must provide a non-null value, as its type dictates the key type.");
			}
			m_keyName = keyName;
			if (valueName != null)
			{
				m_valueName = valueName;
			}
			if (value is string || value is int || value is byte[])
			{
				m_value = value;
			}
			else
			{
				m_value = value.ToString();
			}
		}

		private void OnChanged()
		{
			if (ChangedStorage != null)
			{
				ChangedStorage(this, new EventArgs());
			}
		}
	}
	public class RegistryKeyList : NotifyListBase
	{
		public RegistryKey this[int index] => (RegistryKey)ListGetItem(index);

		public override bool IsFixedSize => false;

		public override bool IsReadOnly => false;

			}
		}
	}
	public class SfxPrefix : INotifyChanges
	{
		private ChangedEventHandler ChangedStorage;

		internal MemoryFile m_sourceModule;

		public long SourceModuleSize
		{
			get
			{
				if (!m_sourceModule.Exists)
				{
					return 0L;
				}
				return m_sourceModule.Size;
			}
		}

		event ChangedEventHandler INotifyChanges.Changed
		{
			add
			{
				ChangedStorage = (ChangedEventHandler)Delegate.Combine(ChangedStorage, value);
			}
			remove
			{
				ChangedStorage = (ChangedEventHandler)Delegate.Remove(ChangedStorage, value);
			}
		}

		public SfxPrefix()
		{
			Licenser.VerifyLicense(FeatureID.NewSfx);
			m_sourceModule = new MemoryFile();
		}

		public SfxPrefix(AbstractFile sfxModule)
		{
			Licenser.VerifyLicense(FeatureID.NewSfx);
			SetSourceModule(sfxModule);
		}

		internal SfxPrefix(MemoryFile storageFile, int dummy)
		{
			Licenser.VerifyLicense(FeatureID.NewSfx);
			SetInternalSourceModule(storageFile);
		}

		public void SetSourceModule(AbstractFile sfxModule)
		{
			if (sfxModule == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("sfxModule", "The SFX module cannot be null. To remove any ZIP file SFX, set the ZipArchive's SfxPrefix property to null.");
			}
			using Stream prefixSource = sfxModule.OpenRead(FileShare.Read);
			SetInternalSourceModule(CreatePrefixStorage(prefixSource, -1L));
		}

		protected virtual void SetInternalSourceModule(MemoryFile storageFile)
		{
			if (storageFile == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("storageFile", "The storage file cannot be null.");
			}
			m_sourceModule = storageFile;
			OnChanged();
		}

		protected internal virtual void WriteSfxPrefix(Stream stream)
		{
			if (stream == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("stream", "You must provide the stream to write the prefix to.");
			}
			if (!stream.CanWrite)
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("The stream must be writable.", "stream");
			}
			StreamFile destinationFile = new StreamFile(stream, "stream");
			m_sourceModule.CopyTo(destinationFile, replaceIfExists: true);
		}

		internal static MemoryFile CreatePrefixStorage(Stream prefixSource, long prefixSize)
		{
			if (prefixSource == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("prefixSource");
			}
			if (prefixSize < -1 || prefixSize == 0)
			{
				Xceed.Utils.Exceptions.ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("prefixSize", prefixSize, "The module size can be -1 for up to end-of-file, or a value greater than 0.");
			}
			MemoryFile memoryFile = new MemoryFile();
			if (!memoryFile.Exists)
			{
				memoryFile.Create();
			}
			using Stream dest = memoryFile.OpenWrite(overwrite: true);
			StreamCopy.Copy(prefixSource, dest, prefixSize);
			return memoryFile;
		}

		internal static SfxPrefix CreateSfxPrefix(Stream prefixSource, long prefixSize)
		{
			MemoryFile storageFile = CreatePrefixStorage(prefixSource, prefixSize);
			try
			{
				return new XceedSfxPrefix(storageFile, 0);
			}
			catch (InvalidSfxModuleException)
			{
			}
			catch (LicenseException)
			{
			}
			return new SfxPrefix(storageFile, 0);
		}

		protected virtual void OnChanged()
		{
			if (ChangedStorage != null)
			{
				ChangedStorage(this, new EventArgs());
			}
		}
	}
	public delegate void ConfigurationChangedEventsHandler(object sender, EventArgs e);
	public class StringList : NotifyListBase
	{
		private bool m_fixedSize;

		public string this[int index]
		{
			get
			{
				return (string)ListGetItem(index);
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != null && value.IndexOfAny(new char[2] { '\r', '\n' }) != -1)
				{
					throw new ArgumentException("The value cannot contain a line feed or carriage return.");
				}
				ListSetItem(index, value);
			}
		}

		public override bool IsFixedSize => m_fixedSize;

		public override bool IsReadOnly => false;

		internal StringList()
		{
		}

		internal StringList(string value)
		{
			Add(value);
			m_fixedSize = true;
		}

		internal StringList(string[] fixedContents)
		{
			AddRange(fixedContents);
			m_fixedSize = true;
		}

		internal StringList(StringList template)
			: base(template)
		{
		}

		public bool Contains(string value)
		{
			if (value != null && value.IndexOfAny(new char[2] { '\r', '\n' }) != -1)
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("The value cannot contain a line feed or carriage return.");
			}
			return ListContains(value);
		}

		public int IndexOf(string value)
		{
			if (value != null && value.IndexOfAny(new char[2] { '\r', '\n' }) != -1)
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("The value cannot contain a line feed or carriage return.");
			}
			return ListIndexOf(value);
		}

		public void RemoveAt(int index)
		{
			ListRemoveAt(index);
		}

		public void Insert(int index, string value)
		{
			if (value != null && value.IndexOfAny(new char[2] { '\r', '\n' }) != -1)
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("The value cannot contain a line feed or carriage return.");
			}
			ListInsert(index, value);
		}

		public void Remove(string value)
		{
			if (value != null && value.IndexOfAny(new char[2] { '\r', '\n' }) != -1)
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("The value cannot contain a line feed or carriage return.");
			}
			ListRemove(value);
		}

		public void Clear()
		{
			if (IsFixedSize)
			{
				for (int i = 0; i < base.Count; i = checked(i + 1))
				{
					this[i] = string.Empty;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				ListClear();
			}
		}

		public int Add(string value)
		{
			int num = -1;
			using StringReader stringReader = new StringReader(value);
			string text = null;
			while ((text = stringReader.ReadLine()) != null)
			{
				int num2 = ListAdd(text);
				if (num == -1)
				{
					num = num2;
				}
			}
			return num;
		}

		public void AddRange(string[] values)
		{
			foreach (string value in values)
			{
				Add(value);
			}
		}

		protected override void OnValidate(object value)
		{
			base.OnValidate(value);
			if (!(value is string))
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("Only string types can be added to a StringList.", "value");
			}
		}
	}
	public sealed class Variables
	{
		public const string ErrorNumber = "<ErrorNumber>";

		internal const string ErrorNumberForSfx = "%e";

		public const string DiskNumber = "<DiskNumber>";

		internal const string DiskNumberForSfx = "%n";

		public const string DestinationFile = "<DestinationFile>";

		internal const string DestinationFileForSfx = "%f";

		public const string DestinationFolder = "<DestinationFolder>";

		internal const string DestinationFolderForSfx = "%d";

		public const string SfxFolder = "<SfxFolder>";

		internal const string SfxFolderForSfx = "%e";

		public const string WindowsFolder = "<WindowsFolder>";

		internal const string WindowsFolderForSfx = "%w";

		public const string SystemFolder = "<SystemFolder>";

		internal const string SystemFolderForSfx = "%s";

		public const string TempFolder = "<TempFolder>";

		internal const string TempFolderForSfx = "%t";

		public const string RandomFolder = "<RandomFolder>";

		internal const string RandomFolderForSfx = "%r";

		public const string ProgramFilesFolder = "<ProgramFilesFolder>";

		internal const string ProgramFilesFolderForSfx = "%p";

		public const string SystemDrive = "<SystemDrive>";

		internal const string SystemDriveForSfx = "%v";
	}
	public class XceedSfxPrefix : SfxPrefix
	{
		private const int MinimumVersion = 66304;

		private const int MaximumVersion = 66304;

		private const int VersionMask = 16776960;

		private const int UnicodeVersion = 66304;

		private const int MaximumPasswordSize = 80;

		private const int MaximumFileNameSize = 255;

		private const int MaximumCaptionSize = 30;

		private const int MaximumStringSize = 256;

		private const int MaximumMessageSize = 2000;

		private const int MaximumLicenseSize = 8000;

		private const int MaximumTitleSize = 100;

		private const string ShareNameDialogString = "%s on '%c'";

		private const string NetworkDialogString = "Entire Network";

		private const string TrialTitle = "Xceed Zip Self-Extractor - Trial Version";

		private const string TrialMessage = "This self-extracting zip file was created with the free trial version of Xceed Zip for .NET.  You cannot distribute files created with the free trial version of Xceed Zip for .NET.  Registering your copy of Xceed Zip for .NET will remove this screen.\n\nwww.xceedsoft.com";

		private const string InvalidSfxConfigMessage = "Invalid SFX configuration.";

		private static readonly byte[] SfxSignature = new byte[6] { 20, 201, 43, 112, 92, 69 };

		private static readonly byte[] ConfigSignature = new byte[6] { 154, 175, 177, 138, 175, 174 };

		private static readonly byte[] ConfigSignatureV2 = new byte[8] { 154, 175, 177, 159, 251, 138, 175, 174 };

		private MemoryFile m_configuration;

		private bool m_keepConfig;

		private int m_configVersion;

		private string m_defaultPassword = string.Empty;

		private string m_defaultDestinationFolder = string.Empty;

		private StringList m_executeAfter = new StringList();

		private ExistingFileBehavior m_existingFileBehavior;

		private ExtensionAssociationList m_extensionsToAssociate = new ExtensionAssociationList();

		private FileCopyList m_filesToCopy = new FileCopyList();

		private StringList m_dllsToRegister = new StringList();

		private Icon m_icon;

		private bool m_deleteUnzippedFiles;

		private ProgramGroup m_programGroup = new ProgramGroup("");

		private RegistryKeyList m_registryKeys = new RegistryKeyList();

		private ButtonCaptionList m_buttonCaptions = new ButtonCaptionList();

		private DialogMessageList m_dialogMessages = new DialogMessageList();

		private DialogStringList m_dialogStrings = new DialogStringList();

		private string m_trialTitle = string.Empty;

		private string m_trialMessage = string.Empty;

		private bool m_writeUnicodeStrings;

		public string DefaultPassword
		{

				OnChanged();
			}
		}

			set

				{
					throw new ArgumentException("Unknown enumeration value.", "value");

				OnChanged();
			}
		}

		public ExtensionAssociationList ExtensionsToAssociate => m_extensionsToAssociate;

		{

				return m_icon;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value == null)
				{
					m_icon = null;
				}
				else
				{
					if (value.Width != 32 || value.Height != 32)
					{
						throw new ArgumentException("The icon size must be 32 by 32.", "Icon");
					}
					m_icon = Icon.FromHandle(new Bitmap(value.ToBitmap(), 32, 32).GetHicon());
				}
				OnChanged();
			}
		}

		public XceedSfxPrefix(AbstractFile sourceModule)
			: base(sourceModule)
