		}

		internal XceedSfxPrefix(MemoryFile storageFile, int dummy)
			: base(storageFile, dummy)
		{
			Init();
		}

		~XceedSfxPrefix()
		{
			INotifyChanges executeAfter = m_executeAfter;
			executeAfter.Changed -= MemberChanged;
			executeAfter = m_extensionsToAssociate;
			executeAfter.Changed -= MemberChanged;
			executeAfter = m_filesToCopy;
			executeAfter.Changed -= MemberChanged;
			executeAfter = m_dllsToRegister;
			executeAfter.Changed -= MemberChanged;
			executeAfter = m_registryKeys;
			executeAfter.Changed -= MemberChanged;
			executeAfter = m_buttonCaptions;
			executeAfter.Changed -= MemberChanged;
			executeAfter = m_dialogMessages;
			executeAfter.Changed -= MemberChanged;
			executeAfter = m_dialogStrings;
			executeAfter.Changed -= MemberChanged;
			executeAfter = m_programGroup;
			executeAfter.Changed -= MemberChanged;
		}

		private void Init()
		{
			INotifyChanges executeAfter = m_executeAfter;
			executeAfter.Changed += MemberChanged;
			executeAfter = m_extensionsToAssociate;
			executeAfter.Changed += MemberChanged;
			executeAfter = m_filesToCopy;
			executeAfter.Changed += MemberChanged;
			executeAfter = m_dllsToRegister;
			executeAfter.Changed += MemberChanged;
			executeAfter = m_registryKeys;
			executeAfter.Changed += MemberChanged;
			executeAfter = m_buttonCaptions;
			executeAfter.Changed += MemberChanged;
			executeAfter = m_dialogMessages;
			executeAfter.Changed += MemberChanged;
			executeAfter = m_dialogStrings;
			executeAfter.Changed += MemberChanged;
			executeAfter = m_programGroup;
			executeAfter.Changed += MemberChanged;
		}

		protected override void SetInternalSourceModule(MemoryFile sourceModule)
		{
			try
			{
				using (Stream stream = sourceModule.OpenRead())
				{
					if (!IsXceedSfx(stream, sourceModule.Size))
					{
						throw new InvalidSfxModuleException("This module is not recognized as an Xceed Sfx Module.");
					}
					int xceedSfxVersion = GetXceedSfxVersion(stream);
					if ((xceedSfxVersion & 0xFF000000u) != 268435456 && (xceedSfxVersion & 0xFF000000u) != 536870912)
					{
						throw new InvalidSfxModuleException("This module is not recognized as an Xceed Sfx Module.");
					}
					xceedSfxVersion &= 0xFFFF00;
					if (xceedSfxVersion < 66304 || xceedSfxVersion > 66304)
					{
						throw new InvalidSfxModuleException("The version of this Xceed Sfx Module is not supported.");
					}
					m_configVersion = xceedSfxVersion;
					long position = stream.Position;
					if (position < sourceModule.Size)
					{
						m_keepConfig = true;
						ReadConfiguration(stream, checked(sourceModule.Size - position));
					}
					stream.SetLength(position);
				}
				base.SetInternalSourceModule(sourceModule);
			}
			finally
			{
				m_keepConfig = false;
			}
		}

		protected internal override void WriteSfxPrefix(Stream stream)
		{
			MemoryFile memoryFile = null;
			checked
			{
				if (m_icon != null)
				{
					memoryFile = m_sourceModule;
					m_sourceModule = new MemoryFile();
					memoryFile.CopyTo(m_sourceModule, replaceIfExists: true);
					MemoryStream memoryStream = new MemoryStream();
					try
					{
						m_icon.Save(memoryStream);
					}
					finally
					{
						memoryStream.Close();
					}
					byte[] array = memoryStream.ToArray();
					if (array.Length == 766)
					{
						array[42] = 128;
						byte[] array2 = new byte[24];
						Array.Copy(array, 22, array2, 0, array2.Length);
						long num = -1L;
						using (Stream stream2 = m_sourceModule.OpenRead())
						{
							StreamSearcher streamSearcher = new StreamSearcher(stream2);
							if (streamSearcher.Find(array2))
							{
								num = stream2.Position;
							}
						}
						if (num != -1)
						{
							using Stream stream3 = m_sourceModule.OpenWrite(overwrite: false);
							stream3.Seek(num, SeekOrigin.Begin);
							stream3.Write(array, 22 + array2.Length, array.Length - 22 - array2.Length);
						}
					}
				}
				base.WriteSfxPrefix(stream);
				if (memoryFile != null)
				{
					m_sourceModule.Delete();
					m_sourceModule = memoryFile;
				}
				if (m_configuration == null)
				{
					BinaryWriter binaryWriter = new BinaryWriter(new TransientStream(stream));
					try
					{
						WriteConfigSignature(binaryWriter, m_writeUnicodeStrings);
						ushort key = 15510;
						WriteEncodedBoolean(binaryWriter, m_dialogMessages[Xceed.Zip.Sfx.DialogMessages.DestinationFolder].Length > 0, ref key);
						WriteEncodedBoolean(binaryWriter, m_dialogMessages[Xceed.Zip.Sfx.DialogMessages.Progress].Length > 0, ref key);
						WriteEncodedBoolean(binaryWriter, m_dialogMessages[Xceed.Zip.Sfx.DialogMessages.Password].Length > 0, ref key);
						WriteEncodedBoolean(binaryWriter, m_dialogMessages[Xceed.Zip.Sfx.DialogMessages.CreateFolder].Length > 0, ref key);
						WriteEncodedBoolean(binaryWriter, m_dialogMessages[Xceed.Zip.Sfx.DialogMessages.Success].Length > 0, ref key);
						WriteEncodedBoolean(binaryWriter, m_dialogMessages[Xceed.Zip.Sfx.DialogMessages.Error].Length > 0, ref key);
						WriteEncodedBoolean(binaryWriter, m_dialogMessages[Xceed.Zip.Sfx.DialogMessages.ErrorCreatingFolder].Length > 0, ref key);
						WriteEncodedBoolean(binaryWriter, m_dialogMessages[Xceed.Zip.Sfx.DialogMessages.Introduction].Length > 0, ref key);
						for (int i = 0; i <= 10; i++)
						{
							WriteEncodedString(binaryWriter, m_buttonCaptions[unchecked((Buttons)i)], m_writeUnicodeStrings, ref key);
						}
						WriteEncodedString(binaryWriter, m_defaultPassword, m_writeUnicodeStrings, ref key);
						WriteEncodedWord(binaryWriter, (ushort)m_existingFileBehavior, ref key);
						WriteEncodedString(binaryWriter, ReplaceNewVariables(m_defaultDestinationFolder), m_writeUnicodeStrings, ref key);
						WriteEncodedString(binaryWriter, string.Empty, m_writeUnicodeStrings, ref key);
						string text = string.Empty;
						foreach (FileCopy item in (IEnumerable)m_filesToCopy)
						{
							if (text.Length > 0)
							{
								text += "\r\n";
							}
							string text2 = text;
							text = text2 + "#C" + item.SourceFileName + "|" + item.DestinationFileName;
						}
						foreach (string item2 in (IEnumerable)m_dllsToRegister)
						{
							if (text.Length > 0)
							{
								text += "\r\n";
							}
							text = text + "#R" + item2;
						}
						foreach (RegistryKey item3 in (IEnumerable)m_registryKeys)
						{
							if (text.Length > 0)
							{
								text += "\r\n";
							}
							string text4 = "#K" + item3.KeyName + "|" + item3.ValueName + "|";
							if (item3.Value is int)
							{
								text4 += (int)item3.Value;
							}
							else if (item3.Value is byte[])
							{
								text4 += "X\"";
								byte[] array3 = (byte[])item3.Value;
								foreach (byte b in array3)
								{
									text4 = text4 + "\\x" + b.ToString("X3");
								}
								text4 += "\"";
							}
							else
							{
								text4 = text4 + "\"" + item3.Value.ToString() + "\"";
							}
							text += text4;
						}
						foreach (string item4 in (IEnumerable)m_executeAfter)
						{
							if (text.Length > 0)
							{
								text += "\r\n";
							}
							text += item4;
						}
						WriteEncodedString(binaryWriter, ReplaceNewVariables(text), m_writeUnicodeStrings, ref key);
						WriteEncodedString(binaryWriter, ReplaceNewVariables(m_dialogMessages[Xceed.Zip.Sfx.DialogMessages.DestinationFolder]), m_writeUnicodeStrings, ref key);
						WriteEncodedString(binaryWriter, ReplaceNewVariables(m_dialogMessages[Xceed.Zip.Sfx.DialogMessages.Password]), m_writeUnicodeStrings, ref key);
						WriteEncodedString(binaryWriter, ReplaceNewVariables(m_dialogMessages[Xceed.Zip.Sfx.DialogMessages.InsertLastDisk]), m_writeUnicodeStrings, ref key);
						WriteEncodedString(binaryWriter, ReplaceNewVariables(m_dialogMessages[Xceed.Zip.Sfx.DialogMessages.InsertDisk]), m_writeUnicodeStrings, ref key);
						WriteEncodedString(binaryWriter, ReplaceNewVariables(m_dialogMessages[Xceed.Zip.Sfx.DialogMessages.AbortUnzip]), m_writeUnicodeStrings, ref key);
						WriteEncodedString(binaryWriter, ReplaceNewVariables(m_dialogMessages[Xceed.Zip.Sfx.DialogMessages.CreateFolder]), m_writeUnicodeStrings, ref key);
						WriteEncodedString(binaryWriter, ReplaceNewVariables(m_dialogMessages[Xceed.Zip.Sfx.DialogMessages.Overwrite]), m_writeUnicodeStrings, ref key);
						WriteEncodedString(binaryWriter, ReplaceNewVariables(m_dialogMessages[Xceed.Zip.Sfx.DialogMessages.Progress]), m_writeUnicodeStrings, ref key);
						WriteEncodedString(binaryWriter, ReplaceNewVariables(m_dialogStrings[Xceed.Zip.Sfx.DialogStrings.ProgressBar]), m_writeUnicodeStrings, ref key);
						WriteEncodedString(binaryWriter, ReplaceNewVariables(m_dialogStrings[Xceed.Zip.Sfx.DialogStrings.Title]), m_writeUnicodeStrings, ref key);
						WriteEncodedString(binaryWriter, ReplaceNewVariables(m_dialogStrings[Xceed.Zip.Sfx.DialogStrings.CurrentFolder]), m_writeUnicodeStrings, ref key);
						WriteEncodedString(binaryWriter, "%s on '%c'", m_writeUnicodeStrings, ref key);
						WriteEncodedString(binaryWriter, "Entire Network", m_writeUnicodeStrings, ref key);
						if (Licenser.IsTrial)
						{
							WriteEncodedString(binaryWriter, "Xceed Zip Self-Extractor - Trial Version", m_writeUnicodeStrings, ref key);
						}
						else
						{
							WriteEncodedString(binaryWriter, "", m_writeUnicodeStrings, ref key);
						}
						WriteEncodedString(binaryWriter, ReplaceNewVariables(m_dialogMessages[Xceed.Zip.Sfx.DialogMessages.Success]), m_writeUnicodeStrings, ref key);
						WriteEncodedString(binaryWriter, ReplaceNewVariables(m_dialogMessages[Xceed.Zip.Sfx.DialogMessages.Error]), m_writeUnicodeStrings, ref key);
						WriteEncodedString(binaryWriter, ReplaceNewVariables(m_dialogMessages[Xceed.Zip.Sfx.DialogMessages.ErrorCreatingFolder]), m_writeUnicodeStrings, ref key);
						WriteEncodedString(binaryWriter, ReplaceNewVariables(m_dialogMessages[Xceed.Zip.Sfx.DialogMessages.Introduction]), m_writeUnicodeStrings, ref key);
						if (Licenser.IsTrial)
						{
							WriteEncodedString(binaryWriter, "This self-extracting zip file was created with the free trial version of Xceed Zip for .NET.  You cannot distribute files created with the free trial version of Xceed Zip for .NET.  Registering your copy of Xceed Zip for .NET will remove this screen.\n\nwww.xceedsoft.com", m_writeUnicodeStrings, ref key);
						}
						else
						{
							WriteEncodedString(binaryWriter, "", m_writeUnicodeStrings, ref key);
						}
						WriteEncodedString(binaryWriter, ReplaceNewVariables(m_programGroup.Title), m_writeUnicodeStrings, ref key);
						string text6 = string.Empty;
						foreach (ProgramGroupItem item5 in (IEnumerable)m_programGroup.Items)
						{
							if (text6.Length > 0)
							{
								text6 += "\r\n";
							}
							text6 = text6 + item5.FileToOpen + "|" + item5.Description;
						}
						WriteEncodedString(binaryWriter, ReplaceNewVariables(text6), m_writeUnicodeStrings, ref key);
						string text7 = string.Empty;
						foreach (ExtensionAssociation item6 in (IEnumerable)m_extensionsToAssociate)
						{
							if (text7.Length > 0)
							{
								text7 += "\r\n";
							}
							string text8 = text7;
							text7 = text8 + item6.Extension + "|" + item6.Application + "|" + item6.Description;
						}
						WriteEncodedString(binaryWriter, ReplaceNewVariables(text7), m_writeUnicodeStrings, ref key);
						WriteEncodedBoolean(binaryWriter, m_deleteUnzippedFiles, ref key);
						WriteEncodedString(binaryWriter, ReplaceNewVariables(m_dialogMessages[Xceed.Zip.Sfx.DialogMessages.License]), m_writeUnicodeStrings, ref key);
						WriteEncodedBoolean(binaryWriter, m_dialogMessages[Xceed.Zip.Sfx.DialogMessages.License].Length > 0, ref key);
						WriteEncodedString(binaryWriter, m_buttonCaptions[Buttons.Agree], m_writeUnicodeStrings, ref key);
						WriteEncodedString(binaryWriter, m_buttonCaptions[Buttons.Refuse], m_writeUnicodeStrings, ref key);
						WriteEncodedWord(binaryWriter, 0, ref key);
						WriteEncodedWord(binaryWriter, 0, ref key);
						WriteEncodedWord(binaryWriter, 0, ref key);
						WriteEncodedWord(binaryWriter, 0, ref key);
						WriteEncodedBoolean(binaryWriter, value: false, ref key);
						return;
					}
					finally
					{
						binaryWriter.Close();
					}
				}
				using Stream source = m_configuration.OpenRead();
				StreamCopy.Copy(source, stream, m_configuration.Size);
			}
		}

		protected override void OnChanged()
		{
			if (!m_keepConfig)
			{
				m_configuration = null;
			}
			base.OnChanged();
		}

		internal static bool IsXceedSfx(Stream prefix, long maxSize)
		{
			using Stream stream = new Xceed.Utils.Streams.WindowStream(new TransientStream(prefix), 0L, maxSize);
			StreamSearcher streamSearcher = new StreamSearcher(stream);
			return streamSearcher.Find(SfxSignature);
		}

		internal static int GetXceedSfxVersion(Stream prefix)
		{
			byte[] array = new byte[4];
			checked
			{
				int num;
				for (int i = 0; i < 4; i += num)
				{
					num = prefix.Read(array, i, array.Length - i);
					if (num == 0)
					{
						throw new EndOfStreamException("Premature end of stream reached.");
					}
				}
				return array[3] + (array[2] << 8) + (array[1] << 16) + (array[0] << 24);
			}
		}

		private void ReadConfiguration(Stream moduleStream, long size)
		{
			if (size > int.MaxValue)
			{
				throw new OverflowException("Cannot handle configurations bigger than 2 gigs.");
			}
			MemoryFile memoryFile = new MemoryFile();
			if (!memoryFile.Exists)
			{
				memoryFile.Create();
			}
			using (Stream dest = memoryFile.OpenWrite(overwrite: true))
			{
				StreamCopy.Copy(moduleStream, dest, size);
			}
			m_configuration = memoryFile;
			BinaryReader binaryReader = new BinaryReader(m_configuration.OpenRead());
			try
			{
				ReadConfigSignature(binaryReader, out var readUnicodeStrings);
				m_writeUnicodeStrings = readUnicodeStrings;
				ushort key = 15510;
				ReadEncodedBoolean(binaryReader, ref key);
				ReadEncodedBoolean(binaryReader, ref key);
				ReadEncodedBoolean(binaryReader, ref key);
				ReadEncodedBoolean(binaryReader, ref key);
				ReadEncodedBoolean(binaryReader, ref key);
				ReadEncodedBoolean(binaryReader, ref key);
				ReadEncodedBoolean(binaryReader, ref key);
				ReadEncodedBoolean(binaryReader, ref key);
				for (int i = 0; i <= 10; i = checked(i + 1))
				{
					m_buttonCaptions[(Buttons)i] = ReadEncodedString(binaryReader, 30, readUnicodeStrings, ref key);
				}
				ReadEncodedString(binaryReader, 80, readUnicodeStrings, ref key);
				ushort num = ReadEncodedWord(binaryReader, ref key);
				if (num < 0 || num > 3)
				{
					throw new InvalidSfxModuleException("Invalid SFX configuration.");
				}
				m_existingFileBehavior = (ExistingFileBehavior)num;
				string oldString = ReadEncodedString(binaryReader, 255, readUnicodeStrings, ref key);
				m_defaultDestinationFolder = ReplaceOldVariables(oldString, allowNumbers: false);
				m_executeAfter.Clear();
				m_filesToCopy.Clear();
				m_dllsToRegister.Clear();
				m_registryKeys.Clear();
				oldString = ReadEncodedString(binaryReader, 255, readUnicodeStrings, ref key);
				if (oldString.Length > 0)
				{
					m_executeAfter.Add(ReplaceOldVariables(oldString, allowNumbers: false));
				}
				oldString = ReadEncodedString(binaryReader, 2000, readUnicodeStrings, ref key);
				StringList stringList = new StringList(ReplaceOldVariables(oldString, allowNumbers: false));
				checked
				{
					foreach (string item in (IEnumerable)stringList)
					{
						if (item.Length <= 0)
						{
							continue;
						}
						bool flag = true;
						if (item[0] == '#' && item.Length > 1)
						{
							string text2 = item.Substring(2, item.Length - 2);
							if (text2 != null && text2.Length > 0)
							{
								switch (item[1])
								{
								case 'C':
								case 'c':
								{
									string[] array = text2.Split('|');
									if (array.Length != 2)
									{
										throw new InvalidSfxModuleException("Invalid SFX configuration.");
									}
									m_filesToCopy.Add(array[0], array[1]);
									flag = false;
									break;
								}
								case 'R':
								case 'r':
									m_dllsToRegister.Add(text2);
									flag = false;
									break;
								case 'K':
								case 'k':
								{
									string[] array = text2.Split('|');
									object obj = null;
									if (array.Length != 3)
									{
										throw new InvalidSfxModuleException("Invalid SFX configuration.");
									}
									if (array[2].Length == 0 || array[2].StartsWith("\""))
									{
										if (!array[2].EndsWith("\""))
										{
											throw new InvalidSfxModuleException("Invalid SFX configuration.");
										}
										obj = array[2].Substring(1, array[2].Length - 2);
									}
									else
									{
										try
										{
											obj = int.Parse(array[2]);
										}
										catch
										{
											throw new InvalidSfxModuleException("Invalid SFX configuration.");
										}
									}
									m_registryKeys.Add(array[0], array[1], obj);
									flag = false;
									break;
								}
								}
							}
						}
						if (flag)
						{
							m_executeAfter.Add(item);
						}
					}
					oldString = ReadEncodedString(binaryReader, 256, readUnicodeStrings, ref key);
					m_dialogMessages[Xceed.Zip.Sfx.DialogMessages.DestinationFolder] = ReplaceOldVariables(oldString, allowNumbers: false);
					oldString = ReadEncodedString(binaryReader, 256, readUnicodeStrings, ref key);
					m_dialogMessages[Xceed.Zip.Sfx.DialogMessages.Password] = ReplaceOldVariables(oldString, allowNumbers: false);
					oldString = ReadEncodedString(binaryReader, 256, readUnicodeStrings, ref key);
					m_dialogMessages[Xceed.Zip.Sfx.DialogMessages.InsertLastDisk] = ReplaceOldVariables(oldString, allowNumbers: false);
					oldString = ReadEncodedString(binaryReader, 256, readUnicodeStrings, ref key);
					m_dialogMessages[Xceed.Zip.Sfx.DialogMessages.InsertDisk] = ReplaceOldVariables(oldString, allowNumbers: true);
					oldString = ReadEncodedString(binaryReader, 256, readUnicodeStrings, ref key);
					m_dialogMessages[Xceed.Zip.Sfx.DialogMessages.AbortUnzip] = ReplaceOldVariables(oldString, allowNumbers: false);
					oldString = ReadEncodedString(binaryReader, 256, readUnicodeStrings, ref key);
					m_dialogMessages[Xceed.Zip.Sfx.DialogMessages.CreateFolder] = ReplaceOldVariables(oldString, allowNumbers: false);
					oldString = ReadEncodedString(binaryReader, 256, readUnicodeStrings, ref key);
					m_dialogMessages[Xceed.Zip.Sfx.DialogMessages.Overwrite] = ReplaceOldVariables(oldString, allowNumbers: false);
					oldString = ReadEncodedString(binaryReader, 256, readUnicodeStrings, ref key);
					m_dialogMessages[Xceed.Zip.Sfx.DialogMessages.Progress] = ReplaceOldVariables(oldString, allowNumbers: false);
					oldString = ReadEncodedString(binaryReader, 256, readUnicodeStrings, ref key);
					m_dialogStrings[Xceed.Zip.Sfx.DialogStrings.ProgressBar] = ReplaceOldVariables(oldString, allowNumbers: false);
					oldString = ReadEncodedString(binaryReader, 100, readUnicodeStrings, ref key);
					m_dialogStrings[Xceed.Zip.Sfx.DialogStrings.Title] = ReplaceOldVariables(oldString, allowNumbers: false);
					oldString = ReadEncodedString(binaryReader, 256, readUnicodeStrings, ref key);
					m_dialogStrings[Xceed.Zip.Sfx.DialogStrings.CurrentFolder] = ReplaceOldVariables(oldString, allowNumbers: false);
					ReadEncodedString(binaryReader, 256, readUnicodeStrings, ref key);
					ReadEncodedString(binaryReader, 256, readUnicodeStrings, ref key);
					m_trialTitle = ReadEncodedString(binaryReader, 100, readUnicodeStrings, ref key);
					oldString = ReadEncodedString(binaryReader, 2000, readUnicodeStrings, ref key);
					m_dialogMessages[Xceed.Zip.Sfx.DialogMessages.Success] = ReplaceOldVariables(oldString, allowNumbers: false);
					oldString = ReadEncodedString(binaryReader, 2000, readUnicodeStrings, ref key);
					m_dialogMessages[Xceed.Zip.Sfx.DialogMessages.Error] = ReplaceOldVariables(oldString, allowNumbers: true);
					oldString = ReadEncodedString(binaryReader, 2000, readUnicodeStrings, ref key);
					m_dialogMessages[Xceed.Zip.Sfx.DialogMessages.ErrorCreatingFolder] = ReplaceOldVariables(oldString, allowNumbers: false);
					oldString = ReadEncodedString(binaryReader, 2000, readUnicodeStrings, ref key);
					m_dialogMessages[Xceed.Zip.Sfx.DialogMessages.Introduction] = ReplaceOldVariables(oldString, allowNumbers: false);
					m_trialMessage = ReadEncodedString(binaryReader, 2000, readUnicodeStrings, ref key);
					m_programGroup.Items.Clear();
					oldString = ReadEncodedString(binaryReader, 100, readUnicodeStrings, ref key);
					m_programGroup.Title = ReplaceOldVariables(oldString, allowNumbers: false);
					oldString = ReadEncodedString(binaryReader, 2000, readUnicodeStrings, ref key);
					stringList = new StringList(ReplaceOldVariables(oldString, allowNumbers: false));
					foreach (string item2 in (IEnumerable)stringList)
					{
						string[] array2 = item2.Split('|');
						if (array2 == null || array2.Length != 2)
						{
							throw new InvalidSfxModuleException("Invalid SFX configuration.");
						}
						m_programGroup.Items.Add(array2[1], array2[0]);
					}
					m_extensionsToAssociate.Clear();
					oldString = ReadEncodedString(binaryReader, 2000, readUnicodeStrings, ref key);
					stringList = new StringList(ReplaceOldVariables(oldString, allowNumbers: false));
					foreach (string item3 in (IEnumerable)stringList)
					{
						string[] array3 = item3.Split('|');
						if (array3 == null || array3.Length != 3)
						{
							throw new InvalidSfxModuleException("Invalid SFX configuration.");
						}
						m_extensionsToAssociate.Add(array3[0], array3[1], array3[2]);
					}
					m_deleteUnzippedFiles = ReadEncodedBoolean(binaryReader, ref key);
					oldString = ReadEncodedString(binaryReader, 8000, readUnicodeStrings, ref key);
					m_dialogMessages[Xceed.Zip.Sfx.DialogMessages.License] = ReplaceOldVariables(oldString, allowNumbers: false);
					ReadEncodedBoolean(binaryReader, ref key);
					m_buttonCaptions[Buttons.Agree] = ReadEncodedString(binaryReader, 30, readUnicodeStrings, ref key);
					m_buttonCaptions[Buttons.Refuse] = ReadEncodedString(binaryReader, 30, readUnicodeStrings, ref key);
					ReadEncodedWord(binaryReader, ref key);
					ReadEncodedWord(binaryReader, ref key);
					ReadEncodedWord(binaryReader, ref key);
					ReadEncodedWord(binaryReader, ref key);
					ReadEncodedBoolean(binaryReader, ref key);
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				binaryReader.Close();
			}
		}

		private void MemberChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			OnChanged();
		}

		private static void ReadConfigSignature(BinaryReader reader, out bool readUnicodeStrings)
		{
			bool flag = true;
			bool flag2 = true;
			int num = ConfigSignature.Length;
			int num2 = ConfigSignatureV2.Length;
			for (int i = 0; i < num2 && (i != num || !flag || flag2); i = checked(i + 1))
			{
				byte b = reader.ReadByte();
				if (b != ConfigSignatureV2[i])
				{
					flag2 = false;
				}
				if (i < num && b != ConfigSignature[i])
				{
					flag = false;
				}
				if (!flag && !flag2)
				{
					throw new InvalidSfxModuleException("Invalid SFX configuration.");
				}
			}
			readUnicodeStrings = flag2;
		}

		private static void WriteConfigSignature(BinaryWriter writer, bool writeUnicodeStrings)
		{
			byte[] array = (writeUnicodeStrings ? ConfigSignatureV2 : ConfigSignature);
			writer.Write(array, 0, array.Length);
		}

		private static string ReadEncodedString(BinaryReader reader, int maxLength, bool readAsUnicode, ref ushort key)
		{
			ushort value = reader.ReadUInt16();
			value = ShiftAndEncode(value, 11, key);
			key = ShiftAndEncode(key, 1, 0);
			if (value > maxLength)
			{
				throw new InvalidSfxModuleException("Invalid SFX configuration.");
			}
			checked
			{
				int num = unchecked(checked(value + 1) / 2) * 2;
				byte[] array = new byte[num];
				for (int i = 0; i < num; i += 2)
				{
					ushort value2 = reader.ReadUInt16();
					value2 = ShiftAndEncode(value2, 9, key);
					array[i] = (byte)(value2 & 0xFF);
					array[i + 1] = (byte)(value2 >> 8);
					key = ShiftAndEncode(key, 2, 0);
				}
				if (!readAsUnicode)
				{
					return System.Text.Encoding.Default.GetString(array, 0, value);
				}
				return System.Text.Encoding.Unicode.GetString(array, 0, value);
			}
		}

		private static void WriteEncodedString(BinaryWriter writer, string value, bool writeAsUnicode, ref ushort key)
		{
			checked
			{
				ushort num = (writeAsUnicode ? ((ushort)(value.Length * 2)) : ((ushort)value.Length));
				writer.Write(EncodeAndShift(num, 5, key));
				key = ShiftAndEncode(key, 1, 0);
				byte[] array = (writeAsUnicode ? System.Text.Encoding.Unicode.GetBytes(value) : System.Text.Encoding.Default.GetBytes(value));
				for (int i = 0; i < num; i += 2)
				{
					ushort num2 = array[i];
					if (i + 1 < num)
					{
						num2 |= (ushort)(array[i + 1] << 8);
					}
					writer.Write(EncodeAndShift(num2, 7, key));
					key = ShiftAndEncode(key, 2, 0);
				}
			}
		}

		private static ushort ReadEncodedWord(BinaryReader reader, ref ushort key)
		{
			ushort value = reader.ReadUInt16();
			value = ShiftAndEncode(value, 11, key);
			key = ShiftAndEncode(key, 3, 0);
			return value;
		}

		private static void WriteEncodedWord(BinaryWriter writer, ushort value, ref ushort key)
		{
			writer.Write(EncodeAndShift(value, 5, key));
			key = ShiftAndEncode(key, 3, 0);
		}

		private static bool ReadEncodedBoolean(BinaryReader reader, ref ushort key)
		{
			ushort num = ReadEncodedWord(reader, ref key);
			return num != 0;
		}

		private static void WriteEncodedBoolean(BinaryWriter writer, bool value, ref ushort key)
		{
			WriteEncodedWord(writer, checked((ushort)(value ? 1 : 0)), ref key);
		}

		private static ushort ShiftAndEncode(ushort value, int leftShift, ushort key)
		{
			uint num = value;
			checked
			{
				num = ((num << leftShift) | (num >> 16 - leftShift)) & 0xFFFF;
				num ^= key;
				return (ushort)num;
			}
		}

		private static ushort EncodeAndShift(ushort value, int leftShift, ushort key)
		{
			uint num = value;
			num ^= key;
			checked
			{
				num = ((num << leftShift) | (num >> 16 - leftShift)) & 0xFFFF;
				return (ushort)num;
			}
		}

		private static string ReplaceOldVariables(string oldString, bool allowNumbers)
		{
			if (allowNumbers)
			{
				oldString = oldString.Replace("%e", "<ErrorNumber>");
				oldString = oldString.Replace("%n", "<DiskNumber>");
			}
			oldString = oldString.Replace("%f", "<DestinationFile>");
			oldString = oldString.Replace("%d", "<DestinationFolder>");
			oldString = oldString.Replace("%p", "<ProgramFilesFolder>");
			oldString = oldString.Replace("%r", "<RandomFolder>");
			oldString = oldString.Replace("%e", "<SfxFolder>");
			oldString = oldString.Replace("%v", "<SystemDrive>");
			oldString = oldString.Replace("%s", "<SystemFolder>");
			oldString = oldString.Replace("%t", "<TempFolder>");
			oldString = oldString.Replace("%w", "<WindowsFolder>");
			return oldString;
		}

		private static string ReplaceNewVariables(string oldString)
		{
			oldString = oldString.Replace("<ErrorNumber>", "%e");
			oldString = oldString.Replace("<DiskNumber>", "%n");
			oldString = oldString.Replace("<DestinationFile>", "%f");
			oldString = oldString.Replace("<DestinationFolder>", "%d");
			oldString = oldString.Replace("<ProgramFilesFolder>", "%p");
			oldString = oldString.Replace("<RandomFolder>", "%r");
			oldString = oldString.Replace("<SfxFolder>", "%e");
			oldString = oldString.Replace("<SystemDrive>", "%v");
			oldString = oldString.Replace("<SystemFolder>", "%s");
			oldString = oldString.Replace("<TempFolder>", "%t");
			oldString = oldString.Replace("<WindowsFolder>", "%w");
			return oldString;
		}
	}

namespace Xceed.Utils.Dates
{
	internal sealed class Dates
	{
		private const ushort DayMask = 31;

		private const ushort MonthMask = 480;

		private const ushort YearMask = 65024;

		private const ushort SecondsMask = 31;

		private const ushort MinutesMask = 2016;

		private const ushort HoursMask = 63488;

		public const ushort DosDateMinValue = 33;

		public const ushort DosTimeMinValue = 0;

		public static DateTime FromDosDateTime(ushort dosDate, ushort dosTime)
		{
			int day = dosDate & 0x1F;
			int month = (dosDate & 0x1E0) >> 5;
			checked
			{
				int year = ((dosDate & 0xFE00) >> 9) + 1980;
				int hour = (dosTime & 0xF800) >> 11;
				int minute = (dosTime & 0x7E0) >> 5;
				int second = (dosTime & 0x1F) * 2;
				return new DateTime(year, month, day, hour, minute, second);
			}
		}

		public static void ToDosDateTime(DateTime dateTime, out ushort dosDate, out ushort dosTime)
		{
			if (dateTime.Year < 1980)
			{
				dosDate = 33;
				dosTime = 0;
				return;
			}
			dosDate = 0;
			dosTime = 0;
			checked
			{
				dosDate |= (ushort)(dateTime.Year - 1980 << 9);
				dosDate |= (ushort)(dateTime.Month << 5);
				dosDate |= (ushort)dateTime.Day;
				dosTime |= (ushort)(dateTime.Hour << 11);
				dosTime |= (ushort)(dateTime.Minute << 5);
				dosTime |= (ushort)unchecked(dateTime.Second / 2);
			}
		}

		public static DateTime FromUnixDateTime(uint unixDateTime)
		{
			return DateTime.FromFileTime((long)unixDateTime * 10000000L + 116444736000000000L);
		}

		public static uint ToUnixDateTime(DateTime dateTime)
		{
			long num = dateTime.ToFileTime();
			if (num < 116444736000000000L)
			{
				return 0u;
			}
			num = checked(num - 116444736000000000L);
			num /= 10000000;
			return (uint)(num & 0xFFFFFFFFu);
		}
	}
}
namespace Xceed.Utils.Encryption
{
	internal class DeriveBytesRfc2898
	{
		private const int BlockSize = 20;

		private HMACSHA1 m_hmacSha1;

		private byte[] m_salt;

		private int m_iterations;

		private byte[] m_buffer;

		private int m_block = 1;

		private int m_endIndex;

		private int m_startIndex;

		internal static byte[] IntToBigEndianArray(int value)
		{
			byte[] bytes = BitConverter.GetBytes(value);
			if (BitConverter.IsLittleEndian)
			{
				byte b = bytes[3];
				bytes[3] = bytes[0];
				bytes[0] = b;
				b = bytes[2];
				bytes[2] = bytes[1];
				bytes[1] = b;
			}
			return bytes;
		}

		public DeriveBytesRfc2898(string password, byte[] salt)
			: this(password, salt, 1000)
		{
		}

		public DeriveBytesRfc2898(string password, byte[] salt, int iterations)
		{
			if (password == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("password");
			}
			if (password.Length == 0)
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("The password cannot be empty.");
			}
			if (salt == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("salt");
			}
			if (salt.Length == 0)
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("The salt cannot be empty.");
			}
			if (iterations < 10)
			{
				throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException("Iterations must be greater than 9.");
			}
			m_hmacSha1 = new HMACSHA1(System.Text.Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(password));
			m_salt = salt.Clone() as byte[];
			m_iterations = iterations;
			m_startIndex = (m_endIndex = 0);
		}

		public byte[] GetBytes(int count)
		{
			if (count <= 0)
			{
				throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException("count");
			}
			byte[] array = new byte[count];
			checked
			{
				int num;
				for (int i = 0; i < count; i += num)
				{
					num = count - i;
					while (num > m_endIndex - m_startIndex)
					{
						num = m_endIndex - m_startIndex;
						if (num == 0)
						{
							UpdateBuffer();
							num = count - i;
						}
					}
					Buffer.BlockCopy(m_buffer, m_startIndex, array, i, num);
					m_startIndex += num;
				}
				return array;
			}
		}

		private void UpdateBuffer()
		{
			checked
			{
				byte[] array = IntToBigEndianArray(m_block++);
				m_hmacSha1.TransformBlock(m_salt, 0, m_salt.Length, m_salt, 0);
				m_hmacSha1.TransformFinalBlock(array, 0, array.Length);
				byte[] array2 = m_hmacSha1.Hash;
				m_hmacSha1.Initialize();
				m_buffer = array2.Clone() as byte[];
				m_startIndex = 0;
				m_endIndex = 20;
				for (int i = 2; i <= m_iterations; i++)
				{
					array2 = m_hmacSha1.ComputeHash(array2);
					for (int j = 0; j < 20; j++)
					{
						m_buffer[j] = unchecked((byte)(m_buffer[j] ^ array2[j]));
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	internal class RijndaelCtrMode : IDisposable
	{
		private int m_blockSize = 16;

		private byte[] m_nonce;

		private byte[] m_encryptionBlock;

		private int m_position;

		private ICryptoTransform m_rijndaelTransform;

		public RijndaelCtrMode(byte[] key, int blockSize)
		{
			if (key == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("key");
			}
			m_rijndaelTransform = new RijndaelManaged
			{
				BlockSize = checked(blockSize * 8),
				Mode = CipherMode.ECB
			}.CreateEncryptor(key, null);
			m_blockSize = blockSize;
			m_encryptionBlock = new byte[m_blockSize];
			m_nonce = new byte[m_blockSize];
			m_position = blockSize;
		}

		public void Encrypt(byte[] data, int offset, int count)
		{
			checked
			{
				for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
				{
					if (m_position == m_blockSize)
					{
						UpdateNonce();
						m_position = 0;
					}
					ref byte reference = ref data[i];
					reference ^= m_encryptionBlock[m_position++];
				}
			}
		}

		private void UpdateNonce()
		{
			checked
			{
				for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++)
				{
					if (m_nonce[i] < byte.MaxValue)
					{
						ref byte reference = ref m_nonce[i];
						reference = (byte)unchecked((int)checked(unchecked((uint)reference) + 1u));
						break;
					}
					m_nonce[i] = 0;
				}
				m_rijndaelTransform.TransformBlock(m_nonce, 0, m_nonce.Length, m_encryptionBlock, 0);
			}
		}

		public void Dispose()
		{
			if (m_rijndaelTransform != null)
			{
				m_rijndaelTransform.Dispose();
			}
		}
	}
}

namespace Xceed.Utils.Exceptions
{
	internal class ZCryptException : Exception
	{
		public ZCryptException(string message)
			: base(message)
		{
		}
	}
	internal class ExceptionFilter
	{
		public static bool IsPublicException(Exception exception)
		{
			if (!(exception is ExecutionEngineException) && !(exception is LicenseException) && !(exception is OutOfMemoryException) && !(exception is SecurityException) && !(exception is UnauthorizedAccessException) && !(exception is ArgumentException))
			{
				return exception is TargetInvocationException;
			}
			return true;
		}
	}
	internal class ThrowException
	{
		private ThrowException()
		{
		}

		public static void ThrowArgumentException(string message, string paramName, Exception innerExcept)
		{
			throw new ArgumentException(message, paramName, innerExcept);
		}

		public static void ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException(string paramName, object value, string message)
		{
			throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(paramName, value, message);
		}

		public static void ThrowLicenseException(Type type, object instance, string message)
		{
			throw new LicenseException(type, instance, message);
		}
	}
}

	internal enum LicenseOrigin
	{

	internal class XceedLicenseManager
	{
		private const int RawKeyStringLen = 19;

		private const string LicKeyVersion = "A";

		private const byte SiteLicense = byte.MaxValue;

		private const uint BitCount = 65u;

		private const int BaseDateYear = 2001;

		private const int BaseDateMonth = 1;

		private const int BaseDateDay = 1;

		private const int NbLookupChar = 32;

		private const uint AdlerNMax = 5552u;

		private const uint AdlerBase = 65521u;

		private const string AlphaNumLookup = "ABJCKTDL4UEMW71FNX52YGP98Z63HRS0";

		private Xceed.Utils.Licensing.LicenseProduct m_Product;

		private byte m_VerMajor;

		private byte m_VerMinor;

		private Xceed.Utils.Licensing.LicenseOrigin m_Originator;

		private ushort m_UniqueSeed;

		private byte m_NbLicenses;

		private string m_Key;

		private DateTime m_Date;

		private static readonly string[] ProductIds = new string[38]
		{
			"", "ZIP", "SFX", "BKP", "WSL", "FTP", "SCO", "BEN", "CRY", "FTB",
			"ZIN", "ABZ", "GRD", "SCN", "ZIC", "SCC", "SUI", "SUN", "FTN", "FTC",
			"CHT", "DWN", "CHW", "IVN", "RDY", "EDN", "ZIL", "TAN", "DGF", "DGP",
			"WAN", "SYN", "ZIX", "ZII", "SFN", "ZRT", "ZRC", null
		};

		private static byte[] NbDaysBits = new byte[15]
		{
			2, 7, 12, 17, 22, 26, 31, 37, 42, 47,
			51, 55, 59, 62, 255
		};

		private static byte[] UniqueSeedBits = new byte[17]
		{
			64, 60, 56, 52, 48, 44, 39, 35, 32, 28,
			23, 19, 14, 10, 5, 1, 255
		};

		private static byte[] ProductCodeBits = new byte[7] { 3, 16, 29, 41, 53, 61, 255 };

		private static byte[] ProductVersionBits = new byte[8] { 4, 15, 25, 34, 43, 50, 58, 255 };

		private static byte[] NbLicensesBits = new byte[9] { 6, 13, 20, 30, 38, 40, 46, 57, 255 };

		private static byte[] OriginCodeBits = new byte[7] { 8, 11, 21, 24, 33, 49, 255 };

		private static byte[] ChecksumBits = new byte[9] { 0, 9, 18, 27, 36, 45, 54, 63, 255 };

		public Xceed.Utils.Licensing.LicenseProduct Product => m_Product;

		public string ProductCodeName => ProductIds[(int)m_Product];

		public byte VerMajor => m_VerMajor;

		public byte VerMinor => m_VerMinor;

		public Xceed.Utils.Licensing.LicenseOrigin Originator => m_Originator;

		public ushort UniqueSeed => m_UniqueSeed;

		public byte NbLicenses => m_NbLicenses;

		public bool IsSiteLicense => m_NbLicenses == byte.MaxValue;

		public DateTime Date => m_Date;

		public XceedLicenseManager()
		{
			m_Product = Xceed.Utils.Licensing.LicenseProduct.InvalidProduct;
			m_Originator = Xceed.Utils.Licensing.LicenseOrigin.InvalidOrigin;
			Reset();
		}

		public static string GetProductCodeName(Xceed.Utils.Licensing.LicenseProduct product)
		{
			return ProductIds[(int)product];
		}

		public void AnalyzeLicense(string key)
		{
			Reset();
			CopyKeyNoDashes(key);
			if (m_Key.Length != 19)
			{
				throw new Xceed.Utils.Licensing.XceedLicenseManagerException(Xceed.Utils.Licensing.LicenseErrorCode.InvalidKeyLength);
			}
			if (!m_Key.EndsWith("A"))
			{
				throw new Xceed.Utils.Licensing.XceedLicenseManagerException(Xceed.Utils.Licensing.LicenseErrorCode.UnsupportedKey);
			}
			DecodeEncodedPart();
			if (m_Product < Xceed.Utils.Licensing.LicenseProduct.XceedZip || m_Product > Xceed.Utils.Licensing.LicenseProduct.XceedRealTimeZipNETCF)
			{
				throw new Xceed.Utils.Licensing.XceedLicenseManagerException(Xceed.Utils.Licensing.LicenseErrorCode.InvalidProductCode);
			}
			if (m_Originator < Xceed.Utils.Licensing.LicenseOrigin.TrialVersion || m_Originator > Xceed.Utils.Licensing.LicenseOrigin.CRM)
			{
				throw new Xceed.Utils.Licensing.XceedLicenseManagerException(Xceed.Utils.Licensing.LicenseErrorCode.InvalidOriginCode);
			}
			if (m_VerMajor > 9 || m_VerMinor > 9)
			{
				throw new Xceed.Utils.Licensing.XceedLicenseManagerException(Xceed.Utils.Licensing.LicenseErrorCode.InvalidVersion);
			}
			DecodeReadablePart();
		}

		private void CopyKeyNoDashes(string key)
		{
			m_Key = "";
			if (key == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < key.Length; i = checked(i + 1))
			{
				if (key[i] != '-')
				{
					m_Key += key[i];
				}
			}
		}

		private void DecodeEncodedPart()
		{
			uint num = 8u;
			num = checked(num + 1);
			byte[] bits = new byte[num];
			DecodeAlphaNumString(bits, num);
			uint num2 = MapBitsToNumber(bits, num, ChecksumBits);
			MapNumberToBits(bits, num, 0u, ChecksumBits);
			if (num2 != CalculateChecksum(bits, num))
			{
				throw new Xceed.Utils.Licensing.XceedLicenseManagerException(Xceed.Utils.Licensing.LicenseErrorCode.ChecksumFailed);
			}
			uint num3 = MapBitsToNumber(bits, num, NbDaysBits);
			m_Date = new DateTime(2001, 1, 1).AddDays(num3);
			num3 = MapBitsToNumber(bits, num, UniqueSeedBits);
			m_UniqueSeed = (ushort)num3;
			num3 = MapBitsToNumber(bits, num, ProductCodeBits);
			m_Product = (Xceed.Utils.Licensing.LicenseProduct)checked((int)num3);
			num3 = MapBitsToNumber(bits, num, ProductVersionBits);
			m_VerMajor = (byte)(num3 / 10);
			m_VerMinor = (byte)(num3 % 10);
			num3 = MapBitsToNumber(bits, num, NbLicensesBits);
			m_NbLicenses = (byte)num3;
			num3 = MapBitsToNumber(bits, num, OriginCodeBits);
			m_Originator = (Xceed.Utils.Licensing.LicenseOrigin)checked((int)num3);
		}

		private void DecodeReadablePart()
		{
			if (!m_Key.StartsWith(ProductIds[(int)m_Product]))
			{
				throw new Xceed.Utils.Licensing.XceedLicenseManagerException(Xceed.Utils.Licensing.LicenseErrorCode.ProductMismatch);
			}
			if (Convert.ToByte(m_Key.Substring(3, 1), CultureInfo.InvariantCulture) != m_VerMajor || Convert.ToByte(m_Key.Substring(4, 1), CultureInfo.InvariantCulture) != m_VerMinor)
			{
				throw new Xceed.Utils.Licensing.XceedLicenseManagerException(Xceed.Utils.Licensing.LicenseErrorCode.VersionMismatch);
			}
		}

		private void DecodeAlphaNumString(byte[] bits, uint bitsLen)
		{
			ushort num = 11;
			uint num2 = 0u;
			uint num3 = 0u;
			uint num4 = 5u;
			checked
			{
				while (num2 < bitsLen && num3 < 65)
				{
					ushort alphaNumValue = GetAlphaNumValue(m_Key[(int)num4++]);
					alphaNumValue = (ushort)(alphaNumValue << unchecked((int)num));
					ref byte reference = ref bits[num2];
					reference = (byte)(reference | unchecked((byte)((alphaNumValue & 0xFF00) >> 8)));
					ref byte reference2 = ref bits[num2 + 1];
					reference2 |= (byte)(alphaNumValue & 0xFF);
					if (num < 8)
					{
						num += 3;
						num2++;
					}
					else
					{
						num -= 5;
					}
					num3 += 5;
				}
			}
		}

		private static uint MapBitsToNumber(byte[] bits, uint bitsLen, byte[] bitMapping)
		{
			if (bits == null || bitsLen == 0)
			{
				throw new Xceed.Utils.Licensing.XceedLicenseManagerException();
			}
			if (bitMapping == null)
			{
				throw new Xceed.Utils.Licensing.XceedLicenseManagerException();
			}
			uint num = 0u;
			for (uint num2 = 0u; bitMapping[num2] != byte.MaxValue; num2 = checked(num2 + 1))
			{
				byte b = bitMapping[num2];
				byte b2 = (byte)(1 << 7 - b % 8);
				if ((bits[b / 8] & b2) != 0)
				{
					num |= (uint)(1 << (int)num2);
				}
			}
			return num;
		}

		private static void MapNumberToBits(byte[] bits, uint bitsLen, uint number, byte[] bitMapping)
		{
			if (bits == null || bitsLen == 0)
			{
				throw new Xceed.Utils.Licensing.XceedLicenseManagerException();
			}
			if (bitMapping == null)
			{
				throw new Xceed.Utils.Licensing.XceedLicenseManagerException();
			}
			int num = 0;
			byte b = 0;
			checked
			{
				for (num = 0; bitMapping[num] != byte.MaxValue; num++)
				{
					if (bitMapping[num] > b)
					{
						b = bitMapping[num];
					}
				}
				if (number >= (uint)(1 << num))
				{
					throw new Xceed.Utils.Licensing.XceedLicenseManagerException();
				}
				if (bitsLen * 8 <= b)
				{
					throw new Xceed.Utils.Licensing.XceedLicenseManagerException();
				}
				for (int i = 0; i < num; i++)
				{
					bool flag = ((long)number & (1 << i)) != 0;
					byte b2 = bitMapping[i];
					unchecked
					{
						byte b3 = (byte)(1 << 7 - b2 % 8);
						if (flag)
						{
							ref byte reference = ref bits[b2 / 8];
							reference = checked((byte)(reference | b3));
						}
						else
						{
							bits[b2 / 8] &= (byte)(~b3);
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}

		private static ushort GetAlphaNumValue(char alphaNum)
		{
			checked
			{
				for (ushort num = 0; num < 32; num = (ushort)unchecked((int)checked(unchecked((uint)num) + 1u)))
				{
					if ("ABJCKTDL4UEMW71FNX52YGP98Z63HRS0"[num] == alphaNum)
					{
						return num;
					}
				}
				return ushort.MaxValue;
			}
		}

		private void Reset()
		{
			m_Key = "";
			m_Product = Xceed.Utils.Licensing.LicenseProduct.InvalidProduct;
			m_VerMajor = 0;
			m_VerMinor = 0;
			m_Originator = Xceed.Utils.Licensing.LicenseOrigin.InvalidOrigin;
			m_NbLicenses = 0;
			m_UniqueSeed = 0;
			m_Date = DateTime.Now;
		}

		private byte CalculateChecksum(byte[] bits, uint bitsLen)
		{
			uint adler = Adler32(0u, m_Key.ToCharArray(), 5u);
			adler = Adler32(adler, bits, bitsLen);
			checked
			{
				ushort num = (ushort)(((adler & 0xFFFF0000u) >> 16) ^ (adler & 0xFFFF));
				return (byte)(((num & 0xFF00) >> 8) ^ (num & 0xFF));
			}
		}

		private static uint Adler32(uint adler, Array buffer, uint bufLen)
		{
			if (buffer == null)
			{
				return 1u;
			}
			uint num = adler & 0xFFFF;
			uint num2 = (adler >> 16) & 0xFFFF;
			int num3 = 0;
			while (bufLen != 0)
			{
				uint num4 = ((bufLen < 5552) ? bufLen : 5552u);
				checked
				{
					bufLen -= num4;
					while (true)
					{
						switch (num4)
						{
						default:
						{
							int num5 = 0;
							while (num5 < 16)
							{
								num += Convert.ToUInt32(buffer.GetValue(num3), CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
								num2 += num;
								num5++;
								num3++;
							}
							num4 -= 16;
							continue;
						}
						case 1u:
						case 2u:
						case 3u:
						case 4u:
						case 5u:
						case 6u:
						case 7u:
						case 8u:
						case 9u:
						case 10u:
						case 11u:
						case 12u:
						case 13u:
						case 14u:
						case 15u:
							do
							{
								num += Convert.ToUInt32(buffer.GetValue(num3), CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
								num3++;
								num2 += num;
							}
							while (--num4 != 0);
							break;
						case 0u:
							break;
						}
						break;
					}
				}
				num %= 65521;
				num2 %= 65521;
			}
			return (num2 << 16) | num;
		}
	}
	internal class XceedLicenseManagerException : ApplicationException
	{
		public XceedLicenseManagerException()
			: base("Error reading the license key")
		{
		}

		public XceedLicenseManagerException(string message)
			: base(message)
		{
		}

		public XceedLicenseManagerException(string message, Exception inner)
			: base(message, inner)
		{
		}

		public XceedLicenseManagerException(Xceed.Utils.Licensing.LicenseErrorCode errorCode)
			: base(errorCode.ToString())
		{
		}
	}
}
