		public override bool CanRead
		{
			get
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				return m_innerStream.CanRead;
			}
		}

		public override bool CanSeek
		{
			get
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				return false;
			}
		}

		public override bool CanWrite
		{
			get
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				return m_innerStream.CanWrite;
			}
		}

		public Stream InnerStream => m_innerStream;

		public override long Length
		{
			get
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				return m_innerStream.Length;
			}
		}

		public override long Position
		{
			get
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				return m_innerStream.Position;
			}
			set
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				throw new NotSupportedException("A ChecksumStream object is not seekable.");
			}
		}

		public int Checksum => (int)m_checksum;

		public int ExpectedChecksum
		{
			get
			{
				return (int)m_expectedChecksum;
			}
			set
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				m_expectedChecksum = (uint)value;
			}
		}

		public ChecksumStream(Stream inner, ChecksumType checksumType)
			: this(inner, checksumType, 0)
		{
		}

		public ChecksumStream(Stream inner, ChecksumType checksumType, int expectedChecksum)
		{
			if (inner == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("inner");
			}
			if (!Enum.IsDefined(typeof(ChecksumType), checksumType))
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("An unknown checksum type was specified.", "checksumType");
			}
			m_innerStream = inner;
			m_checksumType = checksumType;
			m_expectedChecksum = (uint)expectedChecksum;
		}

		public override void Flush()
		{
			CheckIfDisposed();
			m_innerStream.Flush();
		}

		public override long Seek(long offset, SeekOrigin origin)
		{
			CheckIfDisposed();
			throw new NotSupportedException("A ChecksumStream object is not seekable.");
		}

		public override void SetLength(long value)
		{
			CheckIfDisposed();
			m_innerStream.SetLength(value);
		}

		public override int Read(byte[] buffer, int offset, int count)
		{
			CheckIfDisposed();
			if (!CanRead)
			{
				throw new NotSupportedException("The ChecksumStream object does not support reading.");
			}
			if (buffer == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("buffer");
			}
			if (offset < 0)
			{
				Xceed.Utils.Exceptions.ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("offset", offset, "The offset cannot be less than zero.");
			}
			if (count < 0)
			{
				Xceed.Utils.Exceptions.ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("count", count, "The count cannot be less than zero.");
			}
			checked
			{
				if (count > buffer.Length - offset)
				{
					Xceed.Utils.Exceptions.ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("count", count, "The count exceeds the buffer's remaining length after offset.");
				}
				int num = m_innerStream.Read(buffer, offset, count);
				if (num > 0)
				{
					if (m_checksumType == ChecksumType.CRC32)
					{
						m_checksum = CalculateCrc32(buffer, offset, num, m_checksum);
					}
					else
					{
						m_checksum = CalculateAdler32(m_checksum, buffer, (uint)offset, (uint)num);
					}
				}
				else if (count != 0 && m_expectedChecksum != 0 && m_checksum != m_expectedChecksum)
				{
					throw new IOException("The checksum for the data read from the stream does not match the expected checksum.");
				}
				return num;
			}
		}

		public override void Write(byte[] buffer, int offset, int count)
		{
			CheckIfDisposed();
			if (!CanWrite)
			{
				throw new NotSupportedException("The ChecksumStream object does not support writing.");
			}
			if (buffer == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("buffer");
			}
			if (offset < 0)
			{
				Xceed.Utils.Exceptions.ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("offset", offset, "The offset cannot be less than zero.");
			}
			if (count < 0)
			{
				Xceed.Utils.Exceptions.ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("count", count, "The count cannot be less than zero.");
			}
			checked
			{
				if (count > buffer.Length - offset)
				{
					Xceed.Utils.Exceptions.ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("count", count, "The count exceeds the buffer's remaining length after offset.");
				}
				if (count > 0)
				{
					if (m_checksumType == ChecksumType.CRC32)
					{
						m_checksum = CalculateCrc32(buffer, offset, count, m_checksum);
					}
					else
					{
						m_checksum = CalculateAdler32(m_checksum, buffer, (uint)offset, (uint)count);
					}
				}
				m_innerStream.Write(buffer, offset, count);
			}
		}

		internal static uint CalculateCrc32(byte[] buffer, int offset, int count, uint previousCrc)
		{
			if (count == 0)
			{
				return previousCrc;
			}
			previousCrc ^= 0xFFFFFFFFu;
			checked
			{
				offset--;
				while (--count >= 0)
				{
					previousCrc = crcTable[(previousCrc ^ buffer[++offset]) & 0xFF] ^ (previousCrc >> 8);
				}
				return previousCrc ^ 0xFFFFFFFFu;
			}
		}

		public static int CalculateCrc32(byte[] buffer, int offset, int count, int previousCrc)
		{
			if (buffer == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("buffer");
			}
			if (offset < 0)
			{
				Xceed.Utils.Exceptions.ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("offset", offset, "The offset cannot be less than zero.");
			}
			if (count < 0)
			{
				Xceed.Utils.Exceptions.ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("count", count, "The count cannot be less than zero.");
			}
			if (count > checked(buffer.Length - offset))
			{
				Xceed.Utils.Exceptions.ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("count", count, "The count exceeds the buffer's remaining length after offset.");
			}
			return (int)CalculateCrc32(buffer, offset, count, (uint)previousCrc);
		}

		internal static uint UniqueCrc32(ulong crc, ulong input)
		{
			return checked((uint)(crcTable[(crc ^ input) & 0xFF] ^ (crc >> 8)));
		}

		internal static uint CalculateAdler32(uint adler, byte[] buf, uint startindex, uint len)
		{
			uint num = adler & 0xFFFF;
			uint num2 = (adler >> 16) & 0xFFFF;
			if (buf == null)
			{
				return 1u;
			}
			checked
			{
				while (len != 0)
				{
					uint num3 = ((len < 5552) ? len : 5552u);
					len -= num3;
					while (true)
					{
						switch (num3)
						{
						default:
							num += buf[startindex++];
							num2 += num;
							num += buf[startindex++];
							num2 += num;
							num += buf[startindex++];
							num2 += num;
							num += buf[startindex++];
							num2 += num;
							num += buf[startindex++];
							num2 += num;
							num += buf[startindex++];
							num2 += num;
							num += buf[startindex++];
							num2 += num;
							num += buf[startindex++];
							num2 += num;
							num += buf[startindex++];
							num2 += num;
							num += buf[startindex++];
							num2 += num;
							num += buf[startindex++];
							num2 += num;
							num += buf[startindex++];
							num2 += num;
							num += buf[startindex++];
							num2 += num;
							num += buf[startindex++];
							num2 += num;
							num += buf[startindex++];
							num2 += num;
							num += buf[startindex++];
							num2 += num;
							num3 -= 16;
							continue;
						case 1u:
						case 2u:
						case 3u:
						case 4u:
						case 5u:
						case 6u:
						case 7u:
						case 8u:
						case 9u:
						case 10u:
						case 11u:
						case 12u:
						case 13u:
						case 14u:
						case 15u:
							do
							{
								num += buf[startindex++];
								num2 += num;
							}
							while (--num3 != 0);
							goto case 0u;
						case 0u:
							unchecked
							{
								num %= 65521;
								num2 %= 65521;
								break;
							}
						}
						break;
					}
				}
				return (num2 << 16) | num;
			}
		}

		public static int CalculateAdler32(byte[] buffer, int offset, int count, int previousAdler)
		{
			if (buffer == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("buffer");
			}
			if (offset < 0)
			{
				Xceed.Utils.Exceptions.ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("offset", offset, "The offset cannot be less than zero.");
			}
			if (count < 0)
			{
				Xceed.Utils.Exceptions.ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("count", count, "The count cannot be less than zero.");
			}
			checked
			{
				if (count > buffer.Length - offset)
				{
					Xceed.Utils.Exceptions.ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("count", count, "The count exceeds the buffer's remaining length after offset.");
				}
				return (int)CalculateAdler32((uint)previousAdler, buffer, (uint)offset, (uint)count);
			}
		}

		protected override void Dispose(bool disposing)
		{
			if (!m_disposed)
			{
				if (disposing)
				{
					m_innerStream.Close();
				}
				m_innerStream = null;
				m_disposed = true;
			}
		}

		private void CheckIfDisposed()
		{
			if (m_disposed)
			{
				throw new ObjectDisposedException("ChecksumStream", "Cannot access a stream once it has been closed.");
			}
		}

		private ChecksumStream()
		{
		}

		~ChecksumStream()
		{
			Dispose(disposing: false);
		}
	}
	internal enum ChecksumType
	{
		Adler32,
		CRC32
	}
	internal class CombinedStream : Stream
	{
		private bool m_disposed;

		private Stream m_innerStream;

		private byte[] m_leadingBytes;

		private long m_initialPosition;

		private int m_leadingBytesRead;

		public override bool CanRead
		{
			get
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				return m_innerStream.CanRead;
			}
		}

		public override bool CanSeek
		{
			get
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				return m_innerStream.CanSeek;
			}
		}

		public override bool CanWrite => false;

		public override long Length
		{
			get
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				if (m_innerStream.Length < m_initialPosition)
				{
					throw new NotSupportedException("The length of the inner stream must be greater than its initial position.");
				}
				return checked(m_leadingBytes.Length + m_innerStream.Length - m_initialPosition);
			}
		}

		public override long Position
		{
			get
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				long position = m_innerStream.Position;
				if (position < m_initialPosition)
				{
					throw new NotSupportedException("The inner stream's current position must be greater than its initial position.");
				}
				if (m_leadingBytesRead != m_leadingBytes.Length)
				{
					return m_leadingBytesRead;
				}
				return checked(m_leadingBytesRead + position - m_initialPosition);
			}
			set
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				if (!CanSeek)
				{
					throw new NotSupportedException("The inner stream is not seekable");
				}
				if (value < 0)
				{
					throw new NotSupportedException("Cannot seek before the beginning of the stream.");
				}
				checked
				{
					if (value < m_leadingBytes.Length)
					{
						m_leadingBytesRead = (int)value;
						return;
					}
					m_leadingBytesRead = m_leadingBytes.Length;
					m_innerStream.Position = value - m_leadingBytes.Length + m_initialPosition;
				}
			}
		}

		public CombinedStream(byte[] buffer, Stream inner)
		{
			if (buffer == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("buffer");
			}
			if (inner == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("inner");
			}
			m_innerStream = inner;
			try
			{
				m_initialPosition = inner.Position;
			}
			catch
			{
			}
			m_leadingBytes = buffer;
		}

		public override void Flush()
		{
			CheckIfDisposed();
			m_innerStream.Flush();
		}

		public override long Seek(long offset, SeekOrigin origin)
		{
			CheckIfDisposed();
			if (!CanSeek)
			{
				throw new NotSupportedException("The inner stream is not seekable");
			}
			checked
			{
				switch (origin)
				{
				case SeekOrigin.Begin:
					Position = offset;
					break;
				case SeekOrigin.End:
					Position = Length + offset;
					break;
				case SeekOrigin.Current:
					Position += offset;
					break;
				}
				return Position;
			}
		}

		public override void SetLength(long value)
		{
			CheckIfDisposed();
			if (value < 0)
			{
				Xceed.Utils.Exceptions.ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("value", value, "The length of the stream must be greater than zero.");
			}
			checked
			{
				if (value < m_leadingBytes.Length)
				{
					m_innerStream.SetLength(m_initialPosition);
					byte[] array = new byte[value];
					if (value > 0)
					{
						Array.Copy(m_leadingBytes, 0, array, 0, (int)value);
					}
					m_leadingBytes = array;
					if (m_leadingBytesRead > value)
					{
						m_leadingBytesRead = (int)value;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					m_innerStream.SetLength(value - m_leadingBytes.Length + m_initialPosition);
				}
			}
		}

		public override int Read(byte[] buffer, int offset, int count)
		{
			CheckIfDisposed();
			if (!CanRead)
			{
				throw new NotSupportedException("The inner stream does not support reading.");
			}
			if (buffer == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("buffer");
			}
			if (offset < 0)
			{
				Xceed.Utils.Exceptions.ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("offset", offset, "The offset must be greater than zero.");
			}
			if (count < 0)
			{
				Xceed.Utils.Exceptions.ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("count", count, "Count must be greater than zero.");
			}
			checked
			{
				if (count > buffer.Length - offset)
				{
					Xceed.Utils.Exceptions.ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("count", count, "Count must be less than the buffer's remaining length after the specified offset.");
				}
				if (m_leadingBytesRead < m_leadingBytes.Length)
				{
					if (m_leadingBytes.Length - m_leadingBytesRead < count)
					{
						count = m_leadingBytes.Length - m_leadingBytesRead;
					}
					Array.Copy(m_leadingBytes, m_leadingBytesRead, buffer, offset, count);
					m_leadingBytesRead += count;
					return count;
				}
				return m_innerStream.Read(buffer, offset, count);
			}
		}

		public override void Write(byte[] buffer, int offset, int count)
		{
			throw new NotSupportedException("A CombinedStream object does not support writing.");
		}

		protected override void Dispose(bool disposing)
		{
			if (!m_disposed)
			{
				try
				{
					Flush();
				}
				catch
				{
				}
				if (disposing)
				{
					m_innerStream.Close();
				}
				m_innerStream = null;
				m_leadingBytes = null;
				m_disposed = true;
			}
		}

		private void CheckIfDisposed()
		{
			if (m_disposed)
			{
				throw new ObjectDisposedException("CombinedStream", "Cannot access a stream once it has been closed.");
			}
		}

		private CombinedStream()
		{
		}

		~CombinedStream()
		{
			Dispose(disposing: false);
		}
	}
	internal class HeaderFooterStream : Stream
	{
		private bool m_disposed;

		private Stream m_innerStream;

		private int m_reservedFooter;

		private byte[] m_bufferedFooter;

		private int m_bufferedFooterSize;

		private bool m_transient;

		private bool m_endOfStream;

		public override bool CanRead
		{
			get
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				return m_innerStream.CanRead;
			}
		}

		public override bool CanSeek
		{
			get
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				return m_innerStream.CanSeek;
			}
		}

		public override bool CanWrite
		{
			get
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				return m_innerStream.CanWrite;
			}
		}

		public override long Length
		{
			get
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				return m_innerStream.Length;
			}
		}

		public override long Position
		{
			get
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				return m_innerStream.Position;
			}
			set
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				m_innerStream.Position = value;
			}
		}

		public Stream InnerStream
		{
			get
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				return m_innerStream;
			}
		}

		public int ReservedFooter
		{
			get
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				return m_reservedFooter;
			}
			set
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				if (value < 0)
				{
					Xceed.Utils.Exceptions.ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("ReservedFooter", value, "The reserved footer size must be greater than zero.");
				}
				if (m_bufferedFooterSize != 0)
				{
					throw new NotSupportedException("An attempt was made to set the reserved footer twice.");
				}
				m_reservedFooter = value;
				m_bufferedFooter = new byte[value];
			}
		}

		public bool Transient
		{
			get
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				return m_transient;
			}
			set
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				m_transient = value;
			}
		}

		public HeaderFooterStream(Stream innerStream)
		{
			if (innerStream == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("innerStream");
			}
			m_innerStream = innerStream;
		}

		public override void Flush()
		{
			CheckIfDisposed();
			m_innerStream.Flush();
		}

		public override long Seek(long offset, SeekOrigin origin)
		{
			CheckIfDisposed();
			return m_innerStream.Seek(offset, origin);
		}

		public override void SetLength(long value)
		{
			CheckIfDisposed();
			m_innerStream.SetLength(value);
		}

		public override int Read(byte[] buffer, int offset, int count)
		{
			CheckIfDisposed();
			if (m_endOfStream)
			{
				return 0;
			}
			int num = 0;
			checked
			{
				if (m_reservedFooter == 0)
				{
					num = m_innerStream.Read(buffer, offset, count);
				}
				else
				{
					byte[] array = buffer;
					int num2 = offset;
					int num3 = count;
					bool flag = false;
					if (count <= m_reservedFooter)
					{
						array = new byte[m_reservedFooter + count];
						num2 = 0;
						num3 = m_reservedFooter + count;
						flag = true;
					}
					if (m_bufferedFooterSize > 0)
					{
						Array.Copy(m_bufferedFooter, 0, array, num2, m_bufferedFooterSize);
						num2 += m_bufferedFooterSize;
						num3 -= m_bufferedFooterSize;
					}
					num = m_bufferedFooterSize;
					while (num3 > 0)
					{
						int num4 = m_innerStream.Read(array, num2, num3);
						if (num4 == 0)
						{
							break;
						}
						num += num4;
						num2 += num4;
						num3 -= num4;
					}
					if (num < m_reservedFooter)
					{
						throw new IOException("There is not enough data in the stream.");
					}
					Array.Copy(array, num2 - m_reservedFooter, m_bufferedFooter, 0, m_reservedFooter);
					m_bufferedFooterSize = m_reservedFooter;
					num -= m_reservedFooter;
					if (flag)
					{
						Array.Copy(array, 0, buffer, offset, num);
					}
				}
				if (num == 0)
				{
					m_endOfStream = true;
				}
				return num;
			}
		}

		public override void Write(byte[] buffer, int offset, int count)
		{
			CheckIfDisposed();
			m_innerStream.Write(buffer, offset, count);
		}

		public int GetFooter(byte[] buffer, int index)
		{
			CheckIfDisposed();
			if (buffer == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("buffer");
			}
			if (index < 0)
			{
				Xceed.Utils.Exceptions.ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("index", index, "The index cannot be less than zero.");
			}
			if (checked(buffer.Length - index) < ReservedFooter)
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("The buffer cannot hold all the buffered footer.", "buffer");
			}
			if (ReservedFooter > 0)
			{
				Array.Copy(m_bufferedFooter, 0, buffer, index, ReservedFooter);
			}
			return ReservedFooter;
		}

		~HeaderFooterStream()
		{
			Dispose(disposing: false);
		}

		protected override void Dispose(bool disposing)
		{
			if (!m_disposed)
			{
				if (disposing && !m_transient)
				{
					m_innerStream.Close();
				}
				m_innerStream = null;
				m_disposed = true;
			}
		}

		private void CheckIfDisposed()
		{
			if (m_disposed)
			{
				throw new ObjectDisposedException("TransientStream", "Cannot access a stream once it has been closed.");
			}
			if (m_innerStream == null)
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException("An attempt was made to dispose of a non-initialized FormattedChecksumStream.");
			}
		}
	}
	internal class NotifyStream : Stream
	{
		private bool m_preventRead;

		private bool m_preventWrite;

		private bool m_disposed;

		private Stream m_innerStream;

		private DataNotifyStreamArgs m_notifyStreamArgs;

		private bool m_transient;

		public override bool CanRead
		{
			get
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				if (!m_preventRead)
				{
					return m_innerStream.CanRead;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		public override bool CanSeek
		{
			get
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				return m_innerStream.CanSeek;
			}
		}

		public override bool CanWrite
		{
			get
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				if (!m_preventWrite)
				{
					return m_innerStream.CanWrite;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		public override long Length
		{
			get
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				return m_innerStream.Length;
			}
		}

		public override long Position
		{
			get
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				return m_innerStream.Position;
			}
			set
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				m_innerStream.Position = value;
			}
		}

		public Stream InnerStream => m_innerStream;

		public bool Transient
		{
			get
			{
				return m_transient;
			}
			set
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				m_transient = value;
			}
		}

		public event ReadingFromStreamEventHandler ReadingFromStream;

		public event WritingToStreamEventHandler WritingToStream;

		public event Xceed.Utils.Streams.ClosingStreamEventHandler ClosingStream;

		public NotifyStream(Stream inner, object userData)
		{
			if (inner == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("inner");
			}
			m_innerStream = inner;
			m_notifyStreamArgs = new DataNotifyStreamArgs(userData);
		}

		public void PreventRead()
		{
			m_preventRead = true;
		}

		public void PreventWrite()
		{
			m_preventWrite = true;
		}

		public override void Flush()
		{
			CheckIfDisposed();
			m_innerStream.Flush();
		}

		public override long Seek(long offset, SeekOrigin origin)
		{
			CheckIfDisposed();
			return m_innerStream.Seek(offset, origin);
		}

		public override void SetLength(long value)
		{
			CheckIfDisposed();
			m_innerStream.SetLength(value);
		}

		public override int Read(byte[] buffer, int offset, int count)
		{
			CheckIfDisposed();
			if (!CanRead)
			{
				throw new NotSupportedException("The NotifyStream object does not support reading.");
			}
			int num = m_innerStream.Read(buffer, offset, count);
			OnReadingFromStream(buffer, offset, num);
			return num;
		}

		public override void Write(byte[] buffer, int offset, int count)
		{
			CheckIfDisposed();
			if (!CanWrite)
			{
				throw new NotSupportedException("The NotifyStream object does not support writing.");
			}
			if (buffer == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("buffer");
			}
			if (offset < 0)
			{
				Xceed.Utils.Exceptions.ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("offset", offset, "The offset cannot be less than zero.");
			}
			if (count < 0)
			{
				Xceed.Utils.Exceptions.ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("count", count, "The count cannot be less than zero.");
			}
			if (count > checked(buffer.Length - offset))
			{
				Xceed.Utils.Exceptions.ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("count", count, "The count exceeds the buffer's remaining length after offset.");
			}
			OnWritingToStream(buffer, offset, count);
			m_innerStream.Write(buffer, offset, count);
		}

		~NotifyStream()
		{
			Dispose(disposing: false);
		}

		protected override void Dispose(bool disposing)
		{
			if (m_disposed)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (disposing)
			{
				if (!m_transient)
				{
					m_innerStream.Close();
				}
				OnClosingStream();
			}
			m_innerStream = null;
			m_disposed = true;
		}

		private void CheckIfDisposed()
		{
			if (m_disposed)
			{
				throw new ObjectDisposedException("NotifyStream", "Cannot access a stream once it has been closed.");
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnReadingFromStream(byte[] buffer, int offset, int count)
		{
			ReadingFromStreamEventHandler readingFromStream = this.ReadingFromStream;
			if (readingFromStream != null)
			{
				m_notifyStreamArgs.SetBuffer(buffer, offset, count);
				readingFromStream(this, m_notifyStreamArgs);
				m_notifyStreamArgs.ResetBuffer();
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnWritingToStream(byte[] buffer, int offset, int count)
		{
			WritingToStreamEventHandler writingToStream = this.WritingToStream;
			if (writingToStream != null)
			{
				m_notifyStreamArgs.SetBuffer(buffer, offset, count);
				writingToStream(this, m_notifyStreamArgs);
				m_notifyStreamArgs.ResetBuffer();
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnClosingStream()
		{
			this.ClosingStream?.Invoke(this, m_notifyStreamArgs);
		}
	}
	internal class NotifyStreamArgs : EventArgs
	{
		private object m_userData;

		public object UserData
		{
			get
			{
				return m_userData;
			}
			set
			{
				m_userData = value;
			}
		}

		public NotifyStreamArgs(object userData)
		{
			m_userData = userData;
		}
	}
	internal class DataNotifyStreamArgs : NotifyStreamArgs
	{
		private byte[] m_buffer;

		private int m_offset;

		private int m_count;

		public byte[] Buffer => m_buffer;

		public int Offset => m_offset;

		public int Count => m_count;

		public DataNotifyStreamArgs(object userData)
			: base(userData)
		{
		}

		internal void SetBuffer(byte[] buffer, int offset, int count)
		{
			m_buffer = buffer;
			m_offset = offset;
			m_count = count;
		}

		internal void ResetBuffer()
		{
			m_buffer = null;
			m_offset = 0;
			m_count = 0;
		}
	}
	internal delegate void ReadingFromStreamEventHandler(object sender, DataNotifyStreamArgs args);
	internal delegate void WritingToStreamEventHandler(object sender, DataNotifyStreamArgs args);
	internal delegate void ClosingStreamEventHandler(object sender, NotifyStreamArgs args);
	internal class PositionedStream : Stream
	{
		private bool m_disposed;

		private Stream m_innerStream;

		private long m_position;

		public override bool CanSeek => false;

		public override bool CanRead => m_innerStream.CanRead;

		public override bool CanWrite => m_innerStream.CanWrite;

		public Stream InnerStream => m_innerStream;

		public override long Length
		{
			get
			{
				throw new NotSupportedException("Cannot get the length of a non-seekable stream.");
			}
		}

		public override long Position
		{
			get
			{
				return m_position;
			}
			set
			{
				throw new NotSupportedException("Cannot change the position of a non-seekable stream.");
			}
		}

		public PositionedStream(Stream stream, long position)
		{
			if (stream == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("stream");
			}
			if (position < 0)
			{
				throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException("position", position, "The starting position cannot be less than zero.");
			}
			if (stream.CanSeek)
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("The inner stream cannot be seekable.", "stream");
			}
			m_innerStream = stream;
			m_position = position;
		}

		public PositionedStream(Stream stream)
			: this(stream, 0L)
		{
		}

		public override void Flush()
		{
			m_innerStream.Flush();
		}

		public override int Read(byte[] buffer, int offset, int count)
		{
			int num = 0;
			checked
			{
				try
				{
					num = m_innerStream.Read(buffer, offset, count);
					return num;
				}
				finally
				{
					m_position += num;
				}
			}
		}

		public override int ReadByte()
		{
			int num = m_innerStream.ReadByte();
			checked
			{
				if (num != -1)
				{
					m_position++;
				}
				return num;
			}
		}

		public override long Seek(long offset, SeekOrigin origin)
		{
			throw new NotSupportedException("Cannot change the position of a non-seekable stream.");
		}

		public override void SetLength(long value)
		{
			throw new NotSupportedException("Cannot set the length of this stream.");
		}

		public override void Write(byte[] buffer, int offset, int count)
		{
			m_innerStream.Write(buffer, offset, count);
			checked
			{
				m_position += count;
			}
		}

		public override void WriteByte(byte value)
		{
			m_innerStream.WriteByte(value);
			checked
			{
				m_position++;
			}
		}

		protected override void Dispose(bool disposing)
		{
			if (!m_disposed)
			{
				if (disposing)
				{
					m_innerStream.Close();
				}
				m_innerStream = null;
				m_disposed = true;
			}
		}

		private void CheckIfDisposed()
		{
			if (m_disposed)
			{
				throw new ObjectDisposedException("PositionedStream", "Cannot access a stream once it has been closed.");
			}
		}
	}
	internal class StreamCopy
	{
		internal static long Copy(Stream source, Stream dest, long size)
		{
			if (source == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("source");
			}
			if (dest == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("dest");
			}
			if (size < -1 || size == 0)
			{
				Xceed.Utils.Exceptions.ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("size", size, "The size can be -1 for up to end-of-stream copy, or a value greater than 0.");
			}
			byte[] array = new byte[8192];
			long num = 0L;
			checked
			{
				while (size == -1 || num < size)
				{
					int num2 = array.Length;
					if (size != -1 && num2 > size - num)
					{
						num2 = (int)(size - num);
					}
					int num3 = source.Read(array, 0, num2);
					if (num3 == 0)
					{
						if (size != -1)
						{
							throw new EndOfStreamException("Premature end of stream reached.");
						}
						size = num;
					}
					else
					{
						dest.Write(array, 0, num3);
						num += num3;
					}
				}
				return size;
			}
		}
	}
	internal class StreamSearcher
	{
		protected const int DefaultBufferSize = 32768;

		protected Stream m_stream;

		private int m_bufferSize = 32768;

		public int BufferSize
		{
			get
			{
				return m_bufferSize;
			}
			set
			{
				if (m_stream.CanSeek)
				{
					m_bufferSize = ((value > 0) ? value : 32768);
					return;
				}
				if (value == 0 || value == 1)
				{
					m_bufferSize = 1;
					return;
				}
				throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException("The buffer size cannot be greater than one for a non-seekable stream.");
			}
		}

		public StreamSearcher(Stream stream)
			: this(stream, 0)
		{
		}

		public StreamSearcher(Stream stream, int bufferSize)
		{
			if (stream == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("stream");
			}
			m_stream = stream;
			BufferSize = bufferSize;
		}

		public bool Find(bool value)
		{
			return InternalFind(BitConverter.GetBytes(value));
		}

		public bool Find(char value)
		{
			return InternalFind(BitConverter.GetBytes(value));
		}

		public bool Find(double value)
		{
			return InternalFind(BitConverter.GetBytes(value));
		}

		public bool Find(short value)
		{
			return InternalFind(BitConverter.GetBytes(value));
		}

		public bool Find(int value)
		{
			return InternalFind(BitConverter.GetBytes(value));
		}

		public bool Find(long value)
		{
			return InternalFind(BitConverter.GetBytes(value));
		}

		public bool Find(float value)
		{
			return InternalFind(BitConverter.GetBytes(value));
		}

		public bool Find(ushort value)
		{
			return InternalFind(BitConverter.GetBytes(value));
		}

		public bool Find(uint value)
		{
			return InternalFind(BitConverter.GetBytes(value));
		}

		public bool Find(ulong value)
		{
			return InternalFind(BitConverter.GetBytes(value));
		}

		public bool Find(byte[] value)
		{
			if (value == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("value");
			}
			return InternalFind(value);
		}

		public bool ReverseFind(bool value)
		{
			return InternalReverseFind(BitConverter.GetBytes(value));
		}

		public bool ReverseFind(char value)
		{
			return InternalReverseFind(BitConverter.GetBytes(value));
		}

		public bool ReverseFind(double value)
		{
			return InternalReverseFind(BitConverter.GetBytes(value));
		}

		public bool ReverseFind(short value)
		{
			return InternalReverseFind(BitConverter.GetBytes(value));
		}

		public bool ReverseFind(int value)
		{
			return InternalReverseFind(BitConverter.GetBytes(value));
		}

		public bool ReverseFind(long value)
		{
			return InternalReverseFind(BitConverter.GetBytes(value));
		}

		public bool ReverseFind(float value)
		{
			return InternalReverseFind(BitConverter.GetBytes(value));
		}

		public bool ReverseFind(ushort value)
		{
			return InternalReverseFind(BitConverter.GetBytes(value));
		}

		public bool ReverseFind(uint value)
		{
			return InternalReverseFind(BitConverter.GetBytes(value));
		}

		public bool ReverseFind(uint value, bool withinRange)
		{
			return InternalReverseFind(BitConverter.GetBytes(value), withinRange);
		}

		public bool ReverseFind(ulong value)
		{
			return InternalReverseFind(BitConverter.GetBytes(value));
		}

		public bool ReverseFind(byte[] value)
		{
			if (value == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("value");
			}
			return InternalReverseFind(value);
		}

		protected StreamSearcher()
		{
		}

		protected virtual bool FindImpl(byte[] value)
		{
			int num = 0;
			byte[] array = new byte[BufferSize];
			int num2 = 0;
			int num3 = 0;
			checked
			{
				while ((num = m_stream.Read(array, 0, array.Length)) > 0)
				{
					num2 = 0;
					while (num2 < num)
					{
						if (array[num2] == value[num3])
						{
							num3++;
						}
						else if (num3 > 0)
						{
							num2 -= num3;
							num3 = 0;
						}
						num2++;
						if (num3 >= value.Length)
						{
							if (num - num2 != 0)
							{
								m_stream.Seek(-(num - num2), SeekOrigin.Current);
							}
							return true;
						}
					}
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		protected virtual bool ReverseFindImpl(byte[] value, bool withinRange)
		{
			int num = 0;
			long num2 = m_stream.Length;
			byte[] array = new byte[1024];
			int num3 = 0;
			int num4 = value.Length;
			bool flag = false;
			checked
			{
				while (num2 > 0)
				{
					if (num2 < array.Length)
					{
						m_stream.Seek(0L, SeekOrigin.Begin);
					}
					else
					{
						m_stream.Seek(-array.Length, SeekOrigin.Current);
					}
					if ((num = m_stream.Read(array, 0, (num2 < array.Length) ? ((int)num2) : array.Length)) > 0)
					{
						num3 = array.Length;
						while (num3 > 0)
						{
							if (array[--num3] == value[--num4])
							{
								if (num4 == 0)
								{
									m_stream.Seek(-(m_stream.Length - num2 + num - num3 - value.Length), SeekOrigin.End);
									return true;
								}
							}
							else
							{
								num3 += value.Length - num4 - 1;
								num4 = value.Length;
							}
						}
						num2 -= num;
					}
					if (num2 > array.Length)
					{
						m_stream.Seek(-array.Length, SeekOrigin.Current);
					}
					if (!flag)
					{
						if (withinRange)
						{
							return false;
						}
						array = new byte[32768];
						flag = true;
					}
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		private bool InternalFind(byte[] value)
		{
			if (value.Length == 0)
			{
				return true;
			}
			return FindImpl(value);
		}

		private bool InternalReverseFind(byte[] value)
		{
			if (value.Length == 0)
			{
				return true;
			}
			return ReverseFindImpl(value, withinRange: false);
		}

		private bool InternalReverseFind(byte[] value, bool withinRange)
		{
			if (value.Length == 0)
			{
				return true;
			}
			return ReverseFindImpl(value, withinRange);
		}
	}
	internal class TransientStream : Stream
	{
		private bool m_disposed;

		private Stream m_innerStream;

		public override bool CanRead
		{
			get
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				return m_innerStream.CanRead;
			}
		}

		public override bool CanSeek
		{
			get
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				return m_innerStream.CanSeek;
			}
		}

		public override bool CanWrite
		{
			get
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				return m_innerStream.CanWrite;
			}
		}

		public override long Length
		{
			get
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				return m_innerStream.Length;
			}
		}

		public override long Position
		{
			get
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				return m_innerStream.Position;
			}
			set
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				m_innerStream.Position = value;
			}
		}

		public TransientStream(Stream innerStream)
		{
			if (innerStream == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("innerStream");
			}
			m_innerStream = innerStream;
		}

		public override void Flush()
		{
			CheckIfDisposed();
			m_innerStream.Flush();
		}

		public override long Seek(long offset, SeekOrigin origin)
		{
			CheckIfDisposed();
			return m_innerStream.Seek(offset, origin);
		}

		public override void SetLength(long value)
		{
			CheckIfDisposed();
			m_innerStream.SetLength(value);
		}

		public override int Read(byte[] buffer, int offset, int count)
		{
			CheckIfDisposed();
			return m_innerStream.Read(buffer, offset, count);
		}

		public override void Write(byte[] buffer, int offset, int count)
		{
			CheckIfDisposed();
			m_innerStream.Write(buffer, offset, count);
		}

		public override int ReadByte()
		{
			CheckIfDisposed();
			return m_innerStream.ReadByte();
		}

		public override void WriteByte(byte value)
		{
			CheckIfDisposed();
			m_innerStream.WriteByte(value);
		}

		protected override void Dispose(bool disposing)
		{
			if (!m_disposed)
			{
				m_innerStream = null;
				m_disposed = true;
			}
		}

		private void CheckIfDisposed()
		{
			if (m_disposed)
			{
				throw new ObjectDisposedException("TransientStream", "Cannot access a stream once it has been closed.");
			}
		}

		private TransientStream()
		{
		}

		~TransientStream()
		{
			Dispose(disposing: false);
		}
	}
	internal class WindowStream : Stream
	{
		private bool m_disposed;

		private Stream m_innerStream;

		private long m_beginOffset;

		private long m_length;

		public override bool CanRead
		{
			get
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				return m_innerStream.CanRead;
			}
		}

		public override bool CanSeek
		{
			get
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				return m_innerStream.CanSeek;
			}
		}

		public override bool CanWrite
		{
			get
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				return m_innerStream.CanWrite;
			}
		}

		public override long Length
		{
			get
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				if (m_length == -1)
				{
					if (m_innerStream.CanSeek)
					{
						return checked(m_innerStream.Length - m_beginOffset);
					}
					throw new NotSupportedException("Cannot get the length of this non-seekable stream.");
				}
				return m_length;
			}
		}

		public override long Position
		{
			get
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				CheckInnerStream();
				return checked(m_innerStream.Position - m_beginOffset);
			}
			set
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				if (value < 0)
				{
					Xceed.Utils.Exceptions.ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("Position", value, "The posision in the stream cannot be set to a negative value.");
				}
				if (m_length != -1 && value > m_length)
				{
					Xceed.Utils.Exceptions.ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("Position", value, "The position in the stream cannot be set beyond the end of the stream.");
				}
				m_innerStream.Position = checked(value + m_beginOffset);
			}
		}

		public WindowStream(Stream inner, long offset, long length)
		{
			if (inner == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("inner");
			}
			m_innerStream = inner;
			m_beginOffset = offset;
			m_length = length;
			checked
			{
				if (m_innerStream.CanSeek)
				{
					if (m_innerStream.Position < offset)
					{
						m_innerStream.Position = offset;
					}
					else if (length != -1 && m_innerStream.Position > offset + length)
					{
						m_innerStream.Position = offset + length;
					}
				}
				CheckInnerStream();
			}
		}

		public WindowStream(Stream inner, long length)
			: this(inner, inner.Position, length)
		{
		}

		public WindowStream(Stream inner)
			: this(inner, inner.Position, checked(inner.Length - inner.Position))
		{
		}

		public override void Flush()
		{
			CheckIfDisposed();
			m_innerStream.Flush();
		}

		public override long Seek(long offset, SeekOrigin origin)
		{
			CheckIfDisposed();
			CheckInnerStream();
			if (!Enum.IsDefined(typeof(SeekOrigin), origin))
			{
				Xceed.Utils.Exceptions.ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("origin", origin, "The seek origin is not a valid SeekOrigin member.");
			}
			long num = 0L;
			checked
			{
				switch (origin)
				{
				case SeekOrigin.Begin:
					num = offset;
					offset += m_beginOffset;
					break;
				case SeekOrigin.Current:
					num = m_innerStream.Position - m_beginOffset + offset;
					break;
				case SeekOrigin.End:
					if (m_length != -1)
					{
						num = m_length + offset;
						offset -= m_innerStream.Length - m_beginOffset - m_length;
					}
					else
					{
						num = m_innerStream.Length + offset;
					}
					break;
				}
				if (num < 0)
				{
					offset -= num;
				}
				else if (m_length != -1 && num > m_length)
				{
					offset -= num - m_length;
				}
				return m_innerStream.Seek(offset, origin) - m_beginOffset;
			}
		}

		public override void SetLength(long value)
		{
			CheckIfDisposed();
			CheckInnerStream();
			m_innerStream.SetLength(checked(m_beginOffset + value));
			if (m_length != -1)
			{
				m_length = value;
			}
		}

		public override int Read(byte[] buffer, int offset, int count)
		{
			CheckIfDisposed();
			CheckInnerStream();
			if (!CanRead)
			{
				throw new NotSupportedException("The WindowStream object does not support reading.");
			}
			if (buffer == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("buffer");
			}
			if (offset < 0)
			{
				Xceed.Utils.Exceptions.ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("offset", offset, "The offset cannot be less than zero.");
			}
			if (count < 0)
			{
				Xceed.Utils.Exceptions.ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("count", count, "The count cannot be less than zero.");
			}
			checked
			{
				if (count > buffer.Length - offset)
				{
					Xceed.Utils.Exceptions.ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("count", count, "The count exceeds the buffer's remaining length after offset.");
				}
				if (m_length != -1 && m_innerStream.Position - m_beginOffset + count > m_length)
				{
					count = (int)(m_length - (m_innerStream.Position - m_beginOffset));
				}
				return m_innerStream.Read(buffer, offset, count);
			}
		}

		public override void Write(byte[] buffer, int offset, int count)
		{
			CheckIfDisposed();
			CheckInnerStream();
			if (!CanWrite)
			{
				throw new NotSupportedException("The WindowStream object does not support writing.");
			}
			if (buffer == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("buffer");
			}
			if (offset < 0)
			{
				Xceed.Utils.Exceptions.ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("offset", offset, "The offset cannot be less than zero.");
			}
			if (count < 0)
			{
				Xceed.Utils.Exceptions.ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("count", count, "The count cannot be less than zero.");
			}
			checked
			{
				if (count > buffer.Length - offset)
				{
					Xceed.Utils.Exceptions.ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("count", count, "The count exceeds the buffer's remaining length after offset.");
				}
				long num = ((m_length != -1) ? m_innerStream.Length : 0);
				m_innerStream.Write(buffer, offset, count);
				if (m_length != -1)
				{
					m_length += m_innerStream.Length - num;
				}
			}
		}

		protected override void Dispose(bool disposing)
		{
			if (!m_disposed)
			{
				if (disposing)
				{
					m_innerStream.Close();
				}
				m_innerStream = null;
				m_disposed = true;
			}
		}

		private void CheckIfDisposed()
		{
			if (m_disposed)
			{
				throw new ObjectDisposedException("WindowStream", "Cannot access a stream once it has been closed.");
			}
		}

		private WindowStream()
		{
		}

		~WindowStream()
		{
			Dispose(disposing: false);
		}

		private void CheckInnerStream()
		{
			if (m_innerStream.Position < m_beginOffset || (m_length != -1 && m_innerStream.Position > checked(m_beginOffset + m_length)))
			{
				throw new IOException("The inner stream of the WindowStream has been accessed directly, preventing the WindowStream from working properly.");
			}
		}
	}
	internal class ZCrypt
	{
		private struct zcryptStructure
		{
			public uint[] Keys;

			public byte[] Header;

			public string Password;

			public long UserSeed;
		}

		private const ushort HeaderLength = 12;

		private static byte[] m_zcryptFast = new byte[127]
		{
			201, 247, 24, 143, 138, 30, 93, 42, 246, 12,
			105, 25, 62, 11, 47, 219, 218, 143, 42, 248,
			141, 29, 106, 41, 225, 11, 96, 32, 71, 78,
			42, 231, 230, 250, 95, 230, 188, 27, 92, 44,
			224, 12, 105, 35, 62, 53, 43, 159, 237, 204,
			45, 251, 255, 30, 93, 94, 235, 55, 92, 94,
			118, 11, 25, 216, 217, 143, 21, 244, 143, 111,
			101, 29, 224, 8, 110, 46, 125, 11, 25, 161,
			157, 212, 43, 143, 132, 26, 27, 123, 238, 63,
			112, 44, 69, 55, 38, 235, 157, 183, 26, 180,
			255, 93, 43, 110, 172, 79, 36, 94, 118, 11,
			25, 216, 217, 143, 10, 254, 185, 27, 114, 29,
			237, 8, 27, 5, 64, 11, 96
		};

		private zcryptStructure m_zcrypt;

		public ZCrypt(Stream stream, string password, long userSeed)
		{
			m_zcrypt.Password = password;
			m_zcrypt.UserSeed = userSeed;
			m_zcrypt.Header = new byte[12];
			ReadHeader(stream);
		}

		public ZCrypt(string password, long userSeed, out byte[] header)
		{
			m_zcrypt.Password = password;
			m_zcrypt.UserSeed = userSeed;
			m_zcrypt.Header = new byte[12];
			WriteHeader();
			header = m_zcrypt.Header;
		}

		public void Crypt(byte[] buffer, int offset, int count)
		{
			checked
			{
				for (int i = offset; i < count + offset; i++)
				{
					byte b = DecryptByte();
					UpdateKeys(buffer[i]);
					buffer[i] = (byte)(b ^ buffer[i]);
				}
			}
		}

		public void Decrypt(byte[] buffer, int offset, int count)
		{
			checked
			{
				for (int i = offset; i < count + offset; i++)
				{
					buffer[i] ^= DecryptByte();
					UpdateKeys(buffer[i]);
				}
			}
		}

		private void ReadHeader(Stream stream)
		{
			stream.Read(m_zcrypt.Header, 0, 12);
			InitializeKeys();
			Decrypt(m_zcrypt.Header, 0, 12);
			if (m_zcrypt.Header[11] != ((m_zcrypt.UserSeed >> 8) & 0xFF))
			{
				throw new ZCryptException("Invalid decryption Password");
			}
		}

		private void WriteHeader()
		{
			RandomizeHeader();
			InitializeKeys();
			Crypt(m_zcrypt.Header, 0, 12);
		}

		private byte DecryptByte()
		{
			ushort num = (ushort)(m_zcrypt.Keys[2] | 2);
			return (byte)(((ushort)(num * (num ^ 1)) >> 8) & 0xFF);
		}

		private void RandomizeHeader()
		{
			long num = DateTime.Now.Ticks ^ (m_zcrypt.UserSeed << 5);
			checked
			{
				for (int i = 0; i < 10; i++)
				{
					m_zcrypt.Header[i] = (byte)unchecked(checked(((num >> i * 3) & 0xFF) + i * 23) % 256);
				}
				m_zcrypt.Header[10] = (byte)(m_zcrypt.UserSeed & 0xFF);
				m_zcrypt.Header[11] = (byte)((m_zcrypt.UserSeed >> 8) & 0xFF);
			}
		}

		private void InitializeKeys()
		{
			byte[] bytes = System.Text.Encoding.ASCII.GetBytes(m_zcrypt.Password);
			m_zcrypt.Keys = new uint[3];
			m_zcrypt.Keys[0] = 305419896u;
			m_zcrypt.Keys[1] = 591751049u;
			m_zcrypt.Keys[2] = 878082192u;
			if (m_zcryptFast[m_zcrypt.Keys[1] % 16] == 0)
			{
				throw new ZCryptException("Cannot initialize random encryption header");
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < bytes.Length; i = checked(i + 1))
			{
				UpdateKeys(bytes[i]);
			}
		}

		private void UpdateKeys(byte b)
		{
			m_zcrypt.Keys[0] = ChecksumStream.UniqueCrc32(m_zcrypt.Keys[0], b);
			m_zcrypt.Keys[1] += m_zcrypt.Keys[0] & 0xFF;
			m_zcrypt.Keys[1] = m_zcrypt.Keys[1] * 134775813 + 1;
			m_zcrypt.Keys[2] = ChecksumStream.UniqueCrc32(m_zcrypt.Keys[2], m_zcrypt.Keys[1] >> 24);
		}
	}
	internal class ZCryptStream : Stream
	{
		private bool m_disposed;

		private Stream m_innerStream;

		private ZCrypt m_zcrypt;

		private bool m_transient;

		private byte[] m_header = new byte[0];

		private bool m_headerWritten;

		public override bool CanRead
		{
			get
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				return m_innerStream.CanRead;
			}
		}

		public override bool CanSeek
		{
			get
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				return m_innerStream.CanSeek;
			}
		}

		public override bool CanWrite
		{
			get
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				return m_innerStream.CanWrite;
			}
		}

		public override long Length
		{
			get
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				return m_innerStream.Length;
			}
		}

		public override long Position
		{
			get
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				return m_innerStream.Position;
			}
			set
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				m_innerStream.Position = value;
			}
		}

		public bool Transient
		{
			get
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				return m_transient;
			}
			set
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				m_transient = value;
			}
		}

		public ZCryptStream(Stream inner, string password, long userSeed, bool encrypt)
		{
			if (inner == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("inner");
			}
			if (password == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("password");
			}
			if (password.Length == 0)
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("The password is an empty string.", "password");
			}
			m_innerStream = inner;
			if (encrypt)
			{
				m_zcrypt = new ZCrypt(password, userSeed, out m_header);
			}
			else
			{
				m_zcrypt = new ZCrypt(inner, password, userSeed);
			}
		}

		public override void Flush()
		{
			CheckIfDisposed();
			m_innerStream.Flush();
		}

		public override long Seek(long offset, SeekOrigin origin)
		{
			CheckIfDisposed();
			return m_innerStream.Seek(offset, origin);
		}

		public override void SetLength(long value)
		{
			CheckIfDisposed();
			m_innerStream.SetLength(value);
		}

		public override int Read(byte[] buffer, int offset, int count)
		{
			CheckIfDisposed();
			if (!CanRead)
			{
				throw new NotSupportedException("The stream does not support reading.");
			}
			if (buffer == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("buffer");
			}
			if (offset < 0)
			{
				Xceed.Utils.Exceptions.ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("offset", offset, "The offset cannot be less than zero.");
			}
			if (count < 0)
			{
				Xceed.Utils.Exceptions.ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("count", count, "The count cannot be less than zero.");
			}
			if (count > checked(buffer.Length - offset))
			{
				Xceed.Utils.Exceptions.ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("count", count, "The count exceeds the buffer's remaining length after offset.");
			}
			int num = m_innerStream.Read(buffer, offset, count);
			if (num != 0)
			{
				m_zcrypt.Decrypt(buffer, offset, num);
			}
			return num;
		}

		public override void Write(byte[] buffer, int offset, int count)
		{
			CheckIfDisposed();
			if (!CanWrite)
			{
				throw new NotSupportedException("The stream does not support writing.");
			}
			if (buffer == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("buffer");
			}
			if (offset < 0)
			{
				Xceed.Utils.Exceptions.ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("offset", offset, "The offset cannot be less than zero.");
			}
			if (count < 0)
			{
				Xceed.Utils.Exceptions.ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("count", count, "The count cannot be less than zero.");
			}
			if (count > checked(buffer.Length - offset))
			{
				Xceed.Utils.Exceptions.ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("count", count, "The count exceeds the buffer's remaining length after offset.");
			}
			if (!m_headerWritten && m_header.Length > 0)
			{
				m_innerStream.Write(m_header, 0, m_header.Length);
				m_headerWritten = true;
			}
			m_zcrypt.Crypt(buffer, offset, count);
			m_innerStream.Write(buffer, offset, count);
		}

		private ZCryptStream()
		{
		}

		~ZCryptStream()
		{
			Dispose(disposing: false);
		}

		protected override void Dispose(bool disposing)
		{
			if (!m_disposed)
			{
				try
				{
					Flush();
				}
				catch
				{
				}
				if (disposing && !m_transient)
				{
					m_innerStream.Close();
				}
				m_innerStream = null;
				m_disposed = true;
			}
		}

		private void CheckIfDisposed()
		{
			if (m_disposed)
			{
				throw new ObjectDisposedException("ZCryptStream", "Cannot access a stream once it has been closed.");
			}
		}
	}
}
namespace Xceed.Utils.Strings
{
	internal class Encoding
	{
		private static System.Text.Encoding m_oemEncoding = System.Text.Encoding.Default;

		private static bool m_codePageSet = false;

		public static System.Text.Encoding OEMEncoding
		{
			get
			{
				if (!m_codePageSet)
				{
					int codepage = 437;
					try
					{
						Microsoft.Win32.RegistryKey registryKey = Registry.LocalMachine.OpenSubKey("SYSTEM\\CurrentControlSet\\Control\\Nls\\CodePage");
						codepage = int.Parse((string)registryKey.GetValue("OEMCP"));
					}
					catch
					{
					}
					try
					{
						m_oemEncoding = System.Text.Encoding.GetEncoding(codepage);
					}
					catch
					{
						m_oemEncoding = System.Text.Encoding.Default;
					}
					m_codePageSet = true;
				}
				return m_oemEncoding;
			}
		}
	}
	internal class StringCheck
	{
		public static string ReplaceNull(string value)
		{
			if (value == null)
			{
				return string.Empty;
			}
			return value;
		}
	}
}
namespace Xceed.Zip
{
	public class ZippedFolder : AbstractFolder
	{
		internal ZipHandler m_zipHandler;

		internal string m_fullName = string.Empty;

		public virtual string Comment
		{
			get
			{
				try
				{
					if (m_isRoot)
					{
						return m_zipHandler.ZipComment;
					}
					return GetItemHandler().FileComment;
				}
				catch (Exception except)
				{
					throw PrepareException(except);
				}
			}
			set
			{
				try
				{
					if (m_isRoot)
					{
						m_zipHandler.ZipComment = value;
					}
					else
					{
						GetItemHandler().FileComment = value;
					}
				}
				catch (Exception except)
				{
					throw PrepareException(except);
				}
			}
		}

		public virtual int DiskNumber
		{
			get
			{
				try
				{
					if (m_isRoot)
					{
						uint lastDiskNumber = m_zipHandler.LastDiskNumber;
						if (lastDiskNumber == uint.MaxValue)
						{
							return 0;
						}
						return (int)(lastDiskNumber + 1);
					}
					uint diskNumber = GetItemHandler().DiskNumber;
					if (diskNumber == uint.MaxValue)
					{
						return 0;
					}
					return (int)(diskNumber + 1);
				}
				catch (Exception except)
				{
					throw PrepareException(except);
				}
			}
		}

		public ExtraHeaders ExtraHeaders
		{
			get
			{
				ExtraHeaders extraHeaders = ExtraHeaders.None;
				ZipItemHandler itemHandler = m_zipHandler.GetItemHandler(m_fullName);
				foreach (ExtraHeader item in (IEnumerable)itemHandler.ExtraHeaders)
				{
					switch (item.Signature)
					{
					case 21838:
						extraHeaders |= ExtraHeaders.Unicode;
						break;
					case 10:
						extraHeaders |= ExtraHeaders.FileTimes;
						break;
					case 21589:
						extraHeaders |= ExtraHeaders.ExtendedTimeStamp;
						break;
					}
				}
				return extraHeaders;
			}
			set
			{
				if ((value & (ExtraHeaders.Unicode | ExtraHeaders.FileTimes | ExtraHeaders.ExtendedTimeStamp)) != value)
				{
					throw new ArgumentException("Unknown extra header flag.", "DefaultExtraHeaders");
				}
				FileSystemEventsSession session = FileSystemEvents.Empty.CreateSession(this, null);
				m_zipHandler.AddRefModify(session);
				try
				{
					ZipItemHandler itemHandler = m_zipHandler.GetItemHandler(m_fullName);
					ExtraHeader extraHeader = itemHandler.ExtraHeaders.Find(21838);
					if ((value & ExtraHeaders.Unicode) == ExtraHeaders.Unicode)
					{
						if (extraHeader == null)
						{
							itemHandler.ExtraHeaders.Add(new UnicodeExtraHeader());
							m_zipHandler.Dirty = Dirtiness.Dirty;
						}
					}
					else if (extraHeader != null)
					{
						itemHandler.ExtraHeaders.Remove(extraHeader);
						m_zipHandler.Dirty = Dirtiness.Dirty;
					}
					extraHeader = itemHandler.ExtraHeaders.Find(10);
					if ((value & ExtraHeaders.FileTimes) == ExtraHeaders.FileTimes)
					{
						if (extraHeader == null)
						{
							itemHandler.ExtraHeaders.Add(new FileTimesExtraHeader());
							m_zipHandler.Dirty = Dirtiness.Dirty;
						}
					}
					else if (extraHeader != null)
					{
						itemHandler.ExtraHeaders.Remove(extraHeader);
						m_zipHandler.Dirty = Dirtiness.Dirty;
					}
					extraHeader = itemHandler.ExtraHeaders.Find(21589);
					if ((value & ExtraHeaders.ExtendedTimeStamp) == ExtraHeaders.ExtendedTimeStamp)
					{
						if (extraHeader == null)
						{
							itemHandler.ExtraHeaders.Add(new ExtendedTimeStampExtraHeader());
							m_zipHandler.Dirty = Dirtiness.Dirty;
						}
					}
					else if (extraHeader != null)
					{
						itemHandler.ExtraHeaders.Remove(extraHeader);
						m_zipHandler.Dirty = Dirtiness.Dirty;
					}
				}
				finally
				{
					m_zipHandler.ReleaseModify(session);
				}
			}
		}

		public override AbstractFile HostFile => m_zipHandler.LastZipFile;

		protected override string DoName
		{
			get
			{
				try
				{
					return Xceed.Utils.Strings.StringCheck.ReplaceNull(Path.GetFileName(m_fullName.TrimEnd(Path.DirectorySeparatorChar)));
				}
				catch (Exception except)
				{
					throw PrepareException(except);
				}
			}
			set
			{
				try
				{
					ZipItemHandler itemHandler = GetItemHandler();
					itemHandler.FullName = ZipHandler.GetParentFullName(m_fullName) + value + Path.DirectorySeparatorChar;
					m_fullName = itemHandler.FullName;
				}
				catch (Exception except)
				{
					throw PrepareException(except);
				}
			}
		}

		protected override string DoFullName
		{
			get
			{
				try
				{
					return m_fullName;
				}
				catch (Exception except)
				{
					throw PrepareException(except);
				}
			}
		}

		protected override FileAttributes DoAttributes
		{
			get
			{
				try
				{
					return GetItemHandler().FileAttributes;
				}
				catch (Exception except)
				{
					throw PrepareException(except);
				}
			}
			set
			{
				try
				{
					GetItemHandler().FileAttributes = value;
				}
				catch (Exception except)
				{
					throw PrepareException(except);
				}
			}
		}

		protected override bool DoHasCreationDateTime
		{
			get
			{
				if (DoIsRoot)
				{
					return false;
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

		protected override DateTime DoCreationDateTime
		{
			get
			{
				try
				{
					return GetItemHandler().CreationDateTime;
				}
				catch (Exception except)
				{
					throw PrepareException(except);
				}
			}
			set
			{
				try
				{
					GetItemHandler().CreationDateTime = value;
				}
				catch (Exception except)
				{
					throw PrepareException(except);
				}
			}
		}

		protected override DateTime DoLastWriteDateTime
		{
			get
			{
				try
				{
					return GetItemHandler().LastModifiedDateTime;
				}
				catch (Exception except)
				{
					throw PrepareException(except);
				}
			}
			set
			{
				try
				{
					if (value != DateTime.MinValue)
					{
						GetItemHandler().LastModifiedDateTime = value;
					}
				}
				catch (Exception except)
				{
					throw PrepareException(except);
				}
			}
		}

		protected override bool DoHasLastAccessDateTime
		{
			get
			{
				if (DoIsRoot)
				{
					return false;
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

		protected override DateTime DoLastAccessDateTime
		{
			get
			{
				try
				{
					return GetItemHandler().LastAccessDateTime;
				}
				catch (Exception except)
				{
					throw PrepareException(except);
				}
			}
			set
			{
				try
				{
					GetItemHandler().LastAccessDateTime = value;
				}
				catch (Exception except)
				{
					throw PrepareException(except);
				}
			}
		}

		protected override AbstractFolder DoParentFolder
		{
			get
			{
				try
				{
					if (base.IsRoot)
					{
						return null;
					}
					string parentFullName = ZipHandler.GetParentFullName(m_fullName);
					if (parentFullName.Length == 1)
					{
						return new ZipArchive(m_zipHandler);
					}
					return new ZippedFolder(m_zipHandler, parentFullName);
				}
				catch (Exception except)
				{
					throw PrepareException(except);
				}
			}
		}

		protected override AbstractFolder DoRootFolder
		{
			get
			{
				try
				{
					return new ZipArchive(m_zipHandler);
				}
				catch (Exception except)
				{
					throw PrepareException(except);
				}
			}
		}

		protected override bool DoExists
		{
			get
			{
				try
				{
					FileSystemEventsSession session = FileSystemEvents.Empty.CreateSession(this, null);
					m_zipHandler.Refresh(session);
					return GetItemHandler().Exists;
				}
				catch (Exception except)
				{
					throw PrepareException(except);
				}
			}
		}

		public ZippedFolder(AbstractFile zipFile, string folderName)
			: this(FileSystemEvents.Empty, null, zipFile, folderName)
		{
		}

		public ZippedFolder(FileSystemEvents events, object userData, AbstractFile zipFile, string folderName)
		{
			Licenser.VerifyLicense();
			if (events == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("events");
			}
			if (zipFile == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("zipFile");
			}
			if (folderName == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("folderName");
			}
			folderName = folderName.TrimEnd();
			if (folderName.Length == 0)
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("The folder name is empty or contains only whitespace.", "folderName");
			}
			if (folderName.IndexOfAny(InvalidChars) != -1)
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("The folder name contains invalid characters.", "folderName");
			}
			folderName = folderName.Replace(Path.AltDirectorySeparatorChar, Path.DirectorySeparatorChar);
			if (!folderName.StartsWith(Path.DirectorySeparatorChar.ToString()))
			{
				folderName = Path.DirectorySeparatorChar + folderName;
			}
			if (!folderName.EndsWith(Path.DirectorySeparatorChar.ToString()))
			{
				folderName += Path.DirectorySeparatorChar;
			}
			FileSystemEventsSession session = events.CreateSession(this, userData);
			m_zipHandler = ZipHandler.GetZipHandler(session, zipFile);
			m_fullName = folderName;
			m_isRoot = folderName.Length == 1;
		}

		internal ZippedFolder(ZipHandler handler, string fullName)
		{
			if (handler == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("handler");
			}
			if (fullName == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("fullName");
			}
			m_zipHandler = ZipHandler.GetZipHandler(handler);
			m_fullName = fullName;
			m_isRoot = fullName.Length == 1;
		}

		~ZippedFolder()
		{
			if (m_zipHandler != null)
			{
				ZipHandler.RemoveZipHandler(m_zipHandler);
			}
		}

		protected override FileSystemItem[] DoGetChildItems(FileSystemEventsSession session)
		{
			try
			{
				ICollection childItems = GetItemHandler().ChildItems;
				ArrayList arrayList = new ArrayList(childItems.Count);
				foreach (ZipItemHandler item in childItems)
				{
					if (item.Exists)
					{
						if (item.IsFolder)
						{
							arrayList.Add(new ZippedFolder(m_zipHandler, item.FullName));
						}
						else
						{
							arrayList.Add(new ZippedFile(m_zipHandler, item.FullName));
						}
					}
				}
				return (FileSystemItem[])arrayList.ToArray(typeof(FileSystemItem));
			}
			catch (Exception except)
			{
				throw PrepareException(except);
			}
		}

		protected override AbstractFile DoGetFile(FileSystemEventsSession session, string fileName)
		{
			try
			{
				return new ZippedFile(m_zipHandler, m_fullName + fileName);
			}
			catch (Exception except)
			{
				throw PrepareException(except);
			}
		}

		protected override AbstractFolder DoGetFolder(FileSystemEventsSession session, string folderName)
		{
			try
			{
				return new ZippedFolder(fullName: (folderName.Length != 0) ? (m_fullName + folderName + Path.DirectorySeparatorChar) : m_fullName, handler: m_zipHandler);
			}
			catch (Exception except)
			{
				throw PrepareException(except);
			}
		}

		protected override void DoCreate(FileSystemEventsSession session)
		{
			try
			{
				if (base.Exists)
				{
					throw new ItemAlreadyExistsException(this);
				}
				if (!base.ParentFolder.Exists)
				{
					throw new FileSystemInternalException("An unexpected attempt was made to create an item in a folder that does not exist.", this);
				}
				m_zipHandler.AddRefModify(session);
				try
				{
					GetItemHandler().Create();
				}
				finally
				{
					m_zipHandler.ReleaseModify(session);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception except)
			{
				throw PrepareException(except);
			}
		}

		protected override void DoDelete(FileSystemEventsSession session)
		{
			try
			{
				if (!base.Exists)
				{
					throw new ItemDoesNotExistException(this);
				}
				if ((base.Attributes & FileAttributes.ReadOnly) == FileAttributes.ReadOnly)
				{
					throw new ItemIsReadOnlyException(this, null);
				}
				m_zipHandler.AddRefModify(session);
				try
				{
					GetItemHandler().Delete();
				}
				finally
				{
					m_zipHandler.ReleaseModify(session);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception except)
			{
				throw PrepareException(except);
			}
		}

		protected override void DoRefresh(FileSystemEventsSession session)
		{
			try
			{
				m_zipHandler.Refresh(session);
			}
			catch (Exception except)
			{
				throw PrepareException(except);
			}
		}

		protected override FileSystemItem ListCopyTo(FileSystemEventsSession session, bool replaceExistingFiles)
		{
			m_zipHandler.AddRefStream(session);
			try
			{
				return base.ListCopyTo(session, replaceExistingFiles);
			}
			finally
			{
				m_zipHandler.ReleaseStream(session, null);
			}
		}

		protected override void EnsureDateIsValid(DateTime value)
		{
		}

		public override bool IsSameAs(FileSystemItem target)
		{
			if (target == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (target is ZippedFolder zippedFolder)
			{
				if (m_zipHandler == zippedFolder.m_zipHandler)
				{
					return m_fullName == zippedFolder.m_fullName;
				}
				return false;
			}
			return false;
		}

		protected override bool IsPathRooted(string path)
		{
			return false;
		}

		protected override void DoApplyPropertiesTo(FileSystemEventsSession session, FileSystemItem targetItem)
		{
			try
			{
				if (targetItem is ZippedFolder zippedFolder)
				{
					try
					{
						zippedFolder.Comment = Comment;
					}
					catch (FileSystemException)
					{
					}
				}
				else if (targetItem is ZippedFile zippedFile)
				{
					try
					{
						zippedFile.Comment = Comment;
					}
					catch (FileSystemException)
					{
					}
				}
			}
			catch (Exception except)
			{
				throw PrepareException(except);
			}
			base.DoApplyPropertiesTo(session, targetItem);
		}

		internal ZipItemHandler GetItemHandler()
		{
			ZipItemHandler itemHandler = m_zipHandler.GetItemHandler(m_fullName);
			if (itemHandler == null)
			{
				throw new FileSystemInternalException("The main ZipHandler did not return a valid ZipItemHandler.");
			}
			return itemHandler;
		}

		private Exception PrepareException(Exception except)
		{
			if (except is FileSystemException ex)
			{
				if (except is ZipException { ZipFile: null } ex2)
				{
					ex2.ZipFile = m_zipHandler.LastZipFile;
				}
				if (ex.Item == null)
				{
					ex.Item = this;
				}
				return ex;
			}
			if (except is NotSupportedException)
			{
				return new FileSystemNotSupportedException(this, except);
			}
			if (Xceed.Utils.Exceptions.ExceptionFilter.IsPublicException(except))
			{
				return except;
			}
			return new FileSystemInternalException(this, except);
		}
	}
	public class ZipArchive : ZippedFolder, IBatchUpdateable
	{
		public override string Comment
		{
			get
			{
				try
				{
					return m_zipHandler.ZipComment;
				}
				catch (Exception except)
				{
					throw PrepareException(except);
				}
			}
			set
			{
				if (value.Length > 65535)
				{
					throw new ArgumentException("The zip comment can not exceed 65535 characters.");
				}
				try
				{
					m_zipHandler.ZipComment = value;
				}
				catch (Exception except)
				{
					throw PrepareException(except);
				}
			}
		}
