		public static ExtraHeaders DefaultExtraHeaders
		{
			get
			{
				ExtraHeaders extraHeaders = ExtraHeaders.None;
				foreach (ExtraHeader item in (IEnumerable)ZipHandler.DefaultExtraHeaders)
				{
					switch (item.Signature)
					{
					case 21838:
						extraHeaders |= ExtraHeaders.Unicode;
						break;
					case 10:
						extraHeaders |= ExtraHeaders.FileTimes;
						break;
					case 21589:
						extraHeaders |= ExtraHeaders.ExtendedTimeStamp;
						break;
					}
				}
				return extraHeaders;
			}
			set
			{
				if ((value & (ExtraHeaders.Unicode | ExtraHeaders.FileTimes | ExtraHeaders.ExtendedTimeStamp)) != value)
				{
					throw new ArgumentException("Unknown extra header flag.", "DefaultExtraHeaders");
				}
				ExtraHeader extraHeader = ZipHandler.DefaultExtraHeaders.Find(21838);
				if ((value & ExtraHeaders.Unicode) == ExtraHeaders.Unicode)
				{
					if (extraHeader == null)
					{
						ZipHandler.DefaultExtraHeaders.Add(new UnicodeExtraHeader());
					}
				}
				else if (extraHeader != null)
				{
					ZipHandler.DefaultExtraHeaders.Remove(extraHeader);
				}
				extraHeader = ZipHandler.DefaultExtraHeaders.Find(10);
				if ((value & ExtraHeaders.FileTimes) == ExtraHeaders.FileTimes)
				{
					if (extraHeader == null)
					{
						ZipHandler.DefaultExtraHeaders.Add(new FileTimesExtraHeader());
					}
				}
				else if (extraHeader != null)
				{
					ZipHandler.DefaultExtraHeaders.Remove(extraHeader);
				}
				extraHeader = ZipHandler.DefaultExtraHeaders.Find(21589);
				if ((value & ExtraHeaders.ExtendedTimeStamp) == ExtraHeaders.ExtendedTimeStamp)
				{
					if (extraHeader == null)
					{
						ZipHandler.DefaultExtraHeaders.Add(new ExtendedTimeStampExtraHeader());
					}
				}
				else if (extraHeader != null)
				{
					ZipHandler.DefaultExtraHeaders.Remove(extraHeader);
				}
			}
		}

		public ZipArchive(AbstractFile zipFile)
			: this(FileSystemEvents.Empty, null, zipFile)
		{
		}

		public ZipArchive(FileSystemEvents events, object userData, AbstractFile zipFile)
			: base(events, userData, zipFile, Path.DirectorySeparatorChar.ToString())
		{
			if (events == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("events");
			}
		}

		public void BeginUpdate()
		{
			BeginUpdate(FileSystemEvents.Empty, null);
		}

		public void BeginUpdate(FileSystemEvents events, object userData)
		{
			if (events == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("events");
			}
			try
			{
				FileSystemEventsSession fileSystemEventsSession = events.CreateSession(this, userData);
				fileSystemEventsSession.CurrentItem = this;
				m_zipHandler.AddRefModify(fileSystemEventsSession);
			}
			catch (Exception except)
			{
				throw PrepareException(except);
			}
		}

		public void EndUpdate()
		{
			EndUpdate(FileSystemEvents.Empty, null);
		}

		public void EndUpdate(FileSystemEvents events, object userData)
		{
			if (events == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("events");
			}
			try
			{
				FileSystemEventsSession fileSystemEventsSession = events.CreateSession(this, userData);
				fileSystemEventsSession.CurrentItem = this;
				m_zipHandler.ReleaseModify(fileSystemEventsSession);
			}
			catch (Exception except)
			{
				throw PrepareException(except);
			}
		}

		internal ZipArchive(ZipHandler handler)
			: base(handler, Path.DirectorySeparatorChar.ToString())
		{
		}

		private Exception PrepareException(Exception except)
		{
			if (except is FileSystemException ex)
			{
				if (except is ZipException { ZipFile: null } ex2)
				{
					ex2.ZipFile = m_zipHandler.LastZipFile;
				}
				if (ex.Item == null)
				{
					ex.Item = this;
				}
				return ex;
			}
			if (Xceed.Utils.Exceptions.ExceptionFilter.IsPublicException(except))
			{
				return except;
			}
			return new FileSystemInternalException(this, except);
		}
	}
	public delegate void ReadingZipItemProgressionEventHandler(object sender, ItemProgressionEventArgs e);
	public delegate void BuildingZipItemProgressionEventHandler(object sender, ItemProgressionEventArgs e);
	public delegate void BuildingZipByteProgressionEventHandler(object sender, ByteProgressionEventArgs e);
	public delegate void CopyingZipByteProgressionEventHandler(object sender, ByteProgressionEventArgs e);
	public delegate void GatheringZipContentByteProgressionEventHandler(object sender, ByteProgressionEventArgs e);
	public delegate void DiskRequiredEventHandler(object sender, DiskRequiredEventArgs e);
	public class ZipEvents : FileSystemEvents
	{
		protected internal virtual bool WantsReadingZipItemProgression => this.ReadingZipItemProgression != null;

		protected internal virtual bool WantsBuildingZipItemProgression => this.BuildingZipItemProgression != null;

		protected internal virtual bool WantsBuildingZipByteProgression => this.BuildingZipByteProgression != null;

		protected internal virtual bool WantsCopyingZipByteProgression => this.CopyingZipByteProgression != null;

		protected internal virtual bool WantsGatheringZipContentByteProgression => this.GatheringZipContentByteProgression != null;

		protected internal virtual bool WantsDiskRequired => this.DiskRequired != null;

		public event ReadingZipItemProgressionEventHandler ReadingZipItemProgression;

		public event BuildingZipItemProgressionEventHandler BuildingZipItemProgression;

		public event BuildingZipByteProgressionEventHandler BuildingZipByteProgression;

		[Obsolete("This event is no longer raised as the zip file is now created directly in its final destination. You can use the GatheringZipContentByteProgression event to retrieve progress information during the step preceding the creation of the zip file.")]
		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		public event CopyingZipByteProgressionEventHandler CopyingZipByteProgression;

		public event GatheringZipContentByteProgressionEventHandler GatheringZipContentByteProgression;

		public event DiskRequiredEventHandler DiskRequired;

		public override FileSystemEventsSession CreateSession(object sender, object userData)
		{
			return new ZipEventsSession(this, sender, userData);
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnReadingZipItemProgression(object sender, ItemProgressionEventArgs e)
		{
			ValidateParams(sender, e);
			if (this.ReadingZipItemProgression != null)
			{
				this.ReadingZipItemProgression(sender, e);
			}
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnBuildingZipItemProgression(object sender, ItemProgressionEventArgs e)
		{
			ValidateParams(sender, e);
			if (this.BuildingZipItemProgression != null)
			{
				this.BuildingZipItemProgression(sender, e);
			}
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnBuildingZipByteProgression(object sender, ByteProgressionEventArgs e)
		{
			ValidateParams(sender, e);
			if (this.BuildingZipByteProgression != null)
			{
				this.BuildingZipByteProgression(sender, e);
			}
		}

		[Obsolete("This method should no longer be called as the zip file is now created directly in its final destination. You can override OnGatheringZipContentByteProgression to retrieve progress information during the step preceding the creation of the zip file.")]
		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		protected internal virtual void OnCopyingZipByteProgression(object sender, ByteProgressionEventArgs e)
		{
			ValidateParams(sender, e);
			if (this.CopyingZipByteProgression != null)
			{
				this.CopyingZipByteProgression(sender, e);
			}
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnGatheringZipContentByteProgression(object sender, ByteProgressionEventArgs e)
		{
			ValidateParams(sender, e);
			if (this.GatheringZipContentByteProgression != null)
			{
				this.GatheringZipContentByteProgression(sender, e);
			}
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnDiskRequired(object sender, DiskRequiredEventArgs e)
		{
			ValidateParams(sender, e);
			if (this.DiskRequired != null)
			{
				this.DiskRequired(sender, e);
			}
		}
	}
	internal sealed class ZipHandler : IDisposable
	{
		private bool m_disposed;

		private static HybridDictionary mg_zipHandlerTable;

		private int m_referenceCount = 1;

		private int m_totalRefCount;

		private int m_modifyRefCount;

		private AbstractFile m_zipFile;

		private DateTime m_lastWriteTime = DateTime.MaxValue;

		private long m_lastSize;

		private Dirtiness m_dirty;

		private EndHeader m_endHeader;

		private SortedList m_itemHandlers = new SortedList(CaseInsensitiveComparer.DefaultInvariant);

		private string m_defaultDecryptionPassword = string.Empty;

		private string m_defaultEncryptionPassword = string.Empty;

		private EncryptionMethod m_defaultEncryptionMethod = EncryptionMethod.Compatible;

		private int m_defaultEncryptionStrength = 256;

		private CompressionMethod m_defaultCompressionMethod = CompressionMethod.Deflated;

		private CompressionLevel m_defaultCompressionLevel = CompressionLevel.Normal;

		private int m_folderCount;

		private int m_fileCount;

		private long m_compressedSizeTotal;

		private long m_uncompressedSizeTotal;

		private SfxPrefix m_sfxPrefix;

		private long m_splitSize;

		private SplitNameFormat m_splitNameFormat;

		private SplitNameFormat m_activeSplitNameFormat;

		private bool m_allowSpanning;

		private static AbstractFolder mg_defaultTempFolder;

		private AbstractFolder m_tempFolder;

		private static ExtraHeaderList mg_availableExtraHeaders;

		private static ExtraHeaderList mg_defaultExtraHeaders;

		private int m_openStreams;

		private bool m_preventStreams;

		private Stack m_cachedStreams = new Stack();

		private ArrayList m_busyStreams = new ArrayList();

		private AbstractFile m_cachedFile;

		private AbstractFile m_currentFile;

		private uint m_currentDisk = uint.MaxValue;

		public string ZipComment
		{
			get
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				return m_endHeader.ZipComment;
			}
			set
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				FileSystemEventsSession session = FileSystemEvents.Empty.CreateSession(this, null);
				AddRefModify(session);
				try
				{
					if (m_endHeader.ZipComment != value)
					{
						m_endHeader.ZipComment = value;
						Dirty = Dirtiness.Dirty;
					}
				}
				finally
				{
					ReleaseModify(session);
				}
			}
		}

		public string DefaultDecryptionPassword
		{
			get
			{
				return m_defaultDecryptionPassword;
			}
			set
			{
				m_defaultDecryptionPassword = value;
			}
		}

		public string DefaultEncryptionPassword
		{
			get
			{
				return m_defaultEncryptionPassword;
			}
			set
			{
				m_defaultEncryptionPassword = value;
			}
		}

		public EncryptionMethod DefaultEncryptionMethod
		{
			get
			{
				return m_defaultEncryptionMethod;
			}
			set
			{
				if (!Enum.IsDefined(typeof(EncryptionMethod), value))
				{
					throw new ArgumentException("Unknown encryption method.");
				}
				m_defaultEncryptionMethod = value;
			}
		}

		public int DefaultEncryptionStrength
		{
			get
			{
				return m_defaultEncryptionStrength;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != 128 && value != 192 && value != 256)
				{
					throw new ArgumentException("Invalid encryption strength. Possible values are 128, 192 and 256.");
				}
				m_defaultEncryptionStrength = value;
			}
		}

		public CompressionMethod DefaultCompressionMethod
		{
			get
			{
				return m_defaultCompressionMethod;
			}
			set
			{
				switch (value)
				{
				case CompressionMethod.Deflated64:
					Licenser.VerifyLicense(FeatureID.Deflate64);
					break;
				case CompressionMethod.BZip2:
				case CompressionMethod.BWT:
					Licenser.VerifyLicense(FeatureID.BZip2_BWT);
					break;
				case CompressionMethod.PPMd:
					Licenser.VerifyLicense(FeatureID.PPMd);
					break;
				}
				m_defaultCompressionMethod = value;
			}
		}

		public CompressionLevel DefaultCompressionLevel
		{
			get
			{
				return m_defaultCompressionLevel;
			}
			set
			{
				m_defaultCompressionLevel = value;
			}
		}

		public int FolderCount
		{
			get
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				return m_folderCount;
			}
		}

		public int FileCount
		{
			get
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				return m_fileCount;
			}
		}

		public long CompressedSizeTotal
		{
			get
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				return m_compressedSizeTotal;
			}
		}

		public long UncompressedSizeTotal
		{
			get
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				return m_uncompressedSizeTotal;
			}
		}

		public Dirtiness Dirty
		{
			get
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				return m_dirty;
			}
			set
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				m_dirty = value;
			}
		}

		public SfxPrefix SfxPrefix
		{
			get
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				return m_sfxPrefix;
			}
			set
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				FileSystemEventsSession session = FileSystemEvents.Empty.CreateSession(this, null);
				AddRefModify(session);
				try
				{
					if (m_sfxPrefix != value)
					{
						ClearSfxPrefix();
						SetSfxPrefix(value);
						Dirty = Dirtiness.Dirty;
					}
				}
				finally
				{
					ReleaseModify(session);
				}
			}
		}

		public long SplitSize
		{
			get
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				return m_splitSize;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != 0)
				{
					Licenser.VerifyLicense(FeatureID.Spanning);
				}
				CheckIfDisposed();
				if (value < 0)
				{
					Xceed.Utils.Exceptions.ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("SplitSize", value, "The split size cannot be less than zero.");
				}
				m_splitSize = value;
			}
		}

		public SplitNameFormat SplitNameFormat
		{
			get
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				return m_splitNameFormat;
			}
			set
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				if (!Enum.IsDefined(typeof(SplitNameFormat), value))
				{
					throw new ArgumentException("Unknown split name format value.", "SplitNameFormat");
				}
				m_splitNameFormat = value;
			}
		}

		public bool AllowSpanning
		{
			get
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				return m_allowSpanning;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value)
				{
					Licenser.VerifyLicense(FeatureID.Spanning);
				}
				CheckIfDisposed();
				m_allowSpanning = value;
			}
		}

		public AbstractFile CurrentZipFile
		{
			get
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				return m_currentFile;
			}
		}

		public uint CurrentDiskNumber
		{
			get
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				return m_currentDisk;
			}
		}

		public AbstractFile LastZipFile
		{
			get
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				return m_zipFile;
			}
		}

		public uint LastDiskNumber
		{
			get
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				if (m_endHeader == null)
				{
					return uint.MaxValue;
				}
				return m_endHeader.DiskNumber;
			}
		}

		public static AbstractFolder DefaultTempFolder
		{
			get
			{
				return mg_defaultTempFolder;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value == null)
				{
					throw new ArgumentNullException("DefaultTempFolder");
				}
				mg_defaultTempFolder = value;
			}
		}

		public AbstractFolder TempFolder
		{
			get
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				return m_tempFolder;
			}
			set
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				if (value == null)
				{
					throw new ArgumentNullException("TempFolder");
				}
				m_tempFolder = value;
			}
		}

		public static ExtraHeaderList AvailableExtraHeaders => mg_availableExtraHeaders;

		public static ExtraHeaderList DefaultExtraHeaders
		{
			get
			{
				if (mg_defaultExtraHeaders.Count > 0 && !Licenser.AllowExtraHeaders)
				{
					mg_defaultExtraHeaders.Clear();
				}
				return mg_defaultExtraHeaders;
			}
		}

		public void AddRef(FileSystemEventsSession session)
		{
			CheckIfDisposed();
			checked
			{
				m_totalRefCount++;
				_ = 1;
			}
		}

		public void AddRefModify(FileSystemEventsSession session)
		{
			CheckIfDisposed();
			AddRef(session);
			try
			{
				if (checked(++m_modifyRefCount) == 1)
				{
					Refresh(session);
				}
			}
			catch
			{
				Release(session);
				throw;
			}
		}

		public void AddRefStream(FileSystemEventsSession session)
		{
			CheckIfDisposed();
			if (m_preventStreams)
			{
				throw new ZipIOException("The zip file is being updated and cannot be open.");
			}
			AddRef(session);
			try
			{
				if (checked(++m_openStreams) == 1)
				{
					PrepareForReading();
				}
			}
			catch
			{
				Release(session);
				throw;
			}
		}

		public void Release(FileSystemEventsSession session)
		{
			CheckIfDisposed();
			if (m_totalRefCount == 0)
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException("Release was called once more than AddRef. Both calls should always match.");
			}
			checked
			{
				if (--m_totalRefCount == 0 && Dirty == Dirtiness.Dirty)
				{
					m_totalRefCount++;
					try
					{
						Flush(session);
					}
					finally
					{
						m_totalRefCount--;
					}
				}
			}
		}

		public void ReleaseModify(FileSystemEventsSession session)
		{
			CheckIfDisposed();
			if (m_modifyRefCount == 0)
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException("ReleaseModify was called once more than AddRefModify. Both calls should always match.");
			}
			checked
			{
				try
				{
					m_modifyRefCount--;
				}
				finally
				{
					Release(session);
				}
			}
		}

		public void ReleaseStream(FileSystemEventsSession session, Stream stream)
		{
			CheckIfDisposed();
			if (m_openStreams == 0)
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException("ReleaseStream was called once more than AddRefStream. Both calls should always match.");
			}
			try
			{
				if (stream != null)
				{
					m_busyStreams.Remove(stream);
					m_cachedStreams.Push(stream);
				}
				if (checked(--m_openStreams) == 0)
				{
					while (m_cachedStreams.Count > 0)
					{
						(m_cachedStreams.Pop() as Stream).Close();
					}
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				Release(session);
			}
		}

		public void PreventStreams()
		{
			if (m_preventStreams)
			{
				throw new FileSystemInternalException("An attempt was made to lock the ZipHandler when it is already locked.");
			}
			if (m_busyStreams.Count > 0)
			{
				throw new ZipIOException("An attempt was made to change disks while another file is open in the ZipArchive.");
			}
			m_preventStreams = true;
			while (m_cachedStreams.Count > 0)
			{
				(m_cachedStreams.Pop() as Stream).Close();
			}
			if (m_cachedFile != null)
			{
				if (m_cachedFile.Exists)
				{
					m_cachedFile.Delete();
				}
				m_cachedFile = null;
			}
		}

		public void AllowStreams(bool reading)
		{
			if (!m_preventStreams)
			{
				throw new FileSystemInternalException("An attempt was made to unlock the ZipHandler when it is already unlocked.");
			}
			try
			{
				if (reading)
				{
					PrepareForReading();
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				m_preventStreams = false;
			}
		}

		public void Refresh(FileSystemEventsSession session)
		{
			if ((LastDiskNumber != uint.MaxValue && LastDiskNumber != CurrentDiskNumber) || m_modifyRefCount > 0 || m_openStreams > 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			bool flag = true;
			try
			{
				if (m_zipFile.Exists)
				{
					m_zipFile.Refresh();
					flag = m_lastWriteTime != m_zipFile.LastWriteDateTime || m_lastSize != m_zipFile.Size;
				}
			}
			catch
			{
			}
			try
			{
				if (!flag)
				{
					return;
				}
				ResetContents();
				ZipStream zipStream = null;
				if (m_zipFile.Exists && m_zipFile.Size != 0)
				{
					try
					{
						zipStream = OpenRead(session);
					}
					catch (FileSystemNotSupportedException)
					{
					}
				}
				if (zipStream == null)
				{
					m_endHeader = new EndHeader();
					m_currentDisk = uint.MaxValue;
					GetItemHandler(Path.DirectorySeparatorChar.ToString());
					try
					{
						m_lastSize = 0L;
						m_lastWriteTime = m_zipFile.LastWriteDateTime;
						return;
					}
					catch
					{
						return;
					}
				}
				BinaryReader binaryReader = new BinaryReader(zipStream);
				try
				{
					ReadEndHeader(binaryReader, zipStream);
					try
					{
						m_lastSize = m_zipFile.Size;
						m_lastWriteTime = m_zipFile.LastWriteDateTime;
					}
					catch
					{
					}
					ReadCentralDirectory(session, binaryReader, zipStream);
				}
				finally
				{
					binaryReader.Close();
				}
			}
			catch (FileSystemIOException inner)
			{
				throw new ZipIOException(m_zipFile, inner);
			}
			catch (IOException inner2)
			{
				throw new ZipIOException(m_zipFile, inner2);
			}
		}

		public ZipStream OpenRead(FileSystemEventsSession session)
		{
			Stream stream = RawOpenRead(session);
			try
			{
				return new ZipStream(session, this, stream);
			}
			catch
			{
				ReleaseStream(session, stream);
				throw;
			}
		}

		public Stream RawOpenRead(FileSystemEventsSession session)
		{
			AddRefStream(session);
			Stream stream = null;
			try
			{
				if (m_cachedStreams.Count == 0)
				{
					stream = ((m_cachedFile == null) ? CurrentZipFile.OpenRead(session.Events, session.UserData) : m_cachedFile.OpenRead(session.Events, session.UserData));
				}
				else
				{
					stream = m_cachedStreams.Pop() as Stream;
					stream.Seek(0L, SeekOrigin.Begin);
				}
			}
			catch
			{
				ReleaseStream(session, stream);
				throw;
			}
			m_busyStreams.Add(stream);
			return stream;
		}

		public ZipStream OpenWrite(FileSystemEventsSession session)
		{
			bool flag = SplitSize > 0 || (CurrentDiskNumber != 0 && CurrentDiskNumber != uint.MaxValue);
			m_endHeader = new EndHeader(m_endHeader);
			if (flag)
			{
				ChangeDisk(session as ZipEventsSession, 0u, DiskRequiredReason.Updating);
			}
			else
			{
				m_currentFile = m_zipFile;
				m_currentDisk = 0u;
			}
			return new ZipStream(session, this);
		}

		public void ChangeDisk(ZipEventsSession session, uint diskNumber, DiskRequiredReason reason)
		{
			PreventStreams();
			try
			{
				DiskRequiredAction defaultAction = DiskRequiredAction.Fail;
				if (m_activeSplitNameFormat != SplitNameFormat.None || (reason == DiskRequiredReason.Updating && CurrentDiskNumber == 0))
				{
					defaultAction = DiskRequiredAction.Continue;
				}
				DiskRequiredEventArgs e = new DiskRequiredEventArgs(reason, GetSplitFile(LastZipFile, LastDiskNumber, diskNumber, m_activeSplitNameFormat), checked((int)(diskNumber + 1)), SplitSize, defaultAction);
				while (true)
				{
					session?.OnDiskRequired(e);
					if (e.Action == DiskRequiredAction.Fail)
					{
						throw new ZipIOException("The required disk was not inserted or the required zip file part was not provided.");
					}
					if (e.SplitSize != m_splitSize)
					{
						m_splitSize = e.SplitSize;
					}
					if (reason != DiskRequiredReason.Reading || e.ZipFile.Exists)
					{
						break;
					}
					e.Action = DiskRequiredAction.Fail;
				}
				m_currentFile = e.ZipFile;
				m_currentDisk = diskNumber;
			}
			finally
			{
				AllowStreams(reason == DiskRequiredReason.Reading);
			}
		}

		public static ZipHandler GetZipHandler(FileSystemEventsSession session, AbstractFile zipFile)
		{
			if (zipFile == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("zipFile");
			}
			SplitNameFormat splitNameFormat = DeduceSplitNameFormat(ref zipFile);
			object key = zipFile;
			lock (mg_zipHandlerTable.SyncRoot)
			{
				if (mg_zipHandlerTable.Contains(key))
				{
					ZipHandler zipHandler = (ZipHandler)mg_zipHandlerTable[key];
					zipHandler.AddHandlerReference();
					return zipHandler;
				}
				ZipHandler zipHandler2 = new ZipHandler(session, zipFile, splitNameFormat);
				mg_zipHandlerTable.Add(key, zipHandler2);
				zipHandler2.Refresh(session);
				return zipHandler2;
			}
		}

		public static ZipHandler GetZipHandler(ZipHandler handler)
		{
			if (handler == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("handler");
			}
			object lastZipFile = handler.LastZipFile;
			lock (mg_zipHandlerTable.SyncRoot)
			{
				if (!mg_zipHandlerTable.Contains(lastZipFile))
				{
					throw new FileSystemInternalException("Missing ZipHandler in main table.");
				}
				handler.AddHandlerReference();
				return handler;
			}
		}

		public static void UpdateZipHandlerKey(ZipHandler handler, AbstractFile oldZipFile, AbstractFile newZipFile)
		{
			if (handler == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("handler");
			}
			if (oldZipFile == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("oldZipFile");
			}
			if (newZipFile == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("newZipFile");
			}
			object key = oldZipFile;
			lock (mg_zipHandlerTable.SyncRoot)
			{
				if (!mg_zipHandlerTable.Contains(key))
				{
					throw new FileSystemInternalException("Missing ZipHandler in main table.");
				}
				ZipHandler zipHandler = (ZipHandler)mg_zipHandlerTable[key];
				if (zipHandler != handler)
				{
					throw new FileSystemInternalException("Invalid ZipHandler in main table.");
				}
				mg_zipHandlerTable.Remove(key);
				key = newZipFile;
				mg_zipHandlerTable.Add(key, handler);
			}
		}

		public static void RemoveZipHandler(ZipHandler handler)
		{
			if (handler == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("handler");
			}
			if (handler.m_disposed)
			{
				return;
			}
			object lastZipFile = handler.LastZipFile;
			lock (mg_zipHandlerTable.SyncRoot)
			{
				if (!mg_zipHandlerTable.Contains(lastZipFile))
				{
					throw new FileSystemInternalException("Missing ZipHandler in main table.");
				}
				ZipHandler zipHandler = (ZipHandler)mg_zipHandlerTable[lastZipFile];
				if (zipHandler != handler)
				{
					throw new FileSystemInternalException("Invalid ZipHandler in main table.");
				}
				if (handler.ReleaseHandlerReference())
				{
					mg_zipHandlerTable.Remove(lastZipFile);
					handler.Dispose();
				}
			}
		}

		public static string GetParentFullName(string fullName)
		{
			try
			{
				return Path.GetDirectoryName(fullName.TrimEnd(Path.DirectorySeparatorChar)).TrimEnd(Path.DirectorySeparatorChar) + Path.DirectorySeparatorChar;
			}
			catch (PathTooLongException inner)
			{
				throw new FileSystemIOException("The parent's full name is too long for item : " + fullName, null, inner);
			}
		}

		public ZipItemHandler GetItemHandler(string fullName)
		{
			if (m_itemHandlers.Contains(fullName))
			{
				return (ZipItemHandler)m_itemHandlers[fullName];
			}
			ZipItemHandler zipItemHandler = new ZipItemHandler(this, fullName);
			if (fullName.Length > 1)
			{
				ZipItemHandler itemHandler = GetItemHandler(GetParentFullName(fullName));
				itemHandler.AddChild(zipItemHandler);
			}
			m_itemHandlers.Add(fullName, zipItemHandler);
			return zipItemHandler;
		}

		public void AddItemHandler(ZipItemHandler item)
		{
			string fullName = item.FullName;
			if (m_itemHandlers.Contains(fullName))
			{
				ZipItemHandler zipItemHandler = m_itemHandlers[fullName] as ZipItemHandler;
				while (zipItemHandler != null && zipItemHandler.Exists && (!zipItemHandler.IsFolder || zipItemHandler.FolderPersistent))
				{
					item.IncrementFullName();
					fullName = item.FullName;
					zipItemHandler = ((!m_itemHandlers.Contains(fullName)) ? null : (m_itemHandlers[fullName] as ZipItemHandler));
				}
				if (zipItemHandler != null)
				{
					item.Replace(zipItemHandler);
					m_itemHandlers.Remove(fullName);
				}
			}
			ZipItemHandler itemHandler = GetItemHandler(GetParentFullName(fullName));
			itemHandler.AddChild(item);
			m_itemHandlers.Add(fullName, item);
		}

		public void RemoveItemHandler(string fullName)
		{
			if (m_itemHandlers.Contains(fullName))
			{
				m_itemHandlers.Remove(fullName);
			}
		}

		public bool ItemExists(string fullName)
		{
			if (m_itemHandlers.Contains(fullName))
			{
				ZipItemHandler zipItemHandler = m_itemHandlers[fullName] as ZipItemHandler;
				return zipItemHandler.Exists;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public void Dispose()
		{
			Dispose(disposing: true);
			GC.SuppressFinalize(this);
		}

		~ZipHandler()
		{
			Dispose(disposing: false);
		}

		private void Dispose(bool disposing)
		{
			if (!m_disposed)
			{
				m_cachedStreams = null;
				m_busyStreams = null;
				m_cachedFile = null;
				m_endHeader = null;
				m_disposed = true;
			}
		}

		private void CheckIfDisposed()
		{
			if (m_disposed)
			{
				throw new ObjectDisposedException("ZipHandler", "Cannot access a disposed object.");
			}
		}

		static ZipHandler()
		{
			mg_zipHandlerTable = new HybridDictionary();
			mg_defaultTempFolder = new DiskFolder(Path.GetTempPath());
			mg_availableExtraHeaders = new ExtraHeaderList();
			mg_defaultExtraHeaders = new ExtraHeaderList();
			UnicodeExtraHeader value = new UnicodeExtraHeader();
			FileTimesExtraHeader value2 = new FileTimesExtraHeader();
			ExtendedTimeStampExtraHeader value3 = new ExtendedTimeStampExtraHeader();
			Zip64ExtraHeader value4 = new Zip64ExtraHeader();
			AesExtraHeader value5 = new AesExtraHeader();
			mg_availableExtraHeaders.Add(value);
			mg_availableExtraHeaders.Add(value2);
			mg_availableExtraHeaders.Add(value3);
			mg_availableExtraHeaders.Add(value4);
			mg_availableExtraHeaders.Add(value5);
			mg_defaultExtraHeaders.Add(value);
			mg_defaultExtraHeaders.Add(value2);
		}

		private ZipHandler(FileSystemEventsSession session, AbstractFile zipFile, SplitNameFormat splitNameFormat)
		{
			if (zipFile == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("zipFile");
			}
			m_zipFile = zipFile;
			m_currentFile = zipFile;
			m_tempFolder = mg_defaultTempFolder;
			m_splitNameFormat = splitNameFormat;
			m_activeSplitNameFormat = splitNameFormat;
		}

		private void Flush(FileSystemEventsSession session)
		{
			CheckIfDisposed();
			if (Dirty != Dirtiness.Dirty)
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException("Cannot call Flush when not dirty.");
			}
			ZipEventsSession session2 = session as ZipEventsSession;
			checked
			{
				try
				{
					MoveFilesToKeep(session);
					uint lastDiskNumber = LastDiskNumber;
					SplitNameFormat activeSplitNameFormat = m_activeSplitNameFormat;
					m_activeSplitNameFormat = m_splitNameFormat;
					ZipStream zipStream = OpenWrite(session);
					BinaryWriter binaryWriter = new BinaryWriter(zipStream);
					try
					{
						WriteSfxPrefix(zipStream);
						WriteZipToStream(session, binaryWriter);
					}
					finally
					{
						binaryWriter.Close();
					}
					if (SplitNameFormat == SplitNameFormat.PkZip || SplitNameFormat == SplitNameFormat.WinZip)
					{
						m_currentFile.MoveTo(m_zipFile, replaceIfExists: true);
						m_currentFile = m_zipFile;
					}
					else
					{
						UpdateZipHandlerKey(this, m_zipFile, m_currentFile);
						m_zipFile = m_currentFile;
					}
					if (LastDiskNumber < lastDiskNumber && lastDiskNumber != uint.MaxValue)
					{
						m_activeSplitNameFormat = activeSplitNameFormat;
						try
						{
							for (uint num = LastDiskNumber + 1; num <= lastDiskNumber; num++)
							{
								ChangeDisk(session2, num, DiskRequiredReason.Deleting);
								try
								{
									m_currentFile.Delete();
								}
								catch
								{
								}
							}
						}
						catch
						{
						}
						finally
						{
							m_activeSplitNameFormat = m_splitNameFormat;
						}
					}
				}
				catch (FileSystemIOException inner)
				{
					throw new ZipIOException(m_zipFile, inner);
				}
				catch (IOException inner2)
				{
					throw new ZipIOException(m_zipFile, inner2);
				}
				m_dirty = Dirtiness.Clean;
				Refresh(session);
			}
		}

		private void ResetContents()
		{
			CheckIfDisposed();
			m_itemHandlers.Clear();
			m_endHeader = null;
			m_currentDisk = uint.MaxValue;
			if (m_cachedFile != null)
			{
				if (m_cachedFile.Exists)
				{
					m_cachedFile.Delete();
				}
				m_cachedFile = null;
			}
			ClearSfxPrefix();
		}

		private static SplitNameFormat DeduceSplitNameFormat(ref AbstractFile zipFile)
		{
			string fileNameWithoutExtension = Path.GetFileNameWithoutExtension(zipFile.Name);
			string extension = Path.GetExtension(zipFile.Name);
			if (extension.Length > 1)
			{
				AbstractFolder parentFolder = zipFile.ParentFolder;
				if (parentFolder.GetFile(fileNameWithoutExtension + extension.Substring(0, 2) + "01").Exists)
				{
					return SplitNameFormat.WinZip;
				}
				if (parentFolder.GetFile(zipFile.Name + extension.Substring(0, 2) + "01").Exists)
				{
					return SplitNameFormat.PkZip;
				}
				AbstractFile file = parentFolder.GetFile(fileNameWithoutExtension + extension.Substring(0, 2) + "02");
				if (file.Exists)
				{
					bool flag = true;
					try
					{
						int.Parse(extension.Substring(2));
						flag = false;
					}
					catch
					{
					}
					if (flag)
					{
						AbstractFile[] files = parentFolder.GetFiles(false, fileNameWithoutExtension + extension.Substring(0, 2) + "*");
						int num = 2;
						AbstractFile abstractFile = file;
						AbstractFile[] array = files;
						foreach (AbstractFile abstractFile2 in array)
						{
							try
							{
								int num2 = int.Parse(abstractFile2.Name.Substring(checked(fileNameWithoutExtension.Length + 2)));
								if (num2 > num)
								{
									num = num2;
									abstractFile = abstractFile2;
								}
							}
							catch
							{
							}
						}
						zipFile = abstractFile;
					}
					return SplitNameFormat.XceedZipActiveX;
				}
			}
			return SplitNameFormat.None;
		}

		private void ReadCentralDirectory(FileSystemEventsSession session, BinaryReader reader, ZipStream stream)
		{
			CheckIfDisposed();
			ZipEventsSession zipEventsSession = session as ZipEventsSession;
			if (reader == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("reader");
			}
			if (stream == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("stream");
			}
			if (!stream.CanRead)
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("The stream must be readable.", "strea       m");
			}
			if (!stream.CanSeek)
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("The stream must be seekable.", "stream");
			}
			if (m_endHeader.CentralDiskNumber != stream.DiskNumber)
			{
				stream.ChangeDisk(m_endHeader.CentralDiskNumber, DiskRequiredReason.Reading);
			}
			checked
			{
				try
				{
					uint num = m_endHeader.DiskNumber;
					long num2 = m_endHeader.Offset;
					GetItemHandler(Path.DirectorySeparatorChar.ToString());
					zipEventsSession?.InitializeTotalItems((long)m_endHeader.CentralHeadersTotal);
					if (m_endHeader.CentralHeadersTotal != 0)
					{
						num = m_endHeader.CentralDiskNumber;
						num2 = m_endHeader.CentralDirectoryOffset;
						stream.Seek(m_endHeader.CentralDirectoryOffset, SeekOrigin.Begin);
						string text = "\\noname" + CurrentZipFile.LastWriteDateTime.ToString("yyyyMMddHHmmss");
						int num3 = 1;
						for (ulong num4 = 0uL; num4 < m_endHeader.CentralHeadersTotal; num4++)
						{
							byte[] array = reader.ReadBytes(4);
							if (array.Length != 4)
							{
								throw new InvalidZipStructureException("Could not find a central header where one was expected in the zip file.", m_zipFile);
							}
							if (unchecked((long)BitConverter.ToInt32(array, 0)) != 33639248)
							{
								throw new InvalidZipStructureException("Could not find a central header where one was expected in the zip file.", m_zipFile);
							}
							ItemHeader itemHeader = new ItemHeader();
							itemHeader.ReadCentralHeader(reader);
							if (itemHeader.FileName.Length == 0 && !itemHeader.IsFolder)
							{
								itemHeader.FileName = text + "\\noname" + num3;
								num3++;
							}
							if (itemHeader.FileName.Length > 0)
							{
								if (num > itemHeader.LocalHeaderDisk || (num == itemHeader.LocalHeaderDisk && num2 > itemHeader.LocalHeaderOffset))
								{
									num = itemHeader.LocalHeaderDisk;
									num2 = itemHeader.LocalHeaderOffset;
								}
								ZipItemHandler item = new ZipItemHandler(this, itemHeader);
								AddItemHandler(item);
							}
							zipEventsSession?.OnReadingZipItemProgression(m_zipFile);
						}
					}
					zipEventsSession?.OnReadingZipItemCompletion();
					if ((num != 0 || num2 > 0) && (num != 0 || num2 != 4) && m_endHeader.DiskNumber == 0)
					{
						stream.Seek(0L, SeekOrigin.Begin);
						SfxPrefix sfxPrefix = SfxPrefix.CreateSfxPrefix(stream, num2);
						if (sfxPrefix == null)
						{
							throw new FileSystemInternalException("Default prefix could not be created.");
						}
						SetSfxPrefix(sfxPrefix);
					}
				}
				catch (FileSystemIOException inner)
				{
					throw new ZipIOException(m_zipFile, inner);
				}
				catch (IOException inner2)
				{
					throw new ZipIOException(m_zipFile, inner2);
				}
			}
		}

		private void ReadEndHeader(BinaryReader reader, ZipStream stream)
		{
			CheckIfDisposed();
			if (reader == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("reader");
			}
			if (stream == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("stream");
			}
			if (!stream.CanRead)
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("The stream must be readable.", "stream");
			}
			if (!stream.CanSeek)
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("The stream must be seekable.", "stream");
			}
			try
			{
				stream.Seek(0L, SeekOrigin.End);
				StreamSearcher streamSearcher = new StreamSearcher(stream);
				if (!streamSearcher.ReverseFind(101010256u))
				{
					throw new InvalidZipStructureException("The zip end header signature could not be found in the zip file.", m_zipFile);
				}
				m_endHeader = new EndHeader(reader);
				m_currentDisk = m_endHeader.DiskNumber;
				if (!m_endHeader.Zip64Format)
				{
					return;
				}
				Licenser.VerifyLicense(FeatureID.Zip64);
				if (!streamSearcher.ReverseFind(117853008u, withinRange: true))
				{
					return;
				}
				m_endHeader.ReadZip64EndLocator(reader);
				if (m_endHeader.Zip64Format)
				{
					if (m_endHeader.DiskNumber != m_endHeader.Zip64EndHeaderDiskNumber)
					{
						stream.ChangeDisk(m_endHeader.Zip64EndHeaderDiskNumber, DiskRequiredReason.Reading);
					}
					stream.Seek(m_endHeader.Zip64EndHeaderOffset, SeekOrigin.Begin);
					byte[] array = reader.ReadBytes(4);
					if (array.Length != 4)
					{
						throw new InvalidZipStructureException("Could not find a Zip64 End Header where one was expected.", m_zipFile);
					}
					if ((long)BitConverter.ToInt32(array, 0) != 101075792)
					{
						throw new InvalidZipStructureException("Could not find a Zip64 End Header where one was expected.", m_zipFile);
					}
					m_endHeader.ReadZip64EndHeader(reader);
				}
			}
			catch (FileSystemIOException inner)
			{
				throw new ZipIOException(m_zipFile, inner);
			}
			catch (IOException inner2)
			{
				throw new ZipIOException(m_zipFile, inner2);
			}
		}

		private void MoveFilesToKeep(FileSystemEventsSession session)
		{
			if (session is ZipEventsSession zipEventsSession && zipEventsSession.Events.WantsGatheringZipContentByteProgression)
			{
				zipEventsSession.InitializeTotalBytes(CountBytesToKeep());
			}
			session.Events.BeginSubOperation();
			try
			{
				foreach (ZipItemHandler value in m_itemHandlers.Values)
				{
					if (value.Exists && !value.IsFolder)
					{
						value.MoveFromZipToTemp(session);
					}
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				session.Events.EndSubOperation();
			}
		}

		private long CountBytesToKeep()
		{
			long num = 0L;
			foreach (ZipItemHandler value in m_itemHandlers.Values)
			{
				if (value.Exists && !value.IsFolder)
				{
					num = checked(num + value.CompressedSize);
				}
			}
			return num;
		}

		private void WriteZipToStream(FileSystemEventsSession session, BinaryWriter writer)
		{
			CheckIfDisposed();
			if (writer == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("writer");
			}
			m_folderCount = 0;
			m_fileCount = 0;
			m_compressedSizeTotal = 0L;
			m_uncompressedSizeTotal = 0L;
			ZipEventsSession zipEventsSession = session as ZipEventsSession;
			try
			{
				if (zipEventsSession != null)
				{
					long totalItems = 0L;
					long totalBytes = 0L;
					CalculateTotals(ref totalItems, ref totalBytes);
					zipEventsSession.InitializeTotalItems(totalItems);
					zipEventsSession.InitializeTotalBytes(totalBytes);
				}
				if (AllowSpanning || SplitSize > 0)
				{
					writer.Write(134695760u);
				}
				WriteLocalHeaders(session, writer);
				zipEventsSession?.OnBuildingZipItemCompletion();
				m_endHeader.CentralDirectoryOffset = 0L;
				m_endHeader.CentralDirectorySize = 0uL;
				m_endHeader.CentralDiskNumber = 0u;
				m_endHeader.CentralHeadersCurrentDisk = 0uL;
				m_endHeader.CentralHeadersTotal = 0uL;
				WriteCentralDirectory(writer);
				ZipStream zipStream = writer.BaseStream as ZipStream;
				m_endHeader.DiskNumber = CurrentDiskNumber;
				if (m_endHeader.Zip64Format)
				{
					Licenser.VerifyLicense(FeatureID.Zip64);
					m_endHeader.Zip64EndHeaderDiskNumber = CurrentDiskNumber;
					m_endHeader.Zip64EndHeaderOffset = zipStream.Position;
					m_endHeader.WriteZip64EndHeader(zipStream);
					if (m_endHeader.Zip64EndHeaderDiskNumber != CurrentDiskNumber)
					{
						m_endHeader.Zip64EndHeaderDiskNumber = CurrentDiskNumber;
						m_endHeader.CentralHeadersCurrentDisk = 0uL;
						m_endHeader.Zip64EndHeaderOffset = 0L;
						long position = writer.BaseStream.Position;
						writer.Seek(0, SeekOrigin.Begin);
						m_endHeader.WriteZip64EndHeader(zipStream);
						if (m_endHeader.Zip64EndHeaderDiskNumber != CurrentDiskNumber || zipStream.Position != position)
						{
							throw new FileSystemInternalException("The Zip64 ending header spanned on the last disk and could not be rewritten to the same position.");
						}
					}
				}
				m_endHeader.DiskNumber = CurrentDiskNumber;
				m_endHeader.Offset = zipStream.Position;
				m_endHeader.WriteToStream(zipStream);
				if (m_endHeader.DiskNumber != CurrentDiskNumber)
				{
					m_endHeader.DiskNumber = CurrentDiskNumber;
					m_endHeader.CentralHeadersCurrentDisk = 0uL;
					m_endHeader.Offset = 0L;
					long position2 = zipStream.Position;
					writer.Seek(0, SeekOrigin.Begin);
					m_endHeader.WriteToStream(zipStream);
					if (m_endHeader.DiskNumber != CurrentDiskNumber || zipStream.Position != position2)
					{
						throw new FileSystemInternalException("The ending header spanned on the last disk and could not be rewritten to the same position.");
					}
				}
			}
			catch (FileSystemIOException inner)
			{
				throw new ZipIOException(m_zipFile, inner);
			}
			catch (IOException inner2)
			{
				throw new ZipIOException(m_zipFile, inner2);
			}
		}

		private void CalculateTotals(ref long totalItems, ref long totalBytes)
		{
			checked
			{
				foreach (ZipItemHandler value in m_itemHandlers.Values)
				{
					if (value.Exists && (!value.IsFolder || value.FolderPersistent))
					{
						totalItems++;
						totalBytes += value.CompressedSize;
					}
				}
			}
		}

		private void WriteLocalHeaders(FileSystemEventsSession session, BinaryWriter writer)
		{
			if (writer == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("writer");
			}
			ZipEventsSession zipEventsSession = session as ZipEventsSession;
			foreach (ZipItemHandler value in m_itemHandlers.Values)
			{
				if (value != null && value.Exists && (!value.IsFolder || value.FolderPersistent))
				{
					zipEventsSession?.OnBuildingZipItemProgression(m_zipFile);
					value.WriteLocalHeader(session, writer.BaseStream as ZipStream);
				}
			}
		}

		private void WriteCentralDirectory(BinaryWriter writer)
		{
			if (writer == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("writer");
			}
			foreach (ZipItemHandler value in m_itemHandlers.Values)
			{
				if (value != null && value.Exists && (!value.IsFolder || value.FolderPersistent))
				{
					value.WriteCentralHeader(writer.BaseStream as ZipStream, m_endHeader);
				}
			}
		}

		private void WriteSfxPrefix(ZipStream stream)
		{
			if (stream == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("stream");
			}
			if (!stream.CanWrite)
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("The stream must be writable.", "stream");
			}
			if (m_sfxPrefix == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			stream.AllowBreak = false;
			try
			{
				m_sfxPrefix.WriteSfxPrefix(stream);
			}
			finally
			{
				stream.AllowBreak = true;
			}
		}

		private void ClearSfxPrefix()
		{
			INotifyChanges sfxPrefix = m_sfxPrefix;
			if (sfxPrefix != null)
			{
				sfxPrefix.Changed -= SfxPrefixChanged;
			}
			m_sfxPrefix = null;
		}

		private void SetSfxPrefix(SfxPrefix prefix)
		{
			if (prefix != null)
			{
				((INotifyChanges)prefix).Changed += SfxPrefixChanged;
			}
			m_sfxPrefix = prefix;
		}

		private void SfxPrefixChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			FileSystemEventsSession session = FileSystemEvents.Empty.CreateSession(this, null);
			AddRefModify(session);
			try
			{
				Dirty = Dirtiness.Dirty;
			}
			finally
			{
				ReleaseModify(session);
			}
		}

		private AbstractFile GetSplitFile(AbstractFile zipFile, uint lastDiskNumber, uint currentDiskNumber, SplitNameFormat splitNameFormat)
		{
			if (splitNameFormat == SplitNameFormat.None)
			{
				return zipFile;
			}
			string fileNameWithoutExtension = Path.GetFileNameWithoutExtension(zipFile.Name);
			string text = Path.GetExtension(zipFile.Name);
			if (text == null || text.Length < 2)
			{
				text = ".zip";
			}
			currentDiskNumber++;
			lastDiskNumber++;
			if (currentDiskNumber == 1 && splitNameFormat == SplitNameFormat.XceedZipActiveX)
			{
				fileNameWithoutExtension += ".zip";
			}
			else
			{
				if (lastDiskNumber != 0 && currentDiskNumber == lastDiskNumber && (splitNameFormat == SplitNameFormat.PkZip || splitNameFormat == SplitNameFormat.WinZip))
				{
					return zipFile;
				}
				fileNameWithoutExtension = ((splitNameFormat != SplitNameFormat.PkZip) ? (fileNameWithoutExtension + text.Substring(0, 2) + currentDiskNumber.ToString("D2")) : ((currentDiskNumber <= 99) ? (fileNameWithoutExtension + text + text.Substring(0, 2) + currentDiskNumber.ToString("D2")) : (fileNameWithoutExtension + text + "." + currentDiskNumber.ToString("D2"))));
			}
			return zipFile.ParentFolder.GetFile(fileNameWithoutExtension);
		}

		private void AddHandlerReference()
		{
			checked
			{
				m_referenceCount++;
			}
		}

		private bool ReleaseHandlerReference()
		{
			if (m_referenceCount == 0)
			{
				throw new FileSystemInternalException("Invalid ZipHandler reference count.");
			}
			return checked(--m_referenceCount) == 0;
		}

		private void PrepareForReading()
		{
			if (m_cachedFile == null)
			{
				Stream stream = null;
				stream = ((m_cachedStreams.Count != 1) ? CurrentZipFile.OpenRead() : ((Stream)m_cachedStreams.Pop()));
				if (!stream.CanSeek)
				{
					Stream stream2 = null;
					try
					{
						m_cachedFile = new MemoryFile();
						m_cachedFile.Create();
						stream2 = m_cachedFile.OpenWrite(overwrite: true);
						if (!stream2.CanSeek)
						{
							throw new FileSystemNotSupportedException("Files created in the TempFolder must provide seekable streams.", TempFolder);
						}
						StreamCopy.Copy(stream, stream2, -1L);
						stream2.Close();
						stream2 = m_cachedFile.OpenRead();
						m_cachedStreams.Push(stream2);
						return;
					}
					catch
					{
						stream2?.Close();
						if (m_cachedFile != null)
						{
							if (m_cachedFile.Exists)
							{
								m_cachedFile.Delete();
							}
							m_cachedFile = null;
						}
						throw;
					}
					finally
					{
						stream.Close();
					}
				}
				m_cachedStreams.Push(stream);
			}
			else
			{
				Stream obj2 = m_cachedFile.OpenRead();
				m_cachedStreams.Push(obj2);
			}
		}
	}
	internal sealed class ZipItemHandler
	{
		private ZipHandler m_zipHandler;

		private AbstractFile m_tempFile;

		private ItemHeader m_header;

		private bool m_folderPersistent = true;

		private bool m_exists;

		public SortedList m_children = new SortedList(CaseInsensitiveComparer.DefaultInvariant);

		private int m_openReadRefCount;

		private bool m_isFileShareRead = true;

		private bool m_isOpenWrite;

		private int m_copyIndex;

		private string m_copyTemplate = string.Empty;

		public string FullName
		{
			get
			{
				return m_header.FileName;
			}
			set
			{
				string fullName = FullName;
				if (!(value != fullName))
				{
					return;
				}
				FileSystemEventsSession session = FileSystemEvents.Empty.CreateSession(this, null);
				m_zipHandler.AddRefModify(session);
				try
				{
					if (m_zipHandler.ItemExists(value))
					{
						throw new ItemAlreadyExistsException(null);
					}
					SortedList sortedList = m_children.Clone() as SortedList;
					m_zipHandler.RemoveItemHandler(fullName);
					m_zipHandler.RemoveItemHandler(value);
					m_header.FileName = value;
					m_zipHandler.AddItemHandler(this);
					foreach (ZipItemHandler value2 in sortedList.Values)
					{
						string text = value2.FullName.Substring(fullName.Length);
						value2.FullName = value + text;
					}
					ZipItemHandler itemHandler = m_zipHandler.GetItemHandler(ZipHandler.GetParentFullName(value));
					itemHandler.m_children.Remove(fullName);
					if (Exists)
					{
						m_zipHandler.Dirty = Dirtiness.Dirty;
					}
				}
				finally
				{
					m_zipHandler.ReleaseModify(session);
				}
			}
		}

		public ZipHandler ZipHandler => m_zipHandler;

		public bool IsFolder => m_header.IsFolder;

		public FileAttributes FileAttributes
		{
			get
			{
				return m_header.FileAttributes;
			}
			set
			{
				if ((value & FileAttributes.Directory) == 0 && IsFolder)
				{
					throw new ArgumentException("A folder can't be turned into a file.");
				}
				if ((value & FileAttributes.Directory) != 0 && !IsFolder)
				{
					throw new ArgumentException("A file can't be turn into a folder.");
				}
				if (m_header.FileAttributes == value)
				{
					return;
				}
				FileSystemEventsSession session = FileSystemEvents.Empty.CreateSession(this, null);
				m_zipHandler.AddRefModify(session);
				try
				{
					m_header.FileAttributes = value;
					if (IsFolder)
					{
						FolderPersistent = true;
					}
					if (Exists)
					{
						m_zipHandler.Dirty = Dirtiness.Dirty;
					}
				}
				finally
				{
					m_zipHandler.ReleaseModify(session);
				}
			}
		}

		public DateTime CreationDateTime
		{
			get
			{
				return m_header.CreationDateTime;
			}
			set
			{
				if (!(m_header.CreationDateTime != value))
				{
					return;
				}
				FileSystemEventsSession session = FileSystemEvents.Empty.CreateSession(this, null);
				m_zipHandler.AddRefModify(session);
				try
				{
					m_header.CreationDateTime = value;
					if (IsFolder)
					{
						FolderPersistent = true;
					}
					if (Exists)
					{
						m_zipHandler.Dirty = Dirtiness.Dirty;
					}
				}
				finally
				{
					m_zipHandler.ReleaseModify(session);
				}
			}
		}

		public DateTime LastModifiedDateTime
		{
			get
			{
				return m_header.LastModifiedDateTime;
			}
			set
			{
				if ((!IsFolder && Encrypted && EncryptionMethod == EncryptionMethod.Compatible && m_header.HasDescriptorHeader) || !(m_header.LastModifiedDateTime != value))
				{
					return;
				}
				FileSystemEventsSession session = FileSystemEvents.Empty.CreateSession(this, null);
				m_zipHandler.AddRefModify(session);
				try
				{
					m_header.LastModifiedDateTime = value;
					if (IsFolder)
					{
						FolderPersistent = true;
					}
					if (Exists)
					{
						m_zipHandler.Dirty = Dirtiness.Dirty;
					}
				}
				finally
				{
					m_zipHandler.ReleaseModify(session);
				}
			}
		}

		public DateTime LastAccessDateTime
		{
			get
			{
				return m_header.LastAccessedDateTime;
			}
			set
			{
				if (!(m_header.LastAccessedDateTime != value))
				{
					return;
				}
				FileSystemEventsSession session = FileSystemEvents.Empty.CreateSession(this, null);
				m_zipHandler.AddRefModify(session);
				try
				{
					m_header.LastAccessedDateTime = value;
					if (IsFolder)
					{
						FolderPersistent = true;
					}
					if (Exists)
					{
						m_zipHandler.Dirty = Dirtiness.Dirty;
					}
				}
				finally
				{
					m_zipHandler.ReleaseModify(session);
				}
			}
		}

		public bool Exists
		{
			get
			{
				return m_exists;
			}
			set
			{
				if (!m_exists && value)
				{
					ZipItemHandler itemHandler = m_zipHandler.GetItemHandler(ZipHandler.GetParentFullName(FullName));
					itemHandler.Exists = true;
				}
				m_exists = value;
			}
		}

		public CompressionLevel CompressionLevel
		{
			get
			{
				return m_header.CompressionLevel;
			}
			set
			{
				m_header.CompressionLevel = value;
			}
		}

		public CompressionMethod CompressionMethod
		{
			get
			{
				return (CompressionMethod)m_header.CompressionMethod;
			}
			set
			{
				switch (value)
				{
				case CompressionMethod.Deflated64:
					Licenser.VerifyLicense(FeatureID.Deflate64);
					break;
				case CompressionMethod.BZip2:
				case CompressionMethod.BWT:
					Licenser.VerifyLicense(FeatureID.BZip2_BWT);
					break;
				case CompressionMethod.PPMd:
					Licenser.VerifyLicense(FeatureID.PPMd);
					break;
				}
				m_header.CompressionMethod = (ZipCompressionMethod)checked((ushort)value);
			}
		}

		public uint Crc32
		{
			get
			{
				return m_header.Crc32;
			}
			set
			{
				m_header.Crc32 = value;
			}
		}

		public long CompressedSize
		{
			get
			{
				return m_header.CompressedSize;
			}
			set
			{
				m_header.CompressedSize = value;
			}
		}

		public long UncompressedSize
		{
			get
			{
				return m_header.UncompressedSize;
			}
			set
			{
				m_header.UncompressedSize = value;
			}
		}

		public string FileComment
		{
			get
			{
				return m_header.FileComment;
			}
			set
			{
				if (!(m_header.FileComment != value))
				{
					return;
				}
				FileSystemEventsSession session = FileSystemEvents.Empty.CreateSession(this, null);
				m_zipHandler.AddRefModify(session);
				try
				{
					m_header.FileComment = value;
					if (IsFolder)
					{
						FolderPersistent = true;
					}
					if (Exists)
					{
						m_zipHandler.Dirty = Dirtiness.Dirty;
					}
				}
				finally
				{
					m_zipHandler.ReleaseModify(session);
				}
			}
		}

		public bool Encrypted => m_header.Encrypted;

		public EncryptionMethod EncryptionMethod => m_header.EncryptionMethod;

		public int EncryptionStrength => m_header.EncryptionStrength;

		public bool FolderPersistent
		{
			get
			{
				return m_folderPersistent;
			}
			set
			{
				m_folderPersistent = value;
			}
		}

		public ICollection ChildItems => m_children.Values;

		public uint DiskNumber => m_header.LocalHeaderDisk;

		public bool UsesTempFile => m_tempFile != null;

		public ExtraHeaderList ExtraHeaders => m_header.ExtraHeaders;

		public ZipItemHandler(ZipHandler zipHandler, ItemHeader header)
		{
			if (zipHandler == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("zipHandler");
			}
			if (header == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("header");
			}
			m_zipHandler = zipHandler;
			m_header = header;
			m_exists = true;
		}

		public ZipItemHandler(ZipHandler zipHandler, string fullName)
		{
			if (zipHandler == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("zipHandler");
			}
			if (fullName == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("fullName");
			}
			m_zipHandler = zipHandler;
			m_header = new ItemHeader(fullName);
			m_exists = fullName.Length == 1;
			if (fullName.EndsWith(Path.DirectorySeparatorChar.ToString()))
			{
				m_folderPersistent = false;
			}
		}

		private Stream OpenRawRead(FileSystemEventsSession session)
		{
			try
			{
				Stream stream = null;
				if (m_tempFile == null)
				{
					if (m_zipHandler.CurrentDiskNumber != m_header.LocalHeaderDisk)
					{
						m_zipHandler.ChangeDisk(session as ZipEventsSession, m_header.LocalHeaderDisk, DiskRequiredReason.Reading);
					}
					ZipStream zipStream = m_zipHandler.OpenRead(session);
					try
					{
						zipStream.Seek(m_header.LocalHeaderOffset, SeekOrigin.Begin);
						byte[] array = new byte[4];
						zipStream.Read(array, 0, array.Length);
						if ((long)BitConverter.ToInt32(array, 0) != 67324752)
						{
							throw new InvalidZipStructureException("Could not find a local header where one was expected in the zip file.", m_zipHandler.LastZipFile);
						}
						BinaryReader binaryReader = new BinaryReader(new TransientStream(zipStream));
						try
						{
							if (!m_header.ReadLocalHeader(binaryReader) && m_zipHandler.Dirty == Dirtiness.Clean)
							{
								session.OnException(new InvalidZipStructureException("The information contained in the local header does not match the information of the central header.", m_zipHandler.LastZipFile));
							}
						}
						finally
						{
							binaryReader.Close();
						}
						zipStream.Lock(CompressedSize);
						return new Xceed.Utils.Streams.WindowStream(zipStream, CompressedSize);
					}
					catch
					{
						zipStream.Close();
						throw;
					}
				}
				return m_tempFile.OpenRead();
			}
			catch (FileSystemIOException inner)
			{
				throw new ZipIOException(m_zipHandler.LastZipFile, inner);
			}
			catch (IOException inner2)
			{
				throw new ZipIOException(m_zipHandler.LastZipFile, inner2);
			}
		}

		public Stream OpenRead(FileSystemEventsSession session, FileShare share, string decryptionPassword)
		{
			if (!Exists)
			{
				throw new ItemDoesNotExistException(null);
			}
			try
			{
				if (m_isOpenWrite)
				{
					throw new FileSystemIOException("The file is currently opened for writing.", null, null);
				}
				if (m_openReadRefCount != 0)
				{
					if (!m_isFileShareRead)
					{
						throw new FileSystemIOException("The file is already opened for reading, and FileShare.Read is denied.", null, null);
					}
					if (share == FileShare.None)
					{
						throw new FileSystemIOException("The file is currently opened for reading.", null, null);
					}
				}
				Stream stream = null;
				m_zipHandler.AddRef(session);
				checked
				{
					try
					{
						stream = OpenRawRead(session);
						try
						{
							m_openReadRefCount++;
							m_isFileShareRead = (share & FileShare.Read) != 0;
							NotifyStream notifyStream = new NotifyStream(stream, m_header);
							notifyStream.ClosingStream += OnClosingReadingStreamEvent;
							stream = notifyStream;
						}
						catch
						{
							stream.Close();
							throw;
						}
					}
					catch
					{
						m_zipHandler.Release(session);
						throw;
					}
				}
				try
				{
					bool flag = true;
					if (Encrypted)
					{
						if (decryptionPassword.Length == 0)
						{
							throw new InvalidDecryptionPasswordException("The decryption password is empty.", m_zipHandler.LastZipFile);
						}
						switch (m_header.EncryptionMethod)
						{
						case EncryptionMethod.Compatible:
						{
							long userSeed = (m_header.HasDescriptorHeader ? m_header.OriginalEncryptionKey : (Crc32 >> 16));
							stream = new ZCryptStream(stream, decryptionPassword, userSeed, encrypt: false);
							break;
						}
						case EncryptionMethod.WinZipAes:
							Licenser.VerifyLicense(FeatureID.WinZipAes);
							stream = new AesEncryptionStream(stream, decryptionPassword, m_header.EncryptionStrength, writing: false);
							flag = false;
							break;
						default:
							throw new FileSystemInternalException("Unexpected encryption method.");
						}
					}
					try
					{
						stream = new CompressedStream(stream, CompressionMethod, CompressionLevel);
					}
					catch (ArgumentException inner)
					{
						throw new InvalidZipStructureException("The file is compressed with an unknown compression method.", m_zipHandler.LastZipFile, inner);
					}
					if (flag)
					{
						return new ChecksumStream(stream, ChecksumType.CRC32, (int)Crc32);
					}
					return stream;
				}
				catch
				{
					stream.Close();
					throw;
				}
			}
			catch (CompressionException inner2)
			{
				throw new ZipIOException("An exception occurred in the compression engine.", m_zipHandler.LastZipFile, inner2);
			}
			catch (IOException inner3)
			{
				throw new ZipIOException(null, inner3);
			}
		}

		public Stream OpenWrite(FileShare share, CompressionMethod compressionMethod, CompressionLevel compressionLevel, string encryptionPassword, EncryptionMethod encryptionMethod, int encryptionStrength)
		{
			if (IsFolder)
			{
				throw new FileSystemInternalException("An unexpected attempt was made to open a folder for writing.", m_zipHandler.LastZipFile);
			}
			if (!Exists)
			{
				throw new ItemDoesNotExistException(null);
			}
			if (m_isOpenWrite)
			{
				throw new FileSystemIOException("The file is currently opened for writing.", null, null);
			}
			if (m_openReadRefCount > 0)
			{
				throw new FileSystemIOException("The file is currently opened for reading.", null, null);
			}
			Stream stream = null;
			NotifyStream notifyStream = null;
			AbstractFile abstractFile = null;
			try
			{
				m_isOpenWrite = true;
				if (m_tempFile == null)
				{
					m_tempFile = m_zipHandler.TempFolder.CreateTempFile();
				}
				FileSystemEventsSession session = FileSystemEvents.Empty.CreateSession(this, null);
				m_zipHandler.AddRefModify(session);
				try
				{
					stream = m_tempFile.OpenWrite(overwrite: true, share);
					notifyStream = new NotifyStream(OpenWrite(stream, compressionMethod, compressionLevel, encryptionPassword, encryptionMethod, encryptionStrength), m_header);
					notifyStream.WritingToStream += OnWritingToStreamEvent;
					notifyStream.ClosingStream += OnClosingWritingStreamEvent;
				}
				catch
				{
					m_zipHandler.ReleaseModify(session);
					throw;
				}
				m_header.UncompressedSize = 0L;
				m_header.CompressedSize = 0L;
				m_header.Crc32 = 0u;
				m_header.CompressionMethod = (ZipCompressionMethod)checked((ushort)compressionMethod);
				m_header.CompressionLevel = compressionLevel;
				m_header.Encrypted = encryptionPassword.Length != 0;
				m_header.EncryptionMethod = encryptionMethod;
				m_header.EncryptionStrength = encryptionStrength;
				m_header.HasDescriptorHeader = true;
				return notifyStream;
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				notifyStream?.Close();
				stream?.Close();
				if (abstractFile != null)
				{
					try
					{
						abstractFile.Delete();
					}
					catch
					{
					}
					m_tempFile = null;
				}
				if (ex is FileSystemIOException || ex is IOException)
				{
					throw new ZipIOException(m_zipHandler.LastZipFile, ex);
				}
				throw;
			}
		}

		private Stream OpenWrite(Stream stream, CompressionMethod compressionMethod, CompressionLevel compressionLevel, string encryptionPassword, EncryptionMethod encryptionMethod, int encryptionStrength)
		{
			if (!Exists)
			{
				throw new ItemDoesNotExistException(null);
			}
			switch (compressionMethod)
			{
			case CompressionMethod.Deflated64:
				Licenser.VerifyLicense(FeatureID.Deflate64);
				break;
			case CompressionMethod.BZip2:
			case CompressionMethod.BWT:
				Licenser.VerifyLicense(FeatureID.BZip2_BWT);
				break;
			case CompressionMethod.PPMd:
				Licenser.VerifyLicense(FeatureID.PPMd);
				break;
			}
			CompressedStream compressedStream = null;
			Stream inner = stream;
			try
			{
				if (encryptionPassword.Length > 0)
				{
					switch (encryptionMethod)
					{
					case EncryptionMethod.Compatible:
						inner = new ZCryptStream(stream, encryptionPassword, (int)m_header.EncryptionKey, encrypt: true);
						break;
					case EncryptionMethod.WinZipAes:
						Licenser.VerifyLicense(FeatureID.WinZipAes);
						inner = new AesEncryptionStream(stream, encryptionPassword, encryptionStrength, writing: true);
						break;
					default:
						throw new FileSystemInternalException("Unexpected encryption method.");
					}
				}
				compressedStream = new CompressedStream(inner, compressionMethod, compressionLevel);
				return compressedStream;
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				if (compressedStream != null)
				{
					compressedStream.Close();
				}
				else
				{
					stream?.Close();
				}
				if (ex is CompressionException)
				{
					throw new ZipIOException("An exception occurred in the compression engine.", m_zipHandler.LastZipFile, ex);
				}
				throw;
			}
		}

		public void MoveFromZipToTemp(FileSystemEventsSession session)
		{
			if (IsFolder)
			{
				throw new FileSystemInternalException("Cannot move a folder's data to temp storage.");
			}
			if (m_tempFile != null)
			{
				return;
			}
			AbstractFile abstractFile = m_zipHandler.TempFolder.CreateTempFile();
			ZipEventsSession zipEventsSession = session as ZipEventsSession;
			zipEventsSession?.InitializeCurrentTotalBytes(CompressedSize);
			try
			{
				using (Stream stream = OpenRawRead(session))
				{
					using Stream stream2 = abstractFile.OpenWrite(session.Events, session.UserData, overwrite: true);
					byte[] array = new byte[8192];
					int num = 0;
					int num2 = 0;
					while ((num = stream.Read(array, 0, array.Length)) > 0)
					{
						if (zipEventsSession != null && num2 > 0)
						{
							zipEventsSession.OnGatheringZipContentByteProgression(num2);
						}
						num2 = num;
						stream2.Write(array, 0, num);
					}
				}
				zipEventsSession?.OnGatheringZipContentByteCompletion();
				m_tempFile = abstractFile;
			}
			catch
			{
				abstractFile.Delete();
				throw;
			}
		}

		public void WriteCentralHeader(ZipStream stream, EndHeader endHeader)
		{
			if (stream == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("stream");
			}
			if (endHeader == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("endHeader");
			}
			int size = 0;
			long centralDirectoryOffset = m_header.WriteCentralHeader(stream, out size);
			uint currentDiskNumber = m_zipHandler.CurrentDiskNumber;
			if (endHeader.CentralHeadersTotal == 0)
			{
				endHeader.CentralDirectoryOffset = centralDirectoryOffset;
				endHeader.CentralDiskNumber = m_zipHandler.CurrentDiskNumber;
			}
			else if (m_zipHandler.CurrentDiskNumber != currentDiskNumber)
			{
				endHeader.CentralHeadersCurrentDisk = 0uL;
			}
			checked
			{
				endHeader.CentralDirectorySize += (ulong)size;
				endHeader.CentralHeadersCurrentDisk++;
				endHeader.CentralHeadersTotal++;
			}
		}

		public void WriteLocalHeader(FileSystemEventsSession session, ZipStream stream)
		{
			if (stream == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("stream");
			}
			try
			{
				if (IsFolder)
				{
					m_header.HasDescriptorHeader = false;
				}
				else if (!Encrypted || EncryptionMethod != EncryptionMethod.Compatible)
				{
					m_header.HasDescriptorHeader = false;
				}
				int count = m_header.ExtraHeaders.Count;
				while (checked(count--) > 0)
				{
					ExtraHeader extraHeader = m_header.ExtraHeaders[count];
					if (!(extraHeader is UnknownExtraHeader) && ZipHandler.AvailableExtraHeaders.Find(extraHeader.Signature) == null)
					{
						m_header.ExtraHeaders.RemoveAt(count);
					}
				}
				int size = 0;
				long localHeaderOffset = m_header.WriteLocalHeader(stream, out size);
				m_header.LocalHeaderOffset = localHeaderOffset;
				m_header.LocalHeaderDisk = m_zipHandler.CurrentDiskNumber;
				if (!IsFolder)
				{
					WriteData(session, stream);
					WriteDescriptorHeader(stream);
				}
			}
			catch (FileSystemIOException inner)
			{
				throw new ZipIOException(m_zipHandler.LastZipFile, inner);
			}
			catch (IOException inner2)
			{
				throw new ZipIOException(m_zipHandler.LastZipFile, inner2);
			}
			finally
			{
				try
				{
					if (m_tempFile != null)
					{
						m_tempFile.Delete();
						m_tempFile = null;
					}
				}
				catch
				{
				}
			}
		}

		public void WriteData(FileSystemEventsSession session, Stream stream)
		{
			Stream stream2 = null;
			TransientStream transientStream = null;
			Stream stream3 = null;
			ZipEventsSession zipEventsSession = session as ZipEventsSession;
			try
			{
				byte[] array = new byte[8192];
				int num = 0;
				stream2 = OpenRawRead(session);
				transientStream = new TransientStream(stream);
				stream3 = transientStream;
				if (m_tempFile == null)
				{
					zipEventsSession?.InitializeCurrentTotalBytes(CompressedSize);
				}
				else
				{
					zipEventsSession?.InitializeCurrentTotalBytes(m_tempFile.Size);
				}
				try
				{
					zipEventsSession?.OnBuildingZipByteProgression(0L);
					while ((num = stream2.Read(array, 0, array.Length)) > 0)
					{
						stream3.Write(array, 0, num);
						zipEventsSession?.OnBuildingZipByteProgression(num);
					}
				}
				catch
				{
					zipEventsSession?.OnBuildingZipByteCompletion();
					throw;
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				stream2?.Close();
				stream3?.Close();
			}
		}

		public void WriteDescriptorHeader(ZipStream stream)
		{
			if (!m_header.HasDescriptorHeader || (stream.CanSeek && m_zipHandler.CurrentDiskNumber == m_header.LocalHeaderDisk && (!Encrypted || EncryptionMethod != EncryptionMethod.Compatible)))
			{
				if (m_header.HasDescriptorHeader)
				{
					m_header.HasDescriptorHeader = false;
					long position = stream.Position;
					stream.Seek(m_header.LocalHeaderOffset, SeekOrigin.Begin);
					int size = 0;
					m_header.WriteLocalHeader(stream, out size);
					stream.Seek(position, SeekOrigin.Begin);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				m_header.HasDescriptorHeader = true;
				int size2 = 0;
				m_header.WriteDescriptorHeader(stream, out size2);
			}
		}

		public void Create()
		{
			DateTime now = DateTime.Now;
			if (LastModifiedDateTime == DateTime.MinValue)
			{
				LastModifiedDateTime = now;
				CreationDateTime = now;
				LastAccessDateTime = now;
			}
			if (m_tempFile == null && !IsFolder)
			{
				m_tempFile = m_zipHandler.TempFolder.CreateTempFile();
			}
			if (IsFolder)
			{
				FolderPersistent = true;
			}
			m_zipHandler.Dirty = Dirtiness.Dirty;
			m_exists = true;
		}

		public void Delete()
		{
			try
			{
				if (m_tempFile != null)
				{
					m_tempFile.Delete();
					m_tempFile = null;
				}
			}
			catch
			{
			}
			m_zipHandler.Dirty = Dirtiness.Dirty;
			m_exists = false;
		}

		public void Replace(ZipItemHandler replaced)
		{
			if (m_children.Count > 0 || !m_exists || (IsFolder && !m_folderPersistent) || m_isOpenWrite || m_openReadRefCount > 0 || m_tempFile != null)
			{
				throw new FileSystemInternalException("Only a newly created ZipItemHandler can replace another ZipItemHandler.");
			}
			if (replaced.m_isOpenWrite || replaced.m_openReadRefCount > 0 || replaced.m_tempFile != null)
			{
				throw new FileSystemInternalException("Only an idle ZipItemHandler can get replaced.");
			}
			m_children = replaced.m_children;
		}

		public void AddChild(ZipItemHandler child)
		{
			if (m_children.Contains(child.FullName))
			{
				m_children.Remove(child.FullName);
			}
			m_children.Add(child.FullName, child);
			if (!m_exists && child.Exists)
			{
				Exists = true;
			}
		}

		public void IncrementFullName()
		{
			checked
			{
				if (++m_copyIndex == 1)
				{
					string text = m_header.FileName;
					string extension = Path.GetExtension(text);
					if (extension.Length > 0)
					{
						text = text.Substring(0, text.Length - extension.Length);
					}
					m_copyTemplate = $"{text}({{0}}){extension}";
				}
				m_header.FileName = string.Format(m_copyTemplate, m_copyIndex.ToString());
			}
		}

		private void OnReadingFromStreamEvent(object sender, DataNotifyStreamArgs args)
		{
		}

		private void OnWritingToStreamEvent(object sender, DataNotifyStreamArgs args)
		{
			ItemHeader itemHeader = (ItemHeader)args.UserData;
			if (!itemHeader.Encrypted || itemHeader.EncryptionMethod != EncryptionMethod.WinZipAes)
			{
				itemHeader.Crc32 = ChecksumStream.CalculateCrc32(args.Buffer, args.Offset, args.Count, itemHeader.Crc32);
			}
			checked
			{
				itemHeader.UncompressedSize += args.Count;
			}
		}

		private void OnClosingWritingStreamEvent(object sender, NotifyStreamArgs args)
		{
			NotifyStream notifyStream = sender as NotifyStream;
			notifyStream.WritingToStream -= OnWritingToStreamEvent;
			notifyStream.ClosingStream -= OnClosingWritingStreamEvent;
			m_header.CompressedSize = m_tempFile.Size;
			if (m_header.CompressedSize == 0)
			{
				m_header.CompressionMethod = ZipCompressionMethod.Stored;
				m_header.CompressionLevel = CompressionLevel.None;
				m_header.Encrypted = false;
			}
			if (!m_header.Encrypted || m_header.EncryptionMethod != EncryptionMethod.Compatible)
			{
				m_header.LastModifiedDateTime = DateTime.Now;
				m_header.LastAccessedDateTime = m_header.LastModifiedDateTime;
			}
			m_zipHandler.Dirty = Dirtiness.Dirty;
			m_isOpenWrite = false;
			FileSystemEventsSession session = FileSystemEvents.Empty.CreateSession(this, null);
			m_zipHandler.ReleaseModify(session);
		}

		private void OnClosingReadingStreamEvent(object sender, NotifyStreamArgs args)
		{
			NotifyStream notifyStream = sender as NotifyStream;
			notifyStream.ClosingStream -= OnClosingReadingStreamEvent;
			checked
			{
				m_openReadRefCount--;
				m_isFileShareRead = true;
				FileSystemEventsSession session = FileSystemEvents.Empty.CreateSession(this, null);
				m_zipHandler.Release(session);
			}
		}
	}

		};

		protected override ProductVersion[] LatestVersions => new ProductVersion[4]
		{
			new ProductVersion(Xceed.Utils.Licensing.LicenseProduct.XceedZipNETSfx, 3, 6),
			new ProductVersion(Xceed.Utils.Licensing.LicenseProduct.XceedZipNET, 3, 6),
			new ProductVersion(Xceed.Utils.Licensing.LicenseProduct.XceedFtpNET, 3, 6),
			new ProductVersion(Xceed.Utils.Licensing.LicenseProduct.XceedWorkflowActivitiesNET, 1, 1)
		};

		protected override Type LicenseeType => typeof(Licenser);

		public static Xceed.Utils.Licensing.XceedLicense CreateLicense(string licenseKey, Type licenserType)
		{
			Xceed.Utils.Licensing.XceedLicense xceedLicense = new ZipLicense();
			xceedLicense.Initialize(licenseKey, licenserType);
			if (xceedLicense.LicenseKey == null || xceedLicense.LicenseKey.Length == 0)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return xceedLicense;
		}

		private ZipLicense()
		{
		}
	}
	public class ZippedFile : AbstractFile
	{
		private ZipHandler m_zipHandler;

		private string m_fullName = string.Empty;

		public virtual CompressionLevel CompressionLevel
		{
			get
			{
				try
				{
					return GetItemHandler().CompressionLevel;
				}
				catch (Exception except)
				{
					throw PrepareException(except);
				}
			}
		}

		public virtual CompressionMethod CompressionMethod
		{
			get
			{
				try
				{
					return GetItemHandler().CompressionMethod;
				}
				catch (Exception except)
				{
					throw PrepareException(except);
				}
			}
		}

		public virtual int Crc32
		{
			get
			{
				try
				{
					return (int)GetItemHandler().Crc32;
				}
				catch (Exception except)
				{
					throw PrepareException(except);
				}
			}
		}

		public virtual long CompressedSize
		{
			get
			{
				try
				{
					return GetItemHandler().CompressedSize;
				}
				catch (Exception except)

					throw PrepareException(except);
				}
			}
		}
