		public virtual int DiskNumber
		{
			get
			{
				try
				{
					uint diskNumber = GetItemHandler().DiskNumber;
					if (diskNumber == uint.MaxValue)
					{
						return 0;
					}
					return (int)(diskNumber + 1);
				}
				catch (Exception except)
				{
					throw PrepareException(except);
				}
			}
		}

		public ExtraHeaders ExtraHeaders
		{
			get
			{
				ExtraHeaders extraHeaders = ExtraHeaders.None;
				ZipItemHandler itemHandler = m_zipHandler.GetItemHandler(m_fullName);
				foreach (ExtraHeader item in (IEnumerable)itemHandler.ExtraHeaders)
				{
					switch (item.Signature)
					{
					case 21838:
						extraHeaders |= ExtraHeaders.Unicode;
						break;
					case 10:
						extraHeaders |= ExtraHeaders.FileTimes;
						break;
					case 21589:
						extraHeaders |= ExtraHeaders.ExtendedTimeStamp;
						break;
					}
				}
				return extraHeaders;
			}
			set
			{
				if ((value & (ExtraHeaders.Unicode | ExtraHeaders.FileTimes | ExtraHeaders.ExtendedTimeStamp)) != value)
				{
					throw new ArgumentException("Unknown extra header flag.", "DefaultExtraHeaders");
				}
				FileSystemEventsSession session = FileSystemEvents.Empty.CreateSession(this, null);
				m_zipHandler.AddRefModify(session);
				try
				{
					ZipItemHandler itemHandler = m_zipHandler.GetItemHandler(m_fullName);
					ExtraHeader extraHeader = itemHandler.ExtraHeaders.Find(21838);
					if ((value & ExtraHeaders.Unicode) == ExtraHeaders.Unicode)
					{
						if (extraHeader == null)
						{
							itemHandler.ExtraHeaders.Add(new UnicodeExtraHeader());
							m_zipHandler.Dirty = Dirtiness.Dirty;
						}
					}
					else if (extraHeader != null)
					{
						itemHandler.ExtraHeaders.Remove(extraHeader);
						m_zipHandler.Dirty = Dirtiness.Dirty;
					}
					extraHeader = itemHandler.ExtraHeaders.Find(10);
					if ((value & ExtraHeaders.FileTimes) == ExtraHeaders.FileTimes)
					{
						if (extraHeader == null)
						{
							itemHandler.ExtraHeaders.Add(new FileTimesExtraHeader());
							m_zipHandler.Dirty = Dirtiness.Dirty;
						}
					}
					else if (extraHeader != null)
					{
						itemHandler.ExtraHeaders.Remove(extraHeader);
						m_zipHandler.Dirty = Dirtiness.Dirty;
					}
					extraHeader = itemHandler.ExtraHeaders.Find(21589);
					if ((value & ExtraHeaders.ExtendedTimeStamp) == ExtraHeaders.ExtendedTimeStamp)
					{
						if (extraHeader == null)
						{
							itemHandler.ExtraHeaders.Add(new ExtendedTimeStampExtraHeader());
							m_zipHandler.Dirty = Dirtiness.Dirty;
						}
					}
					else if (extraHeader != null)
					{
						itemHandler.ExtraHeaders.Remove(extraHeader);
						m_zipHandler.Dirty = Dirtiness.Dirty;
					}
				}
				finally
				{
					m_zipHandler.ReleaseModify(session);
				}
			}
		}

		public override AbstractFile HostFile => m_zipHandler.LastZipFile;

		protected override string DoName
		{
			get
			{
				try
				{
					return Xceed.Utils.Strings.StringCheck.ReplaceNull(Path.GetFileName(m_fullName));
				}
				catch (Exception except)
				{
					throw PrepareException(except);
				}
			}
			set
			{
				try
				{
					ZipItemHandler itemHandler = GetItemHandler();
					itemHandler.FullName = ZipHandler.GetParentFullName(m_fullName) + value;
					m_fullName = itemHandler.FullName;
				}
				catch (Exception except)
				{
					throw PrepareException(except);
				}
			}
		}

		protected override string DoFullName

				try

					return m_fullName;
				}
				catch (Exception except)

				}
			}
		}

		protected override bool DoHasCreationDateTime => true;

		protected override AbstractFolder DoParentFolder
		{
			get
			{
				try
				{
					string parentFullName = ZipHandler.GetParentFullName(m_fullName);
					if (parentFullName.Length == 1)
					{
						return new ZipArchive(m_zipHandler);
					}
					return new ZippedFolder(m_zipHandler, parentFullName);
				}
				catch (Exception except)
				{
					throw PrepareException(except);
				}
			}
		}

		protected override AbstractFolder DoRootFolder
		{
			get
			{
				try
				{
					return new ZipArchive(m_zipHandler);
				}
				catch (Exception except)
				{
					throw PrepareException(except);
				}
			}
		}

		protected override bool DoExists
		{
			get
			{
				try
				{
					FileSystemEventsSession session = FileSystemEvents.Empty.CreateSession(this, null);
					m_zipHandler.Refresh(session);
					return GetItemHandler().Exists;
				}
				catch (Exception except)
				{
					throw PrepareException(except);
				}
			}
		}

		protected override long DoSize
		{
			get
			{
				if (!base.Exists)
				{
					throw new ItemDoesNotExistException(this);
				}
				try
				{
					return GetItemHandler().UncompressedSize;
				}
				catch (Exception except)
				{
					throw PrepareException(except);
				}
			}
		}

		public ZippedFile(AbstractFile zipFile, string fileName)
			: this(FileSystemEvents.Empty, null, zipFile, fileName)
		{
		}

		public ZippedFile(FileSystemEvents events, object userData, AbstractFile zipFile, string fileName)
		{
			Licenser.VerifyLicense();
			if (events == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("events");
			}
			if (zipFile == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("zipFile");
			}
			if (fileName == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("fileName");
			}
			fileName = fileName.TrimEnd();
			if (fileName.Length == 0)
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("The file name is empty or contains only whitespace.", "fileName");
			}
			if (fileName.IndexOfAny(InvalidChars) != -1)
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("The file name contains invalid characters.", "fileName");
			}
			fileName = fileName.Replace(Path.AltDirectorySeparatorChar, Path.DirectorySeparatorChar);
			if (fileName.EndsWith(Path.DirectorySeparatorChar.ToString()))
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("The file name represents a folder.", "fileName");
			}
			if (!fileName.StartsWith(Path.DirectorySeparatorChar.ToString()))
			{
				fileName = Path.DirectorySeparatorChar + fileName;
			}
			FileSystemEventsSession session = events.CreateSession(this, userData);
			m_zipHandler = ZipHandler.GetZipHandler(session, zipFile);
			m_fullName = fileName;
		}

		public Stream OpenRead(string decryptionPassword)
		{
			return OpenRead(FileShare.Read, decryptionPassword);
		}

		public Stream OpenRead(FileShare share, string decryptionPassword)
		{
			return OpenRead(FileSystemEvents.Empty, null, share, decryptionPassword);
		}

		public Stream OpenRead(FileSystemEvents events, object userData, string decryptionPassword)
		{
			return OpenRead(events, userData, FileShare.Read, decryptionPassword);
		}

		public Stream OpenRead(FileSystemEvents events, object userData, FileShare share, string decryptionPassword)
		{
			if (events == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("events");
			}
			if (share != FileShare.None && share != FileShare.Read)
			{
				throw new FileSystemNotSupportedException("ZippedFile objects can only be opened with FileShare.None or FileShare.Read for reading.", this);
			}
			try
			{
				FileSystemEventsSession fileSystemEventsSession = events.CreateSession(this, userData);
				fileSystemEventsSession.CurrentItem = this;
				return DoOpenRead(fileSystemEventsSession, share, decryptionPassword);
			}
			catch (Exception except)
			{
				throw PrepareException(except);
			}
		}

		public Stream OpenWrite(bool overwrite, CompressionMethod compressionMethod, CompressionLevel compressionLevel, string encryptionPassword)
		{
			return OpenWrite(overwrite, FileShare.None, compressionMethod, compressionLevel, encryptionPassword);
		}

		public Stream OpenWrite(CompressionMethod compressionMethod, CompressionLevel compressionLevel, string encryptionPassword, EncryptionMethod encryptionMethod, int encryptionStrength)
		{
			return OpenWrite(FileSystemEvents.Empty, null, compressionMethod, compressionLevel, encryptionPassword, encryptionMethod, encryptionStrength);
		}

		public Stream OpenWrite(bool overwrite, FileShare share, CompressionMethod compressionMethod, CompressionLevel compressionLevel, string encryptionPassword)
		{
			return OpenWrite(FileSystemEvents.Empty, null, overwrite, share, compressionMethod, compressionLevel, encryptionPassword);
		}

		public Stream OpenWrite(FileSystemEvents events, object userData, bool overwrite, CompressionMethod compressionMethod, CompressionLevel compressionLevel, string encryptionPassword)
		{
			return OpenWrite(events, userData, overwrite, FileShare.None, compressionMethod, compressionLevel, encryptionPassword);
		}

		public Stream OpenWrite(FileSystemEvents events, object userData, CompressionMethod compressionMethod, CompressionLevel compressionLevel, string encryptionPassword, EncryptionMethod encryptionMethod, int encryptionStrength)
		{
			if (events == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("events");
			}
			if ((base.Attributes & FileAttributes.ReadOnly) == FileAttributes.ReadOnly)
			{
				throw new ItemIsReadOnlyException(this);
			}
			try
			{
				FileSystemEventsSession fileSystemEventsSession = events.CreateSession(this, userData);
				fileSystemEventsSession.CurrentItem = this;
				return DoOpenWrite(fileSystemEventsSession, overwrite: true, FileShare.None, compressionMethod, compressionLevel, encryptionPassword, encryptionMethod, encryptionStrength);
			}
			catch (Exception except)
			{
				throw PrepareException(except);
			}
		}

		public Stream OpenWrite(FileSystemEvents events, object userData, bool overwrite, FileShare share, CompressionMethod compressionMethod, CompressionLevel compressionLevel, string encryptionPassword)
		{
			if (events == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("events");
			}
			if (!overwrite)
			{
				throw new FileSystemNotSupportedException(this);
			}
			if ((base.Attributes & FileAttributes.ReadOnly) == FileAttributes.ReadOnly)
			{
				throw new ItemIsReadOnlyException(this);
			}
			if (share != FileShare.None)
			{
				throw new FileSystemNotSupportedException("ZippedFile objects can only be opened with FileShare.None for writing.", this);
			}
			try
			{
				FileSystemEventsSession fileSystemEventsSession = events.CreateSession(this, userData);
				fileSystemEventsSession.CurrentItem = this;
				return DoOpenWrite(fileSystemEventsSession, overwrite, share, compressionMethod, compressionLevel, encryptionPassword);
			}
			catch (Exception except)
			{
				throw PrepareException(except);
			}
		}

		internal ZippedFile(ZipHandler handler, string fullName)
		{
			if (handler == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("handler");
			}
			if (fullName == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("fullName");
			}
			m_zipHandler = ZipHandler.GetZipHandler(handler);
			m_fullName = fullName;
		}

		~ZippedFile()
		{
			if (m_zipHandler != null)
			{
				ZipHandler.RemoveZipHandler(m_zipHandler);
			}
		}

		protected override Stream DoOpenRead(FileSystemEventsSession session, FileShare share)
		{
			try
			{
				if (!base.Exists)
				{
					throw new ItemDoesNotExistException(this);
				}
				if (share != FileShare.None && share != FileShare.Read)
				{
					throw new FileSystemNotSupportedException("ZippedFile objects can only be opened with FileShare.None or FileShare.Read for reading.", this);
				}
				return DoOpenRead(session, share, m_zipHandler.DefaultDecryptionPassword);
			}
			catch (Exception except)
			{
				throw PrepareException(except);
			}
		}

		protected virtual Stream DoOpenRead(FileSystemEventsSession session, FileShare share, string decryptionPassword)
		{
			try
			{
				return GetItemHandler().OpenRead(session, share, decryptionPassword);
			}
			catch (Exception except)
			{
				throw PrepareException(except);
			}
		}

		protected override Stream DoOpenWrite(FileSystemEventsSession session, bool overwrite, FileShare share)
		{
			try
			{
				if (!base.Exists)
				{
					throw new ItemDoesNotExistException(this);
				}
				if (!overwrite)
				{
					throw new FileSystemNotSupportedException(this);
				}
				if ((base.Attributes & FileAttributes.ReadOnly) == FileAttributes.ReadOnly)
				{
					throw new ItemIsReadOnlyException(this);
				}
				if (share != FileShare.None)
				{
					throw new FileSystemNotSupportedException("ZippedFile objects can only be opened with FileShare.None for writing.", this);
				}
				return DoOpenWrite(session, overwrite, share, m_zipHandler.DefaultCompressionMethod, m_zipHandler.DefaultCompressionLevel, m_zipHandler.DefaultEncryptionPassword, m_zipHandler.DefaultEncryptionMethod, m_zipHandler.DefaultEncryptionStrength);
			}
			catch (Exception except)
			{
				throw PrepareException(except);
			}
		}

		protected virtual Stream DoOpenWrite(FileSystemEventsSession session, bool overwrite, FileShare share, CompressionMethod compressionMethod, CompressionLevel compressionLevel, string encryptionPassword)
		{
			try
			{
				return GetItemHandler().OpenWrite(share, compressionMethod, compressionLevel, encryptionPassword, EncryptionMethod.Compatible, 0);
			}
			catch (Exception except)
			{
				throw PrepareException(except);
			}
		}

		protected virtual Stream DoOpenWrite(FileSystemEventsSession session, bool overwrite, FileShare share, CompressionMethod compressionMethod, CompressionLevel compressionLevel, string encryptionPassword, EncryptionMethod encryptionMethod, int encryptionStrength)
		{
			try
			{
				return GetItemHandler().OpenWrite(share, compressionMethod, compressionLevel, encryptionPassword, encryptionMethod, encryptionStrength);
			}
			catch (Exception except)
			{
				throw PrepareException(except);
			}
		}

		protected override void DoCreate(FileSystemEventsSession session)
		{
			try
			{
				if (base.Exists)
				{
					throw new ItemAlreadyExistsException(this);
				}
				if (!base.ParentFolder.Exists)
				{
					throw new FileSystemInternalException("An unexpected attempt was made to create an item in a folder that does not exist.", this);
				}
				m_zipHandler.AddRefModify(session);
				try
				{
					GetItemHandler().Create();
				}
				finally
				{
					m_zipHandler.ReleaseModify(session);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception except)
			{
				throw PrepareException(except);
			}
		}

		protected override void DoDelete(FileSystemEventsSession session)
		{
			try
			{
				if (!base.Exists)
				{
					throw new ItemDoesNotExistException(this);
				}
				if ((base.Attributes & FileAttributes.ReadOnly) == FileAttributes.ReadOnly)
				{
					throw new ItemIsReadOnlyException(this);
				}
				m_zipHandler.AddRefModify(session);
				try
				{
					GetItemHandler().Delete();
				}
				finally
				{
					m_zipHandler.ReleaseModify(session);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception except)
			{
				throw PrepareException(except);
			}
		}

		protected override void DoRefresh(FileSystemEventsSession session)
		{
			try
			{
				m_zipHandler.Refresh(session);
			}
			catch (Exception except)
			{
				throw PrepareException(except);
			}
		}

		protected override void EnsureDateIsValid(DateTime value)
		{
		}

		public override bool IsSameAs(FileSystemItem target)
		{
			if (target == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (target is ZippedFile zippedFile)
			{
				if (m_zipHandler == zippedFile.m_zipHandler)
				{
					return m_fullName == zippedFile.m_fullName;
				}
				return false;
			}
			return false;
		}

		protected override bool IsPathRooted(string path)
		{
			return false;
		}

		protected override void DoApplyPropertiesFrom(FileSystemEventsSession session, FileSystemItem sourceItem)
		{
			if (m_zipHandler.DefaultEncryptionPassword.Length > 0 && m_zipHandler.DefaultEncryptionMethod == EncryptionMethod.Compatible && sourceItem.HasLastWriteDateTime)
			{
				try
				{
					base.LastWriteDateTime = sourceItem.LastWriteDateTime;
				}
				catch
				{
				}
			}
		}

		protected override void DoApplyPropertiesTo(FileSystemEventsSession session, FileSystemItem targetItem)
		{
			try
			{
				if (targetItem is ZippedFile zippedFile)
				{
					try
					{
						zippedFile.Comment = Comment;
					}
					catch (FileSystemException)
					{
					}
				}
				else if (targetItem is ZippedFolder zippedFolder)
				{
					try
					{
						zippedFolder.Comment = Comment;
					}
					catch (FileSystemException)
					{
					}
				}
			}
			catch (Exception except)
			{
				throw PrepareException(except);
			}
			base.DoApplyPropertiesTo(session, targetItem);
		}

		private ZipItemHandler GetItemHandler()
		{
			ZipItemHandler itemHandler = m_zipHandler.GetItemHandler(m_fullName);
			if (itemHandler == null)
			{
				throw new FileSystemInternalException("The main ZipHandler did not return a valid ZipItemHandler.");
			}
			return itemHandler;
		}

		private Exception PrepareException(Exception except)
		{
			if (except is FileSystemException ex)
			{
				if (except is ZipException { ZipFile: null } ex2)
				{
					ex2.ZipFile = m_zipHandler.LastZipFile;
				}
				if (ex.Item == null)
				{
					ex.Item = this;
				}
				return ex;
			}
			if (except is NotSupportedException)
			{
				return new FileSystemNotSupportedException(this, except);
			}
			if (Xceed.Utils.Exceptions.ExceptionFilter.IsPublicException(except))
			{
				return except;
			}
			return new FileSystemInternalException(this, except);
		}
	}
	internal class ZipStream : Stream
	{
		private bool m_disposed;

		private FileSystemEventsSession m_session;

		private ZipHandler m_handler;

		private Stream m_innerStream;

		private bool m_writing;

		private bool m_allowBreak = true;

		private long m_position = -1L;

		private long m_spanOffset;

		private bool m_locked;

		private long m_lockCount;

		public override bool CanRead
		{
			get
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				return m_innerStream.CanRead;
			}
		}

		public override bool CanSeek
		{
			get
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				return m_innerStream.CanSeek;
			}
		}

		public override bool CanWrite
		{
			get
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				return m_innerStream.CanWrite;
			}
		}

		public override long Length
		{
			get
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				return checked(m_innerStream.Length + m_spanOffset);
			}
		}

		private long InnerPosition
		{
			get
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				if (m_position == -1)
				{
					return m_innerStream.Position;
				}
				return m_position;
			}
			set
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				m_innerStream.Position = value;
			}
		}

		public override long Position
		{
			get
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				return checked(InnerPosition + m_spanOffset);
			}
			set
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				if (m_spanOffset != 0)
				{
					throw new ZipIOException("Cannot seek within a file when reading a spanned zip file.");
				}
				InnerPosition = value;
			}
		}

		public Stream InnerStream
		{
			get
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				return m_innerStream;
			}
		}

		public bool AllowBreak
		{
			get
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				return m_allowBreak;
			}
			set
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				m_allowBreak = value;
			}
		}

		internal uint DiskNumber
		{
			get
			{
				CheckIfDisposed();
				return m_handler.CurrentDiskNumber;
			}
		}

		public ZipStream(FileSystemEventsSession session, ZipHandler handler, Stream readStream)
		{
			if (handler == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("handler");
			}
			if (readStream == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("readStream");
			}
			if (!readStream.CanRead)
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("The inner stream must be readable.");
			}
			if (!readStream.CanSeek)
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("The inner stream must be seekable.");
			}
			m_innerStream = readStream;
			m_session = session;
			m_handler = handler;
		}

		public ZipStream(FileSystemEventsSession session, ZipHandler handler)
		{
			if (handler == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("handler");
			}
			AbstractFile currentZipFile = handler.CurrentZipFile;
			if (!currentZipFile.Exists)
			{
				currentZipFile.Create();
			}
			m_innerStream = currentZipFile.OpenWrite(overwrite: true);
			m_session = session;
			m_handler = handler;
			m_writing = true;
			if (!m_innerStream.CanSeek)
			{
				m_position = 0L;
			}
		}

		public override void Flush()
		{
			CheckIfDisposed();
			m_innerStream.Flush();
		}

		public override long Seek(long offset, SeekOrigin origin)
		{
			CheckIfDisposed();
			return m_innerStream.Seek(offset, origin);
		}

		public override void SetLength(long value)
		{
			CheckIfDisposed();
			m_innerStream.SetLength(value);
		}

		public override int Read(byte[] buffer, int offset, int count)
		{
			CheckIfDisposed();
			if (m_locked)
			{
				if (m_lockCount == 0)
				{
					return 0;
				}
				if (count > m_lockCount)
				{
					count = (int)m_lockCount;
				}
			}
			int num = m_innerStream.Read(buffer, offset, count);
			checked
			{
				if (num == 0 && m_handler.LastDiskNumber != 0 && m_handler.LastDiskNumber != uint.MaxValue)
				{
					ChangeDisk(DiskNumber + 1, DiskRequiredReason.Reading);
					return Read(buffer, offset, count);
				}
				if (m_locked)
				{
					m_lockCount -= num;
				}
				return num;
			}
		}

		public override void Write(byte[] buffer, int offset, int count)
		{
			CheckIfDisposed();
			checked
			{
				if (m_handler.SplitSize > 0)
				{
					if (InnerPosition == m_handler.SplitSize || (!m_allowBreak && InnerPosition + count > m_handler.SplitSize))
					{
						ChangeDisk(DiskNumber + 1, DiskRequiredReason.SplitSizeReached);
					}
					if (InnerPosition + count > m_handler.SplitSize)
					{
						if (!m_allowBreak)
						{
							throw new ZipIOException("The split size is too small.");
						}
						int num = unchecked((int)(m_handler.SplitSize - InnerPosition));
						InternalWrite(buffer, offset, num);
						Write(buffer, offset + num, count - num);
						return;
					}
				}
				InternalWrite(buffer, offset, count);
			}
		}

		public void WriteNoBreak(byte[] buffer, int offset, int count)
		{
			m_allowBreak = false;
			try
			{
				Write(buffer, offset, count);
			}
			finally
			{
				m_allowBreak = true;
			}
		}

		private void InternalWrite(byte[] buffer, int offset, int count)
		{
			bool flag = false;
			_ = InnerPosition;
			checked
			{
				do
				{
					flag = false;
					try
					{
						m_innerStream.Write(buffer, offset, count);
						if (m_handler.AllowSpanning)
						{
							m_innerStream.Flush();
						}
						if (m_position != -1)
						{
							m_position += count;
						}
					}
					catch (IOException)
					{
						if (!m_handler.AllowSpanning)
						{
							throw;
						}
						ChangeDisk(DiskNumber + 1, DiskRequiredReason.DiskFull);
						flag = true;
						_ = InnerPosition;
					}
				}
				while (flag);
			}
		}

		internal void Lock(long lockCount)
		{
			if (lockCount < 0)
			{
				Xceed.Utils.Exceptions.ThrowException.ThrowArgumentOutOfRangeException("lockCount", lockCount, "The lock count cannot be less than zero.");
			}
			if (DiskNumber == uint.MaxValue)
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException("Cannot lock while current disk number is unknown.");
			}
			m_locked = true;
			m_lockCount = lockCount;
		}

		internal void ChangeDisk(uint diskNumber, DiskRequiredReason reason)
		{
			bool flag = false;
			if (m_writing && InnerPosition == 0)
			{
				flag = true;
			}
			checked
			{
				if (m_writing)
				{
					m_innerStream.Close();
				}
				else
				{
					m_spanOffset += m_innerStream.Position;
					m_handler.ReleaseStream(m_session, m_innerStream);
				}
				m_innerStream = null;
				if (flag)
				{
					m_handler.CurrentZipFile.Delete();
					diskNumber--;
				}
				m_handler.ChangeDisk(m_session as ZipEventsSession, diskNumber, reason);
				if (m_writing)
				{
					AbstractFile currentZipFile = m_handler.CurrentZipFile;
					if (!currentZipFile.Exists)
					{
						currentZipFile.Create();
					}
					m_innerStream = currentZipFile.OpenWrite(m_session.Events, m_session.UserData, overwrite: true);
					if (m_position != -1)
					{
						m_position = 0L;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					m_innerStream = m_handler.RawOpenRead(m_session);
				}
			}
		}

		protected override void Dispose(bool disposing)
		{
			if (m_disposed)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (disposing && m_innerStream != null)
			{
				if (m_writing)
				{
					m_innerStream.Close();
				}
				else
				{
					m_handler.ReleaseStream(m_session, m_innerStream);
				}
			}
			m_innerStream = null;
			m_disposed = true;
		}

		private void CheckIfDisposed()
		{
			if (m_disposed)
			{
				throw new ObjectDisposedException("TransientStream", "Cannot access a stream once it has been closed.");
			}
		}
	}
}
